Our code is available at https://github.com/XinshuangL/Diverse-Video.

</details>


### [109] [SegMoTE: Token-Level Mixture of Experts for Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19213)
*Yujie Lu,Jingwen Li,Sibo Ju,Yanzhou Su,he yao,Yisong Liu,Min Zhu,Junlong Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SegMoTE提出了一种高效自适应的医学图像分割框架，在保留SAM原始接口和零样本泛化能力的同时，仅引入少量可学习参数实现跨模态适配，并通过渐进式提示分词机制实现全自动分割。在不到现有大规模数据集1%的MedSeg-HQ上训练即达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用交互式分割模型（如SAM）迁移到医学影像领域面临两大瓶颈：缺乏针对模态和解剖任务的适配机制导致泛化不足；当前医学适配方法在大量异质数据集上无筛选微调，引入噪声监督、成本高且导致负迁移。

Method: 提出SegMoTE框架，保留SAM的提示接口和高效推理，仅引入少量可学习参数实现动态跨模态适配；设计渐进式提示分词机制（progressive prompt tokenization）实现全自动分割，降低标注依赖。

Result: 在精心筛选的数据集MedSeg-HQ（规模不足现有大型数据集的1%）上训练，SegMoTE在多种成像模态和解剖任务中均达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: SegMoTE是首个在极低标注成本下将通用分割模型高效、鲁棒、可扩展地适配到医学领域的工作，推动了基础视觉模型在临床应用中的实际部署。

Abstract: Medical image segmentation is vital for clinical diagnosis and quantitative analysis, yet remains challenging due to the heterogeneity of imaging modalities and the high cost of pixel-level annotations. Although general interactive segmentation models like SAM have achieved remarkable progress, their transfer to medical imaging still faces two key bottlenecks: (i) the lack of adaptive mechanisms for modality- and anatomy-specific tasks, which limits generalization in out-of-distribution medical scenarios; and (ii) current medical adaptation methods fine-tune on large, heterogeneous datasets without selection, leading to noisy supervision, higher cost, and negative transfer. To address these issues, we propose SegMoTE, an efficient and adaptive framework for medical image segmentation. SegMoTE preserves SAM's original prompt interface, efficient inference, and zero-shot generalization while introducing only a small number of learnable parameters to dynamically adapt across modalities and tasks. In addition, we design a progressive prompt tokenization mechanism that enables fully automatic segmentation, significantly reducing annotation dependence. Trained on MedSeg-HQ, a curated dataset less than 1% of existing large-scale datasets, SegMoTE achieves SOTA performance across diverse imaging modalities and anatomical tasks. It represents the first efficient, robust, and scalable adaptation of general segmentation models to the medical domain under extremely low annotation cost, advancing the practical deployment of foundation vision models in clinical applications.

</details>


### [110] [Unmixing ATR-μFTIR spectroscopic images of cross-sections of historical oil paintings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06673)
*Shivam Pande,Nicolas Nadisic,Francisco Mederos-Henry,Aleksandra Pizurica*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于无监督CNN自编码器的ATR-μFTIR高光谱图像盲解混方法，通过WSAD损失函数自动处理带间可靠性和光谱伪影，成功应用于凡·艾克兄弟《根特祭坛画》的颜料层分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前ATR-μFTIR高光谱成像数据分析严重依赖人工与参考库比对的流程，耗时、主观且难以规模化；光谱混合、大气伪影和采集噪声等问题加剧了解译难度。

Method: 采用无监督CNN自编码器进行盲解混，通过基于块的建模利用局部空间结构；提出加权光谱角度距离（WSAD）损失函数，自动根据空间平坦度、邻域一致性和光谱粗糙度计算带间可靠性权重，以抑制超过1500个波段上的大气与采集伪影。

Result: 在《根特祭坛画》ATR-μFTIR截面数据上，WSAD相比标准SAD训练显著改善了污染敏感光谱区域的可解译性，端元光谱与丰度图的估计更合理。

Conclusion: 所提出的无监督CNN自编码器结合WSAD损失能有效实现ATR-μFTIR高光谱图像的盲解混，降低对人工标注的依赖，提升文化遗产材料分析的自动化与可扩展性。

Abstract: Spectroscopic imaging (SI) has become central to heritage science because it enables non-invasive, spatially resolved characterisation of materials in artefacts. In particular, attenuated total reflection Fourier transform infrared microscopy (ATR-$μ$FTIR) is widely used to analyse painting cross-sections, where a spectrum is recorded at each pixel to form a hyperspectral image (HSI). Interpreting these data is difficult: spectra are often mixtures of several species in heterogeneous, multi-layered and degraded samples, and current practice still relies heavily on manual comparison with reference libraries. This workflow is slow, subjective and hard to scale. We propose an unsupervised CNN autoencoder for blind unmixing of ATR-$μ$FTIR HSIs, estimating endmember spectra and their abundance maps while exploiting local spatial structure through patch-based modelling. To reduce sensitivity to atmospheric and acquisition artefacts across more than 1500 bands, we introduce a weighted spectral angle distance (WSAD) loss with automatic band-reliability weights derived from robust measures of spatial flatness, neighbour agreement and spectral roughness. Compared with standard SAD training, WSAD improves interpretability in contamination-prone spectral regions. We demonstrate the method on an ATR-$μ$FTIR cross-section from the Ghent Altarpiece by the Van Eyck brothers.

</details>


### [111] [PARSE: Part-Aware Relational Spatial Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07704)
*Yinuo Bai,Peijun Xu,Kuixiang Shao,Yuyang Jiao,Jingxuan Zhang,Kaixin Yao,Jiayuan Gu,Jingyi Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PARSE是一个显式建模物体部件间几何交互关系的框架，通过部件级装配图（PAG）和空间配置求解器实现无碰撞、物理有效的3D场景生成；基于该框架构建的PARSE-10K数据集（10,000个室内场景）和部件级接触图，可提升VL模型的空间推理能力，并改善3D生成模型的物理真实感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间关系表示（语言介词、物体级场景图）过于粗糙，无法精确指定物体哪些区域实际支撑、包含或接触彼此，导致布局模糊且物理不一致，亟需部件级（part-level）的空间关系建模。

Method: 提出PARSE框架，核心包括：(1) 部件级装配图（PAG），编码特定物体部件间的几何关系；(2) 部件感知空间配置求解器，将PAG关系转化为几何约束，组装无碰撞、物理有效的场景；(3) 基于真实图像布局先验和部件标注形状库构建PARSE-10K数据集（10,000个3D室内场景）及其密集接触结构和部件级接触图。

Result: 在PARSE-10K上微调Qwen3-VL，提升了物体级布局推理和部件级关系理解；将PAG作为结构先验用于3D生成模型，显著改善了场景的物理真实感和结构复杂性。

Conclusion: PARSE推动了基于几何基础的空间推理，支持生成物理一致的3D场景。

Abstract: Inter-object relations underpin spatial intelligence, yet existing representations -- linguistic prepositions or object-level scene graphs -- are too coarse to specify which regions actually support, contain, or contact one another, leading to ambiguous and physically inconsistent layouts. To address these ambiguities, a part-level formulation is needed; therefore, we introduce PARSE, a framework that explicitly models how object parts interact to determine feasible and spatially grounded scene configurations. PARSE centers on the Part-centric Assembly Graph (PAG), which encodes geometric relations between specific object parts, and a Part-Aware Spatial Configuration Solver that converts these relations into geometric constraints to assemble collision-free, physically valid scenes. Using PARSE, we build PARSE-10K, a dataset of 10,000 3D indoor scenes constructed from real-image layout priors and a curated part-annotated shape database, each with dense contact structures and a part-level contact graph. With this structured, spatially grounded supervision, fine-tuning Qwen3-VL on PARSE-10K yields stronger object-level layout reasoning and more accurate part-level relation understanding; furthermore, leveraging PAGs as structural priors in 3D generation models leads to scenes with substantially improved physical realism and structural complexity. Together, these results show that PARSE significantly advances geometry-grounded spatial reasoning and supports the generation of physically consistent 3D scenes.

</details>


### [112] [The Dual Mechanisms of Spatial Variable Binding in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22278)
*Kelly Cui,Nikhil Prakash,Shoval Messica,Ayush Raina,David Bau,Antonio Torralba,Tamar Rott Shaham*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示了视觉语言模型（VLM）中空间变量绑定的双重机制：语言模型骨干的中间层编码内容无关的空间关系，但仅起次要作用；主导来源是视觉编码器，其表示编码了物体布局且全局分布于所有图像token（包括背景区域），增强这些视觉空间表示可提升模型在COCO等复杂自然图像上的空间绑定性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对视觉语言模型如何实现物体与其属性、空间关系之间的绑定（即变量绑定）的计算机制尚不清楚，缺乏对空间信息在模型内部何处以及如何被表示的深入分析。

Method: 通过系统性的机制分析实验，分别考察语言模型骨干的中间层和视觉编码器在空间变量绑定中的作用，并通过增强视觉编码器产生的全局空间表示（跨越所有图像token）来验证其主导地位。

Result: 1) 语言模型骨干中间层编码内容无关的空间关系，但仅起次要作用；2) 视觉编码器是空间信息的主导来源，其表示全局分布于视觉token中，超出物体区域延伸至背景；3) 增强视觉空间表示可提升不同规模模型在COCO数据集上的空间绑定性能。

Conclusion: 明确了VLM中空间变量绑定的计算机制，强调了视觉编码器在其中的核心作用，为理解多模态模型的空间推理提供了新见解。

Abstract: Many multimodal tasks, such as image captioning and visual question answering, require vision-language models (VLMs) to bind objects with their properties and spatial relations. Yet it remains unclear where and how such associations are computed within VLMs. In this work, we show that VLMs rely on two concurrent mechanisms to represent spatial variable binding. In the language model backbone, intermediate layers represent content-independent spatial relations on top of visual tokens corresponding to objects. However, this mechanism plays only a secondary role in shaping model predictions. Instead, the dominant source of spatial information originates in the vision encoder, whose representations encode the layout of objects and are directly exploited by the language model backbone. Notably, this spatial signal is distributed globally across visual tokens, extending beyond object regions into surrounding background areas. We show that enhancing these vision-derived spatial representations globally across all image tokens improves spatial variable binding performance across models of various sizes on complex natural images from the COCO datasets. Together, our results clarify how spatial variable binding is computed within VLMs and highlight the central role of vision encoders in enabling it.

</details>


### [113] [OmniSch: A Multimodal PCB Schematic Benchmark For Structured Diagram Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00270)
*Taiting Lu,Kaiyuan Lin,Yuxin Tian,Mingjia Wang,Yubo Wang,Muchuan Wang,Sharique Khatri,Akshit Kartik,Yixi Wang,Amey Santosh Rane,Yida Wang,Sung-Liang Chen,Yifan Yang,Yi-Chao Chen,Yincheng Jin,Mahanth Gowda*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OmniSch，首个评估大语言模型（LMMs）理解印制电路板（PCB）原理图并构建空间加权网表图（包含元件属性、连接性和几何信息）的综合基准。包含1,854张真实原理图，涵盖视觉定位、图推理、几何推理和工具增强推理四个任务。实验表明当前LMMs在原理图工程理解上存在严重不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LMMs在视觉定位、文档理解和图表推理方面进展迅速，但它们将PCB原理图转换为机器可读的空间加权网表图（同时捕获元件属性、连接性和几何信息）的能力尚未被探索，而这种图表示是电子设计自动化（EDA）工作流程的基础。现有工作在原理图理解与网表图构建方面存在空白。

Method: 构建OmniSch基准，包含1,854张真实世界PCB原理图，设计四项评估任务：(1) 原理图实体的视觉定位（109.9K标注实例，423.4K语义标签对齐到视觉区域）；(2) 图推理（理解拓扑关系）；(3) 几何推理（为每条连接构建布局相关的权重）；(4) 工具增强的智能体推理（调用外部工具辅助完成前三项任务）。

Result: 当前LMMs在原理图工程解释上存在显著差距，包括：(1) 细粒度定位不可靠；(2) 布局到图的解析脆弱；(3) 全局连接性推理不一致；(4) 视觉探索效率低下。

Conclusion: 现有LMMs在理解PCB原理图工程制图方面能力严重不足，无法可靠完成从原理图到空间加权网表图的转换任务，表明该领域仍有巨大的改进空间。

Abstract: Recent large multimodal models (LMMs) have made rapid progress in visual grounding, document understanding, and diagram reasoning tasks. However, their ability to convert Printed Circuit Board (PCB) schematic diagrams into machine-readable spatially weighted netlist graphs, jointly capturing component attributes, connectivity, and geometry, remains largely underexplored, despite such graph representations are the backbone of practical electronic design automation (EDA) workflows. To bridge this gap, we introduce OmniSch, the first comprehensive benchmark designed to assess LMMs on schematic understanding and spatial netlist graph construction. OmniSch contains 1,854 real-world schematic diagrams and includes four tasks: (1) visual grounding for schematic entities, with 109.9K grounded instances aligning 423.4K diagram semantic labels to their visual regions; (2) diagram-to-graph reasoning, understanding topological relationship among diagram elements; (3) geometric reasoning, constructing layout-dependent weights for each connection; and (4) tool-augmented agentic reasoning for visual search, invoking external tools to accomplish (1)-(3). Our results reveal substantial gaps of current LMMs in interpreting schematic engineering artifacts, including unreliable fine-grained grounding, brittle layout-to-graph parsing, inconsistent global connectivity reasoning and inefficient visual exploration.

</details>


### [114] [Rein3D: Reinforced 3D Indoor Scene Generation with Panoramic Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10578)
*Dehui Wang,Rong Wei,Yue Shi,Congsheng Xu,Shoufa Chen,Dingxiang Luo,Tianshuo Yang,Xiaokang Yang,Wei Sui,Yusen Qin,Rui Tang,Yao Mu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Rein3D提出了一种"恢复-精炼"框架，通过将显式3D高斯泼溅与视频扩散模型的时间连续先验耦合，从稀疏输入重建完整360度室内环境，在长距离相机探索上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在从稀疏输入推断大量缺失几何时难以保持全局一致性，常产生局部合理但全局不一致的重建结果。

Method: 采用"恢复-精炼"范式：(1) 径向探索策略从原点出发渲染不完美的全景视频，揭示粗3DGS初始化中被遮挡的区域；(2) 全景视频到视频扩散模型恢复序列，再经视频超分增强；(3) 精炼视频作为伪真值更新全局3D高斯场。同时构建了PanoV2V-15K数据集。

Result: 生成逼真且全局一致的3D场景，在长距离相机探索任务上显著优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: Rein3D通过结合3DGS与视频扩散先验，有效解决了稀疏输入下室内场景重建的全局一致性问题。

Abstract: The growing demand for Embodied AI and VR applications has highlighted the need for synthesizing high-quality 3D indoor scenes from sparse inputs. However, existing approaches struggle to infer massive amounts of missing geometry in large unseen areas while maintaining global consistency, often producing locally plausible but globally inconsistent reconstructions. We present Rein3D, a framework that reconstructs full 360-degree indoor environments by coupling explicit 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) with temporally coherent priors from video diffusion models. Our approach follows a "restore-and-refine" paradigm: we employ a radial exploration strategy to render imperfect panoramic videos along trajectories starting from the origin, effectively uncovering occluded regions from a coarse 3DGS initialization. These sequences are restored by a panoramic video-to-video diffusion model and further enhanced via video super-resolution to synthesize high-fidelity geometry and textures. Finally, these refined videos serve as pseudo-ground truths to update the global 3D Gaussian field. To support this task, we construct PanoV2V-15K, a dataset of over 15K paired clean and degraded panoramic videos for diffusion-based scene restoration. Experiments demonstrate that Rein3D produces photorealistic and globally consistent 3D scenes and significantly improves long-range camera exploration compared with existing baselines.

</details>


### [115] [Topology-Aware Skeleton Detection via Lighthouse-Guided Structured Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20123)
*Daoyong Fu,Xiang Zhang,Zhaohuan Zhan,Fan Yang,Ke Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对现有骨架检测方法忽视结构连续性的问题，本文提出Lighthouse-Skel，一种基于灯塔引导结构化推理的拓扑感知骨架检测方法。该方法通过双分支协同检测框架联合学习骨架置信场与结构锚点（端点和交叉点），并利用灯塔引导的拓扑补全策略，以检测到的交叉点和断点为灯塔，沿低代价路径重连不连续的骨架片段，从而在不牺牲检测精度的前提下显著提升骨架连续性和结构完整性。在四个公开数据集上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然图像中物体骨架对姿态或运动变化敏感，导致骨架结构易发生显著变化、检测困难且常出现不连续问题。现有方法仅关注点级骨架点检测，忽略了结构连续性对恢复完整骨架的重要性。

Method: 提出Lighthouse-Skel方法，包含：(1) 双分支协同检测框架，联合学习骨架置信场与结构锚点（端点/交叉点），通过点分支的空间分布引导网络关注拓扑脆弱区域；(2) 灯塔引导拓扑补全策略，以检测到的交叉点和断点为灯塔，沿低代价路径重连不连续骨架片段。

Result: 在四个公开数据集上，所提方法在保持竞争性检测精度的同时，显著提升了骨架连通性和结构完整性。

Conclusion: 本文验证了拓扑感知的结构化推理能够有效解决骨架检测中的不连续性问题，为提升骨架结构的完整性提供了新思路。

Abstract: In natural images, object skeletons are used to represent geometric shapes. However, even slight variations in pose or movement can cause noticeable changes in skeleton structure, increasing the difficulty of detecting the skeleton and often resulting in discontinuous skeletons. Existing methods primarily focus on point-level skeleton point detection and overlook the importance of structural continuity in recovering complete skeletons. To address this issue, we propose Lighthouse-Skel, a topology-aware skeleton detection method via lighthouse-guided structured inference. Specifically, we introduce a dual-branch collaborative detection framework that jointly learns skeleton confidence field and structural anchors, including endpoints and junction points. The spatial distributions learned by the point branch guide the network to focus on topologically vulnerable regions, which improves the accuracy of skeleton detection. Based on the learned skeleton confidence field, we further propose a lighthouse-guided topology completion strategy, which uses detected junction points and breakpoints as lighthouses to reconnect discontinuous skeleton segments along low-cost paths, thereby improving skeleton continuity and structural integrity. Experimental results on four public datasets demonstrate that the proposed method achieves competitive detection accuracy while substantially improving skeleton connectivity and structural integrity.

</details>


### [116] [Sketch2Arti: Sketch-based Articulation Modeling of CAD Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25781)
*Yi Yang,Hao Pan,Yijing Cui,Alla Sheffer,Changjian Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Sketch2Arti是一个基于草图交互的CAD物体关节建模系统，用户通过简单2D草图指定运动意图，系统自动识别可动部件并预测其运动参数，支持多关节迭代建模、类别无关训练和内部结构补全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关节建模方法依赖手动标注或预定义类别信息，无法利用设计师自然的草图表达来高效生成3D关节模型，亟需一种从2D草图直接推断3D关节结构与运动参数的自动化方法。

Method: 从选定视角输入2D草图和CAD模型，自动发现对应可动部件并预测运动参数；采用类别无关训练策略；对缺乏内部结构的壳体模型，通过用户草图引导实现可控内部补全。

Result: 实验和用户评估证明了Sketch2Arti在关节建模的有效性、可控性和泛化能力，能处理多样物体且不依赖类别信息。

Conclusion: Sketch2Arti首次实现了基于草图的CAD物体关节建模，桥接了2D草图与3D关节模型之间的鸿沟，为交互式3D建模提供了高效、直观的新范式。

Abstract: Articulation modeling aims to infer movable parts and their motion parameters for a 3D object, enabling interactive animation, simulation, and shape editing. In this paper, we present Sketch2Arti, the first sketch-based articulation modeling system for CAD objects. Our key observation is that designers naturally communicate articulation intent through lightweight sketches (e.g., arrows and strokes) that indicate how parts should move, yet translating such sketches into articulated 3D models remains largely manual. Sketch2Arti bridges this gap by enabling users to specify articulation through simple 2D sketches drawn from a chosen viewpoint. Given a CAD model and user sketches, our approach automatically discovers the corresponding movable parts and predicts their motion parameters, allowing iterative modeling of multiple articulations on complex objects with fine-grained control. Importantly, Sketch2Arti is trained in a category-agnostic manner without requiring object category information, leading to strong generalization to diverse objects beyond existing articulation datasets. Moreover, for shell models lacking interior structures, Sketch2Arti supports controllable internal completion guided by user sketches, generating plausible internal components consistent with the existing geometry and predicted motion constraints. Comprehensive experiments and user evaluations demonstrate the effectiveness, controllability, and generalization of Sketch2Arti. The code, dataset, and the prototype system are at https://arlo-yang.github.io/Sketch2Arti.

</details>


### [117] [You Only Landmark Once: Lightweight U-Net Face Super Resolution with YOLO-World Landmark Heatmaps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14166)
*Riccardo Carraro,Anna Briotto,Endi Hysa,Marco Fiorucci,Lamberto Ballan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种轻量级U-Net架构，利用YOLO-World生成的热力图作为引导损失，在不增加对抗训练或辅助网络的情况下，实现16×16到128×128的人脸超分辨率重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖复杂网络架构、对抗训练或独立对齐网络，导致模型复杂度高、计算成本大。本文旨在以轻量级网络实现极端放大因子下的人脸超分辨率，避免额外开销。

Method: 基于轻量级U-Net架构，使用YOLO-World开放词汇目标检测器生成热力图定位关键面部特征（眼睛、鼻子、嘴），将热力图转换为空间权重形成引导损失，强调语义重要区域的重建误差，无需辅助训练或独立对齐网络。

Result: 在对齐的CelebA数据集上，所提损失函数持续提升了定量指标，生成了更清晰、更逼真的重建结果。

Conclusion: 轻量级网络能够有效利用检测驱动的先验知识实现感知上令人信服的极端放大，无需对抗训练或增加计算成本。

Abstract: Face image super-resolution aims to recover high-resolution facial images from severely degraded inputs. Under extreme upscaling factors, fine facial details are often lost, making accurate reconstruction challenging. Existing methods typically rely on heavy network architectures, adversarial training schemes, or separate alignment networks, increasing model complexity and computational cost. To address these issues, we propose a lightweight U-Net based-architecture designed to reconstructs $128{ \times }128$ facial images from severely degraded $16{ \times }16$ inputs, achieving an $8 \times $ magnification. A key contribution is a novel auxiliary-training-free supervision strategy that leverages heatmaps generated by YOLO-World, an open-vocabulary object detector, to localize key facial features such as eyes, nose, and mouth. These heatmaps are converted into spatial weights to form a heatmap-guided loss that emphasizes reconstruction errors in semantically important regions. Unlike prior methods that require dedicated landmark or alignment networks, our approach directly reuses detector outputs as supervision, maintaining an efficient training and inference pipeline. Experiments on the aligned CelebA dataset demonstrate that the proposed loss consistently improves quantitative metrics and produces sharper, more realistic reconstructions. Overall, our results show that lightweight networks can effectively exploit detection-driven priors for perceptually convincing extreme upscaling, without adversarial training or increased computational cost.

</details>


### [118] [Physics Guided Conditional Diffusion Framework for Generative Inverse Design of Manufacturable Metasurface based Absorbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19611)
*Vineetha Joy,Jamshed Palai,Satwik Sahu,Anshuman Kumar,Amit Sethi,Hema Singh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种物理引导的条件质量增强扩散框架，用于超表面吸收器的逆向设计。该框架通过特征级线性调制实现连续频谱条件的稳定生成，集成预训练代理仿真器确保电磁一致性，并在2-18 GHz频率范围内实现了极低的频谱均方误差（0.0006）和高频带对齐精度（0.958），生成时间约30秒，而传统方法需数月。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于迭代全波仿真的逆向设计方法计算成本高昂，难以处理大设计空间；现有生成模型条件可控性差且缺乏制造感知能力。因此需要一种既能满足严苛频谱规格又能保证可制造性的高效逆向设计方法。

Method: 提出物理引导条件质量增强扩散框架：1) 引入制造感知约束确保设计可制造性；2) 采用特征级线性调制（FiLM）机制在去噪层级中传播连续频谱条件；3) 在扩散训练流程中集成预训练代理电磁仿真器，将电磁一致性直接嵌入生成学习过程。

Result: 在2-18 GHz频段生成物理可实现的超表面设计，平均频谱均方误差低至0.0006，频带对齐精度达0.958。生成时间约30秒，而传统方法需数月。实验测量验证了模型效率。此外，框架能够为同一目标响应生成结构不同但频谱一致的多种超表面设计，解决了逆向电磁设计的非唯一性问题。

Conclusion: 所提出的扩散框架实现了高效、可控且制造感知的超表面逆向设计，显著优于传统方法，具备实际应用价值。

Abstract: Inverse design of metasurfaces under continuous electromagnetic constraints requires generation of geometries that simultaneously satisfy stringent spectral specifications and remain manufacturable. Conventional approaches based on iterative full wave simulations are computationally prohibitive for large design spaces, while existing generative models often suffer from poor conditional controllability and limited fabrication awareness. In this regard, we propose a physics guided condition quality enhanced diffusion framework for the inverse design of metasurface based absorbers. Fabrication-aware constraints are incorporated to ensure practical realizability of the generated designs. The framework introduces a conditioning mechanism for continuous spectral specifications, wherein feature-wise linear modulation propagates the condition across the denoising hierarchy, enabling stable and accurate generation with improved spectral controllability. Further, to embed EM consistency directly into the generative learning process, a pre trained surrogate EM simulator is integrated within the diffusion training pipeline. The proposed framework generated physically realizable metasurface designs for diverse reflection characteristics in the frequency range of 2 to 18 GHz, achieving a very low average spectral mean squared error of 0.0006 and a high band alignment accuracy of 0.958. The framework also addresses the fundamentally non-unique nature of inverse EM design by enabling structured multimodal generation of geometrically distinct yet spectrally consistent metasurface designs for the same target response. The proposed model produces the suitable design in approximately 30 seconds, whereas the conventional approach can take several months under comparable computational resources. The efficiency of the model is also established via experimental measurements.

</details>


### [119] [Focus-then-Context: Subject-Centric Progressive Visual Token Reduction for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20950)
*Yulin Zhao,Zheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SPpruner，一种模拟人类视觉"聚焦-上下文"机制的渐进式视觉标记缩减范式，通过主体中心化方法在保持高精度（仅0.6%准确率下降）的同时实现最高2.53倍加速和67%的计算量缩减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉标记缩减方法虽能减轻计算负担，但无意中保留了与用户查询严格对齐的孤立视觉主体，未能充分探索显著主体及其上下文关系。

Method: 1) 焦点识别模块：显式建模视觉显著性与语义相关性之间的交互，挖掘全面的视觉主体谱系；2) 上下文感知结构扫描模块：聚合邻域区域的上下文线索，恢复全局关系依赖以维护保留主体的结构完整性。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-VL上仅保留22.2%视觉标记实现2.53倍加速；在LLaVA上实现67% FLOPs缩减，仅0.6%准确率下降，持续优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: SPpruner通过模拟人类视觉感知的"聚焦-上下文"机制，有效平衡了视觉标记缩减效率与语义完整性，为VLM推理加速提供了新范式。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) face a bottleneck of prohibitive computational costs arising from massive visual token sequences during inference. Existing vision token reduction methods alleviate this burden, but they unintentionally preserve the isolated visual subject strictly aligned with the user's query, which fails to substantially explore salient subjects and their contextual relationships. In this paper, we propose SPpruner, a subject-centric progressive reduction paradigm that emulates the \textit{Focus-then-Context} mechanism of the human visual perception system. Specifically, we first construct a focus identification module to explicitly model the interplay between visual saliency and semantic relevance. Herein, it can excavate the comprehensive visual subject spectrum to ensure a high-fidelity representation of visual input. Subsequently, a context-aware structural scanning module is developed to aggregate contextual cues from neighboring regions. As such, it can effectively restore global relational dependencies to uphold the structural integrity of the preserved subjects. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our paradigm consistently outperforms SOTA methods, achieving up to 2.53 times speedup with only 22.2% of visual tokens retained in Qwen2.5-VL and a 67% FLOPs reduction on LLaVA with a negligible 0.6% accuracy drop.

</details>


### [120] [Stream3D: Sequential Multi-View 3D Generation via Evidential Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21472)
*Kaichen Zhou,Zeyang Bai,Xinhai Chang,Mengyu Wang,Paul Liang,Fangneng Zhan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Stream3D 是一种无需训练的视频流式 3D 生成机制，通过紧凑的证据记忆缓存最富信息的历史帧，将冻结的视图条件 3D 生成器转化为跨块一致的流式生成器，在保持恒定的内存占用下显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的视觉观测常以长单目流的形式到达，但现有视图条件 3D 生成器逐帧独立处理会导致严重的时序不一致性。

Method: 提出 Stream3D，一种无需训练的流式机制，通过维护紧凑的**证据记忆**（evidential memory），基于**证据分数机制**选择性缓存最富信息的历史帧，并动态更新以保持固定大小，从而将冻结的视图条件 3D 生成器转化为流式生成器。

Result: 在真实与合成流式基准上，Stream3D 在光度指标和几何指标上均优于 KV-cache 复用、基于流的特征编辑等潜在传输基线方法。

Conclusion: Stream3D 在不更改底层生成器（无需重新训练、架构修改或辅助损失）的前提下，有效解决了长单目流式输入下的时序一致性问题，且内存占用不随序列长度线性增长。

Abstract: View-conditioned 3D generators such as SAM 3D, TRELLIS, and Hunyuan3D produce high-quality object reconstructions from a single view, but real-world visual observation often arrives as long monocular streams. Naively applying these generators to each streaming frame independently leads to severe temporal inconsistency in the generated results. To address this problem, we propose Stream3D, the first training-free streaming mechanism that turns a frozen view-conditioned 3D generator into a streaming generator with constant cross-chunk memory. Stream3D achieves this by maintaining a compact evidential memory, which selectively caches the most informative historical frames based on a proposed evidence score mechanism. As the stream progresses, the memory dynamically updates to retain a fixed number of informative frames, preventing the memory footprint from growing linearly with sequence length. This also prevents degradation over long sequences and keeps the underlying generator completely unchanged without retraining, architectural modifications, or auxiliary losses. Evaluated on both realistic and synthetic streaming benchmarks, Stream3D outperforms latent-transport baselines, including KV-cache reuse and flow-based feature editing, across both photometric and geometric metrics. More details can be found at: https://stream-3d.github.io/stream3d.github.io/.

</details>


### [121] [ActQuant: Sub-4-bit Action-Guided Quantization for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24011)
*Arash Akbari,Arman Akbari,Masih Eskandar,Qitao Tan,Yixiao Chen,Jingwu Luo,Bertha Pangaribuan,Liyun Zhang,Jennifer Dy,Geng Yuan,Xue Lin,Gaowen Liu,Stratis Ioannidis,Yanzhi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ActQuant，一个面向视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的行动引导混合精度后训练量化框架，能在低于3 bits-per-weight的极端量化下保持高性能，同时配套OmniModel.cpp将量化模型部署到C/C++运行时，在LIBERO基准和真实UR3机械臂上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在具身智能中表现出色，但计算量巨大，难以部署在边缘平台。现有的后训练量化方法在激进的低比特（<4-bit）量化下性能严重下降，亟需一种能在极端量化下保持动作预测精度的量化方案。

Method: ActQuant包含两个阶段：(1) 张量间比特分配器——根据每个权重矩阵对预测智能体动作的贡献度分配单独的位宽；(2) 张量内尺度优化器——利用动作感知曲率调整逐块量化尺度，将动态范围集中在影响控制最关键的权重上。同时提出OmniModel.cpp，将量化后的架构转换为原生C/C++运行时，配备高效低比特内核。

Result: 在LIBERO基准上，ActQuant是唯一能在≤3 bits-per-weight下运行的方法，OpenVLA-OFT保留95.0%，π₀.₅保留94.8%；推进到2.5 bpw时OpenVLA-OFT保留90.1%，骨干网络从14.3 GB压缩至2.7 GB（5.3倍）。在真实UR3机械臂上，量化后的π₀.₅保持基线成功率，内存占用减少2.5倍。

Conclusion: ActQuant通过动作引导的混合精度后训练量化，在极端低位宽下实现了VLA模型的有效压缩与部署，且不牺牲任务性能，为边缘具身智能部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models exhibit remarkable action generation for embodied intelligence, but their heavy compute make deployment on edge platforms impractical. Aggressive, sub-4-bit weight quantization is the natural solution, yet existing post-training quantization (PTQ) methods suffer severe performance degradation in this regime. To address this, we introduce ActQuant, an action-guided mixed-precision PTQ framework that operates in two stages: (1) an inter-tensor bit allocator that assigns each weight matrix a single bit-width based on how much it contributes to predicting the agent's actions; (2) an intra-tensor scale optimizer tunes per-block quantization scales using action-aware curvature, so that dynamic range is concentrated on the weights most influential for control. To deliver the on-device benefits of our aggressive quantization, we further introduce OmniModel.cpp, an agentic conversion pipeline that ports architectures into a native C/C++ runtime with efficient low-bit kernels. We evaluate ActQuant both in simulation and on a real-world 6-DoF UR3 arm, with all models deployed through OmniModel.cpp. On the LIBERO benchmark, ActQuant is the only method that operates at or below 3 bits-per-weight, retaining 95.0% on OpenVLA-OFT and 94.8% on $π_{0.5}$. Pushed further, ActQuant reaches 2.5 bpw at 90.1% on OpenVLA-OFT, compressing the backbone from 14.3 GB to 2.7 GB (5.3$\times$). On the physical UR3 arm, $π_{0.5}$ quantized with ActQuant retains the baseline's success rate while reducing the memory footprint by 2.5$\times$.

</details>


### [122] [Broadband Hyperspectral 3D Imaging using Dispersed Structured Light](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25757)
*Suhyun Shin,Yunseong Moon,Ryota Maeda,David B. Lindell,Kiriakos N. Kutulakos,Seung-Hwan Baek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种宽带高光谱3D成像方法（BH3D），利用单个光谱仪和立体可见光/SWIR相机系统，在450-1500nm范围内同时重建密集高光谱反射率和精确3D几何，解决了传统方法仅限于可见光波段且需要复杂多光谱仪设计的难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统高光谱3D成像局限于可见光窄波段，无法捕获近红外和短波红外（SWIR）波段中丰富的材料次表面属性和成分信息，且现有方案需要复杂的多光谱仪设计，限制了实际应用。

Method: 提出单光谱仪BH3D系统，采用可见光相机和SWIR相机构成立体设置，将色散结构光扩展到宽带范围；通过建模宽带色散结构光的图像形成过程，联合估计高光谱反射率和深度信息。

Result: 在真实场景中实现平均光谱角0.13 rad、均方根误差0.03、平均深度误差4.5 mm的准确重建；成功演示了同色异谱材料识别、不透明层穿透成像、钞票隐藏特征揭示及血管显现等应用。

Conclusion: BH3D方法突破了传感器限制，利用单个光谱仪实现了可见光到SWIR的宽带高光谱3D成像，为材料分析、生物医学成像和安全检测等领域提供了新的技术途径。

Abstract: Hyperspectral 3D imaging enables the capture of dense spectral information and scene geometry but has traditionally been confined to narrow spectral windows, typically the visible range. In this work, we introduce a broadband hyperspectral 3D imaging (BH3D) method to extend this capability across the full visible-near-infrared and short-wavelength infrared (SWIR) spectrum (450-1500 nm). This broad coverage is critical as it captures complementary physical cues: visible wavelengths reveal surface appearance, while SWIR bands provide insight into subsurface properties and material composition. However, realizing BH3D is challenging due to fundamental sensor constraints between visible-spectrum silicon and SWIR-spectrum InGaAs sensors, which necessitate complex multi-spectrograph designs. Here we propose a single-spectrograph BH3D system, using a stereo setup comprising visible and SWIR cameras, that reconstructs dense broadband hyperspectral reflectance together with accurate 3D geometry. Our key idea is to extend dispersed structured light to the broadband regime using a single spectrograph. We model the image formation of broadband dispersed structured light, and estimate hyperspectral reflectance and depth. We validate our approach on diverse real-world scenes, demonstrating accurate reconstruction with a mean spectral angle mapper of 0.13 rad, root mean square error of 0.03, and mean depth error of 4.5 mm. We further demonstrate identifying metameric materials, performing imaging through opaque layers, uncovering hidden features on banknotes, and revealing blood vessels.

</details>


### [123] [An Analysis Focused on Womens Safety: Can VAD Models Be Enhanced by a Multi-modal Dataset?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25806)
*Sangeeta .,Maddikuntla Sai Prajwal,Debi Prosad Dogra,Kamalakar Vijay Thakare,Hyungjoo Jung,Ig-Jae Kim,Heeseung Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了ExtrAnom数据集，一个包含1001个视频的女性安全异常检测多模态基准，涵盖5种女性犯罪类型（跟踪、抢链、绑架、暗杀、骚扰），包含低光、低分辨率、远景等复杂场景，每个视频配有4个文本描述，实验表明现有数据集和方法不足以检测女性中心异常。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频异常检测数据集缺乏针对女性安全领域的专门数据，无法有效覆盖如抢链、跟踪、不当接触等女性犯罪模式，且存在光照、分辨率、拍摄距离等场景局限性。

Method: 构建ExtrAnom数据集：包含1001个视频（500正常+501异常），覆盖5类女性犯罪；视频包含低光(8%)、低分辨率(13%)、远景(15%)等挑战性场景；每个视频配有4个文本描述（1个人工+3个LLM生成）；在多个SOTA方法上进行了单模态和多模态基准测试。

Result: 实验表明现有数据集（XD-Violence、UCF-Crime、UCA）和SOTA方法在检测女性中心异常方面表现不足，验证了ExtrAnom数据集对女性安全异常检测任务的重要性。

Conclusion: ExtrAnom数据集填补了女性安全视频异常检测领域的空白，支持跨模态和VLM验证，有助于准确检测女性中心异常模式并生成视频级描述。

Abstract: Women's safety and security are paramount for a modern society. Crimes against women occur in daylight as well as in low-light conditions. Often, such events are captured through real-world surveillance cameras that operate at lower resolutions. Despite substantial progress in CV-related research, video anomaly detection (VAD) focused on women's safety has not yet been adequately addressed. Existing video anomaly datasets contain well-lit, high-resolution, close-shot videos, and fail to represent women-centric anomalies such as chain snatching, stalking, inappropriate touch, and other subtle forms of crime against women. To address these problems, we propose the ExtrAnom dataset, a new multi-modal benchmark containing 1001 videos with textual descriptions, 500 normal and 501 anomalous, classified into 5 different types of women-centric crimes. The dataset comprises low-light (8%), low-resolution videos (13%), long-shot (15%), along with daylight (64%) anomalous videos. And it covers anomalous events like stalking (3.9%), chain snatching (17.6%), kidnapping (7.3%), assassinations (2.3%), harassment (18.9%), and normal (50%). Each video is supplemented with 4 textual annotations, including one human-generated and three LLM-generated descriptions, enabling cross-modal and VLM-based validations. The aim of creating a women-centric dataset is to accurately detect the women-centric anomaly patterns, which are possible to observe visually. The dataset supplements the VLMs to accurately generate video-level descriptions. ExtrAnom has been benchmarked against popular unimodal and multi-modal VAD datasets (e.g., XD-Violence, UCF-Crime, and UCA) and SOTA methods. Experiments reveal that the existing datasets are insufficient to train models for detecting women-centric anomalies.

</details>


### [124] [Reason-Then-Retrieve for CoVR-R with Structured Edit Prompts and Dense-Sparse Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02450)
*DongQing Liu,MengShi Qi,HongWei Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CoVR-R，一个基于Qwen3.5-27B的零样本推理-检索流水线，用于解决推理感知的组合视频检索任务。通过生成检索导向的结构化描述和融合密集检索与TF-IDF排名，在验证集和测试集上取得了优异的性能（R@1分别为80.81和89.73）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合视频检索（CoVR）任务中，目标视频并非直接描述，而是需要通过参考视频和编辑指令推理出细粒度变化（如对象身份、动作顺序、最终状态、手部交互、场景转换），现有方法难以处理这种推理需求。

Method: 构建零样本推理-检索流水线：(1) 对每个图库视频，用Qwen3.5-27B生成检索导向的结构化描述，并通过池化生成令牌的隐藏状态（带令牌权重）得到密集嵌入；(2) 对每个查询，先对参考视频和指令进行编辑推理，再生成目标视频描述，其隐藏状态作为查询嵌入；(3) 补充TF-IDF分支对生成文本进行检索，用分裂特定权重融合两个排名。

Result: 验证集：R@1=80.81, R@5=94.86, R@10=97.11, R@50=98.59；盲测集：R@1=89.73, R@5=95.79, R@10=96.63, R@50=97.98。

Conclusion: 提出的零样本推理-检索流水线在CoVR-R基准上取得了当前最佳成绩，验证了生成式推理与多分支检索融合在复杂视频编辑检索任务中的有效性。

Abstract: CoVR-R studies reason-aware composed video retrieval: given a reference video and an edit instruction, the system must retrieve the target video that satisfies the edit. The main difficulty is that the target is not described directly; it must be inferred from fine-grained changes in object identity, action order, final state, hand interaction, and scene transition. We build a zero-shot reason-then-retrieve pipeline around Qwen3.5-27B. For each gallery video, the model generates a retrieval-oriented structured description and a dense embedding by pooling generated-token hidden states with token-dependent weights. For each query, the model first performs edit reasoning over the reference video and instruction, then generates a target-video description whose hidden states serve as the query embedding. We complement dense retrieval with a TF-IDF branch over the generated texts and fuse the two rankings with split-specific weights. On validation, the current best submission reaches 80.81 at R@1, 94.86 at R@5, 97.11 at R@10, and 98.59 at R@50. On the blind test split, it reaches 89.73 at R@1, 95.79 at R@5, 96.63 at R@10, and 97.98 at R@50.

</details>


### [125] [Pixel Cube: Diffusion-based Portrait Video Relighting Through Realistic Lighting Reproduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02919)
*Yufan Zhang,Yu Ji,Ayo Ajiboye,Rundi Wu,Yu Guo,Changxi Zheng,Jinwei Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于扩散模型的动态人像视频重光照方法，利用混合训练数据集（真实拍摄+渲染数据）和HDR环境图控制，实现了具有真实感、时间一致性和身份保持的视频重光照，在真实感、光照和谐性和时间一致性方面达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态人像视频重光照在真实感和时间一致性方面仍存在挑战，现有方法难以同时保证光照和谐、身份保持和时序稳定性。作者旨在利用预训练视频扩散模型的图像先验，结合LED光照系统采集的高质量数据，解决这一问题。

Method: 1) 构建LED光照系统用于真实光照模拟和高速视频重光照数据采集；2) 构建包含真实拍摄和渲染数据的混合训练数据集；3) 以逐帧HDR环境图作为光照控制信号，利用预训练视频扩散模型的先验知识训练生成模型；4) 额外使用合成背景图像控制相机曝光水平和色调。

Result: 模型在未见过的数据上具有良好的泛化能力，在真实感、光照和谐性和时间一致性方面达到SOTA性能，能够忠实保持主体的表情、肤色、皱纹和胡须等细微面部特征。

Conclusion: 基于扩散模型并结合混合训练数据与HDR环境图控制的方法能够有效实现动态人像视频的真实感重光照，在保持身份特征的同时实现时序一致且光照和谐的视觉效果，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: We present a diffusion-based method for relighting dynamic portrait videos with photorealism and temporal consistency. Our method is fueled by a hybrid training dataset that consists of real-captured and rendered dynamic portrait videos with diverse subject appearances, facial motions, head poses, and known lighting conditions. Specifically, we construct an LED-based lighting system for realistic lighting emulation and high-speed video relighting data acquisition. By leveraging the image priors embedded in pre-trained video diffusion models, and using per-frame high dynamic range (HDR) environment map as lighting control, we train a high-performance generative model for realistic and identity-preserving dynamic portrait video relighting. In addition to the environment map control, our model uses a synthesized background image to enable control on the camera's exposure level and color tone. Our model can produce temporally consistent relit portrait video that looks realistic and harmonious under a provided new environment and faithfully preserve the subject's expression and fine facial features, including skin tone, wrinkles, and facial hair. Our model generalizes well to unseen data, in terms of the subject appearance, motion, and lighting condition. We perform extensive experiments on relighting in-the-wild videos with various environment maps and demonstrate practical applications on portrait photography. Results show that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance in photorealism, lighting harmony, and temporal consistency.

</details>


### [126] [MorphoQuant: Modality-Aware Quantization for Omni-modal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04349)
*Yue Wu,Changyuan Wang,Zixuan Wang,Shilin Ma,Yansong Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MorphoQuant框架，通过分布感知偏置补偿（DABC）和形态导向量化函数优化（MDQFO）解决全模态大模型4比特量化中的跨模态分布异质性和异常值问题，在ScienceQA上W4A4模型达76.63%，超越W4A16基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统后训练量化方法在处理4比特全模态大模型时，面临跨模态分布极度异质性和异常值模式差异显著的挑战，导致量化精度严重下降。

Method: 提出MorphoQuant框架，包含两个核心组件：1) 分布感知偏置补偿（DABC），选择性地将长尾异常值吸收到通道级偏置中，保护异常值幅度的同时维持高精度离散化；2) 形态导向量化函数优化（MDQFO），联合优化量化网格与偏置掩码，实现跨模态细粒度对齐。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-Omni上，W4A4模型在ScienceQA达76.63%，显著优于现有SOTA W4A4方法，甚至超越W4A16基线，在MMMU、Video-MME等基准上也表现优异。

Conclusion: MorphoQuant实现了全模态大模型4比特量化的精度-效率优异权衡，验证了模态感知量化策略的有效性。

Abstract: Conventional Post-Training Quantization (PTQ) methods struggle with 4-bit Omni-modal Large Language Models (OLLMs) due to the extreme distribution heterogeneity and disparate outlier patterns across modalities. To address this, we propose MorphoQuant, a modality-aware PTQ framework engineered to preserve cross-modal morphology and mitigate outlier loss. Specifically, we introduce Distribution-Aware Bias Compensation (DABC), which selectively absorbs long-tailed outliers into channel-wise biases. This mechanism safeguards outlier magnitudes while maintaining high-precision discretization for dense inliers, thereby preserving accurate discretization across diverse modal distribution. Complementing this, we propose Morphology-Directed Quantization Function Optimization (MDQFO) to co-optimize the quantization grid with the bias mask, ensuring fine-grained alignment across modalities. Extensive evaluations on Qwen2.5-Omni across benchmarks like MMMU and Video-MME demonstrate our approach's superiority. Notably, our W4A4 model achieves 76.63% on ScienceQA, significantly outperforming SOTA W4A4 methods and surprisingly surpassing the W4A16 baseline, which fully demonstrates the exceptional accuracy-efficiency trade-off of our framework.

</details>


### [127] [Selective Coupling of Decoupled Informative Regions: Masked Attention Alignment for Data-Free Quantization of Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04373)
*Biao Qian,Yang Wang,Yong Wu,Jungong Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MaskAQ，一种面向视觉Transformer的无数据量化方法，通过掩码注意力对齐解决合成样本与量化模型之间的分布不匹配问题。核心思想是利用自注意力机制中语义集中于稀疏信息区域的特点，通过差分熵最大化解耦信息区域，并采用掩码注意力对齐目标对齐全精度模型与量化模型，同时引入周期性样本刷新策略适应量化模型的动态变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无数据量化方法在ViT上存在合成样本与量化模型期望输入分布之间的分布不匹配问题，导致性能次优。

Method: 1) 差分熵最大化解耦信息区域；2) 掩码注意力对齐目标对齐全精度模型与量化模型；3) 周期性样本刷新策略适应量化模型动态变化。

Result: 在多种骨干网络和下游任务上，MaskAQ性能优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: MaskAQ通过聚焦自注意力机制中的信息区域并进行对齐，有效提升了ViT无数据量化的性能。

Abstract: Data-Free Quantization (DFQ) addresses data security concerns by synthesizing samples, without accessing real data. It has garnered increasing attention in the context of Vision Transformers (ViTs), owing to the superiority of the self-attention mechanism compared to classical convolutional operation. However, previous DFQ arts for ViTs often suffer from a distribution mismatch between synthetic samples and input distribution expected by quantized models Q, resulting in the suboptimal performance. In this paper, we propose a novel Masked Attention Alignment approach for Data-Free Quantization of ViTs, named MaskAQ, revealing that: 1) the semantics in the self-attention mechanism is predominantly localized to a sparse subset of patches, called informative regions; 2) the informative regions dominate the mutual information between synthetic samples and Q's outputs. To these ends, we incorporate differential entropy maximum over patch similarity of synthetic samples, to decouple informative regions from noisy background. To couple with varied Q, the informative regions are selected to align full-precision models with Q via a masked attention alignment objective, thus yielding high-quality synthetic samples. Furthermore, a periodic sample refreshing strategy comes up to endow MaskAQ with the capacity to continually adapt to the evolving state of Q throughout the training process, to preserve desirable mutual information with synthetic samples. Extensive experiments verify the merits of MaskAQ over state-of-the-art approaches across multiple backbones and downstream tasks. Our code is available at https://github.com/hfutqian/MaskAQ.

</details>


### [128] [Faithful, Enriched, and Precise: Benchmarking Natural-Science Illustration Generation by T2I models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05949)
*Yifan Chang,Jiaxin Ai,Jianwen Sun,Yuandong Pu,Siqi Luo,Liangliang Zhao,Yuchen Ren,Minghao Liu,Yunfei Yu,Yu Qiao,Kaipeng Zhang,Yihao Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FEPBench是一个针对科学插图生成的细粒度评估基准，它通过原子集标注从视觉、文本、关系和布局四个维度评估T2I模型，发现现有SOTA模型在文本渲染、推理丰富度及生成丰富性与精确性平衡方面存在明显瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准大多仅从整体层面评估科学插图生成质量，忽略了细粒度元素，且缺乏对模型科学推理能力和输出简洁性的量化评估。

Method: 从多学科高质量科学插图中构建FEPBench基准，借助多模态大语言模型和人类专家进行细粒度原子集标注，从指令遵循度、推理丰富度和语义精确度三个维度，并分解到视觉、文本、关系、布局四种元素类型进行系统评估。

Result: SOTA闭源模型（如GPT Image 2和Nano Banana Pro）存在文本渲染瓶颈、推理丰富度有限，且在生成丰富性与精确性之间难以平衡。

Conclusion: FEPBench为科学插图生成领域提供了细粒度评估标准，揭示了当前T2I模型的不足，为模型改进和实际部署提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Scientific illustrations are essential tools for communicating research findings, especially in natural science, where they visualize complex concepts and processes. As Text-to-Image (T2I) models become increasingly capable, researchers have started to use them for scientific illustration generation. However, existing benchmarks often assess outputs at a holistic level, overlooking fine-grained elements, while scientific reasoning ability and output conciseness remain under-quantified. We introduce FEPBench, a benchmark built from carefully selected high-quality scientific illustrations across multiple disciplines and layout types. With the assistance of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) and human experts, we provide fine-grained atom set annotations and systematically evaluate T2I models along three dimensions: instruction faithfulness, reasoning enrichment, and semantic precision. Our evaluation further decomposes model performance across visual, textual, relation, and layout elements. Results show that even state-of-the-art (SOTA) closed-source models, such as GPT Image 2 and Nano Banana Pro, still suffer from text-rendering bottlenecks, limited reasoning enrichment, and difficulty balancing generation richness with precision. These findings provide practical guidance for improving and deploying T2I models in scientific illustration generation. Benchmark data, atom set annotations, and evaluation code will be released by us.

</details>


### [129] [Global-Local Monte Carlo Tree Search in Vision-Language Models for Text-to-3D Indoor Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06002)
*Mengshi Qi,Wei Deng,Xianlin Zhang,Huadong Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于LVLM的文本驱动3D室内场景生成方法，将任务建模为带全局树和局部树的树搜索问题，采用PRM引导的MCTS算法进行高效搜索，并引入分层场景表示和扩散模型纹理生成，最终在自建的大规模多样化数据集3DTindo-bench上取得了优于现有方法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LVLM的文本生成3D室内场景方法采用链式顺序决策机制，无法修正早期决策，导致错误传播。因此需要一种能够回溯和多次尝试的规划方法。

Method: 1) 将任务建模为树搜索问题，包含全局树（迭代放置物体）和局部树（细化放置参数）；2) 提出分层场景表示（房间/区域/地板物体/支撑物体）；3) 采用PRM引导的MCTS方法，PRM剪枝不必要的分支，MCTS平衡探索与利用；4) 利用预训练扩散模型预测场景中所有物体的纹理；5) 构建3DTindo-bench数据集（65种场景类型，3250条指令）。

Result: 在自建的3DTindo-bench数据集上，所提方法在生成3D场景的真实感方面优于当前最先进方法。

Conclusion: 将文本到3D室内场景生成建模为树搜索问题，结合分层表示和PRM引导的MCTS，可以有效克服链式决策的误差传播问题，生成更逼真的3D场景。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models have achieved significant reasoning performance in various tasks. However, there are few studies on text-to-3D indoor scene generation with LVLMs. The main challenge is that prevailing LVLM-based methods employ chain-of-thought sequential decision mechanisms that cannot revise earlier decisions, causing error propagation. In this paper, we consider the task as a planning problem constrained by spatial and layout commonsense. To solve this problem, we model it as a tree search problem with global and local trees, which differs from existing sequential decision-making approaches. In the global tree, we place each object iteratively and explore multiple attempts like humans furnishing a room, where the problem space is represented as a tree. To effectively search the tree, we propose a hierarchical scene representation and a PRM-guided MCTS method. This representation abstracts a scene into room level, region level, floor object level, and supported object level. The PRM-guided MCTS method uses the PRM to prune unnecessary branches and the MCTS algorithm to balance exploration and exploitation to get an optimal solution with fewer attempts. In the local tree, it further decomposes the placement of each object into finer sub-steps, including the specific placement parameters. To make the whole appearance of the scene consistent, we leverage pre-trained diffusion image generative models to predict textures for all the objects in the scene. As existing benchmarks for text-to-3D indoor scene generation remain limited in scale and diversity, we collect a new large-scale diverse dataset that contains 65 scene types and 3250 instructions with diverse sizes, layouts, and styles, named 3DTindo-bench, to better assess the capability of the state-of-the-art models. Our experiments show that our method generates more realistic 3D scenes than state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [130] [LoomVideo: Unifying Multimodal Inputs into Video Generation and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06042)
*Jianzong Wu,Hao Lian,Jiongfan Yang,Dachao Hao,Ye Tian,Yunhai Tong,Jingyuan Zhu,Biaolong Chen,Qiaosong Qi,Aixi Zhang,Wanggui He,Mushui Liu,Jinlong Liu,Pipei Huang,Hao Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LoomVideo是一个5B参数的高效统一视频生成与编辑框架，通过Deepstack注入机制、零开销Scale-and-Add条件化方法和Negative Temporal RoPE策略，在显著降低计算开销的同时实现了SOTA或极具竞争力的性能，推理速度相比同类模型加速至少5.41倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一视频生成与编辑模型大多依赖13B以上的超大模型，并通过拼接序列令牌的方式引入源视频条件，导致序列长度翻倍、自注意力机制计算复杂度成倍增加，带来难以承受的计算开销。亟需一种高效架构来打破这一瓶颈。

Method: (1) 用多模态大语言模型(MLLM)替代标准文本编码器，提取丰富的多模态特征；(2) 提出Deepstack注入机制，将多层MLLM特征与扩散Transformer(DiT)对齐；(3) 提出零开销Scale-and-Add条件化方法，直接将干净源视频潜变量缩放并加到带噪目标潜变量上，避免令牌拼接；(4) 集成Negative Temporal RoPE策略处理多参考图像。

Result: 在综合基准上达到SOTA或极具竞争力的性能；在电商和时尚生成场景中表现出显著优势；受益于零开销条件化机制，推理速度相比同能力模型加速至少5.41倍。

Conclusion: LoomVideo以5B的紧凑参数规模，通过创新的零开销条件化设计，实现了高效且高性能的统一视频生成与编辑，为构建实用化视频基础模型提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Developing unified video generation and editing models capable of interpreting interleaved multimodal inputs is a promising yet challenging frontier field. Existing unified frameworks predominantly rely on massive models (typically 13B parameters or more) and incorporate source video conditions for editing by concatenating sequence tokens. This concatenation inevitably doubles the sequence length, quadrupling the computational complexity of the self-attention mechanism and introducing prohibitive overhead. To address these bottlenecks, we present LoomVideo, a highly efficient 5B-parameter unified architecture for both video generation and editing. LoomVideo replaces the standard text encoder with a Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM) and employs Deepstack injection mechanism to align multi-layer MLLM features with the Diffusion Transformer (DiT). Crucially, we introduce a zero-overhead Scale-and-Add conditioning approach for video editing. By scaling and directly adding the clean source video latent to the noised target latent, this elegant design eliminates the need for token concatenation, drastically reducing computational cost while maintaining robust capabilities for complex, non-rigid edits. Furthermore, a Negative Temporal RoPE strategy is seamlessly integrated to handle multiple reference images. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our compact 5B model achieves state-of-the-art or highly competitive performance across comprehensive benchmarks, exhibiting exceptional superiority in e-commerce and fashion generation scenarios. Benefiting from the zero-overhead conditioning mechanism, LoomVideo achieves at least a 5.41x acceleration in inference speed compared to models of similar capabilities, paving the way for highly practical and efficient video foundation models.

</details>


### [131] [LLM-Conditioned Synthesis of Pathological Gaits via Structured Gait-Language Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06048)
*Mritula Chandrasekaran,Sanket Kachole,Jarek Francik,Dimitrios Makris*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种多模态LLM引导的病理感知3D步态数据合成框架，通过结构化文本描述生成固定长度的骨骼步态序列，用于病理步态分类任务。该方法结合运动分词、病理感知语言条件、LLM语义增强和语言到步态生成，关键贡献是设计了病理分词器以保留病理特征。实验表明合成数据可提升下游分类性能，GRU分类器在留一法协议下达到92.77%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 病理步态数据集稀缺，受限于隐私、招募、成本和运动变异性问题，亟需一种可控的合成方法生成高质量的病理步态数据以支持下游分类任务。

Method: 提出多模态LLM引导框架，包含四个模块：运动分词（病理感知分词器，保留病理运动特征）、病理感知语言条件、LLM语义增强、语言到步态生成。通过结构化文本描述生成固定长度的合成骨骼步态序列。

Result: 合成数据与真实数据结合训练GRU分类器，在留一法协议下达到92.77%准确率，优于仅使用真实数据的基线。

Conclusion: LLM引导的病理步态合成框架能有效生成保留病理特征的合成数据，提升下游分类性能，为缓解病理步态数据稀缺问题提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Pathological gait datasets remain scarce due to privacy, recruitment, cost, and movement variability. Our work presents a multimodal LLM-guided framework for pathology-aware 3D gait data synthesis from structured textual descriptions. The proposed method generates fixed-length synthetic skeleton-based gait sequences for pathological gait classification tasks. The framework combines motion tokenisation, pathology-aware language conditioning, LLM-based semantic augmentation, and language-to-gait generation. A key contribution is the proposed pathological tokeniser, which is designed to preserve pathology-specific motion characteristics during discrete representation learning. Experiments suggest that the proposed synthetic sequences improve downstream classification for recurrent classifiers when combined with real data. The best result is obtained using a GRU classifier trained with real and synthetic samples, achieving 92.77\% accuracy under a leave-one-subject-out protocol.

</details>


### [132] [Symb-xMIL: Symbolic Explanations for Multiple Instance Learning in Digital Pathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06224)
*Yanqing Luo,Julius Hense,Niklas Prenißl,Andreas Mock,Klaus-Robert Müller,Thomas Schnake,Mina Jamshidi Idaji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Symb-xMIL的符号可解释多实例学习后处理解释框架，通过量化模型行为与人类可读逻辑规则（AND/OR/NOT）的对齐程度，将MIL模型的解释从视觉热图扩展到结构化的规则推理，并在合成数据和真实病理数据集上验证了其有效性和临床相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MIL解释方法主要依赖热图突出重要区域，但无法解释模型如何组合不同组织区域的特征来做出决策，尤其当决策依赖特征间交互时，可解释性受限。

Method: 提出Symb-xMIL后处理解释框架，量化MIL模型行为与人类可读逻辑规则（AND、OR、NOT等）的对齐程度，通过对齐分数揭示模型预测背后的语义模式。

Result: 在合成MIL数据上能可靠恢复真实逻辑规则；在临床肿瘤检测任务中发现异构决策模式并暴露隐藏模型错误；在TCGA-HNSCC的HPV预测任务中，精炼了患者生存分层，超越了单纯HPV状态的临床效果。

Conclusion: Symb-xMIL将MIL的可解释性从视觉归因扩展到结构化、基于规则的推理，实现了更透明和语义化的模型预测解释。

Abstract: Explanations of multiple instance learning (MIL) models are widely used for validation and discovery in digital histopathology. Existing methods primarily rely on heatmaps that highlight influential regions but do not explain how evidence from different tissue regions is combined to produce a prediction. This limits interpretability, especially when decisions depend on interactions between tissue features. We introduce Symbolic explainable MIL (Symb-xMIL), a post-hoc explanation framework that quantifies how a MIL model's behavior aligns with human-readable decision rules, expressed as logical relationships (e.g., AND, OR, NOT) between input features. These alignment scores reveal semantic patterns underlying the model's predictions. We evaluate Symb-xMIL on synthetic and real-world histopathology datasets. On synthetic MIL data, Symb-xMIL reliably recovers ground-truth logical rules. In a clinical tumor detection task, the best-aligned rules uncover heterogeneous decision patterns and expose hidden model errors. On an HPV-prediction task on TCGA-HNSCC, a cohort of head and neck cancer, our framework refines patient survival stratification beyond HPV status with potential clinical relevance. Overall, Symb-xMIL extends MIL explainability beyond visual attribution toward structured, rule-based reasoning, enabling more transparent and semantically grounded interpretation of model predictions.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [133] [Robots Need More than VLA and World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06556)
*Elis Karcini,Faisal Mehrban,Quang Nguyen,Mac Schwager,Arash Ajoudani,Cesar Cadena,Jan Peters,Marco Hutter,Haitham Bou-Ammar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文论证机器人通用智能的核心瓶颈不仅是策略扩展（收集更多数据、训练更大VLA模型），更在于缺乏将非结构化行为数据（人类运动、网络视频、仿真数据等）转化为具身机器人监督信号的机制，并提出了四个缺失的接口组件作为下一代机器人研究的关键方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前将机器人通用智能简单视为策略扩展问题的框架是不完整的，大量非结构化行为数据（人类运动、网络视频、仿真交互演示等）蕴含丰富信息但因缺乏具身动作标签、任务语义和奖励结构而无法被机器人策略直接利用。

Method: 作为立场论文（position paper），本文通过识别四个关键缺失接口（数据接口、具身接口、世界模型接口、奖励接口），并系统梳理机器人基础模型、跨具身数据集、视频学习、世界模型和奖励建模等领域的最新进展，提出研究议程。

Result: 明确了限制机器人从广泛物理世界学习的四个核心瓶颈：自动标注非结构化行为的数据接口缺失、将人类运动重定向到机器人动作的具身接口缺失、基于物理的3D推理世界模型接口缺失、以及从视频和语言推断任务进展与成功的奖励接口缺失。

Conclusion: 机器人研究应超越仅收集机器人演示数据的范式，构建能利用更广泛物理世界数据的学习系统，重点发展上述四个接口组件。

Abstract: Generalist robot intelligence is often framed as a policy-scaling problem: collect more robot demonstrations, train larger Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models, and expect broader generalisation. In this position paper, we argue that this framing is incomplete. The central bottleneck is not only policy learning, but the absence of mechanisms that convert the world's abundant unstructured behavioural data into grounded robot supervision. Human motion, internet video, simulation rollouts, and interactive demonstrations contain rich information about tasks, goals, contacts, failures, and physical constraints, yet most of this information is not directly usable by robot policies because it lacks embodiment-specific action labels, task semantics, and reward structure. We identify four missing components for the next generation of robotics: data interfaces for autolabelling unstructured behaviour, embodiment interfaces for retargeting human motion to robot actions, world-model interfaces for physics-grounded 3D reasoning, and reward interfaces for inferring task progress and success from video and language. We survey recent progress in robot foundation models, cross-embodiment datasets, learning from video, world models, and reward modelling, and propose a research agenda for building robotics systems that can learn not only from robot demonstrations, but from the broader physical world.

</details>


### [134] [PhyRoGen: Synthetic Generation of Physical Robot Manipulation Puzzles Using Procedural Content Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06569)
*Lennart Julian Droß,Andreas Orthey,Marc Toussaint*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PhyRoGen框架，利用程序化内容生成技术自动生成机器人操作拼图数据集，解决了传统手动生成训练数据耗时费力的问题。该框架能生成具有互锁物体依赖关系的物理拼图，在1-300秒内通过采样规划算法求解，并在物理仿真中用KUKA LBR iiwa机器人验证了所有生成拼图的可操作性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作物理拼图对自动组装与拆解任务至关重要，但学习操作技能需要大量训练数据，而传统数据生成过程耗时繁琐，亟需自动化解决方案。

Method: 提出PhyRoGen（Physical Robot Manipulation Puzzle Generation）框架，基于程序化内容生成（PCG）自动合成操作拼图数据集，可生成具有互锁物体依赖关系的物理拼图，即一个关节物体必须先被操作后另一个才能移动。基于该框架定义了6个具体生成器，生成24个物理拼图。

Result: 所有24个拼图在1-300秒内通过基于采样的规划算法求解成功；在物理仿真中使用KUKA LBR iiwa机器人验证了每个生成拼图的可操作性。

Conclusion: PhyRoGen能够程序化生成独特且可解的机器人操作拼图，为操作算法基准测试和鲁棒基础模型开发提供了关键工具。

Abstract: Robot manipulation of physical puzzles is important for automatic assembly and disassembly tasks. However, to enable robots to solve physical puzzles, manipulation skills need to be learned, which requires large training datasets, the generation of which is often time consuming and tedious. To overcome this problem, we propose the Physical Robot Manipulation Puzzle Generation framework (PhyRoGen), which leverages procedural content generation (PCG) for automated generation of synthetic datasets of manipulation puzzles. PhyRoGen is a general-purpose puzzle generator, which can generate physical puzzles with interlocking object dependencies, where one articulated object must be manipulated before another can be moved. Based upon PhyRoGen, we define six concrete generators which we use to generate 24 physical puzzles. By using a benchmarking framework, we are able to solve all puzzles in 1 to 300 seconds using sampling-based planning algorithms. Finally, we demonstrate that every generated puzzle is manipulatable by using a KUKA LBR iiwa robot in a physical simulation. This shows that our framework is able to procedurally generate unique, solvable robot manipulation puzzles, which is a crucial ingredient to benchmark manipulation algorithms and to develop robust foundation models.

</details>


### [135] [ChronoForest: Closed-Loop Multi-Tree Diffusion Planning for Efficient Bridge Search and Route Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06618)
*Jungmin Seo,Jaesik Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ChronoForest 是一种闭环规划系统，通过锚链树扩散规划器和在线多树协调器，利用短程时间距离和搜索时桥接证据，实现远距离路径规划，在多个基准测试上取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线导航中难以收集足够丰富的长程数据，但实际智能体仍需解决长距离任务并保证路径级效率而不仅是可行性。同时，微观上组合短程片段产生搜索成本与路径质量的权衡，宏观上航点排序需要比较起讫点和中间锚点间的成对旅行成本，而这些在规划前未知且仅通过长程时间距离估计时可靠性下降。

Method: 提出 ChronoForest，包含锚链树扩散规划器（anchor-chaining tree diffusion planner）和在线多树协调器（online multi-tree orchestrator）。使用时间距离进行短程引导和节点评估，利用搜索时的桥接证据验证长程锚点连通性，并在线反复求解路径。

Result: 在 OGBench AntMaze-Stitch 的中等、大型和超大分片上分别达到 99.8%、99.3% 和 99.5% 的成功率，超大拼接任务成功率较先前基于扩散的结果提升最多 34.5 个百分点。在哈密顿路径组合基准上，在线重新求解纠正了较差的时间排序并提升路径质量，同时显著低于穷举规划的计算成本。

Conclusion: ChronoForest 通过耦合局部桥接搜索与在线路径重新求解，有效解决了仅依赖短程离线轨迹时的长程路径规划难题，在保证路径质量的同时显著提升了成功率和计算效率。

Abstract: How can we plan long-horizon routes that reach designated goals, visit required waypoints, and remain short when only short-horizon offline trajectories are available? This problem matters in offline navigation because collecting sufficiently rich long-horizon data is difficult, yet real agents must still solve long-range tasks with route-level efficiency rather than mere feasibility. The difficulty is twofold: at the microscopic level, composing many short-horizon segments creates a trade-off between search cost and path quality, while at the macroscopic level, waypoint ordering requires comparing pairwise travel costs among start, goal, and waypoint anchors that are unknown before planning and increasingly unreliable when estimated only from long-range temporal distance.
  In this paper, we propose ChronoForest, a closed-loop planning system that couples local bridge search and online route re-solving through an anchor-chaining tree diffusion planner and an online multi-tree orchestrator. ChronoForest uses temporal distance for short-range guidance and node evaluation, while using search-time bridge evidence to validate long-range anchor connectivity and repeatedly re-solve the route. On OGBench AntMaze-Stitch, ChronoForest achieves 99.8%, 99.3%, and 99.5% success on the medium, large, and giant splits and improves giant-stitch success by up to 34.5 points over prior reported diffusion-based results. On Hamiltonian route-composition benchmarks, online re-solving corrects poor temporal orderings and improves route quality while remaining substantially cheaper than exhaustive planning.

</details>


### [136] [What Matters When Cotraining Robot Manipulation Policies on Everyday Human Videos?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06627)
*Richard Li,Aditya Prakash,Andrew Wen,Saurabh Gupta,Yilun Du,Pulkit Agrawal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了如何利用日常互联网视频（而非精心策划的人机协同数据集）来增强机器人操作策略的协同训练。通过一个包含532段人类视频、28小时高质量三角测量手部标注的数据集，发现手部姿态质量影响迁移效果，但即便手部标注准确，视觉与策略网络仍需针对不同本体进行专门化处理才能克服运动差异。提出的协同训练方法在低机器人数据场景下，六项操作任务的绝对成功率平均提升29.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有用于机器人操作策略协同训练的人类视频数据集大多需要精心策划演示（动作需模拟机器人行为）且依赖专用硬件捕捉3D手部姿态。互联网视频资源更丰富，但尚不清楚哪些因素能促成从这些视频到机器人的有效迁移。

Method: 构建了一个包含532段人类视频、28小时高质量三角测量手部标注的新数据集，包含自然动作。通过实验探究手部姿态质量、网络架构专门化等因素对迁移效果的影响。

Result: 手部姿态质量影响迁移效果；但即使手部标注准确，视觉与策略网络仍需针对不同本体进行专门化处理才能克服固有运动差异。所提协同训练方法在低机器人数据场景下，六项操作任务的绝对成功率平均提升29.7%。

Conclusion: 互联网视频可用于机器人操作策略的协同训练，但需注意手部姿态质量和网络专门化设计。该方法在低数据场景下具有显著的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Human video datasets used for cotraining robot manipulation policies largely consist of curated demonstrations where motions are orchestrated to resemble robot behavior and 3D hand poses are captured with specialized hardware. A more plentiful source of data is everyday Internet video, but it is an open question what factors enable transfer from such videos to robots. We investigate this using a new dataset of 532 human videos with 28 hours of high-quality triangulated hand labels and natural motions. We find that hand pose quality affects transfer, but even with accurate hands, the inherent motion gap hinders transfer unless the vision and policy networks specialize to each embodiment. Our cotraining recipe yields consistent improvements, with an absolute success rate gain of $29.7\%$ in the low-robot-data regime across six manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [137] [On the Hardness of Optimal Motion on Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06686)
*Tzvika Geft*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过将多智能体路径规划(MAPF)问题归约为栈重排问题，证明了在树上所有标准目标（距离、makespan、flowtime）下，标记和2-着色MAPF均为NP-hard。特别地，解决了数十年来悬而未决的树上Pebble Motion问题（总移动次数最小化）的复杂性，并为着色Pebble Motion（2种颜色）给出了首个任意图类上的紧致NP-hard结果。这些结果统一并加强了多个基本运动模型的障碍边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 树上MAPF问题的多个基本情形（包括Pebble Motion和栈重排问题）的复杂性长期悬而未决，需要给出明确的理论刻画。

Method: 将MAPF问题归约为栈重排问题（Stack Rearrangement），进而证明栈重排本身是NP-hard的；通过构造从栈到细分星形图（subdivided stars）的映射，证明在极其简单的树结构上NP-hard性依然成立。

Result: 证明了树上标记MAPF和2-着色MAPF在距离、makespan、flowtime三种目标下均为NP-hard；解决了经典Pebble Motion问题在树上的复杂性（NP-hard）；给出了着色Pebble Motion（2色）的首个紧致NP-hard结果；证明了栈重排问题为NP-hard。

Conclusion: 树上MAPF及相关运动模型存在统一的NP-hard性障碍边界，栈重排问题作为核心归约枢纽，揭示了这些模型之间的深层联系。

Abstract: This paper presents a simple framework that settles the complexity of Multi-Agent Path Finding (MAPF) on trees across standard objectives--distance, makespan, and flowtime--for both labeled and colored variants. In MAPF, agents occupy the vertices of a graph and must move to target vertices without collisions while optimizing a given objective. In the labeled case, the agents are distinct and have respective targets; in the colored case, agents of the same color are interchangeable. While many MAPF variants are known to be intractable, several basic cases on trees have remained open. We prove NP-hardness on trees for both labeled and 2-colored MAPF under all three objectives. In particular, we resolve the classical Pebble Motion problem, where one pebble moves at a time to an adjacent empty vertex and the goal is to minimize the total number of moves. Despite being one of the most basic discrete motion models, its complexity on trees had remained open for several decades. Moreover, for colored Pebble Motion, we give the first hardness result on any graph class, already with two colors, which is tight.
  All of these results are established through the hardness of Stack Rearrangement, itself posed as an open problem, which asks to optimally rearrange items stored in stacks, and which we also prove to be NP-hard. Notably, the connection to stacks yields hardness already on very simple trees--subdivided stars--across all problems. Together, these results reveal a common tractability barrier that permeates several fundamental motion models, thereby unifying and strengthening prior hardness results.

</details>


### [138] [Optimal Control Approach for Non-prehensile Ball Juggling Using a 7-DoF Manipulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06704)
*Joel Ramadani,Vasilije Rakčević,Riddhiman Laha,Arne Sachtler,Valentin Le Mesle,Achim J. Lilienthal,Sami Haddadin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于模型的控制框架，用于7自由度机械臂使用工具进行非抓取式抛球（杂耍）的轨迹生成与稳定控制，采用两阶段最优控制方法离线计算可行运动模式，并实现实时误差修正，在仿真和Franka Emika Panda机器人上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非抓取式操作技能对现实机器人交互至关重要，如托盘中平衡玻璃杯或桌上滑动物品。其中，杂耍是一项高度挑战性的任务，因为需要实现对欠驱动对象的动态稳定化，且系统对控制输入敏感、时序要求苛刻。

Method: 提出一个基于模型的两阶段最优控制框架：(1) 离线计算杂耍轨迹和周期运动模式；(2) 将这些轨迹组织起来，实现无需在线求解最优控制的实时误差修正。系统控制一个7自由度机械臂使用工具进行非抓取式抛球。

Result: 在仿真环境中评估了控制器的性能，并在Franka Emika Panda机器人上进行了实际实验，验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于模型的框架能够有效生成杂耍轨迹并稳定周期运动，通过离线计算与实时修正的结合，解决了非抓取式操作中混合动力学系统的控制难题。

Abstract: Non-prehensile object manipulation skills are important for real-world robot interactions, enabling highly dynamic tasks such as balancing a glass on a tray or the controlled sliding of items on a table. Among such tasks, those characterised by high-speed manipulation requirements and general sensitivity of the resulting hybrid dynamics are particularly hard to accomplish. Within these, juggling can be seen as a highly challenging maneuver to be solved. The key to robotic juggling is achieving dynamic stabilisation of an underactuated object. Since the object does not possess the ability of self-correction, its stability is entirely dependent on the forces applied to it. This creates a system that is sensitive to control inputs, where timing is critical to continuously counteract deviations and maintain the desired behavior. We develop a systematic method to control a 7-degree-of-freedom manipulator performing non-prehensile ball juggling with a tool. Our primary contribution is a model-based framework for generating juggling trajectories and stabilizing a periodic juggling motion for this hybrid system. The framework incorporates a two-stage optimal control approach to compute the underlying feasible motion patterns required for stable juggling. Offline-computed trajectories are then organised to enable real-time error correction without solving optimal control problems online. We demonstrate the effectiveness of the resulting controller by first evaluating its performance in a simulation environment and performing an experiment using a Franka Emika Panda robot.

</details>


### [139] [SCOUT: Semantic scene COverage via Uncertainty-guided Traversal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06721)
*Junyu Mao,Sara Ayoubi,Vishnu D. Sharma,Ilija Hadžić,Matthew Andrews*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SCOUT是一个在线语义探索框架，通过将主动遍历与概率场景图构建耦合，使机器人能够逐步理解环境，将语义场景完备性作为操作目标而非被动副产品。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D场景图管线将感知视为固定数据集的后处理阶段，将场景表示与决定观察内容的决策过程解耦，导致机器人无法在持续运行中主动理解环境。

Method: SCOUT利用先验2D占据地图和带位姿的RGB-D观测，增量构建不确定性感知的3D场景图（节点维护融合几何和开放词汇物体标签的后验信念，边编码结构关系）；通过不确定性引导的遍历规划器选择视点，平衡期望语义确定性增益、几何覆盖增益和移动成本。

Result: 机器人能够在需要额外证据时重新访问模糊物体，在场景不完整时扩展到未探索的自由空间，实现了语义场景完备性作为操作目标。

Conclusion: SCOUT将语义场景完备性从语义映射的被动副产品转变为可操作目标，推动了在最少人工干预下对演化室内环境进行巡逻、更新和推理的自主智能体发展。

Abstract: Robots that operate over extended periods should not merely visit space; they should progressively understand it. Yet most 3D scene graph pipelines treat perception as a post-processing stage over a fixed dataset, decoupling scene representation from the decisions that determine what is observed in the first place. We present SCOUT, an online semantic exploration framework that closes this loop by coupling active traversal with probabilistic scene graph construction. Given a prior 2D occupancy map and posed RGB-D observations, SCOUT incrementally builds an uncertainty-aware 3D scene graph whose nodes maintain fused geometry and posterior beliefs over open-vocabulary object labels, while edges encode structural relations such as on, inside, belong, and next to. These beliefs are fed back to an uncertainty-guided traversal planner, which selects viewpoints by balancing expected semantic certainty gain, geometric coverage gain, and travel cost. In this way, the robot revisits ambiguous objects when additional evidence matters and expands into unseen free space when the scene remains incomplete. The resulting system treats semantic scene completeness as an operational objective rather than a passive by-product of semantic mapping, moving toward autonomous agents that can patrol, update, and reason about evolving indoor environments with minimal human intervention.

</details>


### [140] [IDDMBSE: Integrating Data-Driven and Model-Based Systems Engineering for Trusted Autonomous Cyber-Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06727)
*John S. Baras,Sai Sandeep Damera,Ryan Matheu,Clinton Enwerem,Praveen M. S. Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出IDDMBSE，一种集成数据驱动与基于模型的系统工程方法论，通过扩展MBSE V流程在每个步骤融入数据驱动循环，并构建了包含PERFECT、TRADES-X和VERITAS的开源工具链，在自主地面机器人全生命周期中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主CPS位于MBSE与数据驱动的ML/AI交叉领域，但缺乏能原生覆盖两者的集成系统工程方法论。

Method: IDDMBSE方法论：扩展MBSE V流程，在每个阶段增加数据驱动循环，基于SysML、自主栈和混合模型驱动+数据驱动的权衡架构。工具链：PERFECT（SysML到ROS自主栈映射）、TRADES-X（设计空间探索分解）、VERITAS（形式化+数据驱动+运行时验证融合）。

Result: 在可信自主地面机器人全生命周期验证，涵盖传感器选择、风险敏感路径规划、行为树任务验证、保形预测鲁棒感知、多机器人协调等，在Isaac Sim对抗地形测试场中完成实验。

Conclusion: IDDMBSE有效桥接了MBSE与ML/AI，正在SysML v2/KerML基础上重构以实现语言原生可组合性和更紧密的ML/AI集成。

Abstract: Autonomous cyber-physical systems (CPS) sit at the intersection of Model-Based Systems Engineering (MBSE) and data-driven Machine Learning and Artificial Intelligence (ML/AI), yet no integrated Systems Engineering (SE) methodology natively spans both. We address this gap with IDDMBSE, an Integrated Data-Driven and Model-Based Systems Engineering methodology that extends the rigorous MBSE V-process with a data-driven loop at every step, anchored in SysML, the autonomy stack, and a hybrid model-based plus data-driven trade-off architecture. We instantiate IDDMBSE as an interoperable, open-source tool chain: PERFECT, which maps SysML system architectures to executable ROS autonomy stacks for scalable performance evaluation; TRADES-X, which decomposes design-space exploration into a model-based optimization stage followed by a data-driven evaluation stage; and VERITAS, which combines formal, data-driven, and runtime verification into a single assurance workflow. We demonstrate IDDMBSE on a Trusted Autonomous Ground Robot across its development lifecycle, spanning sensor-suite selection, risk-sensitive path planning, behavior-tree task verification, conformal-prediction-based robust perception, and assured multi-robot coordination, all exercised in a contested-terrain Isaac Sim test range that we release with the tool chain. We close by sketching how IDDMBSE is being re-formulated on SysML v2 / KerML foundations to enable language-native composability and tighter ML/AI integration.

</details>


### [141] [AxisGuide: Grounding Robot Action Coordinate System in RGB Observations for Robust Visuomotor Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06761)
*Jiyun Jang,Yujin Sung,Woosung Joung,Daewon Chae,Sangwon Lee,Sohwi Kim,Jinkyu Kim,Jungbeom Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AxisGuide方法，通过在RGB图像中显式渲染机器人基座坐标系轴（+x, +y, +z）作为额外的提示通道，以解决视觉运动操控策略在分布偏移下因缺乏动作坐标理解而导致的低级动作执行失败问题。实验表明该方法显著提升了策略性能与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模行为克隆训练的视觉运动操控策略具备良好的语义场景理解能力，但在分布偏移（如物体位置变化）下低级动作执行可靠性显著下降。作者认为根本原因在于策略缺乏对机器人基座坐标系在图像空间中含义的理解，即动作坐标解释能力不足。

Method: AxisGuide是一种轻量级引导方法，利用相机参数和末端执行器位姿，在每帧相机视角中渲染出机器人基座坐标系的+x、+y、+z轴，并将这些轴信息作为额外的提示通道（cue channels）与原始RGB观测拼接，显式地向策略传达动作坐标在图像空间中的几何含义。

Result: 在LIBERO仿真环境和真实机器人场景中的大量实验表明，AxisGuide带来了显著的性能提升和更强的泛化能力，证明了显式动作坐标提示对于学习可靠且可迁移的通用视觉运动策略的有效性。

Conclusion: AxisGuidea通过显式可视化动作坐标系统，有效弥合了语义场景理解与动作坐标解释之间的鸿沟，为提升行为克隆策略在分布偏移下的低级动作执行可靠性和泛化性提供了一种轻量级且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Visuomotor manipulation policies trained via large-scale behavior cloning have achieved strong semantic scene understanding, yet often fail to reliably execute correct low-level actions under distribution shifts. For example, even in a simple pickup task with identical scene layouts, camera viewpoints, and illumination, performance can degrade substantially when the object is placed at unseen locations. We argue that this gap arises from insufficient action understanding, namely the inability to interpret the robot's base-frame action coordinate system in image space. To address this issue, we introduce AxisGuide, a lightweight guidance method that bridges semantic scene understanding and action-coordinate interpretation. Using camera parameters and end-effector poses, AxisGuide renders the robot base-frame axes in each camera view and augments RGB observations with a small set of cue channels that explicitly visualize the meaning of the +x, +y, and +z motions in image space. Extensive evaluations in both the LIBERO simulation and real-world environments demonstrate that AxisGuide yields substantial performance gains and improved generalization, highlighting the effectiveness of explicit action-coordinate cues for learning reliable and transferable generalist visuomotor policies.

</details>


### [142] [Multi-Robot Planning and Control from CCTV Camera Networks in a Real Warehouse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06762)
*Luke Robinson,Benjamin Ramtoula,Anas Izaaryene,Paul Newman,Daniele De Martini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了一种仅依靠外部摄像头网络（30个CCTV摄像头）和边缘计算实现多机器人（4台）在真实仓库环境中的离车控制与协调系统，无需机器人携带任何任务专用导航硬件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将移动机器人的感知与计算从车端转移到环境中的固定摄像头，实现可扩展的自主性，并将这一思想从单机器人扩展到真实仓库中的多机器人协调编队。

Method: 系统完全在图像空间运行，基于未标定的像素级拓扑相机图；采用分层规划器为每台机器人选择相机序列并规划图像空间运动；通过优先-联合策略协调机器人，将相机重叠区域视为共享资源（一次仅允许一台机器人占用）以避免碰撞和死锁。

Result: 在包含4台机器人、30个摄像头、6条27米通道的真实仓库中成功验证，报告了任务时间和协调统计数据。

Conclusion: 据作者所知，这是首个仅使用外部摄像头网络和离车计算实现多机器人规划与协调的现场演示，机器人无需携带任务专用导航硬件。

Abstract: Off-board control of mobile robots from cameras embedded in the environment offers a practical path to scalable autonomy, moving sensing and compute off the robots. We extend this idea from the single-robot case to coordinated fleets in a real warehouse, driving multiple robots with only a distributed CCTV network and edge compute. The system operates entirely in image space over an uncalibrated, pixel-wise topological camera graph, enabling wide-area operation with flexible camera placement. A hierarchical planner selects a camera sequence per robot and plans its image-space motion through each view, coordinating robots with a prioritised-then-joint strategy and treating overlapping camera regions as shared resources held by one robot at a time to prevent collisions and deadlocks. We validate the approach in a real warehouse with four robots and 30 cameras across six 27 m aisles, reporting mission times and coordination statistics. To our knowledge, this is the first field demonstration of multi-robot planning and coordination using only an external camera network and off-board compute, with robots carrying no task-specific navigation hardware.

</details>


### [143] [Learning All-Terrain Locomotion for a Planetary Rover with Actively Articulated Suspension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06790)
*Arthur Bouton,Tristan D. Hasseler,Michael Paton,Travis Brown,Jacob Levy,William Reid,Joshua Martin,Hari Nayar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ERNEST行星探测车，采用主动万向节悬架系统，通过单一强化学习神经网络控制器实现自主障碍协商和异构地形穿越，零样本迁移至物理平台，在多种困难地形上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有行星探测车在复杂松软地形上移动能力受限，被动悬架难以主动适应地形变化，需要开发能充分利用主动悬架能力的统一控制器，避免显式地形分类和控制器切换。

Method: 基于高保真DARTS仿真引擎（结合刚体接触动力学和Bekker-Wong地面力学），通过强化学习训练单个神经网络控制器，并采用策略整合策略合并不同地形特化智能体的经验为统一控制器。

Result: 成功实现岩石地、颠簸陷阱、轮高台阶、沙波纹和沙坡等困难地形的自主穿越；在20°干沙坡上运输成本降低37%；在湿沙（被动悬架完全无法移动）上表现优异。

Conclusion: 所提方法通过单一神经网络控制器充分释放主动万向节悬架能力，实现异构地形上的鲁棒自主导航，并成功实现从仿真到物理平台的零样本迁移。

Abstract: This paper presents ERNEST, a four-wheeled planetary rover concept equipped with a two-degree-of-freedom Active Gimbal Suspension that combines yaw and roll actuation to enable wheel reconfiguration, steering, and active load redistribution. A single neural network controller, trained to track a desired path across challenging terrain, fully unlocks the capabilities of this actuated suspension system for autonomous obstacle negotiation. A reinforcement learning framework is developed using the high-fidelity DARTS simulation engine, which combines rigid-contact dynamics and Bekker-Wong terramechanics, enabling the emergence of locomotion strategies adapted to loose-soil conditions. To obtain a single unified controller across heterogeneous terrains, a policy consolidation strategy merges the experience of terrain-specialized agents into one neural network, eliminating the need for explicit terrain classification and controller switching.
