The resulting controller operates on a combination of proprioceptive and exteroceptive feedback, including sparse stereo-derived terrain elevation, chassis attitude, joint states, and force-torque measurements. Zero-shot transfer to the physical rover is achieved through domain randomization, sensor noise injection, and model-to-real system identification. Experimental results demonstrate autonomous traversal of rock fields, a bump trap, a wheel-high step, sand ripples, and sandy slopes. On a 20° sandy slope, the learned controller reduces the cost of transport by 37% on dry sand despite the additional actuation, and achieves superior performance on wet sand where the passive suspension becomes completely immobilized.

</details>


### [144] [Lane Change Trajectory Planning for Personalized Driving Comfort and Mobility Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06805)
*Haoxuan Dong,Dongjun Li,Ziyou Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种神经网络驱动的换道轨迹规划器，结合三阶多项式轨迹生成器与双头学习模块，通过基于误差胜者逻辑回归的统计门控机制，在个性化舒适/效率偏好与全工况可行性保障之间自适应切换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 换道涉及紧密耦合的纵向与横向运动，且存在显著的个体间差异，导致轨迹规划具有高度个性化特征，现有方法难以在个性化偏好与全工况保障之间取得平衡。

Method: 采用共享骨干网络+双头结构：一个头保障全工况操作可行性，另一个头捕捉驾驶员个性化偏好（舒适或效率）；通过基于误差胜者逻辑回归的统计门控机制，在不同驾驶条件下自适应选择合适的头进行轨迹规划。

Result: 代表性案例和蒙特卡洛模拟表明，所提规划器在个性化数据充足时实现个性化舒适与机动性，在数据不足时由基线头保证轨迹可行性。

Conclusion: 所提方法实现了上下文感知的个性化换道轨迹规划，兼顾了个性化偏好与不同驾驶条件下的安全可行性保障。

Abstract: Lane changing entails simultaneous longitudinal and lateral motions that affect driving comfort and mobility efficiency. Because these motions are tightly coupled and subject to substantial inter-vehicle variability, trajectory planning for lane-change maneuvers is characterized by a highly personalized nature. This study proposes a neural network-driven planner that integrates a third-order polynomial trajectory generator with a learning module that infers optimal trajectory parameters across diverse driving conditions. Using a shared backbone with dual heads, one head ensures all-condition operational guarantees, while the other captures driver-specific preferences for comfort or mobility efficiency. A head-gated switching mechanism, realized through a statistical gate based on error-winner logistic regression, adaptively selects the appropriate head under varying driving conditions, which enables context-aware lane-change trajectory planning. Representative cases and Monte Carlo simulations show that the proposed planner achieves personalized comfort and mobility during lane changes, while the baseline ensures feasible trajectories under driving conditions where personalized data are insufficient or inaccessible.

</details>


### [145] [Three-dimensional hydro-cluttered locomotion by an undulatory robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06829)
*Tianyu Wang,Matthew Fernandez,Galen Tunnicliffe,Nikolas Cornell,Justin Duong,Donoven Dortilus,Zhaochen J. Xu,Patricia Meza,Sean Lublinsky,Darsh Parikh,Jianfeng Lin,Emily Grace,Daniel I. Goldman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一款名为AquaMILR的细长无肢水下机器人，通过可编程身体刚度、深度调节和惯性滚动机制，实现了在充满刚性/柔性杂物的"水杂环境"中的高效三维运动，并在红树林实地验证了其导航与视觉检测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下环境中广泛存在障碍物（水杂），传统机器人难以在此类不可避免接触的环境中可靠运动，缺乏有效的建模与设计原则。

Method: 基于机械智能和类蛇形机器人运动原理，设计了AquaMILR机器人，融合双侧缆绳驱动、可编程身体顺应性、分布式深度调节、耐腐蚀封装及机载电源；通过系统化机器人物理实验和实地红树林测试验证。

Result: 可编程身体刚度能调节身体变形，将身体-环境交互转化为快速稳健的前向运动；深度调节提供三维通路，实现绕障与恢复；在卡滞场景中，惯性引起的滚动可作为自发恢复机制。

Conclusion: 建立了水杂环境运动的设计原则，证明水下机器人可将环境复杂性转化为运动资源，为新一代水下机器人的设计提供了范式。

Abstract: Aquatic robots have expanded human access to underwater environments, yet many underwater spaces contain obstacles that can disrupt open-water locomotion. In "hydro-cluttered" environments, water is interspersed with rigid and flexible clutter, making body-obstacle contact unavoidable. Operating in these spaces requires robots that can regulate and exploit contact, but this regime remains difficult to model or simulate. Building on recent advances in mechanical intelligence in terradynamically capable limbless robotics, we develop principles for 3D aquatic locomotion using AquaMILR, an elongate limbless robot that combines bilateral cable-driven actuation, programmable body compliance, distributed depth regulation, corrosion-resistant enclosures, and onboard power and electronics for untethered field operation. Systematic robophysical experiments reveal that programmable body compliance regulates body deformation and converts body-environment interactions into fast, robust, forward progression across increasing hydro-clutter constraint strength. Depth regulation provides three-dimensional access, allowing the robot to bypass clutter, recover from obstruction, and continue through otherwise inaccessible routes. In potential jamming scenarios, emergent inertia-induced rolling acts as a spontaneous recovery mechanism, freeing the robot from clutter that would otherwise lead to failure and allowing locomotion to continue without additional control. Tests of the robot in an aquatic mangrove field demonstrate that these principles transfer to practical operation, enabling navigation and onboard visual inspection of inaccessible root zones. These results establish principles for hydro-cluttered locomotion and a design paradigm in which aquatic robots exploit environmental complexity as a locomotor resource.

</details>


### [146] [STRIPS-WM: Learning Grounded Propositional STRIPS-style World Models from Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06832)
*Abhiroop Ajith,Constantinos Chamzas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: STRIPS-WM 提出了一种从图像转换中学习 STRIPS 风格世界模型的方法，通过先归纳抽象转换图、学习潜在二值谓词和算子，再将谓词蒸馏到视觉编码器中，实现从图像直接进行经典规划，在视觉重排任务上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人执行长时域视觉操作时，高维图像中包含大量与动作无关的视觉细节，而成功规划依赖于动作相关的事实（当前可做什么以及动作后如何变化）。现有方法难以从原始视觉经验中直接获取具有前提条件和效果的符号化算子表示。

Method: (1) 从图像转换中归纳有限抽象转换图；(2) 学习潜在二值谓词和每个动作标签的一个接地命题算子；(3) 将学到的谓词蒸馏到视觉编码器中，使得可以从新起点和目标图像直接进行经典规划。

Result: 在视觉重排任务上，STRIPS-WM 在图像到规划的成功率上优于视觉 rollout、潜在图搜索和潜在符号化基线方法。

Conclusion: 从视觉经验中学习符号化世界模型是可行的，且学到的稀疏前提和添加/删除效应的算子能够有效支持经典规划，为机器人视觉任务规划提供了一种有前景的范式。

Abstract: Robots performing long-horizon visual manipulation observe high-dimensional images, but successful plans depend on action-relevant facts: what can be done now and what changes afterward. A useful planning representation should discard irrelevant visual details while preserving action applicability and effects. Classical task planners exploit this structure through symbolic operators with preconditions and effects, but obtaining such representations from raw visual experience remains challenging. We study a visual task-planning setting in which a robot receives only image transitions: the current image, executed high-level action, and the resulting image. At test time, given a start image and a goal image, the robot must produce a sequence of high-level actions that reaches the goal. To address this problem, we introduce STRIPS-WM, a framework for learning image-grounded STRIPS-style world models directly from visual transitions. STRIPS-WM first induces a finite abstract transition graph from images, then learns latent binary predicates and one grounded propositional operator per action label. The learned operators form a symbolic action model with sparse preconditions and add/delete effects. Finally, the learned predicates are distilled into a visual encoder, enabling classical planning directly from novel start and goal images. Experiments on visual rearrangement tasks show that STRIPS-WM improves image-to-plan success over the tested visual rollout, latent graph-search and latent-symbolic baselines.

</details>


### [147] [Think Like a Pilot: Fine-Grained Long-Horizon UAV Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06836)
*Xiangyi Zheng,Xiangyu Wang,Qinan Liao,Zimu Tang,Yue Liao,Dongyue Lyu,Guodong Wang,Junjie Liu,Si Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了FLIGHT基准和FLIGHT VLA架构，用于解决语言引导的无人机在长时间跨度指令下的细粒度连续飞行控制问题，通过异步架构解耦低频任务推理与高频连续控制，在多项指标上超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言导航（VLN）基准通常使用离散或粗粒度动作，而无人机视觉-语言-动作（VLA）任务聚焦于短时原子操作，缺乏针对长时间跨度、细粒度连续飞行指令的评估设置。

Method: 提出FLIGHT基准（含细粒度VLN和长程Flow两个子集）和FLIGHT VLA异步架构：低频流式Pilot视觉语言模型负责任务状态推理，高频扩散动作模型负责连续控制，并引入显式的Pilot Reasoning文本监督。

Result: 在闭环评估中，FLIGHT VLA在FLIGHT基准上持续超越代表性的VLN和VLA基线，在多阶段任务完成度、子目标遵循度和终端控制方面表现更强；其训练的流式Pilot Reasoning VLM还提升了无人机视频推理能力。

Conclusion: 所提出的异步解耦架构和显式推理监督机制能有效提升语言引导无人机在长程连续飞行任务中的表现，验证了设计思路的有效性。

Abstract: Language-guided UAV agents must execute long-horizon semantic instructions while producing smooth, physically feasible continuous flight commands, yet existing Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) benchmarks typically use discrete or coarse actions and existing UAV Vision-Language-Action (VLA) tasks focus on short, atomic maneuvers. To address this gap in UAV task settings, we introduce \textbf{FLIGHT}, a \textbf{F}ine-grained \textbf{L}ong-horizon \textbf{I}nstruction-\textbf{G}uided benchmark for \textbf{H}ybrid UAV navigation and reasoning \textbf{T}asks, which combines multi-stage instructions with dense 6-DoF trajectory annotations across two dataset splits: Fine-grained VLN and Long-horizon Flow. To endow the UAV agent with the capability of real-time in-flight reasoning over task execution status and mission planning, while simultaneously accommodating high-frequency, real-time precise control, we further propose \textbf{FLIGHT VLA}, an asynchronous architecture that decouples a low-frequency Streaming Pilot Vision-Language Model (VLM) for task-state reasoning from a high-frequency diffusion action model for continuous control, supervised by explicit \textbf{Pilot Reasoning} texts that summarize the current flight state and anticipate the next subgoal. In closed-loop evaluation, FLIGHT VLA consistently surpasses representative VLN and VLA baselines on our FLIGHT benchmarks, achieving stronger multi-stage completion, subgoal adherence, and terminal control. Its trained Streaming Pilot Reasoning VLM further improves UAV video reasoning, validating the effectiveness of our design.

</details>


### [148] [What Is My Robot Thinking? Design Considerations for Transparent and Trustworthy Shared Autonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06870)
*Atharv Belsare,Zohre Karimi,Connor Mattson,Rushiil Nakka,Daniel S. Brown*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究探究了共享自主系统中界面透明度（反馈模态和信息丰富度）如何影响人机协调与信任，发现提供反馈能显著改善意图对齐并减少纠正干预，参与者偏爱视觉反馈，信息丰富度的偏好取决于任务复杂度，揭示完整信念分布并不能持续改善对齐或信任。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共享自主辅助机器人需要在用户控制与自主辅助之间取得平衡，但由于机器人行为依赖于不可直接观测的内部意图推断，推断目标与用户意图之间的错配会破坏协调与信任。因此，研究如何通过界面透明度设计来改善这一交互问题。

Method: 通过N=25名参与者的用户研究，在两个辅助操作任务中评估反馈模态（视觉vs听觉）和信息丰富度（稀疏vs丰富）对协调和信任的影响。

Result: 提供反馈显著改善了意图对齐并减少了纠正干预；参与者更偏好视觉反馈而非听觉反馈；对信息丰富度的偏好取决于任务复杂度；揭示完整信念分布并未持续改善意图对齐或信任。

Conclusion: 有效透明度主要通过目标可读性来增强协调，而信任取决于任务适切的信息暴露而非最大程度的披露。基于这些发现，提出了设计透明共享自主系统的指导原则。

Abstract: Assistive robots operating under shared autonomy must balance user control with autonomous assistance. Because robot actions depend on internal intent inference that is not directly observable, mismatches between inferred and intended goals can undermine coordination and trust. We investigate how interface-level transparency, including feedback modality (visual vs. auditory) and information richness (sparse vs. rich), shapes interaction in a vision-based shared autonomy system. In a user study with N=25 participants across two assistive manipulation tasks, we evaluate how these designs influence coordination and trust. Providing feedback significantly improves intent alignment and reduces corrective intervention, indicating that making the inferred goal legible accelerates convergence in shared control. Participants preferred visual over auditory feedback, while preferences for sparse versus rich information depended on task complexity. We also found that revealing the full belief distribution did not consistently improve alignment or trust. Together, these findings indicate that effective transparency enhances coordination primarily through goal legibility, while trust depends on task-appropriate information exposure rather than maximal disclosure. Based on these results, we outline guidelines for designing transparent shared autonomy systems.

</details>


### [149] [Neuro-Symbolic Learning for Long-Horizon Task Planning Under Complex Logical Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06877)
*Qiwei Du,Zitong Zhan,Shaoshu Su,Bowen Li,Yi Du,Zhipeng Zhao,Taimeng Fu,Sebastian Scherer,Jiaoyang Li,Chen Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于命令式学习的双层优化方法，通过3R策略（并行修复、重启、回滚）解决神经符号任务规划中因训练-测试不匹配导致的暴露偏差问题，在多个基准测试中实现规划失败率降低80.04%、规划时间减少57.14%，并在四足移动操作机器人上完成仿真与真实世界验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经符号方法在任务规划中利用对象重要性评分剪枝无关对象，但依赖固定离线监督导致训练-测试不匹配：部署时规划器在模型自身不完美预测产生的剪枝搜索空间中运行，产生暴露偏差，降低规划性能。

Method: 将对象重要性学习建模为基于命令式学习的双层优化问题：上层优化神经评分器，下层在评分剪枝后的搜索空间中求解符号规划问题；引入3R策略（并行Repair、Restart、Rollback）为上层学习提供稳定自适应的反馈。

Result: 在三个挑战性基准测试中达到最先进性能，失败率降低80.04%，规划时间减少57.14%；在四足移动操作机器人的仿真和真实世界任务中验证了框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效解决了暴露偏差问题，实现了高效且可部署的神经符号任务规划。

Abstract: Task planning often suffers from severe efficiency bottlenecks when robots must reason over long-horizon action sequences under complex logical constraints, including object affordances, spatial relationships, and sequential action dependencies. Recent neuro-symbolic methods improve planning efficiency by learning object-importance scores to prune task-irrelevant objects, but they typically rely on fixed offline supervision generated from full search spaces. This creates a train-test mismatch: at deployment, the planner operates in pruned search spaces induced by the model's own imperfect predictions, leading to exposure bias and degraded planning performance. To address this challenge, we formulate object-importance learning for task planning as an imperative learning-based bilevel optimization problem. The upper level optimizes a neural scorer, while the lower level solves a symbolic planning problem in the score-pruned search space. To stabilize this learning process, we introduce a 3R strategy into the lower-level planning, using parallel Repair, Restart, and Rollback recovery to provide reliable and adaptive feedback for upper-level learning. Experiments on three challenging benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art performance, including an 80.04% reduction in failure rate and a 57.14% reduction in planning time. We further validate the framework on a quadruped-based mobile manipulator in simulation and the real world, demonstrating its potential for efficient and deployable neuro-symbolic task planning.

</details>


### [150] [A Cross-view Fusion Framework for Robust 6-DoF Grasp Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06878)
*Kangjian Zhu,Haobo Jiang,Jianjun Qian,Jin Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种跨视角融合框架，通过辅助视角缓解遮挡问题，并采用后融合策略避免耗时的多视角重建。自监督对比学习增强跨视角点云特征的空间一致性和方向区分性，跨视角对齐的圆柱积分模块融合抓取相关几何信息，在GraspNet-1Billion基准和实际应用中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有6自由度抓取位姿估计方法在角落视角下受遮挡影响严重，而多视角重建方法耗时且任务无关，因此需要一种高效、鲁棒的跨视角融合方法来解决遮挡问题。

Method: 1) 提出后融合策略，引入辅助视角缓解遮挡但不进行显式多视角重建；2) 自监督对比学习：跨视角点对中，对应同一3D位置视为正样本，对应不同抓取方向视为负样本；3) 跨视角对齐的圆柱积分模块：先按相似度对齐跨视角点与特征，再注册到圆柱坐标系以强调旋转对称几何，交替使用局部自注意力与种子交叉注意力实现单视角内和跨视角间的交互。

Result: 在GraspNet-1Billion基准测试和真实世界应用中取得了强性能。

Conclusion: 跨视角后融合策略结合对比学习与圆柱积分模块，能有效提升角落视角下6-DoF抓取位姿估计的鲁棒性和精度。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a cross-view fusion framework that enhances the robustness of 6-DoF grasp pose estimation in corner views. Our framework alleviates occlusion by incorporating an auxiliary view and avoids the time-consuming, task-agnostic multi-view reconstruction through a post-fusion strategy. To enhance cross-view fusion, we propose a self-supervised contrastive learning strategy that leverages cross-view associations to regularize point cloud features. In brief, a cross-view point pair is considered a match if the two points correspond to the same 3D location, and a non-match if they represent distinct grasp directions. The learning strategy significantly enhances the spatial consistency and direction distinctiveness of point features, thereby facilitating cross-view fusion and improving estimation robustness. Furthermore, we propose a cross-view-aligned cylinder integration module to fuse grasp-relevant geometry into a comprehensive representation. Specifically, the module first aligns the cross-view points and features according to their similarity to enhance the robustness against noise. Subsequently, these points are registered into the cylindrical coordinate frame, emphasizing the rotation-symmetric geometry which is important for grasping. Finally, local self-attention and seed cross-attention layers are alternately employed, respectively enabling interactions within single views and across views, which supports fine-grained representation of grasp-relevant geometry. Our framework achieves strong performance on the GraspNet-1Billion benchmark and in real-world applications. Code is available at https://github.com/KJZhuAutomatic/Cross-view-Grasp.

</details>


### [151] [ActionMap: Robot Policy Learning via Voxel Action Heatmap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06904)
*Pei Yang,Hai Ci,Yanzhe Chen,Qi Lv,Han Cai,Mike Zheng Shou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ActionMap，一种体素热图动作头，用于替代现有VLA模型中的单点预测动作解码器。该方法将动作空间结构化为体素热图，利用邻近动作的几何信息，在LIBERO仿真和Franka真实操作任务上，相比OpenVLA-OFT的L1回归头平均提升8.2%，并且数据效率更高、收敛速度更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型的动作解码器（自回归令牌箱、L1回归、流匹配去噪）均将动作空间视为非结构化空间，未利用邻近动作的几何邻近性进行训练，限制了模型性能。

Method: ActionMap采用体素热图动作头，为每个新动作在动作空间上预测体素热图，每个体素直接存储对应动作的概率，从而利用动作空间的几何结构进行训练。

Result: 在LIBERO仿真和真实Franka操作任务上，ActionMap超越两种架构不同的骨干网络（如比OpenVLA-OFT的L1回归头在LIBERO四套件平均提升8.2%），收敛速度相当或更快，在低数据场景下数据效率显著更高。

Conclusion: 动作表示是影响VLA性能的真实杠杆，与骨干网络或训练配方的进一步扩展不同，跨骨干网络的一致性表明改进行动解码器结构是提升VLA性能的有效方向。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have advanced rapidly across backbones, training recipes, and data scale, yet the action decoder, which converts the backbone's hidden state into a continuous control signal, has barely changed and remains a single-point predictor across the majority of current VLAs. Whether implemented via autoregressive token bins, L1 regression, or flow-matching denoising, the resulting decoder treats the action space as unstructured, leaving the geometric proximity of neighboring actions unexploited during training. To advance this, we introduce ActionMap, a voxel heatmap action head that drops into an existing VLA in place of its native action decoder. For each new action, the head predicts a voxel heatmap over the action space, where each voxel directly stores the probability of the corresponding action. Across LIBERO simulation and real-world Franka manipulation, our heatmap head surpasses two architecturally distinct backbones at matched training steps (e.g., +8.2% over OpenVLA-OFT's L1 regression head on the LIBERO four-suite average), converges at comparable or faster rates on both backbones, and remains markedly more data-efficient at low training data. The cross-backbone consistency indicates that action representation is a real lever for VLA performance, distinct from further backbone or recipe scaling. Project Page: https://github.com/showlab/ActionMap.

</details>


### [152] [T-GMP: Terrain-conditioned Generative Motion Priors for Versatile and Natural Humanoid Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06944)
*Junhong Guo,Hao Hu,Chen Chen,Haoxuan Han,Linao Gong,Xin Yang,Zhicheng He,Yao Su,Fenghua He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种地形条件生成式运动先验（T-GMP）模块，结合条件变分自编码器和对抗学习框架，使双足机器人能够在不同地形上实现既自然又鲁棒的运动，在穿越成功率和运动平滑度上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的双足运动方法依赖固定运动先验，难以适应多变地形环境，在实现类人自然性和鲁棒地形穿越之间缺乏统一框架。

Method: 1) 利用条件变分自编码器从少量专家状态-地形演示中学习地形条件化的潜运动流形；2) 提出立足点惩罚（Foothold Penalty）；3) 将T-GMP集成到对抗学习流水线中，判别器根据局部地形特征动态调节自然性约束。

Result: 在穿越成功率和运动平滑度上优于现有基线方法，同时保持了仿生自然且物理协调的运动。

Conclusion: T-GMP有效解决了双足机器人运动中自然性与鲁棒性的权衡，通过地形条件化的运动先验实现了统一的跨地形策略。

Abstract: Achieving both anthropomorphic naturalness and robust terrain traversal remains a fundamental challenge in humanoid locomotion. Existing Reinforcement Learning (RL) approaches typically rely on fixed motion priors, limiting their adaptability to varying environments. We propose Terrain-conditioned Generative Motion Priors (T-GMP), a module that captures a terrain-conditioned latent motion manifold from a few expert state-terrain demonstrations using a Conditional Variational Autoencoder (CVAE). The learned priors enable smooth style transitions, facilitating a unified policy that adapts to terrain variations. We integrate T-GMP into an adversarial learning pipeline with our proposed Foothold Penalty, where a discriminator dynamically modulates naturalness constraints conditioned on local terrain features, guiding the generation of versatile and human-like motions. Experimental results demonstrate that our method outperforms existing baselines in traversal success rate and motion smoothness, while preserving biomimetically natural and physically coordinated motions.

</details>


### [153] [LIMMT: Less is More for Motion Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06953)
*Yu Guan,Zekun Qi,Chenghuai Lin,Xuchuan Chen,Dairu Liu,Wenyao Zhang,Jilong Wang,Xinqiang Yu,He Wang,Li Yi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LIMMT提出了一种数据驱动的运动跟踪方法，仅使用AMASS数据集不到3%的数据即可达到优于全数据集训练的性能，通过从物理可行性、多样性和复杂性三个维度定义运动数据质量来实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量的运动数据可以引导跟踪策略在训练早期走向更优的优化轨迹，而现有工作缺乏对基于物理的人形运动跟踪的数据层面研究。

Method: 提出LIMMT框架，从物理可行性、多样性和复杂性三个维度定义运动数据质量，进行数据筛选和清洗，并应用于网络来源的运动捕捉数据。

Result: 仅使用AMASS数据集不到3%的数据即可获得比使用完整数据集更好的跟踪性能，在估计的网络运动捕捉数据上也有显著效果。

Conclusion: 数据质量比数量更重要，LIMMT框架通过数据筛选有效提升了物理人形运动跟踪的性能。

Abstract: We argue that high-quality motion data can steer tracking policies toward better optimization trajectories early in training. In this work, we introduce LIMMT (Less Is More for Motion Tracking). To our knowledge, this is the first data-centric study for physics-based humanoid motion tracking. We go beyond simply removing low-quality and erroneous clips, but define motion data quality through three dimensions: physics feasibility, diversity, and complexity. We show that even training with under 3% of AMASS yields better tracking performance than training with the full dataset. We further conduct data cleaning on the estimated web-sourced mocap data. Extensive experiments and analyses validate the effectiveness of our framework.

</details>


### [154] [Compliance-Based Sensor Placement for Force Sensing on a Sensorized Prostate Phantom](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06977)
*Sizhe Tian,Yinoussa Adagolodjo,Jeremie Dequidt*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于柔顺性的传感器布置方法，用于前列腺直肠指诊训练传感体模的力感知。该方法通过加权贪心策略最大化局部力重建能力，优先覆盖临床相关的后部接触区域，在目标区域的重建得分比全局QR策略提高22.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现软体机器人医疗体模中有限且实用的传感配置下的力可观测性，特别是针对前列腺直肠指诊训练中力感知的需求。

Method: 构建有限元仿真数据集，建立将外力输入与压力和位移响应关联的柔顺矩阵；提出加权贪心选择策略，该策略最大化局部力重建能力，优先考虑临床相关的后部接触区域，并避免在感兴趣区域直接放置标记。

Result: 与全局QR布置策略相比，所提方法在目标区域的平均重建得分提高了22.5%。

Conclusion: 区域感知的稀疏传感器布置可以改善软体机器人医疗体模中的力可观测性，同时保持有限且实用的传感配置。

Abstract: This work presents a compliance-based sensor placement method for force sensing on a sensorized prostate phantom designed for Digital Rectal Examination training. The phantom combines three internal pneumatic chambers, used as intrinsic pressure sensors, with ten surface displacement markers. A finite-element simulation dataset is generated by applying external forces at sampled surface locations, from which a compliance matrix relating force inputs to pressure and displacement responses is constructed. Based on this matrix, we propose a weighted greedy selection strategy that maximizes local force reconstructability while prioritizing the clinically relevant posterior contact region and avoiding marker placement directly within the Region of Interest. Compared with a global QR-based placement strategy, the proposed method increases the mean reconstructability score in the target region by 22.5%. These results suggest that region-aware sparse sensor placement can improve force observability in soft robotic medical phantoms while maintaining a limited and practical sensing configuration.

</details>


### [155] [Mission-Level Runtime Assurance Framework for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06996)
*Chieh Tsai,Salim Hariri*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究自动驾驶中高层驾驶指令出现故障时的运行时安全性问题。提出一种评估驾驶安全与任务完成可行性的框架，能检测并拒绝不安全或任务不可行的指令。实验表明，平台级运行时安全无法检测任务级规划故障，而所提框架能有效拒绝任务不可行指令并提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统运行时安全方法主要关注即时车辆安全，忽略了高层指令故障可能导致任务失败（如跳过检查点、进入受限区域、生成无法完成任务的路线）的问题。因此需要一种同时评估驾驶安全与任务完成可行性的框架。

Method: 扩展highway-env引入任务级故障场景（跳过检查点、进入受限区域、生成无法完成任务的路线）；构建运行时监控系统在指令执行前检测并拒绝不安全或任务不可行的指令；基于Simplex-Drive框架实现自适应基线方法用于对比。

Result: 平台级运行时安全无法检测任务级规划故障；所提框架成功拒绝任务不可行指令，并在随机故障条件下提升了任务成功率。

Conclusion: 任务级规划故障是自动驾驶安全不可忽视的维度，仅依赖平台级运行时安全不足以保障完整任务安全；所提框架通过结合驾驶安全与任务可行性评估，能有效提升自动驾驶系统在指令故障下的鲁棒性与任务完成能力。

Abstract: This paper studies runtime safety for autonomous driving when high-level driving commands become faulty or unreliable. Unlike conventional runtime-safety approaches that mainly focus on immediate vehicle safety, the proposed framework evaluates both driving safety and whether the vehicle can still successfully complete its mission before a command is executed. The framework extends highway-env with mission-level fault scenarios such as skipping required checkpoints, entering restricted areas, and generating future routes that can no longer complete the mission successfully. A runtime monitoring system is introduced to detect and reject unsafe or mission-infeasible commands before execution. For comparison, an adapted Simplex-Drive runtime-safety baseline with learning-based driving control, safety fallback control, and runtime controller switching is implemented using the public Simplex-Drive framework. Experimental results show that platform-level runtime safety alone cannot detect mission-level planning faults, while the proposed framework successfully rejects mission-infeasible commands and improves mission success under randomized fault conditions.

</details>


### [156] [Task Editing for Generalizable 3D Visuomotor Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07012)
*Jian-Jian Jiang,YiHan Yang,Lan Wei,Yuming Luo,Xiao-Ming Wu,Xuhang Chen,Bin Fan,Dandan Zhang,Wei-Shi Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Task-Edit框架，通过将任务分解为场景、技能和物体组件并灵活重组，从任务中心编辑视角生成多样化演示轨迹，显著提升3D视觉运动策略在长程操作任务中的效果、泛化性和适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D视觉运动策略依赖大规模真实世界演示数据，采集成本高昂；现有数据增强方法仅进行物体级局部变换，难以生成多样化的场景-技能-物体组合。

Method: 提出Task-Edit框架，将任务分解为场景、技能和物体三个组件，通过任务中心编辑视角灵活重组这些组件，从而生成多样化轨迹。

Result: 在多种真实世界任务和机器人实体上，Task-Edit显著提升了3D视觉运动策略的性能，改善了模型在不同场景设置下的泛化能力，并使模型能够处理扰动抵抗、障碍物避让和未见过的杂乱场景等难以采集的场景。

Conclusion: Task-Edit通过任务分解与重组实现可扩展的演示生成，有效提升长程操作任务的泛化能力，具有有效性、泛化性和适用性三大优势。

Abstract: 3D visuomotor policies offer a promising direction for complex robotic manipulation, as depth maps and point clouds provide rich geometric information for spatial reasoning. However, their success often depends on large-scale real-world demonstrations, which are costly and time-consuming to collect. To this end, existing methods commonly use demonstration generation strategies to improve data efficiency by applying object-centric transformations to human-collected demonstrations, such as varying object poses or scales. While effective for local variation, these transformations largely preserve the original scene structure and skill sequence, limiting their ability to synthesize diverse scene-skill-object combinations for complex tasks. In this paper, we propose Task-Edit, a novel demonstration generation framework that generates diverse trajectories from a task-centric editing perspective. The key insight of Task-Edit is to decompose a task into scene, skill and object components, and flexibly recombine them. In this way, Task-Edit enables scalable demonstration generation and significantly improves generalization for long-horizon manipulation tasks. We evaluate Task-Edit through extensive real-world experiments and demonstrate three advantages: (1) Effectiveness: Task-Edit significantly improves 3D visuomotor policies across various real-world tasks and robot embodiments. (2) Generalizability: Task-Edit improves model generalization across different scenario setups. (3) Applicability: Task-Edit enables models to handle scenarios that are difficult to collect in the real world, including disturbance resistance, obstacle avoidance and unseen cluttered scenes.

</details>


### [157] [A Multi-Operator Mixed-Reality Interface for Multi-Robot Control and Coordination: Co-Located and Private Workspace Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07013)
*Omotoye Shamsudeen Adekoya,Antonio Sgorbissa,Carmine Tommaso Recchiuto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合现实界面HORUS，将单操作员多机器人监督扩展至多操作员协作，支持共置共享工作区和独立私有工作区两种模式。通过36人实验表明，两种模式任务执行效果相当，但共置共享模式显著提升了协作感知、共享理解和交接清晰度，成为更受偏好的协作方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多操作员控制机器人团队需要共享态势感知并防止冲突干预，现有界面多面向单操作员，缺乏对多操作员协作的有效支持。

Method: 基于HORUS界面，提出混合现实架构，融合配准驱动的场景构建、轻量级共享会话同步和每机器人控制租约机制，支持共置共享与私有工作区两种协作模式。

Result: 36人（18对）实验表明两种模式客观任务性能相当，但共置共享模式在协作感知、共享理解和交接清晰度方面显著优于私有工作区模式，且为操作员更偏好的模式。

Conclusion: 物理共置混合现实工作区能有效改善操作员间的协调质量，即使在底层机器人控制工具不变的情况下，也能提升多操作员团队的整体协作表现。

Abstract: Multi-operator control of robot teams requires not only access to the same mission information, but also mechanisms for maintaining shared awareness and preventing conflicting interventions. Building on our previous HORUS interface (Holistic Operational Reality for Unified Systems) we present a mixed-reality interface that extends single-operator multi-robot supervision to collaborative multi-operator use. The system supports two complementary modes: a co-located shared workspace, in which operators observe and manipulate the same mini-map in the same physical location, and a private-workspace mode, in which operators work on the same mission through independently placed local workspaces. The architecture combines registration-driven scene construction, lightweight shared-session synchronization, and per-robot control leases to support collaborative monitoring, tasking, and teleoperation while preventing conflicting commands. We evaluated the approach in a human-subject study with 36 participants (18 pairs) controlling three Nova Carter mobile robots in two search environments. The performance of the objective task was comparable across the two modes, indicating that both modes supported effective mission execution. However, the co-located shared workspace significantly improved perceived collaboration, shared understanding, and handoff clarity, and was the preferred collaborative mode. These results indicate that physically co-locating the MR workspace improves how operators coordinate even when the underlying robot-control tools remain unchanged.

</details>


### [158] [Extending Responsibility-Sensitive Safety for the Assessment of Offloaded Autonomous Driving Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07067)
*Robin Dehler,Aryan Thakur,Michael Buchholz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将责任敏感安全（RSS）模型扩展至自动驾驶功能卸载场景的方法，通过考虑本地与卸载服务组合的不同响应时间，在卸载决策中引入RSS安全约束与回退机制，仅在当前交通状况安全时才允许卸载，否则回退至本地执行，并引入热备份阶段以加速安全切换。实验表明该方法在保障安全性的同时保留了分布式计算的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶系统对安全性有根本性要求，功能卸载虽能提升计算效率和降低能耗，但引入无线V2X通信后导致服务组合响应时间增加且不稳定，直接影响车辆反应时间和安全保证。现有安全框架未充分考虑卸载场景下不同响应时间对安全性的影响。

Method: 1) 扩展RSS定义以显式区分本地与卸载AD服务组合的不同响应时间；2) 将RSS安全约束集成到功能卸载决策中，仅当当前交通状况在对应端到端响应时间下保持安全时才允许卸载；3) 设计违反安全条件时的受控回退机制（回退至本地执行）；4) 引入包含热备份阶段的增强回退策略，实现从卸载到本地服务的更快、更安全切换。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界实验中，所提方法相比现有最先进的功能卸载和安全框架，在安全性上有所提升，同时在安全条件允许时保留了分布式计算的优势。

Conclusion: 通过将RSS安全模型显式纳入自动驾驶功能卸载的决策与回退机制中，可在保持卸载计算效益的同时有效保障系统安全性，热备份策略进一步降低了回退切换风险。

Abstract: Safety is a fundamental requirement in the development of autonomous driving (AD) systems. While function offloading has demonstrated significant benefits in terms of computational efficiency and energy consumption, its application to safety-critical AD functionality introduces new challenges. In particular, offloaded service compositions incur increased and variable response times due to wireless vehicle-to-everything (V2X) communication, which directly affects the vehicle's reaction time and thus its safety guarantees. In this paper, we address this challenge by extending the definitions of Responsibility-Sensitive Safety (RSS) to explicitly account for different response times of local and offloaded AD service compositions. Based on this extension, we propose an integration into function offloading, using the RSS safety constraints for offloading decision-making and fallback mechanisms. Offloaded service compositions are only permitted if the current traffic situation remains safe under the corresponding end-to-end response time. If this condition is violated, the system performs a controlled fallback to local execution. Furthermore, we introduce an enhanced fallback strategy that includes a warm-standby phase for offloaded services, enabling faster and safer transitions from offloaded to local services. The proposed approach is integrated into our AD stack and evaluated in both simulation and the real world. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method improves safety compared to state-of-the-art function offloading and safety frameworks, while preserving the benefits of distributed computation when safety conditions allow.

</details>


### [159] [Predictive Style Matching: Natural and Robust Humanoid Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07083)
*Simeon Nedelchev,Ekaterina Chaikovskaia,Egor Davydenko,Eduard Zaliaev,Roman Gorbachev*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出预测性风格匹配（PSM）方法，通过离线预测器将机器人下半身状态映射到上半身关节和目标步态以塑造奖励，在保持任务型RL抗扰能力的同时将风格误差降低约一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人形机器人运动控制中，任务型奖励导致僵硬不对称步态，运动模仿方法虽提升外观但对扰动敏感，因参考信号可能阻碍平衡所需的瞬态位姿。

Method: PSM使用离线预测器根据机器人下半身状态历史和速度命令，生成可解释的上半身关节和步态目标，以此塑造训练奖励。目标基于状态而非时间索引，预测器仅在训练阶段使用，部署时保持与任务型RL基准相同的感知接口和推理成本。

Result: 在Unitree G1的仿真和硬件实验中，PSM相比任务型RL将上半身风格误差降低约一个数量级，同时保持相似的跌倒恢复率；而运动模仿基线虽风格误差最低，但扰动恢复失败率高出约5倍。

Conclusion: PSM在风格质量和鲁棒性之间取得了更好的平衡，既避免了任务型RL的僵硬步态，又克服了运动模仿对扰动敏感的问题。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has become the prevailing approach to humanoid locomotion control: policies transfer reliably from simulation to hardware and recover gracefully from disturbances.
  Motion quality, however, still lags behind: task-only rewards often converge to stiff, asymmetric gaits, while motion imitation methods improve appearance but become more sensitive to external disturbances because reference signals can oppose the transient poses needed to regain balance.
  We propose Predictive Style Matching, in which an offline predictor maps the robot's lower-body state history and velocity commands to interpretable upper-body joint and gait targets that shape the rewards during training.
  Because the targets are state-conditioned rather than time-indexed and the predictor is used only at training time, the deployed controller inherits the proprioceptive interface and inference cost of a task-only RL baseline.
  On the Unitree G1, in both simulation and hardware, PSM reduces upper-body style error by roughly an order of magnitude over task-only RL while preserving its fall-recovery rate, whereas the motion-imitation baseline attains the lowest style error but fails to recover from disturbances about five times as often.

</details>


### [160] [Dreaming when Necessary: Advancing World Action Models with Adaptive Multi-Modal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07089)
*Yinzhou Tang,Jingbo Xu,Yu Shang,Zihao Song,Chen Gao,Wei Wu,Yong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AdaWAM提出了一种自适应多模态推理的世界动作模型，通过轻量动态路由器在任务执行中自主切换文本推理（任务转换时）和视觉推理（精细操作时），在提升推理效率的同时在仿真和真实世界任务中超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型过度依赖视频预测作为动作先验，缺乏自适应多模态推理能力，在长周期复杂任务上表现受限。作者观察到不同执行上下文需要不同的推理模式（文本推理用于任务转换，视觉推理用于精细操作），由此出发设计自适应方案。

Method: 提出AdaWAM，核心是集成一个轻量动态路由器（dynamic router），在任务执行过程中根据上下文自主触发文本推理或视觉推理，实现多模态推理模式的自适应切换。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界的具身任务上，AdaWAM显著提升了推理效率，并在性能上超越了当前最先进的具身策略。

Conclusion: 自适应多模态推理是提升世界动作模型在复杂长周期任务上效果的关键方向，轻量路由机制可有效平衡不同推理模式的效率与精度需求。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) offer a promising approach to embodied intelligence, yet existing methods rely heavily on video prediction as action priors and lack adaptive multimodal reasoning, limiting their effectiveness on long-horizon, complex tasks. We observe that WAMs require different multimodal reasoning modes under different execution contexts: textual reasoning is essential during task transitions to guide high-level action prediction, while visual reasoning is critical during fine-grained manipulation for precise control. Motivated by this observation, we propose \textbf{AdaWAM}, a world action model with adaptive multimodal reasoning abilities. AdaWAM integrates a lightweight dynamic router that autonomously triggers textual or visual reasoning as needed during task execution. Experiments on both simulated and real-world embodied tasks show that AdaWAM substantially improves inference efficiency while outperforming state-of-the-art embodied policies. Codes and demos are available at: https://adawam.github.io/.

</details>


### [161] [Coarse-to-Control: Action-Token Planning for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07107)
*Jinhao Wu,Shiduo Zhang,Yicheng Liu,Xiaopeng Yu,Sixian Li,Siyin Wang,Hang Zhao,Jing Huo,Yang Gao,Jingjing Gong,Xipeng Qiu,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Coarse-to-Control框架，通过在动作token空间中引入原生规划机制，先预测粗粒度动作token序列作为计划，再生成可执行动作token，显著提升VLA模型在长时序任务中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型直接将观测映射到动作，缺乏显式的中间规划步骤，导致在长时域任务中早期错误会累积放大，性能受限。

Method: 提出Coarse-to-Control框架：策略首先预测一个紧凑的粗粒度动作token序列（plan），该序列概括了预期的未来轨迹；然后基于此计划生成可执行的细粒度动作token（control）。规划与执行共享统一的离散动作词汇表，使得计划紧贴控制流形，提供可直接指导而非需要翻译的抽象提示。

Result: 在LIBERO、SimplerEnv-WidowX和真实世界操作任务上，动作token规划一致优于直接动作生成，在长时域多阶段任务上增益最为显著。

Conclusion: 在VLA模型中引入原生动作token空间规划，能够有效缓解长时序任务中的错误累积问题，提升整体任务完成质量。

Abstract: Most vision-language-action (VLA) models map observations directly to actions without explicit intermediate planning, which limits performance on long-horizon tasks where early mistakes compound. We propose Coarse-to-Control, a plan-execute VLA that introduces planning natively in the action-token space. The key idea is to let the policy first predict a compact sequence of coarse action tokens that summarize the intended future trajectory, and then generate executable action tokens conditioned on this plan. Because both planning and execution share a unified discrete action vocabulary, the plan stays close to the control manifold and provides directly actionable guidance rather than an abstract hint that must be translated back to motor commands. Experiments on LIBERO, SimplerEnv-WidowX, and real-world manipulation tasks show that action-token planning consistently improves over direct action generation, with the largest gains on long-horizon multi-stage tasks.

</details>


### [162] [QuadVerse: An Integrated Framework Aligning Visual-Physical Reality for Quadruped Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07118)
*Yuxiang Chen,Yuanhao Wang,Ziheng Zhang,Meng Zhang,Yu Liu,Yufei Jia,Tiancai Wang,Erjin Zhou,Jin Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出QuadVerse，一个通过重建场景校准视觉、物理和驱动动力学的集成框架，利用3D高斯泼溅重建、接触标定和残差动力学补偿，实现零样本视觉导航策略部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法分别处理视觉或动力学域差，忽略了这些不匹配在机器人状态演化中积累和传播的问题。

Method: 1) 从RGB视频重建几何约束的3D高斯泼溅场景，支持光度逼真的自身视图渲染和碰撞就绪的语义网格提取；2) 网格用于接触标定：初始化空间变化的摩擦先验，通过基于轨迹的后验搜索精炼；3) 训练残差动力学补偿器，在接触标定地形上回放真实轨迹，解缠地形接触误差与驱动器非理想性。

Result: QuadVerse在重建质量和运动跟踪上优于相关基线，实现了鲁棒的零样本视觉导航策略部署，无需任务特定的真实世界数据收集。

Conclusion: 通过集成视觉、接触和动力学三个层面的校准，QuadVerse有效缩小了sim-to-real差距，展示了从视频重建到零样本策略部署的完整流程的可行性。

Abstract: Simulation is central to robot learning, yet the sim-to-real gap remains a major bottleneck.Existing approaches often tackle visual or dynamic gaps separately, overlooking how these individual mismatches accumulate and propagate throughout the robot's state evolution.In this paper, we introduce QuadVerse, an integrated framework that uses reconstructed scenes as a calibration substrate for aligning visual perception, physical interaction, and actuator dynamics.From captured RGB videos, we reconstruct geometry-constrained 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) scenes that support batched photorealistic ego-view rendering and collision-ready semantic mesh extraction. The meshes further enable contact calibration by initializing spatially varying friction priors and refining them through trajectory-based posterior search.To address remaining actuator discrepancies, QuadVerse trains a residual dynamics compensator by replaying real-world trajectories on the contact-calibrated terrain, reducing the entanglement between terrain-induced contact errors and actuator non-idealities.Experiments show that QuadVerse improves reconstruction quality and locomotion tracking over relevant baselines.Leveraging this foundation, we demonstrate robust zero-shot visual-navigation policy deployment without task-specific real-world rollouts.

</details>


### [163] [Test-Time Trajectory Optimization for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07170)
*Yihong Xu,Eloi Zablocki,Yuan Yin,Elias Ramzi,Ellington Kirby,Alexandre Boulch,Matthieu Cord*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TOAD提出了一种测试时交叉熵优化方法，将现有规划器的评分器作为轨迹级奖励函数进行搜索，无需重新训练即可提升多种端到端规划器的性能，在NAVSIM和HUGSIM基准上取得SOTA结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端规划器先生成候选轨迹再评分，评分器无法影响轨迹集合，弱候选集会限制规划性能上限。

Method: 将评分器视为学习到的轨迹级奖励函数，在测试时使用交叉熵方法（CEM）搜索最优轨迹，并以规划器的原始候选集进行热启动，无需重新训练。

Result: 在6种基础规划器上均提升性能：NAVSIM-v1达94.7 PDMS，NAVSIM-v2达56.3 EPDMS，在闭环HUGSIM基准上也取得最优。

Conclusion: TOAD是一种即插即用的后处理优化方法，通过测试时搜索有效突破了原始候选集的质量瓶颈，显著提升现有规划器性能。

Abstract: End-to-end planners for autonomous driving typically generate a set of candidate trajectories, score each one, and return the highest-scoring candidate. However, the scorer is applied only after the proposals are generated and cannot influence the set of trajectories: a weak set of candidates limits planning performance regardless of the scorer's quality. We instead treat the scorer as a learned trajectory-level reward function and search for trajectories that maximize it. Our method, TOAD, runs the Cross-Entropy Method at test time, warm-started from the planner's proposals. It requires no retraining and is plug-and-play for existing planners. Across six base planners, TOAD improves results on NAVSIM-v1 (94.7 PDMS), NAVSIM-v2 (56.3 EPDMS), and the closed-loop HUGSIM benchmark. The code will be made publicly available via the project page: https://valeoai.github.io/TOAD/.

</details>


### [164] [A Causal Probabilistic Framework for Perception-Informed Closed-Loop Simulation of Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07186)
*Zhennan Fei,Rickard Johansson,Mikael Andersson,Matthias Eng,Mattias Eriksson,Kaveh Kianfar,Sadegh Rahrovani,Chris van der Ploeg,Michael Borth,Maren Buermann,Michiel Braat,Henk Goossens,Zijian Han,Majid Khorsand Vakilzadeh,Gabriel Rodrigues de Campos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种感知信息化的SIL测试方法，通过在标准仿真环境中注入基于物理触发条件（如雾、雨、目标合并）的因果概率感知错误模型，弥合理想仿真与现实感知行为之间的差距，为SOTIF（ISO 21448）验证提供可扩展路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SIL仿真框架多采用理想感知模型，绕过了感知算法的功能不足，导致安全评估过于乐观，无法真实反映现实世界的感知行为。

Method: 提出感知信息化的SIL测试方法，将因果概率模型融入标准化场景仿真工具链，系统性地注入源于物理触发条件（雾、雨、目标合并等）的感知错误（如检测丢失、尺寸不准、定位偏移）。

Result: 感知信息化测试能揭示理想SIL环境无法捕获的潜在运行风险。

Conclusion: 该方法为ADAS和ADS的SOTIF（ISO 21448）验证提供了可扩展的途径，弥合了地面实况仿真与现实感知行为之间的差距。

Abstract: Software-in-the-loop (SIL) simulation is a cornerstone for the validation of modern automotive safety functions. However, many current frameworks utilize ideal sensing, which bypasses the functional insufficiencies of perception algorithms, leading to over-optimistic safety assessments. This paper proposes a perception-informed SIL testing methodology that bridges the gap between ground-truth simulation and real-world perception behavior. We present a framework for incorporating causal probabilistic models into standardized, scenario-based simulation toolchains, applicable to both Advanced Driver Assistance Systems (ADAS) and Autonomous Driving Systems (ADS). Our approach enables the systematic injection of realistic perception errors, such as loss of detection, sizing inaccuracies, and positioning offsets, derived from physical triggering conditions like fog, rain, and object-merging scenarios. By evaluating these ``faults'' within a standardized simulation environment, we demonstrate that perception-informed testing reveals latent operational risks that ideal SIL environments fail to capture, providing a scalable pathway for SOTIF (ISO 21448) validation.

</details>


### [165] [Shield-Loco: Shielding Locomotion Policies with Predictive Safety Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07193)
*Aditya Shirwatkar,Sebastian Sanokowski,Shishir Kolathaya,Aaron Johnson,Majid Khadiv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种预测性安全过滤器，通过后置滤波RL策略的接触点输入，在全物理模型中异步搜索更安全的接触序列，利用学习价值函数引导长期回报，在四足机器人密集杂乱环境中显著减少安全违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL策略在动态腿部运动中缺乏对训练时未呈现的安全约束的规避机制，现有安全框架要么依赖无法推理全身行为的降阶模型，要么需要保守恢复控制器降低任务性能。

Method: 提出预测性安全过滤器：预测到碰撞时，基于采样的优化器使用全物理模型异步搜索更安全的接触序列，学习价值函数引导长期回报；三个关键组件包括几何投影、动量增强更新和副本交换，使优化在非连续接触景观中可处理。

Result: 在四足机器人的仿真和真实世界密集杂乱环境中验证，显示安全违规显著减少，且对名义输入的偏离最小。

Conclusion: 所提方法能在保持任务性能的同时有效提升安全性，适用于动态腿部运动的在线安全过滤。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) policies enable dynamic legged locomotion but lack mechanisms to avoid violations of safety constraints that are absent during training. Large-scale offline safe learning is impractical for covering all edge cases. Existing safety frameworks either rely on reduced-order models that cannot reason about whole-body behaviors or require conservative recovery controllers that degrade task performance. We propose a predictive safety filter that post-hoc filters the nominal contact locations fed to the RL policy. When a collision is predicted, a sampling-based optimizer asynchronously searches for safer contact sequences using a full-physics model, while a learned value function bootstraps long-horizon returns. Our three algorithmic components (geometric projection of sampled contacts, momentum-augmented updates, and replica-exchange) make the optimization tractable in a discontinuous contact landscape. We validate the filter on a quadruped robot in dense, cluttered environments, both in simulation and in the real world, showing substantial reductions in safety violations with minimal deviation from the nominal input.

</details>


### [166] [An Abstract Architecture for Explainable Autonomy in Hazardous Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07211)
*Matt Luckcuck,Hazel M Taylor,Marie Farrell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种支持自主系统解释其行为（可解释自主性）的抽象架构，为民用核工业等高风险环境中人类用户信任自主机器人系统提供了设计模板，并通过具体实例展示了该架构的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在危险环境中使用自主机器人系统时，人类用户需要信任这些系统；可解释性与系统可信度密切相关，因此需要将可解释性作为系统设计的一部分，而非事后添加。

Method: 提出了一种支持可解释自主性的抽象架构，作为实现可解释自主系统的设计模板，并给出了在民用核工业中的应用实例。

Result: 提供了一个抽象架构及其实践应用示例，展示了如何在需要工人和监管机构信任决策的场景中实施可解释自主性。

Conclusion: 可解释性应像安全性和安全性一样被设计到系统中，该架构为构建可信任的可解释自主系统提供了设计基础。

Abstract: Autonomous robotic systems are being proposed for use in hazardous environments, often to reduce the risks to human workers. In the immediate future, it is likely that human workers will continue to use and direct these autonomous robots, much like other computerised tools but with more sophisticated decision-making. Therefore, one important area on which to focus engineering effort is ensuring that these users trust the system. Recent literature suggests that explainability is closely related to how trustworthy a system is. Like safety and security properties, explainability should be designed into a system, instead of being added afterwards. This paper presents an abstract architecture that supports an autonomous system explaining its behaviour (explainable autonomy), providing a design template for implementing explainable autonomous systems. We present a worked example of how our architecture could be applied in the civil nuclear industry, where both workers and regulators need to trust the system's decision-making capabilities.

</details>


### [167] [Robotic Policy Adaptation via Weight-Space Meta-Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07217)
*Christian Bianchi,Siamak Yousefi,Alessio Sampieri,Andrea Roberti,Luca Rigazio,Fabio Galasso,Luca Franco*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WIZARD是一个权重空间元学习框架，通过生成任务特定的LoRA参数来适配冻结的VLA策略，无需任务特定微调或动作标签，仅需语言指令和简短演示视频即可在单次前向传播中完成适配。实验表明在LIBERO上性能提升最高达14倍，在真实机器人上也有显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型适应新任务需要任务特定的演示数据、动作标注和额外微调，部署成本高且难以规模化扩展。

Method: 提出WIZARD框架，采用权重空间元学习，在元训练阶段学习将任务证据（语言指令+演示视频）直接映射到专家级LoRA参数更新，在测试阶段仅需单次前向传播即可生成任务特定的适配权重，无需目标任务动作标签或测试时优化。

Result: 在LIBERO基准上，WIZARD在未见数据集集合上性能提升约2倍，在未见任务上提升约14倍。在Franka Emika Panda真实机器人上持续优于基线，表明生成的适配器提供了超越仿真的任务级专门化。

Conclusion: WIZARD通过元学习生成任务特定LoRA参数，有效消除了VLA模型适应新任务时的微调需求，实现了高效的任务级专门化，在仿真和真实场景中都显著提升了性能。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are emerging as a promising paradigm for robotic manipulation, enabling general-purpose policies trained from large corpora of demonstrations and action labels. However, adapting these models to new tasks still typically requires task-specific demonstrations, action annotations, and additional fine-tuning, making deployment costly and difficult to scale.
  We propose WIZARD, a weight-space meta-learning framework that sidesteps task-specific fine-tuning by generating task-specific LoRA parameters for a frozen VLA policy. Given only a language instruction and a short demonstration video, WIZARD predicts the corresponding adaptation weights in a single forward pass, without target-task action labels or test-time optimization. During meta-training, WIZARD learns to map task evidence directly to expert LoRA updates, capturing relationships between tasks in weight space.
  Experiments on LIBERO show that WIZARD improves performance by up to ~2x on unseen dataset collections and up to ~14x on unseen tasks. On a Franka Emika Panda, WIZARD consistently improves over a real-domain adapted baseline, showing that generated adapters provide task-level specialization beyond simulation.

</details>


### [168] [Beyond Waypoints: A Trajectory-Centric Waypointing Paradigm for Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07244)
*Haoxiang Shi,Xiang Deng,Haoyu Zhang,Qiaohui Chu,Yaowei Wang,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对VLN-CE任务中传统三阶段框架预测孤立航点导致不可达或规划-控制不一致的问题，提出轨迹航点范式，通过TSDF引导的扩散策略生成可执行轨迹，并设计轨迹增强导航器实现高层语义决策与低层执行的一致性，在基准测试中取得优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLN-CE方法采用航点预测器-导航器-低层控制器的三阶段解耦框架，但预测的孤立航点常不可达，且规划与执行之间存在不一致性问题。

Method: 提出轨迹航点范式，将每个候选航点锚定在可执行轨迹上；设计TSDF引导的扩散策略作为轨迹航点预测器，利用TSDF引导轨迹生成避开障碍物；提出轨迹增强导航器，将关联轨迹作为额外信息注入规划过程。

Result: 在VLN-CE基准测试上的大量实验表明，所提轨迹航点范式在性能上显著优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 轨迹航点范式通过将航点与可执行轨迹绑定，有效解决了航点不可达和规划-控制不一致问题，是一种更优的VLN-CE解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation in Continuous Environments (VLN-CE) requires agents to follow natural-language instructions while navigating in real-world-like environments. Most VLN-CE approach\-es adopt a three-stage framework: a waypoint predictor proposes navigable waypoints, and a navigator selects the best waypoint, with a low-level controller executing the movement to it. However, this decoupled paradigm often leads to unreachable waypoints or inconsistencies between planning and control. In this work, instead of predicting isolated waypoints, we introduce a novel paradigm called Trajectory Waypoint, which grounds each candidate waypoint in an executable trajectory. To realize this, we design a Trajectory Waypoint Predictor formulated as a TSDF-guided diffusion policy, which steers trajectory generation away from obstacles, inherently ensuring the reachability of the predicted waypoints. We further propose a trajectory-enhanced navigator that injects the associated trajectory as additional information for planning, enabling strict consistency between high-level semantic decisions and low-level execution. Extensive experiments on the VLN-CE benchmark show that our Trajectory Waypoint paradigm achieves superior performance over the baselines.

</details>


### [169] [CAPE: Contrastive Action-conditioned Parallel Encoding for Embodied Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07304)
*Cong Chen,Haowen Wang,Zhixiang Zhang,Pei Ren,Zhengping Che*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CAPE提出了一种对比式动作条件并行编码框架，通过区分不同动作序列导致的未来结果来学习视觉动态模型，在真实世界数据集上优于现有方法，同时显著降低长预测步长下的规划推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉动态模型通过重建未来视觉状态或展开密集潜在表示来学习，将学习能力分散在视觉显著但与规划无关的内容上，而非驱动操作结果的动作条件变化。

Method: 提出CAPE框架：给定初始观测和候选动作序列，单次前向传播解码完整未来潜在轨迹；使用目标收敛对比目标（Goal-Convergent Contrastive Objective）对齐对应相同未来结果的预测，分离对应不同结果的预测。

Result: 在真实世界DROID数据集和零样本迁移到RoboCasa上，CAPE在未来状态检索、离线动作匹配和闭环规划中显著优于先前基线，并在长预测步长下大幅降低规划推理成本。

Conclusion: CAPE通过对比学习聚焦于动作条件变化而非视觉显著内容，实现了更高效的视觉动力学建模和规划性能。

Abstract: Embodied agents need to predict the future consequences of candidate actions in order to plan effectively before execution. Existing visual dynamics models learn by reconstructing future visual states or rolling out dense latent representations, which spreads learning capacity across visually salient but planning-irrelevant content rather than the action-conditioned changes that drive manipulation outcomes. We propose CAPE, a Contrastive Action-conditioned Parallel Encoding framework that learns visual dynamics by distinguishing the future outcomes induced by different action sequences. Given an initial observation and a candidate action sequence, CAPE decodes the full future latent trajectory in a single forward pass and is trained with a Goal-Convergent Contrastive Objective that aligns predictions corresponding to the same future outcome while separating those corresponding to different outcomes. On real-world DROID and zero-shot transfer to RoboCasa, CAPE substantially outperforms prior baselines on future-state retrieval, offline action matching, and closed-loop planning, while notably reducing planning-time inference cost at long prediction horizons.

</details>


### [170] [RhinoVLA Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07383)
*Huixi Intelligence,:,Chen Zhang,Chenyang Zhou,Guanglei Ding,Guanghui He,Haibin Gao,Jiajia Chen,Jianyong Zhang,Lianyi Yu,Ningyi Xu,Ping Xu,Qingchen Li,Yingjun Hu,Yijia Zhang,Yuxi Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RhinoVLA是一个面向边缘SoC（Huixi R1）部署的VLA模型，通过采用token高效的Qwen3-VL骨干网络、连续动作专家以及统一跨机器人接口，在保持与π0.5相当的下游性能的同时，实现了11.69 Hz的端到端推理，满足10 Hz实时闭环控制要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA模型在边缘硬件上实时部署存在延迟挑战，其中VLM视觉和上下文token是主要延迟来源——当模型维度固定时，GEMM主导的投影算子的计算量与输入token数量呈线性增长。

Method: 1) 采用token高效的Qwen3-VL骨干网络和连续动作专家(Action Expert)，减少VLM侧token和计算负担；2) 引入统一接口（View Registry + 72D物理状态-动作槽空间 + 机器人实例LoRA）支持跨机器人学习；3) 通过硬件感知编译、混合精度执行和并行视觉编码进行部署优化。

Result: RhinoVLA在下游性能上与同等参数规模的π0.5相当，在Huixi R1上达到11.69 Hz的端到端推理速度，超过了10 Hz的实时闭环控制目标。

Conclusion: 通过模型-硬件协同设计，RhinoVLA成功在边缘SoC上实现了满足实时性要求的VLA模型部署，且保持了良好的下游任务性能。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown strong potential for robotic manipulation, but real-time deployment on edge hardware remains challenging. In this work, we identify VLM visual and context tokens as a major source of deployment latency: for GEMM-dominated projection operators, computation grows linearly with the number of input tokens when model dimensions are fixed. Motivated by this observation, we propose RhinoVLA, a deployment-oriented VLA model co-designed with the Huixi R1 edge SoC. RhinoVLA adopts a token-efficient Qwen3-VL backbone and a continuous Action Expert, reducing the VLM-side token and computation burden while preserving pretrained multimodal capability. To support cross-robot learning, RhinoVLA further introduces a unified interface that combines View Registry, 72D physical state-action slot space, and robotinstance LoRA, allowing heterogeneous robot observations and action schemas to be aligned under a shared policy. On the deployment side, RhinoVLA is optimized through hardware-aware compilation, mixed-precision execution, and parallel visual encoding. Experiments show that RhinoVLA achieves downstream performance comparable to π0.5 at a similar parameter scale, while reaching 11.69 Hz end-to-end inference on Huixi R1, meeting the 10 Hz real-time closedloop control target. The project will be open-sourced at https://github.com/HuixiAI/RhinoVLA.

</details>


### [171] [Spline Policy: A Structured Representation for Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07386)
*Mengze Tian,Yiming Li,Sichao Liu,Auke Ijspeert,Sylvain Calinon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出样条策略（Spline Policy, SP），用样条参数替代固定分辨率动作块来表示机器人操作策略，可解码为连续轨迹并支持多种结构化操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模仿学习策略使用固定分辨率动作块，简单有效但缺乏几何与时间结构，无法在执行前进行灵活调整。

Method: 将动作块替换为样条参数，保持策略骨干不变；样条可解码为连续轨迹，支持不同时间分辨率查询、参数空间约束或编辑；二次样条可构建距离场导出状态相关向量场，提供局部校正机制。

Result: 在低维运动学习、模拟操作、灵巧操作及真实机器人案例中，SP与多种现代策略骨干（扩散、流匹配、Transformer、VLA）兼容，并展现出紧凑解码、时间重采样、局部校正、不确定性评估和控制器兼容等特性。

Conclusion: 样条策略在保持与现代策略学习者兼容的同时，暴露了有用的运动结构属性，为机器人操作提供了更灵活、可调节的表示方式。

Abstract: Modern imitation-learning policies for robot manipulation often represent actions as fixed-resolution action chunks, which are simple and effective but expose limited geometric and temporal structure before execution. This paper studies Spline Policy (SP), a structured representation that replaces action chunks with spline parameters while keeping the policy backbone unchanged. The predicted spline can be decoded as a compact continuous trajectory, queried at different temporal resolutions, constrained or edited in parameter space, and passed to downstream controllers. For quadratic spline outputs, the same representation can also be converted into a state-dependent vector field through an analytical distance-field construction. Under the regularity and projection assumptions of this construction, the induced dynamics do not increase the distance to the generated spline, yielding a principled local corrective mechanism around the predicted motion. The spline output further supports uncertainty propagation from observations to spline parameters, trajectories, and flow fields, and can be combined with classical control mechanisms such as null-space collision avoidance without retraining the policy backbone. We instantiate SP with diffusion, flow-matching, transformer-based, and vision-language-action backbones. Experiments in low-dimensional motion learning, simulated manipulation under matched backbones, dexterous manipulation, and real-robot case studies show that SP remains compatible with modern policy learners while exposing useful motion-structure properties, including compact decoding, temporal resampling, local correction around predicted motions, uncertainty evaluation, and controller compatibility.

</details>


### [172] [Simulation-Driven Imitation Learning for Biosignals-Free Shared-Autonomy Prosthetic Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07389)
*Kaijie Shi,Wanglong Lu,Huiling Chen,Vinicius Prado da Fonseca,Ting Zou,Hanli Zhao,Xianta Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需生物信号的共享控制假手抓取框架，通过仿真自动生成大量示教数据用于模仿学习，在三个真实场景中抓取成功率超90%，优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于模仿学习的假手共享控制方法因真实人类示教数据收集成本高、变异性大而难以扩展，亟需一种可扩展的仿真数据生成方案。

Method: 构建可扩展仿真框架，自动生成多样化"到达-抓取-提升"演示：结合物理可行的抓取合成、自然的到达轨迹重定向，在程序化生成的室内环境中执行操作，记录腕部相机观测、本体感受和动作数据，构建大规模示教数据集用于模仿学习。

Result: 在三个真实场景中，仿真到真实策略抓取成功率超90%，超越基线方法，展现出更强的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 仿真驱动的训练方法在无需生物信号的共享控制假手抓取中效果显著，数据规模和质量足以支撑有效的策略学习，为假手控制提供了一条可扩展的实用路径。

Abstract: Biosignals-free shared-autonomy control of upper-limb prosthetic hands aims to enable natural and low-effort manipulation without relying on EMG or other physiological signals. Recent imitation-learning-based approaches have shown promising results, but their scalability is limited by the cost and variability of collecting large amounts of real-world human demonstration data. In this work, we present a scalable simulation framework that automatically generates diverse reach-to-grasp demonstrations from a wrist-mounted virtual camera. The framework combines physically feasible grasp synthesis, natural reaching trajectories retargeting, and reach--grasp--lift execution in procedurally generated indoor environments. It records wrist-view observations, proprioception, and actions to build a large-scale demonstration dataset for imitation learning. Through extensive simulation benchmarks, we evaluate object and scene generalization and compare several representative state-of-the-art imitation learning methods. Results show that the simulated demonstrations are sufficiently rich and consistent for effective policy learning. In three realistic settings, the learned sim-to-real policy achieves over 90\% grasp success, surpasses baseline methods, and exhibits stronger generalization, highlighting the promise of simulation-driven training for biosignals-free shared-autonomy prosthetic grasping. The demonstrations are available at \href{https://sites.google.com/view/sim-prosthetic-grasp/home}{https://sites.google.com/view/sim-prosthetic-grasp/home}.

</details>


### [173] [Rapid co-design of Buoyancy-assisted robots for Challenging Locomotion using Gaussian Evolutionary Specialists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07424)
*Ankit Sinha,Nitish Sontakke,Dennis Hong,Yusuke Tanaka,Sehoon Ha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出高斯进化专家（GES）框架，通过解耦设计空间分区与策略学习，为腿式机器人形态与控制协同优化提供高效方案。GES将专家策略分配给不断进化的高斯区域，经训练、探测和区域扩展迭代优化，替代了传统协同优化中昂贵的策略重训练。在BALLU平台上，GES发现的设计性能提升5-25%，硬件实验中优化设计跨越24cm障碍物（比基线提升3倍），同时设计优化时间减少37%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高性能腿式机器人需要联合优化形态与控制，但模型无关的强化学习在协同优化内循环中因反复策略训练而代价高昂；现有通用策略存在行为多样性崩溃问题（收敛到单一策略，跨设计表现次优），而端到端混合专家架构则因表示坍塌而失败。

Method: 提出GES框架，将设计空间分区与策略学习解耦。为每个高斯区域指派专家策略，通过训练、探测和区域扩展三个步骤迭代优化，使专家策略捕获多样化行为。优化后的专家策略集成到设计采样循环中，用直接评估替代昂贵的重新训练。

Result: 在BALLU平台上，GES发现的设计性能比朴素通用策略高5-25%；硬件实验中GES优化设计跨越24cm障碍物，比基线BALLU设计提升3倍；设计优化时间减少37%。

Conclusion: GES通过显式捕获多样化行为并解耦设计分区与策略学习，有效解决了协同优化中策略重训练成本高和行为多样性崩溃的问题，在性能和效率上均取得显著提升。

Abstract: Designing high-performance legged robots requires jointly optimizing morphology and control. Model-free Reinforcement Learning (RL) offers an alternative to model-predictive control for developing robust controllers without explicitly specifying robot dynamics. Thus, we have seen theuse of RL to train controllers and evaluate designs for robot morphology optimization. While RL has shown success inlocomotion, using it in the co-design inner loop is expensive due to repeated policy training. Universal policies conditioned on morphology offer a promising alternative, but suffer from behavioral diversity collapse, converging to a single strategy that performs sub-optimally across designs. On the other hand, end-to-end Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures fail due to a collapse in its representation. We propose Gaussian Evolutionary Specialists (GES), a framework that decouples design-space partitioning from policy learning to capture diverse behaviors explicitly. GES assigns specialist policies to evolving Gaussian regions and iteratively refines them via training, probing, and territory expansion. The resulting specialists are integrated into a design sampling loop, replacing costly re-training with direct evaluation. When tested on the Buoyancy-Assisted Light Legged Unit (BALLU), GES discovers designs with 5 - 25% higher performance than naive universal policies. On hardware, a GES optimized design overcomes a 24 cm tall obstacle - 3x improvement over the baseline BALLU design. Moreover, GES curtails design optimization time by 37%.

</details>


### [174] [Re-imagining ISO 26262 in the Age of Autonomous Vehicles: Enhancing Controllability through Transferability and Predictability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07437)
*Chaitanya Shinde,Hadi Hajieghrary,Paul Schmitt,Adam Shoemaker,Bodo Seifert,Steve Kenner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对ISO 26262标准在自动驾驶环境中缺乏人类驾驶员可控性评估的问题，本文提出将Controllability分解为Transferability（转移能力）和Predictability（可预测性）两个可审计的可量化子概念，并建立数学框架，使自动驾驶系统的安全声明可证伪、可追溯，从而扩展ISO 26262对L4/L5无人驾驶系统的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ISO 26262标准的Severity、Exposure、Controllability风险评估基于人类驾驶范式，但自动驾驶车辆（AV）不存在人类驾驶员，因此需要对Controllability进行重新定义和分解，使其适用于无人驾驶系统。

Method: 将Controllability分解为Transferability（系统将控制权移交给专用后备安全机制的能力）和Predictability（外部主体预测自动驾驶行为的能力）两个子概念；从人机交互原理出发形式化定义Predictability并提供量化数学框架；引入designed-versus-achievable gap来区分架构层面的后备声明与场景条件下的实际可达后备能力。

Result: 提出的度量指标与ISO 26262和ISO/PAS 21448（SOTIF）对齐，使后备和交互声明在ODD切片上可证伪、可追溯。这些维度补充而非替代现有标准，在保留ISO 26262结构的同时将其扩展至SAE L4/L5无人驾驶系统。

Conclusion: 通过引入Transferability和Predictability两个可审计的衡量维度，成功将ISO 26262的Controllability占位符分解为可量化的子概念，扩展了功能安全标准对无人驾驶系统的适用性，同时保持与现有标准的兼容性。

Abstract: The ISO 26262 standard defines functional safety for road vehicles through risk assessments based on Severity, Exposure, and Controllability, grounded in a human-driven vehicle paradigm. In the context of autonomous vehicles (AVs), the absence of a human driver necessitates revisiting these principles. This paper decomposes the Controllability placeholder into two auditable evidence dimensions of ISO 26262 by introducing two measurable sub-concepts: Transferability and Predictability. Transferability extends Controllability to capture AV systems' ability to hand off control to dedicated fallback safety mechanisms, while Predictability captures how easily external agents can anticipate AV behavior. Predictability is formally defined from human-robot interaction-inspired principles, and a mathematical framework is provided to quantify it. A designed-versus-achievable gap is introduced to distinguish architectural fallback claims from scene-conditioned achievable fallback capability. The proposed metrics align with ISO 26262 and ISO/PAS 21448 (SOTIF), rendering fallback and interaction claims falsifiable and traceable across ODD slices. These dimensions complement rather than replace existing standards, and the enhancements preserve the structure of ISO 26262 while extending its applicability to driverless automated systems operating at SAE Levels 4 and 5.

</details>


### [175] [Planning-aligned Token Compression for Long-Context Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07464)
*Zhixuan Liang,Yuxiao Chen,Yurong You,Peter Karkus,Wenhao Ding,Boyi Li,Alexander Popov,Yan Wang,Maximilian Igl,Yiming Li,Danfei Xu,Nikolai Smolyanskiy,Boris Ivanovic,Ping Luo,Marco Pavone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: COMPACT-VA提出一种基于条件VQ-VAE的规划对齐工作记忆框架，通过将扩展时间上下文压缩为有界表示，在同等token预算下提升6%以上的驾驶成功率，并实现3.3倍加速和2.7倍内存缩减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单目视觉-动作模型在编码长时间上下文时产生过长的token序列，超出实时计算预算；而现有token压缩方法采用基于规则的经验性启发式策略（如时间衰减），与规划任务解耦，可能导致丢失决策关键信息。

Method: 基于条件VQ-VAE的规划对齐工作记忆框架：压缩条件包括历史轨迹和学习的规划意图（后验编码器从未来轨迹中蒸馏，先验编码器从压缩观测中预测）；将压缩记忆与预测潜变量拼接后输入策略网络进行端到端优化。

Result: 在相同token预算下，成功率提升6%以上（达68.3%），各项指标一致提升；消融实验验证了规划对齐耦合的有效性；闭环评估表明保持通用驾驶性能的同时实现3.3倍加速和2.7倍内存缩减。

Conclusion: 规划对齐的压缩策略能够保留决策关键信息，显著提升计算效率与驾驶性能，为长上下文实时自动驾驶提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Monolithic vision-action models represent an emerging paradigm in autonomous driving. However, this architecture produces token sequences that quickly exceed real-time computational budgets when encoding extended temporal context for complex interactions. While approaches like linear transformers and external memory try to make the context lightweight, token compression is most compatible with the architecture as it requires no backbone modifications. Yet existing compression adopts rule-based heuristics like temporal decay, decoupled from planning, risking loss of decision-critical information. We propose COMPACT-VA, a planning-aligned working memory framework built on conditional VQ-VAE, compressing extended context into bounded representations. Compression is conditioned on both historical trajectory and a learned planning intent that the posterior encoder distills from future trajectories during training, while the prior encoder learns to predict it from compressed observations. The compressed memory, concatenated with the predicted latent, feeds the policy for end-to-end optimization, planning with retained decision-critical information. We evaluate on high-signal dynamic scenarios where historical context is most critical for behavior correctness (e.g., stop, yield, or proceed), and accordingly design behavioral metrics. Under comparable token budgets, we achieve $>$6% improvement (68.3%) on success rates with consistent gains across metrics. Ablations validate planning-aligned coupling effectiveness. Closed-loop evaluation confirms that COMPACT-VA maintained general driving performance with 3.3* speedup and 2.7* memory reduction over uncompressed processing.

</details>


### [176] [Affordance-Based Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning for Quadruped Pedipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07506)
*Tuba Girgin,Jose Castelblanco,Gabriel Rodriguez,Emre Girgin,Cagri Kilic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种三级分层强化学习框架，利用姿态可供性引导导航策略和操作策略，使四足机器人无需预设计轨迹即可自主完成物体操纵任务，在仿真和真实环境中均验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有四足机器人物体操纵研究多依赖专家设计的高层轨迹，缺乏自主选择交互点和机器人基座姿态的能力，限制了任务的泛化性和自主性。

Method: 提出了三级分层强化学习框架：顶层利用姿态可供性引导导航策略，中间层导航策略驱动底层运动策略，同时操纵策略由交互点可供性引导，实现物体中心的姿态对齐和末端执行器操纵规划。在IsaacSim中训练，进行仿真和真实实验。

Result: 在仿真多个场景中验证了姿态可供性的有效性，在真实环境中完成了多种物体交互任务，形成了物体交互数据集，机器人能自主识别候选姿态并成功执行操纵任务，无需人工引导。

Conclusion: 所提框架能够基于可供性自主识别候选姿态，并在无人类引导下在真实世界中成功执行物体操纵任务，消除了对预设计轨迹的依赖。

Abstract: The object manipulation capabilities of quadruped robots is an open research challenge. While previous studies have focused on low-level policy learning, task execution still relies on expert-designed high-level trajectories. Autonomous selection of both an affordable interaction point on the target object and an affordable robot base pose removes the need for pre-designed trajectories. This study proposes a three-level hierarchical reinforcement learning (RL) framework that utilizes pose affordances to guide the navigation policy, while the navigation policy drives the locomotion policy. In addition, the pedipulation policy is guided by interaction-point affordances, enabling object-centric pose alignment of the quadruped robot and effective end-effector manipulation planning. We train the proposed framework in the IsaacSim ecosystem and evaluate it in both simulation and real-world settings. We investigate the effectiveness of pose affordance across multiple scenarios in simulation while various object interaction tasks are validated on real-world setting forming an object-interaction dataset. The results show that the proposed framework can autonomously identify candidate poses based on their affordance and successfully execute object manipulation tasks in the real world without human guidance.

</details>


### [177] [Error-State LQR Formulation for Quadrotor UAV Trajectory Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.15768)
*Micah Reich*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于误差状态线性二次型调节器（LQR）的框架，用于四旋翼无人机轨迹跟踪控制。该方法利用误差状态动力学和指数坐标表示姿态误差，实现系统线性化以支持实时控制，并结合全状态反馈LQR控制器与级联机体角速度控制器，在动态环境下实现精确稳定的控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四旋翼无人机在动态环境中需要高精度、鲁棒的轨迹跟踪能力，而传统控制方法在非线性、强耦合的动力学系统中难以同时兼顾实时性与稳定性。本文旨在通过误差状态LQR方法解决这一问题。

Method: 采用误差状态动力学建模，使用指数坐标（exponential coordinates）表示姿态误差，将非线性系统线性化；设计基于LQR的全状态反馈控制器用于轨迹跟踪，并级联一个机体角速度控制器以处理执行器动力学。

Result: 提供了误差状态动力学、线性化过程及控制器设计的详细推导，验证了该方法在动态环境下实现精确、稳定四旋翼控制的适用性。

Conclusion: 误差状态LQR框架结合指数坐标姿态表示与级联控制结构，能够有效实现四旋翼无人机在复杂动态环境下的鲁棒轨迹跟踪控制。

Abstract: This article presents an error-state Linear Quadratic Regulator (LQR) formulation for robust trajectory tracking in quadrotor Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs). The proposed approach leverages error-state dynamics and employs exponential coordinates to represent orientation errors, enabling a linearized system representation for real-time control. The control strategy integrates an LQR-based full-state feedback controller for trajectory tracking, combined with a cascaded bodyrate controller to handle actuator dynamics. Detailed derivations of the error-state dynamics, the linearization process, and the controller design are provided, highlighting the applicability of the method for precise and stable quadrotor control in dynamic environments.

</details>


### [178] [Efficient Coordination and Synchronization of Multi-Robot Systems Under Recurring Linear Temporal Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.16531)
*Davide Peron,Victor Nan Fernandez-Ayala,Eleftherios E. Vlahakis,Dimos V. Dimarogonas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合离线规划与在线协调的混合方法，并引入同步机制应对动作延迟，用于多机器人系统在LTL任务规范下的规划问题，在9台机器人实验中验证了适应性，在高达90台智能体的仿真中证明了可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人系统在重复性LTL任务下规划效率低，且动作延迟导致协同执行困难，需要一种兼具效率、适应性和可扩展的解决方案。

Method: 采用自底向上的方法，结合离线计划合成与在线协调，通过实时通信动态调整计划；引入同步机制应对动作延迟；基于Python和ROS2开发软件包。

Result: 9台机器人实验表明适应性优于先前方法；90台智能体仿真验证了计算复杂度降低和可扩展性。

Conclusion: 该方法在保持LTL任务满足性的同时，提升了多机器人系统的适应性和可扩展性，适用于广泛的多机器人应用场景。

Abstract: We consider multi-robot systems under recurring tasks formalized as linear temporal logic (LTL) specifications. To solve the planning problem efficiently, we propose a bottom-up approach combining offline plan synthesis with online coordination, dynamically adjusting plans via real-time communication. To address action delays, we introduce a synchronization mechanism ensuring coordinated task execution, leading to a multi-agent coordination and synchronization framework that is adaptable to a wide range of multi-robot applications. The software package is developed in Python and ROS2 for broad deployment. We validate our findings through lab experiments involving nine robots showing enhanced adaptability compared to previous methods. Additionally, we conduct simulations with up to ninety agents to demonstrate the reduced computational complexity and the scalability features of our work.

</details>


### [179] [A Human-Sensitive Controller: Adapting to Human Musculoskeletal Disorder-Related Constraints via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.10102)
*Vitor Martins,Sara M. Cerqueira,Mercedes Balcells,Elazer R Edelman,Cristina P. Santos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于强化学习的人类感知协作机器人控制策略，通过Q-Learning和Deep Q-Network (DQN)两种方法优化工业环境中工人的肌肉骨骼健康。实验表明，DQN在保持零疼痛风险和安全人体工学水平的前提下，平均缩短了38%的任务完成时间，但存在仿真到现实的差距，需通过微调阶段适应真实环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工作相关肌肉骨骼障碍（WMSDs）在工业环境中持续造成劳动力参与度下降、医疗成本增加和长期残疾问题。现有研究缺乏将曾患有肌肉骨骼障碍的个体重新融入标准工作岗位的有效方案，同时优化全体工人的工效学条件。

Method: 采用强化学习（RL）开发人类感知控制策略，具体实现了Q-Learning和Deep Q-Network (DQN)两种算法，基于个体用户特征进行个性化控制。通过仿真训练后，引入微调阶段以适应真实世界条件。

Result: DQN优于Q-Learning：在保持零疼痛风险和安全的工效学水平下，平均任务完成时间缩短38%。存在仿真到现实（sim-to-real）的差距，但微调阶段成功将策略适配到真实环境。结构化测试协议验证了系统对不同人体测量学的适应性。

Conclusion: 强化学习驱动的协作机器人具有实现更安全、更包容工作环境的潜力，能够适应多样化的人体测量学特征，并有效预防肌肉骨骼损伤。

Abstract: Work-Related Musculoskeletal Disorders continue to be a major challenge in industrial environments, leading to reduced workforce participation, increased healthcare costs, and long-term disability. This study introduces a human-sensitive robotic system aimed at reintegrating individuals with a history of musculoskeletal disorders into standard job roles, while simultaneously optimizing ergonomic conditions for the broader workforce. This research leverages reinforcement learning (RL) to develop a human-aware control strategy for collaborative robots, focusing on optimizing ergonomic conditions and preventing pain during task execution. Two RL approaches, Q-Learning and Deep Q-Network (DQN), were implemented and tested to personalize control strategies based on individual user characteristics. Although experimental results revealed a simulation-to-real gap, a fine-tuning phase successfully adapted the policies to real-world conditions. DQN outperformed Q-Learning by completing tasks faster while maintaining zero pain risk and safe ergonomic levels, achieving on average 38% shorter task completion times across all tested anthropometries. The structured testing protocol confirmed the system's adaptability to diverse human anthropometries, underscoring the potential of RL-driven cobots to enable safer, more inclusive workplaces.

</details>


### [180] [HORUS: A Mixed Reality Interface for Managing Teams of Mobile Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.02622)
*Omotoye Shamsudeen Adekoya,Antonio Sgorbissa,Carmine Tommaso Recchiuto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了HORUS，一个用于同时管理多台移动机器人的混合现实界面，提供状态监控、传感器数据可视化、任务分配和多种遥操作模式，并通过用户研究验证了其在多机器人协调中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合现实界面在移动机器人控制方面已有广泛探索，但将其应用于管理机器人团队的研究十分有限。现有研究缺乏支持多机器人团队同时管理的综合性MR工具。

Method: 设计了HORUS混合现实界面，包含Mini-Map地面站用于监控状态、实时传感器数据投影、任务分配（单个/子集/全体）以及两种遥操作模式（mini-map模式和半沉浸式模式）。通过用户研究，让参与者使用HORUS管理多台移动机器人在环境中寻找线索，模拟搜索与救援任务，并将其与单独机器人遥操作进行对比。

Result: 实验验证了HORUS在多机器人协调中的多功能性和有效性，证明了其在动态团队环境中推进人机协作的潜力。

Conclusion: HORUS作为首个面向多机器人团队管理的综合性MR界面，能够有效提升多机器人协调效率，为搜索与救援等动态团队任务中的人机协作提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Mixed Reality (MR) interfaces have been extensively explored for controlling mobile robots, but there is limited research on their application to managing teams of robots. This paper presents HORUS: Holistic Operational Reality for Unified Systems, a Mixed Reality interface offering a comprehensive set of tools for managing multiple mobile robots simultaneously. HORUS enables operators to monitor individual robot statuses, visualize sensor data projected in real time, and assign tasks to single robots, subsets of the team, or the entire group, all from a Mini-Map (Ground Station). The interface also provides different teleoperation modes: a mini-map mode that allows teleoperation while observing the robot model and its transform on the mini-map, and a semi-immersive mode that offers a flat, screen-like view in either single or stereo view (3D). We conducted a user study in which participants used HORUS to manage a team of mobile robots tasked with finding clues in an environment, simulating search and rescue tasks. This study compared HORUS's full-team management capabilities with individual robot teleoperation. The experiments validated the versatility and effectiveness of HORUS in multi-robot coordination, demonstrating its potential to advance human-robot collaboration in dynamic, team-based environments.

</details>


### [181] [From Pixels to Shelf: An Integrated Robotic System for Autonomous Supermarket Stocking with a Mobile Manipulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.11740)
*Davide Peron,Victor Nan Fernandez-Ayala,Lukas Segelmark*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种集成商用硬件与ROS2架构的模块化机器人系统，用于超市自动上架。该系统利用行为树进行任务规划、微调视觉模型进行物体检测、基于ArUco标记的两步模型预测控制进行精确导航。在700多次上架实验中，拾放操作成功率超过98%，但对比基准表明其性能与成本效益仍低于人类工人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零售环境中（特别是超市）的自动上架面临动态人机交互、空间受限、产品几何形状多样等挑战，现有解决方案在部署性和实用性上存在不足。

Method: 采用模块化设计，集成商用现成硬件与ROS2架构；使用行为树进行任务规划；微调视觉模型实现物体检测；基于ArUco标记的两步模型预测控制框架实现精确货架导航。

Result: 在模拟真实超市条件的实验室实验中，共进行700多次上架操作，拾放任务成功率达98%以上。但对比基准显示，自主系统在性能和成本效益上仍不及人类工人。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了模块化机器人系统在零售环境中自动上架的可行性，同时通过量化与人类工人的差距，指出了当前自主系统在广泛商业部署前仍需改进的关键方向。

Abstract: Autonomous stocking in retail environments, particularly supermarkets, presents challenges due to dynamic human interactions, constrained spaces, and diverse product geometries. This paper introduces an efficient modular robotic system for autonomous shelf stocking, integrating commercially available hardware with a scalable algorithmic architecture. A major contribution of this work is the system integration of off-the-shelf hardware and ROS2-based perception, planning, and control into a single deployable platform for retail environments. Our solution leverages Behavior Trees (BTs) for task planning, fine-tuned vision models for object detection, and a two-step Model Predictive Control (MPC) framework for precise shelf navigation using ArUco markers. Laboratory experiments replicating realistic supermarket conditions demonstrate reliable performance, achieving over 98% success in pick-and-place operations across a total of more than 700 stocking events.
