However, our comparative benchmarks indicate that the performance and cost-effectiveness of current autonomous systems remain inferior to that of human workers, which we use to highlight key improvement areas and quantify the progress still required before widespread commercial deployment can realistically be achieved.

</details>


### [182] [CRAFT: Coaching Reinforcement Learning Autonomously using Foundation Models for Multi-Robot Coordination Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.14380)
*Seoyeon Choi,Kanghyun Ryu,Jonghoon Ock,Negar Mehr*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CRAFT是一个利用基础模型（LLM和VLM）作为“教练”来指导多机器人协调学习的框架。它通过LLM自动将长时域协调任务分解为子任务序列，为每个子任务生成奖励函数，并通过VLM引导的奖励优化循环进行改进。在四足机器人导航和双臂操作任务上验证了有效性，并实现了真实世界迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体强化学习在机器人应用中面临高维连续动作空间、复杂奖励设计和非平稳性等挑战。受人类通过教练指导学习协调的启发，作者利用基础模型的推理能力来充当机器人协调学习的“教练”。

Method: 1) 利用LLM的规划能力自动将长时域协调任务分解为子任务序列；2) 使用LLM生成的奖励函数训练每个子任务；3) 通过VLM引导的奖励优化循环不断细化奖励函数。

Result: 在多四足机器人导航和双臂操作任务上，CRAFT能够学习复杂的协调行为。在多四足导航设置中，学到的策略能够迁移到真实世界。

Conclusion: 基础模型可以有效地作为多机器人协调学习的“教练”，通过自动任务分解和奖励生成与优化，提升MARL在机器人领域的应用效果。

Abstract: Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) provides a powerful framework for learning coordination in multi-agent systems. However, applying MARL to robotics remains challenging due to their high-dimensional continuous joint action spaces, complex reward design, and non-stationarity from concurrently learning agents. On the other hand, humans often learn complex coordination with the help of coaches, who guide learning through carefully designed curricula and detailed feedback. Building on the reasoning capabilities of foundation models, we argue that these models can similarly coach robots to learn coordination. Motivated by this, we propose CRAFT: Coaching Reinforcement learning Autonomously using Foundation models for learning coordination Tasks, a framework that leverages foundation models to act as a "coach" for multi-robot coordination. CRAFT automatically decomposes long-horizon coordination tasks into sequences of subtasks using the planning capability of Large Language Models (LLMs). Then, CRAFT trains each subtask using LLM-generated reward functions, and refines them through a Vision Language Model (VLM)-guided reward-refinement loop. We evaluate CRAFT on multi-quadruped navigation and bimanual manipulation tasks, and demonstrate its capability to learn complex coordination behaviors. In addition, in a multi-quadruped navigation setting, we show that our learned policies transfer to the real world. Project website is https://iconlab.negarmehr.com/CRAFT/

</details>


### [183] [MatterDoor: Sampling Zero-shot Spatio-semantic Priors using Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.11014)
*Subhransu S. Bhattacharjee,Hao Lu,Dylan Campbell,Rahul Shome*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出利用预训练生成视觉模型为零优先级先验，通过VLM引导的外绘、单目深度估计和语义分割，为机器人从门缝观察室内场景时提供隐藏区域的时空语义信息，并在MatterDoor基准测试中验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主机器人仅能通过门缝等部分视角观察房间，墙壁和场景结构隐藏了几何信息和任务相关语义，阻碍安全导航与目标导向动作的执行。

Method: 利用离架预训练生成视觉模型（VLM引导外绘、单目深度估计、语义分割）生成带语义标签的隐藏房间3D点云假设，并在MatterDoor基准（基于Matterport3D的门遮挡室内场景）上进行评估。

Result: 该方法能在无需问题特定微调的情况下，为规划提供有用的时空语义先验，并在生成指标和Stretch机器人目标抓取任务中验证有效性。

Conclusion: 预训练生成模型可作为零优先级先验，弥补机器人部分观测下的隐藏结构信息，支持安全导航和目标导向动作的规划。

Abstract: Autonomous robots often view rooms only partially, through a doorway, where the walls and scene structure hide the geometry and task-relevant semantics needed for safe navigation and goal-directed action. We ask whether off-the-shelf pretrained generative vision models can derive this missing structure as zero-shot offline priors for robot reasoning. Such priors should support spatio-semantic queries over unobserved structure, estimating the target object likelihood in hidden regions and the probability that those regions are occupied. Given an egocentric RGB observation and target query, our pipeline uses VLM-guided outpainting, monocular depth estimation, and semantic segmentation to sample semantically labeled 3D point cloud hypotheses of the hidden room. We introduce MatterDoor, a Matterport3D-derived benchmark of doorway-occluded indoor scenes, and evaluate the resulting priors with generative metrics and simulated Stretch robot object-reaching tasks. Our results suggest that useful spatio-semantic priors for planning can be derived without problem-specific fine-tuning.

</details>


### [184] [ActiveGrasp: Information-Guided Active Grasping with Calibrated Energy-based Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.12795)
*Boshu Lei,Wen Jiang,Kostas Daniilidis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于校准能量模型的SE(3)抓取姿态生成方法，结合主动视角选择策略，通过估计抓取分布的信息增益在有限视角预算下实现密集杂乱环境中的高效抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动抓取方法要么忽略了抓取分布对信息增益估计的重要性，要么使用抓取分布的投影而忽略了SE(3)流形上抓取姿态的结构信息。

Method: 1) 在SE(3)流形上构建校准能量模型以捕获抓取分布的多模态特性，能量水平校准至抓取成功率；2) 基于校准分布的条件信息增益估计，选择下一个最佳视角以驱动机器人探索目标物体的可抓取部分。

Result: 在仿真环境和真实机器人实验中，该方法在有限视角预算下相比先前最优模型能更成功地完成杂乱环境中的抓取任务。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效解决了密集杂乱环境中的主动抓取问题，同时仿真环境可作为可复现平台供未来研究使用。

Abstract: Grasping in a densely cluttered environment is a challenging task for robots. Previous methods tried to solve this problem by actively gathering multiple views before grasp pose generation. However, they either overlooked the importance of the grasp distribution for information gain estimation or relied on the projection of the grasp distribution, which ignores the structure of grasp poses on the SE(3) manifold. To tackle these challenges, we propose a calibrated energy-based model for grasp pose generation and an active view selection method that estimates information gain from grasp distribution. Our energy-based model captures the multi-modality nature of grasp distribution on the SE(3) manifold. The energy level is calibrated to the success rate of grasps so that the predicted distribution aligns with the real distribution. The next best view is selected by estimating the information gain for grasp from the calibrated distribution conditioned on the reconstructed environment, which could efficiently drive the robot to explore affordable parts of the target object. Experiments on simulated environments and real robot setups demonstrate that our model could successfully grasp objects in a cluttered environment with limited view budgets compared to previous state-of-the-art models. Our simulated environment can serve as a reproducible platform for future research on active grasping. The source code of our paper will be made public when the paper is released to the public.

</details>


### [185] [Where to Touch, How to Contact: Hierarchical RL-MPC Framework for Geometry-Aware Long-Horizon Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.10930)
*Zhixian Xie,Yu Xiang,Michael Posa,Wanxin Jin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了分层RL-MPC框架，高层RL预测接触意图（接触位置+物体子目标位姿），底层接触隐式MPC优化局部接触模式并实时重规划，在非抓取操作任务上以10倍少于端到端方法的数据量实现高成功率、强鲁棒性和零样本仿真到现实的迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的灵巧操作需要同时推理全局几何和非光滑接触动力学，端到端策略需要大量数据且从仿真到现实的迁移效果差，现有方法缺乏对问题层次结构的有效利用。

Method: 提出分层强化学习-模型预测控制（RL-MPC）框架：高层使用RL策略预测接触意图（物体-表面接触位置+接触后子目标位姿），底层使用接触隐式MPC优化局部接触模式并通过接触动力学实时重规划，生成鲁棒的机器人动作。

Result: 在几何泛化推动、翻转重定向和环境辅助重定位等非抓取任务上，相比端到端基线方法数据量减少10倍，实现高成功率、强鲁棒性和零样本仿真到现实迁移。

Conclusion: 分层分解（高层几何推理+底层接触动力学优化）是解决灵巧操作中接触丰富问题的有效范式，显著提升数据效率和迁移能力。

Abstract: A key challenge in contact-rich dexterous manipulation is the need to jointly reason over global geometry and nonsmooth contact dynamics. End-to-end policies bypass this complexity, but often require large amounts of data and transfer poorly from simulation to reality. We address the limitations with a simple insight: dexterous manipulation is inherently hierarchical--at a high level, a robot decides where to touch (geometry); at a low level it determines how to move the object through contact dynamics. Building on this insight, we propose a hierarchical RL--MPC framework in which a high-level reinforcement learning (RL) policy predicts a contact intention, a novel object-centric interface that specifies (i) an object-surface contact location and (ii) a post-contact object subgoal pose. Conditioned on the contact intention, a low-level contact-implicit model predictive control (MPC) optimizes local contact modes and real-time (re)plans through contact dynamics to generate robot actions that robustly move the object toward each subgoal. We evaluate the framework on non-prehensile tasks, including geometry-generalized pushing across diverse object shapes, pivoting/flipping-based object reorientation, and environment-assisted object repositioning. It achieves high success rate with substantially reduced data (10 times less than end-to-end baselines), highly robust performance, and zero-shot sim-to-real transfer.

</details>


### [186] [SERNF: Sample-Efficient Real-World Dexterous Policy Fine-Tuning via Action-Chunked Critics and Normalizing Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.09580)
*Chenyu Yang,Denis Tarasov,Davide Liconti,Romain Guntz,Hehui Zheng,Robert K. Katzschmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SERFN是一种基于归一化流的样本高效离策略微调框架，通过精确似然计算和动作块级价值学习，解决了灵巧操作策略微调中的多模态动作分布和长时域信用分配问题，在真实机器人上实现了高效稳定的策略适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界灵巧操作策略的微调面临交互预算有限和动作分布高度多模态的挑战。扩散策略虽具表现力但无法计算似然，无法进行保守的基于似然的微调更新；高斯策略在多模态（尤其是分块执行动作时）会坍塌；标准单步价值函数与分块执行不匹配，导致信用分配不佳。

Method: 提出SERFN框架，核心包括：(1) 使用归一化流（Normalizing Flow）策略为多模态动作块提供精确似然，通过似然正则化实现保守稳定的策略更新，提升样本效率；(2) 引入动作块级评论家（action-chunked critic），评估完整动作序列，使价值估计与策略的时间结构对齐，改善长时域信用分配。

Result: 在两项真实世界灵巧操作任务（用剪刀从盒中取出并切割胶带、手掌朝下抓握的掌内立方体旋转）上进行评估，SERFN在标准方法难以应对的场景下实现了稳定且样本高效的适应性。

Conclusion: 这是首个在真实机器人硬件上实现基于似然的多模态生成策略与块级价值学习相结合的演示，验证了该方法在处理需要精确、灵巧控制的长时域任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Real-world fine-tuning of dexterous manipulation policies remains challenging due to limited real-world interaction budgets and highly multimodal action distributions. Diffusion-based policies, while expressive, do not permit conservative likelihood-based updates during fine-tuning because action probabilities are intractable. In contrast, conventional Gaussian policies collapse under multimodality, particularly when actions are executed in chunks, and standard per-step critics fail to align with chunked execution, leading to poor credit assignment. We present SERFN, a sample-efficient off-policy fine-tuning framework with normalizing flow (NF) to address these challenges. The normalizing flow policy yields exact likelihoods for multimodal action chunks, allowing conservative, stable policy updates through likelihood regularization and thereby improving sample efficiency. An action-chunked critic evaluates entire action sequences, aligning value estimation with the policy's temporal structure and improving long-horizon credit assignment. To our knowledge, this is the first demonstration of a likelihood-based, multimodal generative policy combined with chunk-level value learning on real robotic hardware. We evaluate SERFN on two challenging dexterous manipulation tasks in the real world: cutting tape with scissors retrieved from a case, and in-hand cube rotation with a palm-down grasp -- both of which require precise, dexterous control over long horizons. On these tasks, SERFN achieves stable, sample-efficient adaptation where standard methods struggle.

</details>


### [187] [Predicting Dynamic Map States from Limited Field-of-View Sensor Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.12360)
*Knut Peterson,David Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文探索利用深度学习，通过将动态传感器数据表示为同时包含空间和时间信息的单图像格式，在有限视场条件下预测动态地图状态，并证明现有图像到图像学习模型可在此任务上实现高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实部署中的自主系统常受限于有限视场（FOV），无论是系统设计导致还是意外遮挡或传感器故障。在大视场不可用的情况下，需要基于可用数据推断环境信息并预测周围状态，以维持安全准确的运行。

Method: 将动态传感器数据表示为一种简单的单图像格式，该格式同时捕获空间信息和时间信息，从而使得多种现有的图像到图像（image-to-image）学习模型能够直接应用于有限FOV下的地图状态预测任务。

Result: 在多种不同的传感场景下，所提出的方法能够以高精度预测地图状态，验证了将动态时序数据编码为单图像格式并应用图像到图像模型的可行性和有效性。

Conclusion: 通过将时序动态数据转化为单图像表示，可以充分利用现有成熟的图像到图像学习模型来解决有限视场下的地图状态预测问题，为自主系统在受限感知条件下的安全运行提供了一种有效方案。

Abstract: When autonomous systems are deployed in real-world scenarios, sensors are often subject to limited field-of-view (FOV) constraints, either naturally through system design, or through unexpected occlusions or sensor failures. In conditions where a large FOV is unavailable, it is important to be able to infer information about the environment and predict the state of nearby surroundings based on available data to maintain safe and accurate operation. In this work, we explore the effectiveness of deep learning for dynamic map state prediction based on limited FOV time series data. We show that by representing dynamic sensor data in a simple single-image format that captures both spatial and temporal information, we can effectively use a wide variety of existing image-to-image learning models to predict map states with high accuracy in a diverse set of sensing scenarios.

</details>


### [188] [ScenicRules: An Autonomous Driving Benchmark with Multi-Objective Specifications and Abstract Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.16073)
*Kevin Kai-Chun Chang,Ekin Beyazit,Alberto Sangiovanni-Vincentelli,Tichakorn Wongpiromsarn,Sanjit A. Seshia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ScenicRules基准，用于在随机环境下评估自动驾驶系统在优先级多目标规范下的表现，通过形式化目标、设计分层规则手册和构建场景集合，实验表明该基准与人类驾驶判断一致并能有效暴露智能体故障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶评估基准缺乏多目标优先级规则与形式化环境模型的结合，无法有效处理复杂交通环境中目标冲突和规则上下文依赖的问题。

Method: 1) 形式化一组多样化的目标作为定量评估指标；2) 设计可解释且可适配的分层规则手册框架，编码多目标及其优先级关系；3) 使用Scenic语言构建涵盖多种驾驶场景和近事故情形的形式化场景集合。

Result: 形式化目标和分层规则手册与人类驾驶判断高度一致，基准能有效暴露智能体在优先级目标下的故障。

Conclusion: ScenicRules基准通过结合形式化环境模型和优先级多目标规范，为自动驾驶系统提供了更全面、更贴近真实需求的评估方法。

Abstract: Developing autonomous driving systems for complex traffic environments requires balancing multiple objectives, such as avoiding collisions, obeying traffic rules, and making efficient progress. In many situations, these objectives cannot be satisfied simultaneously, and explicit priority relations naturally arise. Also, driving rules require context, so it is important to formally model the environment scenarios within which such rules apply. Existing benchmarks for evaluating autonomous vehicles lack such combinations of multi-objective prioritized rules and formal environment models. In this work, we introduce ScenicRules, a benchmark for evaluating autonomous driving systems in stochastic environments under prioritized multi-objective specifications. We first formalize a diverse set of objectives to serve as quantitative evaluation metrics. Next, we design a Hierarchical Rulebook framework that encodes multiple objectives and their priority relations in an interpretable and adaptable manner. We then construct a compact yet representative collection of scenarios spanning diverse driving contexts and near-accident situations, formally modeled in the Scenic language. Experimental results show that our formalized objectives and Hierarchical Rulebooks align well with human driving judgments and that our benchmark effectively exposes agent failures with respect to the prioritized objectives. Our benchmark can be accessed at https://github.com/BerkeleyLearnVerify/ScenicRules/.

</details>


### [189] [Chameleon: Control-Indexed Prospective Memory for Visuomotor Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24576)
*Xinying Guo,Chenxi Jiang,Hyun Bin Kim,Yuhang Han,Ying Sun,Yang Xiao,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Chameleon，一种约6000万参数的视觉运动策略，通过控制索引的前瞻性记忆解决机器人观察-动作延迟问题。在Camo-Dataset上将决策成功率从22.5%提升至80.8%，并在LIBERO-10、MemoryBench、MIKASA-Robo等长时记忆基准上达到同尺寸模型最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在观察信息与执行动作之间存在时间延迟（观察-动作延迟），导致最终观察不足以做出决策，需要依赖早期观察的记忆。现有的策略缺乏将记忆作为策略面向问题的系统性处理。

Method: 提出Chameleon：一种~60M参数的视觉运动策略，采用控制索引的前瞻性记忆机制。满足三个要求：可分离性（区分相似历史）、可寻址性（检索与当前决策相关的事件）、前瞻性（将回忆转化为动作就绪状态）。同时引入Camo-Dataset基准数据集，通过视觉模糊决策场景隔离观察-动作延迟。

Result: Camo-Dataset上决策/端到端成功率从22.5%/21.3%提升至80.8%/71.3%；LIBERO-10上87.1%±0.8%，MemoryBench上97.3%±4.5%，MIKASA-Robo上75.1%±1.4%，均达到同尺寸模型SOTA，并超越多个更大规模的VLA基线。

Conclusion: Chameleon通过学习可分离、可寻址和前瞻性的记忆表示，有效解决了观察-动作延迟问题。探针实验和消融研究验证了这些记忆属性是性能提升的关键驱动因素。

Abstract: Robots often observe information that determines a future action long before that action is executed. In a shell game, for example, a robot first sees which cup hides the ball, watches the cups move, and only later needs to choose the correct cup. The final observation alone is not enough for a decision: the correct action depends on an earlier event. We refer to this temporal gap as observation-action delay. It makes memory a policy-facing problem: a policy must keep similar histories distinct, retrieve the past event relevant to the current decision, and convert that recall into an action-ready state. We call these requirements separability, addressability, and prospectiveness. We introduce Chameleon, a ~60M visuomotor policy for control-indexed prospective memory. Chameleon writes embodied event memory, preserves separable histories, retrieves control-relevant traces, and trains the resulting working state to be prospective. We also introduce Camo-Dataset, a real-robot benchmark that isolates observation-action delay by making the decision scene visually ambiguous, so the correct action must be inferred from earlier observations. Chameleon improves decision/end-to-end success on Camo-Dataset from 22.5%/21.3% to 80.8%/71.3%. On public long-horizon memory benchmarks, it achieves 87.1% +/- 0.8% on LIBERO-10, 97.3% +/- 4.5% on MemoryBench, and 75.1% +/- 1.4% on MIKASA-Robo, setting the state of the art for same-size models and exceeding multiple larger VLA baselines under the reported protocols. Probes and ablations show that Chameleon learns separable, addressable, and prospective memory, and that these properties drive its performance gains.

</details>


### [190] [ViVa: A Video-Generative Value Model for Robot Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08168)
*Jindi Lv,Hao Li,Jie Li,Fankun Kong,Yang Wang,Pengfei Yi,Yifei Nie,Xiaofeng Wang,Zheng Zhu,Chaojun Ni,Qiuping Deng,Hengtao Li,Jiancheng Lv,Guan Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ViVa，一种视频生成价值模型，将预训练视频生成器改造为联合预测未来本体感受和标量价值，通过将价值估计建立在预期的具身动态上，利用时空先验实现长远期任务中的可靠价值估计，在三个任务上达到最优指标，并集成到RECAP中实现80%的平均成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于视觉语言模型的价值模型难以捕捉时间动态和物理交互，导致在长期任务中价值估计不可靠。

Method: ViVa将预训练视频生成器改造为同时预测未来本体感受（proprioception）和标量价值（scalar value），通过将价值估计锚定在预期的具身动态上，利用视频生成模型的时空先验实现价值与前瞻性预测的内在耦合。

Result: 在三个任务的基于指标的评估中达到最先进水平，集成到RECAP中平均成功率为80%。

Conclusion: 视频生成模型在价值估计方面具有显著潜力，能够产生可靠的价值信号以准确跟踪任务进度和检测执行错误。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have advanced robot manipulation through large-scale pretraining, but real-world deployment remains challenging due to partial observability and delayed feedback. Reinforcement learning addresses this via value functions, which assess task progress and guide policy improvement. However, existing value models built on vision-language models (VLMs) struggle to capture temporal dynamics and physical interactions, undermining reliable value estimation in long-horizon tasks. In this paper, we propose ViVa, a video-generative value model that repurposes a pretrained video generator to jointly predict future proprioception and a scalar value. By grounding value estimation in anticipated embodiment dynamics, ViVa leverages spatiotemporal priors to intrinsically couple value with foresight beyond static snapshots. ViVa achieves state-of-the-art results in metric-based evaluation across three tasks, producing reliable value signals that accurately track task progress and detect execution errors. Integrated into RECAP, it achieves an average success rate of 80%, highlighting the promise of video-generative models for value estimation.

</details>


### [191] [PathPainter: Transferring the Generalization Ability of Image Generation Models to Embodied Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07496)
*Yijin Wang,Yuru Tian,Xijie Huang,Weiqi Gai,Mo Zhu,Xin Zhou,Yuze Wu,Fei Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种利用BEV图像作为全局先验的导航系统，通过图像生成模型理解自然语言意图并生成可通行掩码，结合跨视图定位解决里程计漂移问题，在无人机平台上成功完成160米户外长距离导航任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BEV图像虽能为导航提供全局信息，但如何充分利用这些信息以及在执行过程中可靠地使用它们仍存在挑战。

Method: 1) 使用图像生成模型从自然语言解析人类意图、识别目标并生成可通行掩码；2) 引入跨视图定位技术，将机器人里程计与BEV地图对齐，减轻常规里程计的长时漂移。

Result: 仅使用常规局部运动规划器，无人机成功完成160米户外长距离导航任务。

Conclusion: 证明了基础模型的世界理解能力可迁移至具身导航任务，使机器人能够受益于现有图像生成模型的强泛化能力。

Abstract: Bird's-eye-view (BEV) images have been widely demonstrated to provide valuable prior information for navigation. Given the global information provided by such views, two key challenges remain: how to fully exploit this information and how to reliably use it during execution. In this paper, we propose a navigation system that uses BEV images as global priors and is designed for ground and near-ground robotic platforms. The system employs an image generation model to interpret human intent from natural language, identify the target destination, and generate traversability masks. During execution, we introduce cross-view localization to align the robot's odometry with the BEV map and mitigate long-term drift in conventional odometry. We conduct extensive benchmark experiments to evaluate the proposed method and further validate it on a UAV platform. Using only a conventional local motion planner, the UAV successfully completes a 160-meter outdoor long-range navigation task. This work demonstrates how the world-understanding capabilities of foundation models can be transferred to embodied navigation, enabling robots to benefit from the strong generalization ability of existing image generation models.

</details>


### [192] [Latent Geometry Beyond Search: Amortizing Planning in World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08732)
*Hoang Nguyen,Xiaohao Xu,Xiaonan Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出在正则化后的潜空间中，用轻量级目标条件逆动力学模型（GC-IDM）替代在线搜索规划，在多个基准任务上匹配或超越传统规划方法，同时将每步决策成本降低100-130倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在视觉世界模型中，什么样的学习表征能不仅预测未来，还能简化控制——即是否可以将在线搜索规划的负担转移到学习推理上。

Method: 利用预训练的LeWorldModel的平滑均匀潜空间几何，提出Goal-Conditioned Inverse Dynamics Model (GC-IDM)，将当前潜状态、目标潜状态和剩余时间步直接映射到下一步动作，从而将规划过程摊销为逆动力学推理。

Result: 在四个基准环境的八种设置中，GC-IDM在七种设置下匹配或超过CEM，每步决策成本降低100-130倍；在与CEM、MPPI、iCEM和基于梯度方法的全面对比中，该结果不依赖于特定优化器。

Conclusion: 在线规划所恢复的结构很大程度已编码在潜表征中；充分结构化的潜空间可以将部分规划负担从在线优化转移到学习推理，为更高效的世界模型控制提供了新思路。

Abstract: Modern vision-based world models can represent observations as compact yet expressive latent manifolds, but fast goal-oriented planning in these spaces remains challenging. This raises a central question: when does a learned representation simplify control, rather than merely enabling prediction? We study this question in a pretrained LeWorldModel, whose latent geometry is regularized for smoothness and uniformity. Our key insight is that, under such geometry, planning can be amortized into a latent inverse-dynamics mapping instead of requiring online search. We therefore replace iterative planning with a lightweight Goal-Conditioned Inverse Dynamics Model (GC-IDM) that maps the current latent state, goal latent state, and remaining horizon directly to the next action. Empirically, across four benchmark environments spanning navigation, contact-rich manipulation, and continuous control, our controller matches or exceeds CEM in seven of eight environment-protocol settings while reducing per-decision cost by 100-130x. A broader sweep over test-time planners (CEM, MPPI, iCEM, and gradient-based methods) shows that this result is not specific to a particular optimizer. These findings suggest that much of the structure recovered by test-time planning is already locally encoded in the latent representation. More broadly, our results indicate that sufficiently structured latent spaces can shift part of the planning burden from online optimization to learned inference. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/hdnndh/Latent-Geometry-Beyond-Search-Amortizing-Planning-in-World-Models .

</details>


### [193] [Trust, Geometry, and Rules: A Credibility-Aware Reinforcement Learning Framework for Safe USV Navigation under Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26974)
*Yuhang Zhang,Shuqi Chai,Yukang Zhang,Liusha Yang,Mingchuan Zhang,Wei Wang,Qingjiang Shi,Quanbo Ge*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种融合置信度感知学习、几何安全屏蔽和连续规则感知嵌入的框架，用于解决感知系统不确定性下USV的自主导航与COLREGs合规问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的方法在感知系统存在未校准不确定性时，状态估计误差导致不可靠的置信状态误导价值函数，且离散交通规则导致学习目标不连续，难以实现安全合规的自主导航。

Method: 1) 置信度加权价值学习(CW-VL)：利用滤波估计协方差与经验误差统计的差异构建动态信任因子，调制评论家的异方差损失；2) 协方差膨胀速度障碍(CI-VO)：将位置估计不确定性映射为角度裕度，形成保守几何屏蔽；3) 风险感知COLREGs职责嵌入：将二元相遇职责松弛为连续规则感知信号，抑制稀疏规则奖励导致的振荡。

Result: 仿真相遇实验表明，该方法在感知不一致性下具有更好的训练鲁棒性，在避碰和COLREGs合规性方面优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 提出的框架有效提升了USV在感知不确定性条件下的自主导航安全性和规则合规性。

Abstract: Autonomous navigation of Unmanned Surface Vehicles (USVs) that is safe and compliant with the International Regulations for Preventing Collisions at Sea (COLREGs) remains a formidable challenge in dynamic maritime environments, particularly when perception systems exhibit miscalibrated uncertainty. Existing Reinforcement Learning (RL)-based methods often falter because state-estimation errors induce unreliable belief states that mislead the value function, while discrete traffic rules introduce discontinuity in the learning objective. To address these challenges, we propose a framework integrating credibility-aware learning, geometric safety shielding, and continuous rule-aware embedding. First, Credibility-Weighted Value Learning (CW-VL) introduces a dynamic trust factor derived from the discrepancy between filter-estimated covariance and empirical error statistics to modulate the critic's heteroscedastic loss, preventing policy overfitting to noisy samples. Second, the Covariance-Inflated Velocity Obstacle (CI-VO) maps position-estimation uncertainty into set-wise angular margins, forming a conservative geometric shield that overrides hazardous exploratory actions. Third, Risk-Aware COLREGs Duty Embedding relaxes binary encounter duties into continuous rule-aware signals, providing smooth sector-transition information and suppressing oscillation from sparse rule rewards. Simulated encounter studies demonstrate improved training robustness against perceptual inconsistency and superior collision avoidance and COLREGs compliance over baselines.

</details>


### [194] [Expanding Spatial and Temporal Context for Robotic Imitation Learning With Scene Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01072)
*Jianing Qian,Qinhe Peng,Emmanuel Panov,Leonor Fermoselle,Dinesh Jayaraman,Bernadette Bucher,Tarik Kelestemur*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出使用场景图作为模仿学习中的显式结构化记忆机制，通过维护动态场景图捕捉物体关系及其随时间演变，使智能体在部分可观测和长时域任务中保留历史上下文，实验证明该方法显著提升了策略性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实环境（如家庭、办公室）存在严重的部分可观测性（空间尺度大），且许多任务涉及需要长时域推理的一系列子任务，现有模仿学习方法难以应对这两类挑战。

Method: 在模仿学习中引入场景图作为显式结构化记忆：维护动态场景图以捕捉物体为中心的关系及其时间演化，使智能体在执行任务时保留相关历史上下文，从而对逐步累积的场景信息进行高效推理。

Result: 在模拟移动操作和真实桌面操作实验中，该方法显著提升了策略性能，尤其在需要长时域推理和在部分可观测性下进行鲁棒泛化的场景中表现突出。

Conclusion: 场景图作为显式结构化记忆机制能够有效解决模仿学习在部分可观测和长时域任务中的局限性，提升策略的泛化能力和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Imitation learning enables robots to learn how to execute tasks via observation. However, real-world environments like homes and offices are often severely partially observed due to their large spatial scales. In addition, many tasks involve executing a series of subtasks requiring autonomous robots to reason over extended time horizons. To address these challenges, we propose using scene graphs as an explicit and structured memory mechanism in imitation learning. By maintaining a dynamic scene graph that captures object-centric relationships and their evolution over time, our method allows the agent to retain relevant historical context during task execution to efficiently reason over incrementally accrued scene information. Our experiments on simulated mobile manipulation and real-world tabletop manipulation demonstrate that our approach substantially improves policy performance, particularly in settings that demand long-term reasoning and robust generalization under partial observability.

</details>


<div id='cs.CY'></div>

# cs.CY [[Back]](#toc)

### [195] [Learning Fair Demand Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06830)
*Adam N. Elmachtoub,Hyemi Kim,Jonathan Y. Tan*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文研究数据驱动定价中公平性约束的位置（需求估计阶段 vs 价格优化阶段）对市场结果的影响，发现对于"均等性公平"，不同策略在不同市场条件下各有优劣；对于"罗尔斯式公平"，两种策略完全等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动定价可能产生歧视性结果，引发公平性担忧；但现有研究缺乏对公平性应嵌入定价管线的哪个阶段、以及不同选择如何影响社会福利的系统性理解。

Method: 构建两阶段决策管线模型（线性需求估计→价格优化），在训练损失、价格、需求三个维度上分别引入均等性公平和罗尔斯式公平约束，对比在需求估计阶段实施公平 vs 在价格优化阶段实施公平这两种策略的社会福利结果，并补充替代需求函数和真实疫苗定价数据案例研究。

Result: 1) 均等化训练损失会导致多解和不良后果；2) 均等性公平下，当市场规模和价格相似时，估计阶段的价格公平更有利于消费者，优化阶段的需求公平更有利于消费者；3) 罗尔斯式公平下，两阶段策略完全等价；4) 案例研究验证了理论发现。

Conclusion: 公平性约束的部署位置至关重要，不同公平类型和社会条件对最优策略选择有显著影响，为公平定价实践提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Data-driven pricing is increasingly prevalent in sectors such as airlines, lending, insurance, and retail. By learning demand models from customer features and setting prices accordingly, these systems may generate discriminatory outcomes that raise fairness concerns. This leads to fundamental questions - how and where should systems incorporate fairness considerations in the pricing pipeline, and how does it ultimately affect societal outcomes? To answer these, we study a stylized model where a seller has a two-stage decision pipeline comprising linear demand model estimation followed by price optimization. The seller considers fairness notions in training loss, price, and demand, under both parity-wise and Rawlsian perspectives.
  We show that equalizing training loss across consumer groups leads to multiple solutions, which in turn can result in undesirable outcomes despite being a standard approach in fair machine learning. Focusing instead on fairness applied directly to prices or demand, we compare two strategies that enforce fairness in either the demand estimation stage or the price optimization stage. For parity-wise fairness, we characterize when each strategy yields higher social welfare under small fairness levels. We show that when market sizes and prices in the dataset are similar, imposing price fairness in the estimation stage is more beneficial to consumers, whereas imposing demand fairness in the optimization stage yields better consumer outcomes. For Rawlsian fairness, the two strategies coincide exactly. Lastly, we extend our model to alternate demand functions and conduct a case study using real-world vaccine pricing data.

</details>


### [196] [Toward a Metaphysics of Learning Analytics: Ontological Positioning of Data, Inference, and Normativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06851)
*Kensuke Takii*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文试图通过追溯学习分析（LA）自身的定义和原则，建立LA的形而上学体系，回答“LA是什么”这一本体论问题。研究识别了数据存在类型、八个本体论前提主体（包括学习者），并通过实然/应然问题阐明LA不从数据推导规范，特别揭示了“规范嵌入型LA”实践与其首要原则的本体论张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LA社区在认识论和伦理辩论上活跃，但形而上学讨论匮乏，缺乏从内部原则推导LA自身身份认同的努力。

Method: 回溯LA自身的定义和原则，从内部推导答案；识别数据的存在类型；确定八个本体论前提主体（包括学习者）；运用实然/应然问题（is/ought problem）进行分析。

Result: 建立了LA的形而上学体系；识别了数据的本体论地位；列出了八个作为本体论前提的主体；揭示了LA不从数据推导规范；指出了“规范嵌入型LA”实践与首要原则之间的本体论张力。

Conclusion: 该形而上学体系并非从外部强加于LA，而是揭示了LA自身一直隐含预设的内容；讨论了与相关领域的联系及该体系的局限性。

Abstract: The Learning Analytics (LA) community has undergone rapid development over the 15 years since the first LAK conference was held. However, while epistemological and ethical debates regarding the philosophical foundations of LA have been vigorous, metaphysical discussions have been sparse, signifying a lack of effort to derive the identity of LA from its internal principles. In this paper, we attempt to establish a metaphysics of LA by addressing the ontological question of ``What is LA?'' We do so by tracing back to LA's own definitions and principles to derive an answer from within LA itself. Specifically, we address what kind of existence the data LA operates on constitutes, identify eight agents including learners as ontological prerequisites, and clarify, via the is/ought problem, that LA does not derive norms from data. In particular, this system reveals that a class of LA practices, here termed \textit{norm-embedded LA}, conflates LA's purpose with its operations, creating an ontological tension with the first principle. We also discuss connections with related fields and the limitations of this system. The metaphysics outlined here is not imposed from outside LA, but surfaces what LA itself has always implicitly presupposed.

</details>


### [197] [AI Sovereignty: A Qualitative Model of Strategic Competition as AI Becomes an Instrument of National Power](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07245)
*Timothy Clancy,Asmeret Naugle*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文首次提出AI主权的定义与概念模型，从微观、中观、宏观三个层面分析国家在AI技术上的独立控制权，识别关键杠杆点（加速器、电力、水、数据集、熟练劳动力）及其通过直接动能行动（如伊朗用无人机攻击数据中心）和间接非动能手段（网络、太空、信息、经济胁迫、外交）发挥作用的方式，预测AI驱动的国家权力竞争将成为21世纪的核心议题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI主权日益成为国家战略竞争的核心，但缺乏明确的定义和概念模型来理解这一动态。高昂的成本和复杂的竞争格局使得国家难以有效导航AI主权的发展路径，亟需建立系统化的分析框架。

Method: 提出AI主权的定义和首个包含微观、中观、宏观贡献因素的定性模型，通过基于模型的定性预测分析竞争动态和AI驱动国家权力的演变潜力。

Result: 识别出关键杠杆点（加速器、电力、水、数据集、熟练劳动力），这些杠杆点可通过战略和操作层面的直接动能行动（如无人机攻击数据中心）和间接非动能手段（网络、太空、信息、经济胁迫、外交）被激活，用于增强自身或削弱对手。

Conclusion: 若假设和假说成立，AI主权竞争将成为21世纪国家提升经济状况、竞争优势和整体国家权力的核心驱动力。

Abstract: AI sovereignty is the extent to which a nation independently controls its artificial intelligence (AI) technologies. The race toward ever-more-sophisticated frontier AI models is of increasing strategic importance, with nations considering how AI might improve their economic situations, competitive advantage, and overall national power. However, the costs of AI sovereignty are enormous, and we lack definitions and conceptual models to navigate evolving AI sovereignty dynamics. We address this gap with definitions relevant to AI sovereignty, along with a first-of-its-kind qualitative model that incorporates micro, meso, and macro contributors. Model-based qualitative forecasts highlight competitive dynamics and evolving potential for AI-driven national power. The model identifies key leverage points that nations can use to enhance their own growth or degrade an adversary's, including consideration of accelerators, electricity, water, data sets and skilled workforce. These leverage points can be activated at strategic and operational levels through both direct kinetic actions, such as Iran's targeting of data centers with drones, and indirect non-kinetic effects including cyber, space, information, economic coercion and diplomacy. If our assumptions and hypotheses are valid, this strategic competition may come to define how nations improve their economic situations, competitive advantage, and overall national power in the 21st Century.

</details>


### [198] [Sustainability by Design in Decentralized Autonomous Organizations: An Empirical Review of Governance, Innovation, and Institutional Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05667)
*Yutian Wang,Luyao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文比较了DAO治理的ERC-8004与企业联盟治理的Google A2A两种智能体互操作标准，通过LLM驱动的比较分析管道研究其治理话语中的社会技术权力结构，为去中心化组织中的可持续创新设计提供依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统创新理论基于层级企业和封闭组织边界，无法解释去中心化数字原生组织（如DAO）中的创新过程。本研究旨在填补这一理论空白，探索区块链驱动的去中心化组织的创新治理机制。

Method: 采用LLM驱动的比较分析管道，整合自动标注、神经主题建模和多层网络分析，对DAO治理的ERC-8004标准与企业财团治理的Google A2A标准进行大规模治理话语分析。

Result: 揭示了两种治理模式（DAO vs 企业联盟）在智能体互操作标准制定过程中的社会技术权力结构和话语差异。

Conclusion: 为学者、政策制定者和设计师提供了如何将创新、技术治理与可持续性相协调的循证洞见，尤其是在未来去中心化组织形式中。

Abstract: Recent innovation theories on economics remain largely grounded in assumptions of hierarchical firms and closed organizational boundaries, offering limited insight into how innovation unfolds within decentralized, digitally native organizations. Decentralized Autonomous Organizations (DAOs) represent an emerging form of innovation ecosystem characterized by blockchain-based transparency, open participation, and token-driven governance, in which sustainability can be embedded directly into organizational design. This study compares two standards, ERC-8004 and Google A2A, who address the same agent interoperability question, while the former is governed by DAO and the latter by corporation consortium. They are examined through an LLM-powered comparative pipeline for large-scale governance discourse analysis, integrating automated annotation, neural topic modeling, and multi-layer network analysis to study socio-technical power structures. The study provides evidence-based insights for scholars, policymakers, and designers seeking to align innovation, technological governance, and sustainability in future organizational forms.

</details>


### [199] [Two-Phase Simulated Annealing for Equitable Team Formation: Eliminating Complaints in Large Engineering Cohorts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07270)
*Yiwei Sun,Xinru Deng,Dimitrios G Papageorgiou*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 提出了一种两阶段算法（图聚类+模拟退火），用于解决大规模工程学生团队组建中偏好满足与公平性的冲突。在238名学生中部署后，正式投诉降至零（基线>30%），GPA方差0.005（历史均值9.74），消除性别孤立个体，偏好满意度94.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学生分组工具要么在优化公平性时忽视偏好（如CATME、Team-Anneal），要么在满足偏好时牺牲均衡性（如自选组队），导致投诉率高达5-35%。需一种能同时兼顾偏好满足与公平性优化的分组方法。

Method: 两阶段算法：阶段1通过图论聚类形成固定三元组，最大化互选偏好以保留社会纽带；阶段2采用模拟退火将三元组配对为六人团队，同时优化GPA方差、性别平衡和规模约束。该分解方式借鉴了材料科学中的分层优化思想。

Result: 238名学生部署：正式投诉为零（基线>30%），GPA方差0.005（历史均值9.74），消除性别孤立个体，偏好满意度94.3%。82个历史分组实例（1,538个团队，6学年）验证表明显著优于传统方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的解耦偏好满足与公平性优化的两阶段方法，能够在不牺牲任一目标的前提下实现高效、可扩展的学生团队组建，并可推广至其他工程教育项目。

Abstract: Contribution: This paper presents a novel two-phase algorithmic approach that decouples preference satisfaction from fairness optimization in student team formation, achieving both objectives without compromise. The method applies simulated annealing -- a core materials science technique -- to an educational challenge, demonstrating pedagogical integration of administrative processes. Background: Forming effective teams in large engineering cohorts (100+ students) requires balancing student preferences, academic fairness, and demographic diversity. Existing tools either optimize for fairness while ignoring preferences (CATME, Team-Anneal) or accommodate preferences while compromising balance (self-selection), leaving complaint rates at 5--35%. Intended Outcomes: Eliminate formal complaints, achieve near-zero GPA variance between teams, prevent gender isolation, and maintain high preference satisfaction while creating a scalable, reproducible solution applicable across engineering programs. Application Design: Phase 1 forms fixed triads through graph-theoretic clustering that maximizes mutual preferences, preserving social bonds. Phase 2 employs simulated annealing to pair triads into teams of six while optimizing GPA variance, gender balance, and size constraints. This decomposition mirrors hierarchical optimization in materials processing. Findings: Deployed across 238 students, the algorithm eliminated formal complaints entirely (vs >30% baseline), achieved GPA variance of 0.005 (vs. historical mean 9.74), eliminated gender-isolated individuals, and maintained 94.3% preference satisfaction. Validation against 82 historical grouping instances (1,538 teams, 6 academic years) confirmed significant improvement over conventional methods.

</details>


### [200] [Measuring Agents in Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.04123)
*Melissa Z. Pan,Negar Arabzadeh,Riccardo Cogo,Yuxuan Zhu,Alexander Xiong,Lakshya A Agrawal,Huanzhi Mao,Emma Shen,Sid Pallerla,Liana Patel,Shu Liu,Tianneng Shi,Xiaoyuan Liu,Jared Quincy Davis,Emmanuele Lacavalla,Alessandro Basile,Shuyi Yang,Paul Castro,Daniel Kang,Koushik Sen,Dawn Song,Joseph E. Gonzalez,Ion Stoica,Matei Zaharia,Marquita Ellis*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 基于对86个已部署系统和20个深度案例访谈的首个系统性研究，发现生产环境中的LLM智能体倾向于使用简单、可控的方法：68%在10步内进行人工干预，70%依赖提示工程而非权重微调，74%主要依靠人工评估。可靠性是最大的开发挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体已在多个行业的生产环境中部署，但缺乏对其成功部署技术方法的系统性理解。

Method: 通过20个深度访谈案例研究和86个已部署系统从业者的调查，对26个领域的生产智能体进行首次系统性研究。

Result: 68%的生产智能体执行不超过10步即需人工干预；70%依赖现成模型的提示工程而非权重微调；74%主要依赖人工评估；可靠性是最主要的开发挑战。

Conclusion: 生产智能体当前采用简单可控的方法构建，可靠性仍是核心挑战，为研究社区提供了部署现实和未被充分探索的研究方向的可见性。

Abstract: LLM-based agents already operate in production across many industries, yet we lack an understanding of what technical methods make deployments successful. We present the first systematic study of Measuring Agents in Production, MAP, using first-hand data from agent developers. We conducted 20 case studies via in-depth interviews and surveyed 86 deployed systems practitioners across 26 domains. We investigate why organizations build agents, how they build them, how they evaluate them, and their top development challenges. Our study finds that production agents are built using simple, controllable approaches: 68% execute at most 10 steps before human intervention, 70% rely on prompting off-the-shelf models instead of weight tuning, and 74% depend primarily on human evaluation. Reliability (consistent correct behavior over time) remains the top development challenge, which practitioners currently address through systems-level design. MAP documents the current state of production agents, providing the research community with visibility into deployment realities and underexplored research avenues.

</details>


### [201] [Training for Technology: Adoption and Productive Use of Generative AI in Legal Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04982)
*Benjamin M. Chen,Hong Bao*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 通过对164名法律学生的随机实验发现，未经培训的LLM访问会降低任务表现（答案更短、错误更多、分数略低），而简短培训则显著提升表现（采纳率41% vs 26%，分数提高0.27个等级点，规则准确性提高）。主分层分析表明培训主要通过提高采纳率而非使用效果发挥作用。挑战了GenAI主要惠及低技能工作者的观点，强调需要同时投资于访问权限和培训指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究定向用户培训能否释放生成式人工智能在专业环境中的生产力潜力，以及未经培训的LLM访问是否会产生反效果。

Method: 采用随机实验设计，将164名法律学生分为三组：无GenAI访问组、可选LLM访问组（未经培训）、可选LLM访问加简短培训组。通过问题发现考试评估表现，并使用主分层分析（principal stratification analysis）考察培训的作用机制。

Result: 未经培训的LLM访问导致答案更短、案例错误更多、分数略低（多数差异未达统计显著）。培训后的参与者采纳率更高（41% vs 26%，p=0.044），分数提高0.27个等级点（p=0.027），规则准确性更高（p=0.014）。主分层分析显示培训主要通过提高采纳率而非改善使用效果发挥作用（采纳下限1.06 > 效果上限0.42）。

Conclusion: 培训是释放GenAI生产力的关键因素，仅提供访问权限可能适得其反。研究还挑战了GenAI主要惠及低技能工作者的普遍观点——未经培训时，高能力从业者选择不采用，而低能力用户采用但效果不佳。实现GenAI的生产力提升需要同时投资于访问权限和培训指导。

Abstract: Can targeted user training unlock the productive potential of generative artificial intelligence in professional settings? We study this question using a randomized experiment in which 164 law students completed an issue-spotting examination under one of three conditions: no GenAI access, optional access to a large language model (LLM), or LLM access with a brief training intervention.
  Untrained LLM access proved counterproductive: relative to participants without any LLM access, untrained users wrote significantly shorter answers, committed more case misstatements, and scored marginally lower, though most differences fall short of conventional significance. Training reversed this pattern. Trained participants adopted the LLM at higher rates (41% vs. 26%; p = 0.044), scored 0.27 grade points higher than untrained users--roughly one fine grade--(p = 0.027), and stated applicable rules more accurately (p = 0.014).
  Principal stratification analysis suggests training operates primarily through adoption rather than effectiveness--the adoption lower bound (1.06) exceeds the effectiveness upper bound (0.42) at strict mean dominance--though confidence intervals are wide.
  More broadly, these findings challenge the view that GenAI primarily benefits lower-skilled workers: without training, higher-ability practitioners opt out while lower-ability users adopt but unproductively. Realizing GenAI's productivity gains requires investment in both access and instruction.

</details>


### [202] [Benchmarking LLMs for Community Governance Simulation with Life-history Narratives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23783)
*Xu Chen,Yuanzi Li,Lei Wang,Nan Lu,Yang Wang,Anding Wang,Lei Shi,Xiaoxing Fu,Ji-Rong Wen*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个涵盖数据集、基准、算法和系统的综合研究框架，用于基于LLM的个体级居民模拟。通过对92位居民进行两小时半结构化访谈构建约120万字的生平叙事数据集，测试18个主流LLM发现加入详细生活经历能提高模拟保真度但增加成本；提出的curriculum-LoRA参数高效个性化框架以约10倍更低的调用成本匹配最强基线的保真度，并集成为闭环政策评估管道，使资源受限的地方政府能够进行居民个体级模拟以预评估社区治理决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM模拟研究仅使用少数人口统计变量生成通用角色类型，不足以支持依赖具体居民观点的社区治理决策。需要弥合这一差距，实现个体级居民模拟。

Method: 构建包含约120万字生平叙事的居民数据集（对92位居民进行两小时半结构化访谈，覆盖9个社区治理领域）；设计基准测试18个主流LLM在四种提示策略下的表现；提出curriculum-LoRA参数高效个性化框架；将算法集成到闭环政策评估管道系统中。

Result: 加入详细生平叙事可提高模拟保真度但增加输入令牌成本；curriculum-LoRA以约10倍更低的每次调用成本匹配最强基线的保真度，在Pareto效率上优于所有测试配置。

Conclusion: 该框架使资源有限的基层行政机构能够实现个体级LLM居民模拟，在现实部署前系统地进行政策预评估，为社区治理决策提供可扩展、可复现的低成本模拟方案。

Abstract: Effective community governance hinges on understanding what specific residents think and need. Recent work has used large language models (LLMs) to simulate human respondents, offering a scalable, reproducible way to study human attitudes and behaviors at low cost. However, these studies typically prompt the model with just a few demographic variables (age, gender, income), simulating only general role types. This is insufficient for community governance, where decisions depend on the views of specific residents. We bridge this gap with an integrated research framework covering dataset, benchmark, algorithm, and system. The dataset comprises approximately 1.2 million characters of first-person narrative collected through two-hour semi-structured interviews with each of 92 residents in an urban community, organized around nine community-governance domains. The benchmark probes 18 mainstream LLMs across four prompting strategies and shows that adding rich life-history profiles meaningfully raises fidelity above the no-profile baseline, but this gain comes with more input tokens per call from the longer prompts they require. The algorithm, curriculum-LoRA, is a parameter-efficient personalization framework that, by closing this fidelity-cost gap, matches the strongest baseline's fidelity at roughly 10x lower per-call cost and Pareto-dominates every configuration tested. The system integrates curriculum-LoRA into a closed-loop policy-evaluation pipeline. Together, these results bring individual-level LLM-based resident simulation within reach of resource-constrained local administrations, enabling community-governance decisions to be systematically pre-evaluated in silico before real-world deployment.

</details>


### [203] [Beyond Tool Adoption: A Practical Five-Stage Developmental Continuum for AI Literacy in Higher Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00038)
*J. Paul Liu,Rachel Levy*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个五阶段AI素养连续体（0-未参与、1-非批判性使用、2-知情使用、3-批判性评估、4-改进），用于描述高等教育中学生对AI的使用发展路径，并基于北卡罗来纳州立大学的实践案例（330+参与者）提供了观察性证据，表明该框架能帮助学习者从回避或盲目依赖转向负责任、批判性的AI参与。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI素养框架提供了能力定义，但缺乏对学习者起点和发展路径的诊断指导；学生对AI工具的使用呈现两极分化（因恐惧/不信任而回避，或因缺乏批判性而盲目依赖），亟需一个可操作的发展性框架来指导教学实践。

Method: 提出五阶段AI素养连续体理论框架，与UNESCO和OECD等国际框架对齐；在北卡罗来纳州立大学开展设计型实施案例，涵盖学分课程和密集工作坊，涉及330+参与者（2024秋-2026春），采用观察性和实践导向的分析方式（未使用经过验证的前后测工具或对照组）。

Result: 参与者表现出从非参与或非批判性使用向知情参与的行为转变；持续且嵌入学科的经验能产生更强的批判性评估和改进导向实践的证据；但研究仅提供观察性和实践层面的发现。

Conclusion: AI素养应被理解为一种发展性能力——理解、评估和负责任地应用AI系统，而非仅工具采纳；该连续体为教育者提供了实用的诊断和教学路径；未来需进一步开展验证性研究，包括课程路径、机会考量、评估策略等方面的深入探讨。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) literacy is increasingly recognized as a foundational competency for all university graduates. Yet students' engagement with AI tools often clusters at two extremes: avoidance driven by fear, mistrust, ethical concern, or lack of access, and uncritical reliance that produces fluent output while masking misunderstanding. Existing AI literacy frameworks provide valuable competency definitions, but most offer limited guidance for diagnosing where learners begin and how they progress toward responsible, critical engagement. This paper proposes a five-stage AI Literacy Continuum: 0) Not Yet Engaged, 1) Uncritical Use, 2) Informed Use, 3) Critical Evaluation, and 4) Improvement --that describes developmental orientations toward AI use in higher education. The continuum complements dimensional frameworks by providing educators with a practical diagnostic and instructional pathway aligned with international frameworks, including UNESCO and OECD. We present a design-based implementation case from North Carolina State University, where credit-bearing courses and intensive hands-on workshops engaged more than 330 participants between Fall 2024 and Spring 2026. Because the implementation did not use a validated pre/post instrument or comparison group, we frame the findings as observational and practice-based: participants exhibited behaviors consistent with movement from non-engagement or uncritical use toward informed engagement, while sustained and discipline-embedded experiences produced stronger evidence of critical evaluation and improvement-oriented practice. We discuss curricular pathways, opportunity considerations, assessment strategies, and argue that AI literacy should be understood not as tool adoption alone but as a developmental capacity to understand, evaluate, and responsibly apply AI systems in disciplinary and societal contexts.

</details>


### [204] [Toward Agentic Governance: What Shapes LLM-Agent Intervention in Public Forums?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00603)
*Luyang Zhang,Yi-Yun Chu,Ramayya Krishnan*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: LLM智能体在论坛审核中的行为存在结构性变异：四种部署选择（模型版本、权重开放状态、服务提供商、系统提示策略）会显著改变代理的响应模式，且权重开放状态比访问界面更能预测代理对可见挑战的拒绝倾向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理越来越多地被用于公共论坛的审核工作流，但其行为在不同调用间存在不一致性，导致基于代理行为的防御措施无法可靠复现。作者希望揭示这种变异的结构性来源，并评估其对论坛治理的影响。

Method: 系统性地研究了四种部署选择（模型版本、权重开放状态、服务提供商、系统提示策略）对LLM代理响应率的影响，并在开放权重和封闭权重家族的多个LLM上进行对比实验，分析代理对可见与隐藏挑战的拒绝倾向差异。

Result: 四种部署选择的组合可在相同的论坛帖子上产生显著不同的干预结果。封闭权重模型在所有情况下对可见挑战的拒绝率更高，而开放权重模型则要么反转这一模式，要么没有显著差异。权重开放状态比访问界面（可见vs隐藏）更能解释代理的拒绝行为差异。

Conclusion: 可审计的论坛代理治理需要关注所有四种部署选择，而不仅仅是模型名称，因为每种选择都会独立地改变代理行为。权重开放状态是影响代理行为一致性和可复现性的关键因素。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly used in moderation-relevant public forum workflows, where their choices to answer, acknowledge, repair, or decline are routinely challenged by users, platforms, and regulators. The same agent often returns different responses on identical content, so any defense based on the agent's behavior cannot be reliably reproduced. The variation is structural. Four deployment choices typically invisible to the operator each shift the agent's response rate, and their combinations can produce substantially different interventions on the same forum posts. The four choices are (1) which model version is currently served, which can change between calls without notice; (2) the model's weight-release status (open-weight, with weights publicly downloadable, vs. closed-weight, with weights held by the provider); (3) which provider serves the request; and (4) which system-prompt policy is in force. Across LLMs spanning both open-weight and closed-weight families, we find that the previously reported tendency to decline more on visible than hidden challenges aligns with the open/closed weight boundary in our panel more than with access surface. Every closed-weight cell declines more on visible challenges; every open-weight cell reverses this or shows no gap. Auditable forum-agent governance requires awareness of all four choices, not just the model name, since each independently shifts behavior.

</details>


### [205] [Warning Message Content Increases Help Seeking in a Large-Scale Dark Web CSAM Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06417)
*Caoilte Ó Ciardha,Joel Scanlan,Tegan Insoll,Juha Nurmi,Nina Vaaranen-Valkonen*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文通过在Tor搜索引擎Ahmia.fi上开展为期140天、涉及近2000万次搜索的实地实验，发现警告信息的内容（特别是以伤害为主题的框架）能显著提高CSAM搜索者点击帮助资源的比率，点击率从干预前的8.73%提升至活动期间的15.67%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 警告信息已被用于阻止个人搜索儿童性虐待材料（CSAM）并促进其寻求帮助，但在高匿名性环境中，关于信息内容效果的大规模现场证据仍然有限。

Method: 在Tor搜索引擎Ahmia.fi上进行实地实验，观察近2000万次搜索（其中超过300万次触发警告），通过随机对照比较、全活动分析和中断时间序列模型，检验不同主题内容和框架的警告信息对用户点击帮助资源行为的影响。

Result: 所有活跃警告信息相较于中性信息均提高了帮助资源的点击率，其中以伤害为主题的信息效果最强；平台层面点击率从干预前的8.73%显著提升至活动期间的15.67%。

Conclusion: 警告信息的内容在塑造用户对干预措施的反应中起关键作用，支持通过优化和调整信息内容来增加对帮助资源参与度的策略。

Abstract: Warning messages have been used to disrupt individuals seeking online child sexual abuse material (CSAM) and promote engagement with support services, yet large-scale field evidence on message content remains limited, particularly in high anonymity environments. This study reports a field experiment on Ahmia.fi, a Tor search engine, examining how warning message content influences behavior. Across a 140-day period, almost 20 million searches were observed, with over 3 million searches containing known CSAM-related terms that triggered a warning linking to an anonymous self-help program. Users were exposed to warning messages varying in thematic content and framing, or a neutral message. Across a randomized comparison, a campaign-wide analysis, and interrupted time series models, message content consistently influenced engagement with help resources. All active messages increased click-through rates to help resources relative to the neutral condition, with a harm-focused message producing the strongest effects. At the platform level, click-through rates increased from 8.73% before the intervention to 15.67% during the campaign. These findings highlight the importance of message content in shaping responses to warning interventions, supporting an approach in which messaging is refined and adapted to increase engagement with support resources.

</details>


<div id='cs.SI'></div>

# cs.SI [[Back]](#toc)

### [206] [Popularity Feedback Constrains Innovation in Cultural Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.09997)
*Lucas Gautheron,Raja Marjieh,Dalton C. Conley,Seth Frey,Hannah Rubin,Mike D. Schneider,Ofer Tchernichovski,Nori Jacoby*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 通过大规模在线实验（N=1008），研究了流行度反馈如何影响集体创造力，发现暴露流行度信息会降低文化多样性、减缓创新速度，并改变选择与创作两个阶段的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的创意过程（从艺术到科学）依赖社会反馈循环，但流行度反馈对集体创造力的影响机制尚不明确。

Method: 大规模在线实验（N=1008），参与者从演化市场中迭代选择图像并生成自己的修改，比较有/无流行度反馈条件下的文化动态。

Result: 流行度反馈降低了文化多样性、延迟了审美改进；选择阶段引发累积优势（偏好流行图像），创作阶段产生较少颠覆性变化且更倾向扩展已有视觉模式。

Conclusion: 文化市场的反馈循环不仅影响选择，还直接或间接地塑造文化创新的形式和方向。

Abstract: Real-world creative processes ranging from art to science rely on social feedback-loops between selection and creation. Yet, the effects of popularity feedback on collective creativity remain poorly understood. We investigate how popularity ratings influence cultural dynamics in a large-scale online experiment where participants ($N = 1\,008$) iteratively \textit{select} images from evolving markets and \textit{produce} their own modifications. Results show that exposing the popularity of images reduces cultural diversity and slows innovation, delaying aesthetic improvements. Popularity feedback is associated with changes to both selection and creative stages. During selection, popularity information triggers cumulative advantage, with participants preferentially building upon popular images, reducing diversity. During creation, participants make less disruptive changes, and are more likely to expand existing visual patterns. Feedback loops in cultural markets thus not only shape selection, but also, directly or indirectly, the form and direction of cultural innovation.

</details>


### [207] [Comparing Sentiment Contagion in AI-Agent and Human Social Networks: Evidence from MOLTBOOK](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06665)
*Elyes Ben chaabane,Savindu Herath,Yash Raj Shrestha*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 研究了一个由语言模型AI代理组成的社交网络MOLTBOOK，分析了290万条帖子和150万条评论。发现负面帖子获得更多回复（吸引注意力），但回复通常转为中性而非保持负面，且负面情绪跨天传播的证据微弱。主要模式是"负面关注+中性化"，而非负面情绪循环。这表明AI社交网络可能抑制情绪极端化，与人类社交网络不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究AI代理在纯AI社交网络中情绪传播的模式：负向情绪是否会扩散蔓延，还是会被回复所平抑。这一问题对于理解自主AI系统在社交互动中的行为模式具有重要意义。

Method: 分析MOLTBOOK——一个由自主语言模型代理组成的社交网络，收集了约290万条帖子和150万条评论数据，对帖子的情感倾向（正面、中性、负面）与回复数量、回复情感倾向以及跨天情感传播情况进行统计分析。

Result: 1）负面帖子获得比中性或正面帖子更多的回复；2）对负面内容的回复通常不会保持负面，而是大多转为中性；3）负面情绪跨天传播的证据很微弱。

Conclusion: AI代理网络的主要模式是"负面关注后中性化"而非"负面情绪循环"。AI代理网络可能抑制情绪极端化，与人类社交网络表现出不同的行为模式，但其行为仍然强烈依赖于交互的组织方式。

Abstract: AI agents are beginning to interact not only with people, but also with one another. We investigate what happens to sentiment in such an AI-only social network: does negativity spread, or do replies calm it down? We study MOLTBOOK, a social network made up of autonomous language-model agents, using almost 2.9 million posts and 1.5 million comments. Negative posts receive many more replies than neutral or positive posts, so negativity still attracts attention. However, replies to negative content usually do not stay negative. They most often become neutral, and there is meager evidence that negative sentiment spreads across days. The main pattern is therefore not a cycle of negativity, but negative attention followed by neutralisation. These findings suggest that AI-agent networks may behave differently from human social networks: they may dampen emotional extremes, while still depending strongly on how interactions are organised.

</details>


### [208] [Technological Fitness and Regional Growth in Japan](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07202)
*Rintaro Karashima,Hiroyasu Inoue*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本研究利用约390万件企业专利数据，通过Fitness-Complexity算法构建日本47个都道府县的技术能力网络，发现区域技术成熟度（Fitness）与后续经济增长呈正相关（β=0.0029, p=0.007），且该效应在初始收入较低的地区更为显著。面板固定效应模型揭示了跨时期正负交替的横截面相关性，证实了面板方法的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究区域技术能力的 sophistication（成熟度）与经济增长之间的关系，弥补既有研究多关注专利数量而忽视技术能力结构复杂性的不足。

Method: 使用1981-2015年约390万件企业专利数据，构建47个都道府县与35个技术类别之间的二部网络，运用Fitness-Complexity算法计算七个五年期的区域Fitness得分，采用包含Driscoll-Kraay标准误的固定效应面板模型进行估计。

Result: 区域Fitness与后续五年实际人均GRP年均增长率呈显著正相关（β=0.0029, p=0.007），该效应仅在同时包含个体和时间固定效应时被检测到；横截面相关性在不同时期符号发生变化；Fitness的增长效应在初始收入较低的地区更强；滞后/领先分析支持Fitness→增长的因果方向。

Conclusion: 技术能力的结构复杂性（而非单纯专利数量）是区域经济增长的重要预测因子，且其贡献在欠发达地区更为突出，面板方法对于识别这种动态关系至关重要。

Abstract: Technological knowledge plays an important role in shaping regional economic performance. This study examines the relationship between the sophistication of regional technological capabilities and economic growth across Japanese prefectures. Using approximately 3.9 million corporate patent records filed from fiscal years 1981 to 2015, we construct bipartite networks linking 47 prefectures to 35 technology classes and apply the Fitness-Complexity algorithm to derive regional Fitness scores for seven five-year periods. We estimate fixed-effects panel models with Driscoll-Kraay standard errors, using the annual average growth rate of real gross regional product per capita over the subsequent five years as the dependent variable. Prefectural Fitness is positively associated with subsequent growth ($\hatβ = 0.0029$, $p = 0.007$) after controlling for initial income, population density, and patenting activity, but this relationship is detectable only when both entity and time fixed effects are included. Cross-sectional correlations between Fitness and subsequent growth change sign across periods, underscoring the importance of the panel approach. The growth effect of Fitness is stronger in prefectures with lower initial income, suggesting that technological sophistication contributes more to growth where there is greater scope for economic expansion. Lag and lead analyses indicate that the relationship runs from Fitness to subsequent growth rather than the reverse.

</details>


### [209] [Information Networks of Stock Prices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07450)
*Muhammad Aldy Hassan,Hokky Situngkir*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文通过对比皮尔逊相关与互信息（MI）在印尼资本市场2015-2025年间2328个滚动窗口中的表现，检验了24种方法配置（3种依赖估计器×2种图过滤方案×4种社区解码器）。结果表明：皮尔逊+MST+Infomap组合在恢复传统行业分类方面最为稳健，而MI自适应分箱在残差信息检测中更为准确，MI与PMFG的协同能揭示超越正式行业边界的隐藏经济子结构（如商品体制的凝聚力），但并未动摇线性相关的主导地位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法仅通过线性视角简化股票价格集体运动中的复杂相互依赖关系，本文旨在探索计算结构网络表示在印尼资本市场中的应用，测试皮尔逊相关与互信息在揭示市场谱系动力学方面的极限。

Method: 在2015-2025年间2328个滚动观察窗口中，检验24种方法配置，涵盖3种依赖估计器（皮尔逊相关、MI自适应分箱、MI-kNN）、2种图过滤方案（最小生成树MST和平面最大过滤图PMFG）以及4种社区解码器。

Result: 皮尔逊+MST+Infomap配置在恢复传统行业分类方面最为稳健；PMFG在揭示局部结构和异质社区方面具有优势；MI自适应分箱在残差信息检测中优于kNN；MI与PMFG的协同能揭示超越正式行业边界的隐藏经济子结构。

Conclusion: MI与PMFG的协同并非要取代线性相关的主导地位，而是为挖掘隐藏的经济子结构提供重要的分析视角，这些子结构长期超越了市场正式部门的刚性边界。

Abstract: The collective movement of stock prices harbors complex interdependencies that are conventionally simplified only through a linear lens. This paper explores computed structural network representations in the Indonesian capital market by testing the limits of Pearson correlation and Mutual Information (MI) in unveiling the spectral dynamics of the market. Across 2,328 rolling observation windows from 2015 to 2025, we examine 24 methodological configurations that combine three dependency estimators (Pearson, MI adaptive binning, and MI-kNN), two graph filtering schemes (Minimum Spanning Tree/MST and Planar Maximally Filtered Graph/PMFG), and four community decoders.
  The empirical results unveil a fundamental reality: topological richness does not always resonate with sectoral classification precision. The Pearson, MST, and Infomap configuration is shown to remain the most robust foundation for recovering conventional sectoral taxonomy. Nevertheless, when deeper observation demands the exposition of local structures and the weave of heterogeneous communities, the architectural relaxation through PMFG demonstrates its superiority. In the realm of residual information detection, MI adaptive binning appears far more proportional than kNN; histogram-based regularization successfully tames empirical noise without sweeping away traces of non-linear dependency. Ultimately, the synergy of MI and PMFG is not positioned to dethrone the dominance of linear correlation, but rather to provide an essential analytical lens for excavating hidden economic sub-structures -- such as the cohesion of commodity regimes -- that have long transcended the rigid boundaries of the market's formal sectors.

</details>


### [210] [What Causes COVID-19 Fear? General Drivers of Fear During a Health Crisis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.20146)
*Daniele Baccega,Paolo Castagno,Antonio Fernández Anta,Juan Marcos Ramirez,Matteo Sereno*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本研究利用德尔菲美国CTIS调查数据，通过概率因果模型分析发现，在新冠疫情期间，信息源接触比人口统计学和流行病学变量更能解释公众对感染恐惧的方差。制度性和专家驱动的信息源增加恐惧，而政治、宗教和替代信息渠道则降低恐惧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究新冠疫情期间影响公众自我报告感染恐惧的决定因素，特别是不同信息源接触如何调节公众情绪反应，以理解信息流行病（infodemic）对心理健康的影响机制。

Method: 采用概率因果模型（Probabilistic Causal Model）估计因果关系的强度，并计算平均处理效应（Average Treatment Effect）以量化不同信息源对恐惧的因果影响，同时进行因果调整以消除混杂偏倚。

Result: 信息源接触对恐惧方差的解释比例高于人口统计学和流行病学变量；经因果调整后，机构性和专家驱动信息源显著增加恐惧，而政治家、宗教领袖及替代信息渠道则与恐惧降低相关。

Conclusion: 信息生态系统在公共卫生危机中扮演塑造公众情绪反应的核心角色，因果推断方法对于研究疫情中的行为反应具有重要价值。

Abstract: The COVID-19 pandemic triggered not only a global health crisis but also an infodemic, where exposure to heterogeneous information sources influenced public emotional responses. In this work, we investigate the determinants of self-reported fear of infection using data from the Delphi US CTIS survey. In particular, we analyze how demographic variables, epidemiological conditions, and exposure to different information sources shape fear levels. We introduce a Probabilistic Causal Model to estimate causal relationship strengths, identifying the variables that most strongly influence fear. Our results indicate that exposure to information sources accounts for a greater proportion of the variance in fear than demographic and epidemiological variables do. We further compute the Average Treatment Effect to quantify the impact of different information sources on fear. After causal adjustment, institutional and expert-driven sources are associated with increased fear levels, whereas politicians, religious leaders, and alternative information channels are associated with reduced fear. These findings highlight both the central role of the information ecosystem in shaping emotional responses during public health crises and the value of causal inference approaches for studying behavioral responses to pandemics.

</details>


### [211] [Two-Path Operators, Triadic Decompositions, and Majorized Quotients for Ego-Centered Network Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10258)
*Moses Boudourides*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文基于双路径（楔形结构）形式化，发展了一种算子视角，将楔形关联矩阵分解为边支撑（三元闭包）部分和非边支撑（开放）部分，研究了压缩支配性自我网络及遍历节点的商/收缩构造，证明了收缩下的双游走传递定理及楔形公平划分的等式刻画，并在十个基准图上进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双路径是聚类、三元闭包、冗余性和中介性背后的基本组合对象。本文旨在建立一种形式化框架，将Burt的结构洞理论与以节点为中心的自我网络联系起来，为理解和压缩社交网络中的结构洞提供数学基础。

Method: 本文提出算子视角，通过楔形关联诱导出规范的双游走矩阵，并将其唯一分解为边支撑（三元）部分和非边支撑（开放）部分。进一步研究商/收缩构造以压缩支配性自我网络集合及遍历节点，并证明收缩下的双游走传递定理及其不等式和等式刻画条件。

Result: 证明了收缩下的双游走传递定理，建立了具有显式非负误差项的不等式，以及基于楔形公平划分的等式刻画条件。在十个基准图上通过表格驱动诊断验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 本文建立的双路径算子框架和收缩理论为理解网络中的结构洞、三元闭包和中介性提供了统一的数学工具，压缩构造为大规模网络分析提供了可行的简化方法。

Abstract: Two-paths (wedges) are the elementary combinatorial objects behind clustering, triadic closure, redundancy, and brokerage. Motivated by a two-path formalism that links Burt's structural holes to node-centered ego networks, we develop an operator viewpoint in which wedge incidence induces a canonical ``two-walk'' matrix and a unique decomposition into an edge--supported (triadic) part and a nonedge-supported (open) part. We then study quotient/contraction constructions designed to compress collections of dominating ego networks together with selected ``traversing'' nodes, and we prove a two--walk transfer theorem under contraction, establishing an inequality with an explicit nonnegative error term and an equality characterization in terms of a wedge--equitable partition. Finally, we illustrate the theory on ten benchmark graphs and their ego-traversing contractions using table-driven diagnostics.

</details>


### [212] [Missing Links in Public Email and Covert Networks: A Comparative Evaluation of Link Prediction, Hyperlink Prediction, and ERGM Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22606)
*Moses Boudourides*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了链路预测(LP)、超链路预测(HP)和ERGM估计在部分观测网络中的缺失链接推断性能，发现LP在二元关系恢复上表现强劲，而CHESHIRE方法在推断高阶群组结构时更具优势，ERGM则提供了可解释的依赖关系补充。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏对二元链接预测、超链接预测和ERGM估计在部分观测网络缺失推断中的系统性比较，需要明确不同方法适用的场景和条件。

Method: 采用统一的掩码协议，移除由被遮蔽超链接诱发的二元证据，确保可比性。LP使用经典启发式方法作为基线，HP通过提升二元分数和CHESHIRE方法对候选高阶结构（团）进行评分，ERGM通过条件连接概率提供可解释的依赖补充。

Result: 在公开电子邮件和秘密网络数据集上，LP在二元关系恢复上表现强劲；HP特别是CHESHIRE在推断高阶群组结构时具有优势；ERGM提供可解释的依赖关系补充。

Conclusion: 本文提供了比较性、可复现的评估，厘清了LP、HP和ERGM估计在网络缺失场景下的各自适用条件。

Abstract: We study missing-link inference in partially observed networks by systematically comparing dyadic link prediction (LP) with hyperlink prediction (HP) and an estimation-based ERGM comparator. LP serves as the primary baseline, using classical heuristics computed on the observed graph. HP extends this framework by scoring candidate higher-order structures (cliques) via lifted dyadic scores and via the CHEbyshev Spectral HyperlInk pREdictor (CHESHIRE). All methods are evaluated under a common masking protocol that removes dyadic evidence induced by held-out hyperlinks to ensure comparability. Across public email and covert-network datasets, LP remains strong for dyadic recovery, while HP -- particularly CHESHIRE -- provides gains when the inferential target is higher-order group structure. ERGMs offer an interpretable dependence-based complement through conditional tie probabilities. The contribution is a comparative, reproducible evaluation clarifying when LP, HP, and ERGM estimation are most appropriate under network missingness.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.NC'></div>

# q-bio.NC [[Back]](#toc)

### [213] [Fixed point compositionality via low-rank gluing rules in inhibition-dominated threshold-linear networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07336)
*Juliana Londono Alvarez*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文在抑制主导阈值线性网络(TLNs)中形式化研究了结构模块性如何支持功能组合性。作者提出了一种名为"低秩粘合"的新型模块化网络装配方法，证明了全局不动点受限于其组成模块局部不动点的组合，并为秩-1粘合子类提供了完整刻画。结果应用于图基网络，将不动点分解规则从组合TLNs推广到广义CTLNs，并展示了这些粘合规则可用于工程设计具有组合大容量可预测吸引子的动力学系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大脑在稳定结构和有限资源上产生灵活复杂行为的机制尚缺乏严格的数学刻画。虽然网络模块性常与组合性相关联，但在非线性网络中这种关系的数学特征化仍然缺失。

Method: 在抑制主导阈值线性网络中引入低秩粘合(low-rank gluings)的模块化网络装配方法。通过将具有任意内部连接性的组件子网络通过特定低秩耦合连接，证明全局不动点受限于局部不动点组合。对于秩-1粘合子类，提供完整刻画以确定哪些局部不动点组合可产生全局不动点。

Result: 1. 证明了低秩粘合网络中全局不动点只能是组件模块局部不动点的组合；2. 对秩-1粘合子类给出了全局不动点与局部不动点组合的完整对应关系；3. 将不动点分解规则从组合TLNs推广到更灵活的广义CTLNs；4. 展示了这些规则可用于构建具有组合大容量可预测吸引子的网络系统，涵盖不动点组合到组合极限环。

Conclusion: 结构模块性通过低秩粘合机制为功能组合性提供了严格的数学基础，证明了结构规则比最初假设的更为鲁棒。这项工作为工程设计具有可预测组合动力学的人工神经网络提供了数学上可处理的配方。

Abstract: Brains routinely generate highly flexible and complex behaviors on a relatively stable structure and limited resources. A key mechanism underlying this ability is compositionality, which allows the brain to efficiently decompose complex tasks into simpler, reusable primitives. While network modularity has often been linked to compositionality in biological and artificial networks, a rigorous mathematical characterization of this relationship in nonlinear networks is still lacking. In this work, we formally investigate how structural modularity supports functional compositionality in inhibition-dominated threshold-linear networks (TLNs). We introduce a novel class of modular network assembly called low-rank gluings, where component subnetworks with arbitrary internal connectivity are connected via specific low-rank couplings. We prove that the global fixed points of these networks are constrained to be combinations of the local fixed points of their constituent modules. For a more structured subclass, called rank-1 gluings, we provide a complete characterization that determines which combinations of local fixed points yield global ones. We apply these results to graph-based networks, extending fixed point decomposition rules from combinatorial threshold-linear networks (CTLNs) to the more flexible family of generalized CTLNs (gCTLNs), thereby proving that these structural rules are more robust than initially posited. Finally, we demonstrate that these gluing rules provide a mathematically tractable recipe for engineering compositional dynamics, enabling the construction of networks with a combinatorially large repertoire of predictable attractors that can be understood from simpler component motifs, ranging from compositions of fixed points to compositional limit cycles.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [214] [The Identity Trap in EEG Foundation Models: A Diagnostic Audit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06647)
*Jun-You Lin,Ying Choon Wu,Tzyy-Ping Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"身份陷阱"概念——EEG基础模型在静息态数据中高精度可能源于学习受试者身份特征而非临床生物标志物。提出FMScope诊断协议(含5项诊断)验证此现象普遍存在(受试者方差占主导)，并通过消除受试者轴、分析1/f非周期性成分等操作揭示其本质是物理基础的捷径学习。微调仅在存在跨受试者共识EEG标记的细胞中增强标签相关方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG基础模型在受试者分离交叉验证中表现高精度，但无法区分其学习的是真实临床生物标志物还是与标签相关的受试者身份特征。这种歧义性问题(Idenity Trap)在微调前无法诊断，亟需一套能够在表征层面进行诊断的标准化协议。

Method: 提出FMScope诊断协议，包含五项诊断:方差分解、受试者轴擦除、非周期性1/f消融、逐层标签探测、以及受试者内方向一致性。在三个预训练FM(LaBraM, CBraMod, REVE)和四个数据集上，按2x2布局(标签与受试者关系x是否存在跨受试者EEG共识标记)进行验证。

Result: (1)身份陷阱普遍存在:12/12对中冷冻受试者方差是随机零假设的13-89倍，微调后进一步上升(+10至+63 pp);擦除受试者轴后，在标签在受试者内变化的场景中标签解码提升(+6至+12 pp主组，+4至+27 pp外部队列)。(2)非周期性1/f是受试者身份的一个载体:消融后LaBraM和CBraMod的受试者探测下降9-19 pp，REVE无此依赖。(3)微调仅在存在文献建立跨受试者标记的细胞中放大标签方差。

Conclusion: 身份陷阱是物理基础的捷径学习实例:偏好线索具有可测量的生理成分，仅靠受试者分离交叉验证无法排除。FMScope能够区分反映生物标志物的增益与反映受试者身份的增益。

Abstract: Objective. EEG foundation models (FMs) report strong accuracy on clinical resting-state EEG. However, high accuracy under subject-disjoint cross-validation remains ambiguous: it can reflect a genuine clinical biomarker, or subject-identity features that correlate with the label. We name this the Identity Trap and ask whether it can be diagnosed at the representation level before fine-tuning.
  Approach. We propose FMScope, a frozen-representation protocol packaging five diagnostics: variance decomposition, subject-axis erasure, aperiodic 1/f ablation, layer-wise label probing, and within-subject direction consistency. We apply it to three pretrained FMs (LaBraM, CBraMod, REVE) across four datasets in a 2x2 layout: subject relation of label x presence of a consensus cross-subject EEG marker.
  Main results. (i) The Identity Trap is universal: frozen subject-variance is 13-89x a random null in 12/12 pairs, rising in all 12 under fine-tuning (+10 to +63 pp). This dominance is a removable linear axis: erasing it improves label decoding where the label varies within subject (+6 to +12 pp in primary cells; +4 to +27 pp across external cohorts). (ii) Aperiodic 1/f is one subject carrier: removing it drops the subject probe by 9-19 pp on LaBraM and CBraMod. REVE saturates subject identity without measurable aperiodic dependence. (iii) Fine-tuning amplifies label-variance only in cells with a literature-established cross-subject marker.
  Significance. The Identity Trap is a physically-grounded instance of shortcut learning: the preferred cue has a measurable physiological component, and subject-disjoint splitting alone cannot rule it out. FMScope separates gains reflecting a biological marker from those reflecting subject identity.

</details>


### [215] [Elmes*: Automated Construction of Fine-Grained Evaluation Rubrics for Large Language Models in Long-Tail Educational Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06546)
*Tao Liu,Ye Lu,Ruohua Zhang,Siyu Song,Wentao Liu,Aimin Zhou,Hao Hao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Elmes*框架，用于构建和优化教学场景下的细粒度评估标准，并构建了包含330个场景的Edu-330基准，发现教育能力是多维度的，不同模型在教学创造力、价值观整合和苏格拉底式引导等方面存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM教育评估基准要么强调领域通识正确性，要么依赖人工设计的评分标准，难以扩展到长尾教学场景，缺乏对模型教学能力的系统评估方法。

Method: 提出Elmes*框架，包含声明式多智能体引擎（教师-学生-评审互动）和SceneGen自进化模块，共同优化评估标准和测试数据；基于专家定义的教学维度构建Edu-330基准（11学科、3年级段、10任务类型、1000+二级指标）。

Result: 顶级LLM在创造力和价值观整合上差异显著；知识强的模型可能在苏格拉底式引导上失败；教育专用模型InnoSpark获得最佳人工评分均值；LLM评审能保持与人类可比的排名但方差更低但存在评审偏好偏差；专家评分的少样本锚定可提升人机对齐度。

Conclusion: Elmes*提供了可扩展的诊断基础设施，用于基于教学法的LLM评估，揭示了教育能力评估需从多维度进行而非仅依赖知识正确性。

Abstract: Evaluating large language models (LLMs) for education requires measuring how models teach, not only what they know. Existing benchmarks emphasize domain-general correctness or depend on manually designed rubrics that scale poorly to long-tail pedagogical scenarios. We introduce Elmes*, an end-to-end framework for constructing, refining, and applying fine-grained scenario-specific rubrics. Elmes* combines a declarative multi-agent engine for teacher--student--judge interactions with SceneGen, a self-evolving module that co-optimizes evaluation criteria and test data from expert-defined pedagogical dimensions. Using Elmes*, we build Edu-330, covering 330 scenarios across 11 subjects, 3 grade bands, and 10 task types, with over 1{,}000 second-level indicators. Experiments on Edu-330 and four expert-authored gold-standard scenarios show that educational capability is multidimensional: top-tier LLMs differ mainly in creativity and values integration, knowledge-strong models may fail at Socratic scaffolding, and the education-specialized InnoSpark achieves the best human-evaluated average score. LLM judges preserve human-comparable rankings with much lower scoring variance, but exhibit judge-specific biases such as self-preference. Ablations show that expert-scored few-shot anchoring improves human--LLM alignment, while reasoning enforcement and greedy decoding are model-dependent. Elmes* thus provides scalable diagnostic infrastructure for pedagogically grounded LLM evaluation.

</details>


### [216] [FAIR-Calib: Frontier-Aware Instability-Reweighted Calibration for Post-Training Quantization of Diffusion Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06547)
*Haoyu Huang,Linlin Yang,Sheng Xu,Boyu Liu,Guodong Guo,Zhongqian Fu,Hang Zhou,Baochang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FAIR-Calib两阶段后训练量化框架，针对扩散大语言模型中写前边缘决策易被量化误差翻转的问题，通过位置先验估计和重加权校准，有效保护脆弱的前沿状态，在LLaDA和Dream模型上（W4A4）显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型（dLLMs）在迭代细化token时存在"稳定性滞后"问题——早期决策在写入后仍很脆弱，而后训练量化（PTQ）误差容易翻转这些写前沿处的边缘决策，且一旦翻转会被永久锁定并放大。

Method: 提出两阶段PTQ框架FAIR-Calib。阶段I：在全精度教师模型上探测，结合前沿命中率和掩码阶段可靠性估计位置先验。阶段II：进行离策略、逐层校准，通过最小化重加权隐状态MSE，优先保护脆弱的前沿状态，无需昂贵的端到端扩散 rollout。理论上证明了加权目标是输出KL散度的代理。

Result: 在LLaDA和Dream模型（W4A4量化）上，FAIR-Calib一致优于现有SOTA基线，显著减少前沿决策翻转并抑制提交后不匹配。

Conclusion: FAIR-Calib通过针对性的前沿状态保护策略，有效解决了dLLMs量化过程中写前边缘决策易被翻转的问题，为扩散语言模型的高效量化提供了新范式。

Abstract: Diffusion Large Language Models (dLLMs) refine tokens iteratively but commit them irreversibly, leading to a "stability lag" where early decisions remain fragile even after being written. We reveal that Post-Training Quantization (PTQ) error easily flips these borderline decisions at the write frontier, which are then permanently locked in and amplified. To address this, we propose Frontier-Aware Instability-Reweighted Calibration (FAIR-Calib), a two-stage PTQ framework for dLLMs. Stage I probes a full-precision teacher to estimate a position prior that combines frontier hits and masked-stage reliability. Stage II performs off-policy, layer-wise calibration by minimizing a reweighted hidden-state MSE, effectively prioritizing the protection of fragile frontier states without requiring expensive end-to-end diffusion rollouts. We further theoretically justify our weighted objective as a surrogate for output KL divergence. Empirically, FAIR-Calib consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines on LLaDA and Dream (W4A4), significantly reducing frontier decision flips and suppressing post-commit mismatches across diverse benchmarks.

</details>


### [217] [Multi-Scale Feature Attention Network for Polymer Classification using THz Dual-Comb Spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06554)
*Roshni Mahtani,Ilán Carretero,Laura Monroy,Aldo Moreno-Oyervides,Oscar Elías Bonilla-Manrique,Rocío del Amor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出多尺度特征注意力网络（MSFAN），结合太赫兹双梳光谱技术，实现对12种聚合物的高精度分类，准确率达85.2%，优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统聚合物鉴定方法（如常规分选和光谱技术）在可靠区分回收塑料方面存在局限性，亟需一种快速、高分辨率、非破坏性的替代方案来确保再生塑料的质量与安全性。

Method: 利用太赫兹双梳光谱获取12种聚合物（包括纯聚合物、多层膜、商业混合物和生物聚合物）的光谱数据；提出MSFAN深度学习框架，包含特征门控信号再校准、多尺度并行卷积捕捉不同频率模式、跨特征注意力与注意力池化机制，以突出最具信息量的太赫兹区域。

Result: MSFAN在分类任务中持续优于现有最先进模型，达到85.2%的分类准确率。

Conclusion: 本研究证明了将太赫兹双梳光谱与深度学习相结合在高效、可扩展且可解释的聚合物分类中的潜力。

Abstract: Reliable polymer identification is essential for ensuring the quality and safety of recycled plastics, yet conventional sorting and spectroscopic techniques often struggle to deliver robust discrimination. Terahertz Dual-Comb Spectroscopy (THz-DCS) offers a promising alternative, providing rapid, high-resolution, and non-destructive measurements. In this work, we leverage THz-DCS to classify 12 types of polymers, including pure polymers, multilayer films, commercial blends, and biopolymers. To handle the complexity of these spectral signals, we propose the Multi-Scale Feature Attention Network (MSFAN), a novel deep learning architecture tailored for THz-DCS data. The framework integrates feature gating for signal recalibration and multi-scale parallel convolutions to capture diverse frequency patterns. These features are further refined through cross-feature attention and attention pooling, enabling the model to intrinsically highlight the most informative THz regions. MSFAN consistently outperforms state-of-the-art models, reaching a classification accuracy of 85.2%. This study demonstrates the potential of combining THz-DCS with deep learning techniques for effective, scalable, and interpretable polymer classification.

</details>


### [218] [MacArena: Benchmarking Computer Use Agents on an Online macOS Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06560)
*Victor Muryn,Maksym Shamrai,Sofiia Mazepa,Yehor Khodysko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MacArena，一个包含421个手动验证任务的macOS GUI基准测试，涵盖50个应用，运行在Apple Silicon上。实验表明，现有模型在跨平台GUI能力上存在显著差距，排名在移植任务和macOS原生任务之间发生反转，领先模型在MacArena子集上落后超过26%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: macOS在GUI代理评估领域未被充分覆盖，现有基准macOSWorld仅覆盖少量第一方应用且任务简单，同时不兼容Apple Silicon。作者认为macOS存在Linux基准无法捕获的独特GUI挑战，需要独立的评估平台。
