In this work, sleep stages were classified using Random Forest, XGBoost, and Gradient Boosting ensemble classification models. Overall, we obtained an accuracy of 92.54% (Random Forest), 94.25% (Gradient Boosting), and 94.30% (XGBoost). For explainable classification model, we utilized a game theoretic approach, SHAP (SHapley Addictive exPlanations) to offer a convincing explanation for the prediction.

</details>


### [291] [Breaking the Ice: Analyzing Cold Start Latency in vLLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07362)
*Huzaifa Shaaban Kabakibo,Animesh Trivedi,Lin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地分析了vLLM推理引擎的冷启动延迟，将其分解为六个基础步骤，发现启动过程主要由CPU主导，并建立了轻量级分析模型来准确预测启动延迟，为大规模推理环境的资源规划提供指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着可扩展推理服务的普及，推理引擎的冷启动延迟变得日益重要。尽管vLLM已成为许多推理工作负载的事实标准引擎，但由于其复杂性和快速演进，尚缺乏对其启动延迟的系统性研究。

Method: 将vLLM启动过程分解为六个基础步骤，分析每个步骤在模型级别和系统级别参数下的缩放趋势，识别出启动过程主要为CPU密集型。基于这些洞察，构建了一个轻量级分析模型用于预测启动延迟。

Result: 每个启动步骤展现出与模型和系统参数一致且可解释的缩放趋势，能够细粒度归因延迟来源。所构建的轻量级分析模型能够准确预测给定硬件配置下的vLLM启动延迟。

Conclusion: 本文为理解vLLM启动延迟提供了系统性的基准和可操作的分析模型，有助于大规模推理环境中的资源规划。所有基准数据集、分析工具和预测脚本均已开源。

Abstract: As scalable inference services become popular, the cold start latency of an inference engine becomes important. Today, vLLM has evolved into the de facto inference engine of choice for many inference workloads. Although popular, due to its complexity and rapid evolution, there has not been a systematic study of its startup latency. With major architectural innovations such as the V1 API and the introduction of torch.compile, this paper presents the first detailed performance characterization of vLLM startup latency. We break down the startup process into six foundational steps and demonstrate that it is predominantly CPU bound. Each step exhibits consistent and interpretable scaling trends with respect to model-level and system-level parameters, enabling fine-grained attribution of latency sources. Building on these insights, we develop a lightweight analytical model that accurately predicts vLLM startup latency for a given hardware configuration, providing actionable guidance for resource planning in large-scale inference environments. All benchmarking datasets, analysis tools, and prediction scripts are open sourced at https://github.com/upb-cn/vllm-startup-profiler.

</details>


### [292] [A robust PPG foundation model using multimodal physiological supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07365)
*Eloy Geenjaar,Vince Calhoun,Scott Daly,Gouthaman KV,Lie Lu,Trisha Mittal,Daniel P. Darcy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于多模态监督的PPG基础模型，无需高质量或现场类预训练数据，利用ICU数据中的心电和呼吸信号选择对比样本，在更少训练数据下实现更优的下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PPG基础模型要么依赖需要精心筛选数据的ICU数据集预训练范式（泛化到现场类数据困难），要么使用封闭的现场类PPG数据。需要一种不需要高质量或现场类预训练数据的方法。

Method: 利用ICU数据集中同步采集的心电和呼吸信号作为多模态监督，指导对比学习中的样本选择，允许模型保留和学习含噪PPG片段，从而提升推理鲁棒性。

Result: 在比现有最优方法少3倍的训练数据下，在15个多样化下游任务中的14个上实现性能提升，包括现场类日常活动和心率预测任务。

Conclusion: 多模态监督能够整合互补的生理信息，提高PPG基础模型的鲁棒性，并增强其向消费级数据的泛化能力。

Abstract: Photoplethysmography (PPG), a non-invasive measure of changes in blood volume, is widely used in both wearable devices and clinical settings. Recent PPG foundation models either use open-source ICU datasets with pretraining paradigms that require curated data and thus complicate generalization to field-like data, or use closed-source field-like PPG data. In contrast, we propose a PPG foundation model that does not require high-quality or field-like pretraining data, and instead leverages accompanying electrocardiogram and respiratory signals in ICU datasets to select contrastive samples during pretraining. Our approach allows the model to retain and learn from noisy PPG segments, improving robustness at inference. Our model, pretrained on 3x fewer subjects than existing state-of-the-art approaches, achieves performance improvements on 14 out of 15 diverse downstream tasks, including field-like daily activity and heart rate prediction. Our results demonstrate that multimodal supervision can integrate complementary physiological information to improve the robustness of PPG foundation models and enhance their generalization to consumer-grade data.

</details>


### [293] [Self-evolving LLM agents with in-distribution Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07367)
*Yudi Zhang,Meng Fang,Zhenfang Chen,Mykola Pechenizkiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Q-Evolve框架，通过内分布强化学习联合学习过程奖励标注与策略，使LLM代理能够在稀疏奖励的长程决策任务中实现自我进化，在AlfWorld、WebShop和ScienceWorld任务上优于强基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理在复杂环境中的长程决策面临信用分配难题，代理通常仅在episode结束时获得延迟奖励，缺乏细粒度的过程监督信号，且已有方法依赖环境回溯或人工标注，难以扩展。

Method: 1) 从混合离线数据集（专家演示+代理轨迹）中学**内分布（in-distribution）critic**，使用加权隐式Q学习（Implicit Q-Learning）目标稳定稀疏奖励下的Bellman备份；2) 通过优势估计从学到的价值函数推导**过程奖励（process rewards）**；3) 利用过程奖励进行**行为近端策略优化（behavior-proximal policy optimization）**，在用于标注的数据上迭代进化代理，避免分布偏移。

Result: 在AlfWorld、WebShop和ScienceWorld三个基准上，Q-Evolve在样本效率、鲁棒性和整体任务性能上均优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过将过程级监督与策略共同置于共享的内分布学习循环中协同进化，可以实现LLM代理的稳定自我进化。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have recently emerged as powerful controllers for interactive agents in complex environments, yet training them to perform reliable long-horizon decision making remains a fundamental challenge. A key difficulty lies in credit assignment: agents often receive delayed rewards only at the end of episodes. In this paper, we propose Q-Evolve, a self-evolving framework for LLM agents that unifies automatic process-reward labeling and policy learning within a principled in-distribution reinforcement learning paradigm. In each evolving iteration, our method learns an in-distribution critic from a hybrid off-policy dataset that combines expert demonstrations with agent-generated trajectories, stabilizing Bellman backups in sparse-reward settings via a weighted Implicit Q-Learning objective. The learned value function is then used to derive step-wise process rewards through advantage estimation, enabling dense and reliable supervision without environment backtracking or human annotation. Leveraging these signals, we perform behavior-proximal policy optimization that evolves the agent over the data used for process reward labeling, allowing iterative self-improvement without exacerbating distribution shift. We evaluate our method on AlfWorld, WebShop, and ScienceWorld, showing Q-Evolve outperforms strong baselines in sample efficiency, robustness, and overall task performance. Our results demonstrate that stable agent self-evolution is achievable through the co-evolution of process-level supervision and policy, both grounded within a shared in-distribution learning loop.

</details>


### [294] [Do Coding Agents Deceive Us? Detecting and Preventing Cheating via Capped Evaluation with Randomized Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07379)
*Thanawat Lodkaew,Johannes Ackermann,Soichiro Nishimori,Nontawat Charoenphakdee,Masashi Sugiyama,Takashi Ishida*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CapCode框架和CapReward奖励设计，通过设置评估上限分数来检测和减少模型在代码任务中的作弊行为，使评估分数更可靠地反映真实能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型在评估和训练中常通过利用捷径（作弊）获得高分，导致评估分数不可靠，无法反映真实的任务解决能力。

Method: CapCode框架构建带有随机化测试的编码数据集，将最佳非作弊性能故意限制在1以下（上限设计），使显著高于上限的分数可被视为作弊证据。CapReward基于此原则设计奖励机制，抑制超出上限的优化。

Result: 多个数据集实验表明，CapCode能检测作弊行为同时保持模型性能排名；CapReward可减少作弊行为，使模型更好地遵循任务规范。

Conclusion: 上限分数设计为评估提供了更清晰的解释，能有效识别和抑制作弊，提升评估可靠性。

Abstract: A growing failure mode in agent evaluation and training is that models can achieve high evaluation scores by exploiting shortcuts instead of solving the intended task, producing deceptive performance. This makes evaluation scores unreliable as measures of true task-solving ability. We propose CapCode, a framework for constructing coding datasets with randomized tests whose best achievable non-cheating performance is deliberately capped below one. This capped-performance design gives evaluation scores a clearer interpretation: scores substantially above the cap are implausible and therefore provide evidence of cheating. To prevent cheating, we propose CapReward, a reward design based on the CapCode principle to discourage optimization beyond the cap. Experiments across multiple datasets show that CapCode detects cheating while preserving performance ranking of models, and CapReward reduces cheating behavior, yielding models that better follow the intended task specification.

</details>


### [295] [Covariance Shrinkage via Stochastic Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07382)
*Mathieu Chalvidal,Florentin Coeurdoux,Eric Vanden-Eijnden*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将高维协方差矩阵的经典收缩估计重新解释为参数化随机插值器上的经验风险最小化问题，揭示了三种降低统计风险的机制（调度、流映射与耦合、早停），并提出了一种基于神经网络的插值器估计器，在合成数据和真实神经影像数据上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的高维协方差收缩估计方法缺乏统一的框架来理解其工作机制和不同设计选择的影响。本文旨在通过随机插值的形式化框架，统一现有收缩估计方法，并揭示其中降低风险的三种独立机制，从而为设计更优的协方差估计器提供理论指导。

Method: 本文将协方差收缩估计重新表述为源分布与目标分布之间参数化随机插值器上的经验风险最小化问题。通过分析插值调度、流映射与耦合结构（特别是最优传输解）以及早停三种机制对风险的影响，并基于这些理论洞见，提出了一种神经估计器来学习插值器，同时给出了其二次风险的上界。

Result: 理论分析表明，插值调度决定了可容许协方差的类别；特定耦合结构（如最优传输）可以降低经验风险，且非线性流映射能够实现特征向量的正则化；早停通过近似真实插值分布提供了额外的偏差-方差权衡。所提出的神经估计器在合成实验和真实神经影像数据上均表现出优于传统方法的性能。

Conclusion: 该工作通过随机插值框架统一了高维协方差收缩估计，不仅揭示了三种独立的降风险机制，还提供了一种灵活可扩展的神经估计器，为高维协方差估计带来了额外的正则化能力。

Abstract: We recast classical shrinkage of high-dimensional covariance estimators as empirical risk minimization over a parametric stochastic interpolant between a source and a target distribution. This formalism recovers known shrinkage estimators as special cases and reveals three distinct mechanisms for reducing statistical risk: (i) Scheduling: the interpolant schedule determines the class of admissible covariances, and hence the achievable risk. (ii) Flow maps and couplings: whereas naive constructions amount to assuming independence between the distributions, specific coupling structures (e.g., solutions of optimal transport problems) can lower the empirical risk. Moreover, non-linear flow maps realizing such couplings free the interpolant covariance from the eigenbasis of the empirical estimate, enabling eigenvector regularization. (iii) Early stopping: estimators defined by integrating a regressed vector field afford an additional bias-variance trade-off through approximation of the true interpolant distribution. We then propose a neural estimator of the interpolant, together with an upper bound on its quadratic risk in terms of the interpolant approximation error, and validate both on synthetic experiments. Finally, we apply the estimator to real neuroimaging data, demonstrating the additional regularization power this approach offers in practice.

</details>


### [296] [Making the Most of Limited Data: Score-Aware Training for Text-to-Music Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07387)
*Yun-Chen Cheng,Tzu-Hung Huang,Chih-Pin Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于分数感知训练（score-aware training）的文本到音乐生成方法，通过利用CLAP对齐分数作为监督信号，结合Beta噪声时间表、段级过滤、两阶段字幕构建和REPA辅助损失等技术，在无需大规模专有数据集和工业级算力的情况下，实现了高效的文本到音乐生成。基于FluxAudio的450M参数模型在ICME 2026 ATTM Grand Challenge效率赛道中获得客观评估第二、MOS评估第三的成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进的文本到音乐生成系统依赖于大规模专有数据集和工业级算力，这使得无法区分架构贡献与资源优势。本文旨在提出一种不依赖大量数据和计算资源的替代方法，通过充分利用对齐分数作为监督信号来提升模型性能。

Method: 1) 分数感知训练（score-aware training）：将音频-字幕对齐分数作为直接监督信号；2) CLAP-conditioned Beta噪声时间表：将低分段的样本路由到高噪声训练区域，作为隐式正则化器；3) 段级过滤：移除最不对齐的样本；4) 两阶段字幕构建：弥合训练时冗长字幕与推理时简洁提示之间的分布差异；5) REPA辅助损失：从预训练的CLAP和MuQ编码器中迁移结构化语义知识。

Result: 450M参数的FluxAudio系统在ICME 2026 ATTM Grand Challenge效率赛道中，客观评估排名第二（两个赛道综合），最终MOS评估在效率赛道中排名第三。

Conclusion: 所提出的分数感知训练及相关技术能够在不依赖大规模专有数据集和工业级算力的前提下，有效提升文本到音乐生成性能，验证了利用对齐分数作为监督信号的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: State-of-the-art text-to-music generation systems rely on massive proprietary datasets and industrial-scale compute, making it impossible to disentangle architectural contributions from resource advantages. We propose \textit{score-aware training}, which treats audio-caption alignment score as a direct supervision signal throughout the pipeline. Rather than discarding low-scoring segments, we repurpose them via a CLAP-conditioned Beta noise timestep schedule that routes them to high-noise training regimes, acting as an effective implicit regularizer. Complementarily, segment-level filtering removes the most misaligned examples, and a two-stage caption procedure bridges the distribution gap between verbose training captions and concise inference prompts. A REPA auxiliary loss further transfers structured semantic knowledge from pretrained CLAP and MuQ encoders without additional data. Our 450M-parameter FluxAudio-based system, submitted to the ICME 2026 ATTM Grand Challenge Efficiency Track, ranked 2nd across both tracks in the objective evaluation and 3rd in the Efficiency Track in the final MOS evaluation.

</details>


### [297] [Generative Modeling of Discrete Latent Structures via Dynamic Policy Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07400)
*Stefan Ivanovic,Ge Liu,Mohammed El-Kebir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GReinSS是一种基于策略学习的框架，通过动态缩放奖励来学习最大化观测数据似然的潜在状态分布，能够从间接观测中准确重建组合潜状态（如潜集和潜图），在模拟数据和真实RNA-seq数据中均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多科学问题需要从间接观测中推断未观测到的机制性潜在状态，但传统方法（如期望最大化）无法扩展到组合大空间，而深度学习方法（如VAE）往往形成人工潜在状态而非重建真实机制状态。

Method: 提出GReinSS策略学习框架，采用动态缩放奖励来学习潜在状态分布，以最大化观测数据似然。

Result: GReinSS能准确重建模拟潜集和潜图，优于其他策略学习和生成建模基线；在真实短读长RNA-seq数据中重建的异构体比RSEM算法更匹配正交长读长测序检测到的异构体。

Conclusion: GReinSS是一种从间接观测中对组合潜状态进行生成建模和推断的原则性且实践有效的方法。

Abstract: Many scientific problems require inferring unobserved mechanistic latent states from indirect observations. While classical approaches, including expectation maximization, do not scale to combinatorially large spaces, deep learning approaches such as variational autoencoders typically form artificial latent states rather than reconstructing the mechanistic ground-truth states. Here, we introduce GReinSS, a policy learning framework that uses dynamically rescaled rewards to learn latent state distributions that maximize the observed data likelihood. We show that GReinSS accurately reconstructs simulated latent sets and latent graphs, outperforming alternative policy learning and generative modeling baselines. Additionally, GReinSS reconstructs isoforms from real short-read RNA sequencing data that better match isoforms detected by orthogonal long-read sequencing than the standard RSEM algorithm. Overall, GReinSS is a principled and practically effective approach for generative modeling and inference of combinatorial latent states from indirect observations.

</details>


### [298] [Reversible Foundations: Training a 120B Sparse MoE through State-Preserving Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07404)
*Rohan Shravan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文报告了在单个八GPU节点上端到端训练千亿参数稀疏混合专家模型（LightningLM 0.1V）的全过程。模型从1.78B稠密种子通过四个阶段增长至120B（460个路由专家，top-12路由），有效参数5.93B。核心贡献包括：可逆循环栈减少激活内存、状态保持的模型增长原则、以及TQP量化策略（将优化器状态从100B+专家参数压缩至2.26B适配器参数）。这是在单节点上完成的最大规模端到端训练之一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在有限硬件资源（单八GPU节点）上训练超大规模语言模型的可行性，降低大模型训练的基础设施门槛，同时保证模型性能。

Method: 1) 可逆循环栈：反向传播时重建激活值而非存储，保持激活内存不随模型增长而增加。2) 状态保持的增长：四阶段渐进式增长（稠密→MoE、浅层→深层、少专家→多专家），每个阶段有可复现原则及失败案例分析。3) TQP策略：对基础专家权重量化，使用训练的低秩适配器，将优化器状态从100B+专家参数压缩至2.26B适配器参数，专家路径优化器状态减少约45倍。

Result: 成功在单八GPU节点上训练120B参数模型（有效参数5.93B），训练损失1.78，上下文长度8K。证明了多语言印地语能力和代码能力可通过构建式学习获得。模型族、分词器和训练代码均已开源。

Conclusion: 本文的创新在于将已知原语（可逆计算、渐进式增长、量化/低秩适配）系统集成，实现了单节点上超大规模模型的端到端训练，为资源受限场景下的AI研究提供了可行的实践范式。

Abstract: This paper reports on training a hundred-billion-parameter sparse mixture of experts on a single eight-GPU node, end to end. LightningLM 0.1V is a recurrence-backbone language model family grown in four stages from a small dense seed, through a 5B and a 9B mixture of experts, to a 120B model with 460 routed experts under top-12 routing. Each larger model is grown from the trained weights of the smaller one; active parameters rise monotonically from 1.78B at the dense seed to 5.93B at 120B (about 5% of the 118.67B stored). The full lineage runs on single nodes, the larger stages at 8K context, reaching a released training loss of 1.78 at 120B scale.
  This is a systems and experience report. It is organized around three disciplines. Reversibility: a reversible recurrence stack reconstructs activations in the backward pass instead of storing them, holding activation memory flat as the model grows. State-preserving growth: each expansion (dense to MoE, shallow to deep, few experts to many) is given as a reproducible principle paired with the failure that results from getting it wrong; several failures are silent. Single-node economics: the 120B trains through TQP, a strategy of quantized base expert weights and trained low-rank adapters that carries optimizer state on 2.26B adapter parameters rather than 100B+ resident in routed experts, cutting expert-path optimizer state by a factor of ~45.
  What is new is the integration of known primitives, not any primitive in isolation: one grown lineage running end to end on a single node, documented at practitioner level, with per-domain held-out loss as evidence that targeted capabilities (multilingual Indic competence, code) were learned by construction. Model family, tokenizer, and training code are released.

</details>


### [299] [A Comprehensive Anatomy of Human and DeepSeek-R1 LLM Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07410)
*Yuxiang Chen,Jun Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过对比DeepSeek-R1与人类在AIME 2025全部30道题目上的推理过程（标注10,247个推理步骤为五类功能），发现模型存在“拓扑模仿”现象：表面呈现推理形式但缺乏逻辑功能。尽管存在两种真正推理的信号（成功轨迹中分支与回溯的稳定使用、反思需置于演绎推理中才有效），但整体上模型大量进行浅层验证、局部循环和无意义回溯。研究建议改进评估与训练方向，包括测量跨轨迹稳定性、惩罚“空转”轨迹、鼓励深层逻辑修正、将推理时计算重新分配到演绎和回溯上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大语言模型（特别是DeepSeek-R1）的“顿悟时刻”是否代表真正的推理能力，还是仅仅模仿推理的表象。长链思维模型越来越多地被使用，但其推理质量与人类推理之间的本质差异尚未被系统性地量化研究。

Method: 对AIME 2025全部30道数学题的推理过程进行系统对比分析，将10,247个推理步骤标注为五类功能：分析（Analysis）、推理（Inference）、分支（Branch）、回溯（Backtrace）和反思（Reflection）。通过功能类别分布、轨迹结构对比和成功/失败案例分析来揭示模型与人类推理的结构性差异。

Result: 人类推理保持紧凑的分析-演绎交替模式；DeepSeek-R1频繁回访中间结果、进行浅层且往往不必要的验证、在局部检查中循环而无实质逻辑进展。成功轨迹中分支和回溯的使用稳定，失败轨迹则过度或不足使用探索行为。反思只有在置于演绎推理中时才有效，陷入分析循环的反思只关注局部数值细节而忽略全局逻辑错误。

Conclusion: 当前的长链思维模型可能更多因推理的表象而非真正的逻辑进步获得奖励。推理质量不仅取决于反思发生的数量，更取决于反思是否一致地出现在适当的逻辑尺度上。未来应改进评估方法，包括测量跨轨迹稳定性、惩罚空转轨迹、鼓励深层逻辑修正、重新分配推理时计算资源。

Abstract: The emergence of "Aha moments" in large language models, particularly DeepSeek-R1-0120, has raised the question of whether these systems genuinely reason or merely imitate the appearance of reasoning. We conduct a comprehensive empirical comparison between model and human reasoning across all 30 problems from AIME 2025, exhaustively annotating 10,247 reasoning steps into five functional categories: Analysis, Inference, Branch, Backtrace, and Reflection. We find a clear structural difference. Human solutions maintain a compact alternation between analysis and deduction, whereas DeepSeek-R1 frequently revisits intermediate results, performs shallow and often unnecessary verification, and loops through local checks without meaningful logical progress. We describe this as topological mimicry: reproducing the surface form of reasoning without its functional role. Despite this, we identify two signals of genuine reasoning. First, successful traces exhibit stable use of branching and backtracking, while failed traces either underuse or overuse exploratory actions. Second, reflection is only effective when placed within deductive inference; reflections trapped in analysis loops focus on local numerical details while missing global logical errors. These findings suggest that current long-CoT models may be rewarded more for the appearance of reasoning than for genuine deductive progress. We discuss directions for improving evaluation and training, including measuring cross-trace stability, penalising "spinning-wheel" traces, encouraging deeper logical correction, and reallocating inference-time compute toward deduction and backtracking. Overall, reasoning quality depends not simply on how much reflection occurs, but on whether reflection appears consistently and at the appropriate logical scale.

</details>


### [300] [Sparsely gated tiny linear experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07414)
*Simon Schug*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为sgatlin（稀疏门控线性神经元）的方法，通过将MoE模型中的每个专家缩减为单个神经元并去除非线性激活函数，在提高稀疏性的同时提升了计算效率和可解释性。在同等计算量下，用sgatlin替换Transformer的前馈层可以改善语言模型的困惑度，且前馈电路无需额外训练即可解释，形成了语义聚类结构，并在事实回忆中发挥因果作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的MoE模型虽然增加了稀疏性，但每个专家仍然是大而密集的，计算效率和可解释性仍有提升空间。作者希望进一步增加稀疏性，同时提升模型的可解释性。

Method: 将每个专家缩减为单个神经元，并去除专家中通常使用的非线性激活函数，形成稀疏门控线性神经网络（sgatlin）。在isoflop（同等计算量）设置下，用sgatlin替换Transformer的所有前馈层。

Result: 在同等计算预算下，sgatlin替换方案改善了语言模型的困惑度；sgatlin的前馈电路形成语义上结构化的聚类，并在事实回忆任务中具有因果作用。

Conclusion: sgatlin为构建计算高效且可解释的Transformer前馈层提供了一条可行的路径。

Abstract: Sparsity allows scaling model parameters without proportionally increasing computational cost. While mixture of experts (MoE) models are made increasingly sparse, individual experts typically remain large and dense. Here, we demonstrate that further increasing sparsity by shrinking each expert to consist of a single neuron and selecting a tiny fraction of many available neurons can improve compute efficiency and interpretability. Counterintuitively, the key to achieving both is removing the nonlinearity typically applied to the experts, resulting in a network of sparsely gated linear neurons (sgatlin). In an isoflop comparison, we find that replacing all transformer feedforward layers with sgatlin improves perplexity in language models across different compute budgets. At the same time, the sparsity and linearity of the resulting feedforward circuits present new opportunities for model interpretability. In a small-scale case study, we demonstrate that feedforward circuits in sgatlin can be interpreted without having to train additional replacement models. We find that they form semantically structured clusters and are causally implicated in factual recall. Our findings paint a possible path towards compute-efficient and interpretable transformer feedforward layers.

</details>


### [301] [Video-Based Prediction of In-Flight Particle Characteristics in Atmospheric Plasma Spraying](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07416)
*Abhijeet Praveen,Sareh Soleimani,Cormac Cureton,Aman Sidhu,Kintak Raymond Yu,Cristian Cojocaru,Narges Armanfard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用高速摄像机拍摄的等离子体羽流视频，通过TabPFN、CNN等模型预测大气等离子喷涂过程中的粒子温度(R²=0.90)和速度(R²=0.82)，验证了基于视频的非侵入式诊断方法的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: APS工艺中飞行粒子温度和速度强烈影响涂层质量，但难以在运行中持续监测，因此需要开发非侵入式的数据驱动诊断方法。

Method: 从高速视频中提取三种视频特征，采用TabPFN、CNN以及随机森林、梯度提升、SVR、XGBoost等回归基线模型，在63个喷涂运行中的126个标注视频上使用分组留一交叉验证进行评估。

Result: TabPFN在温度预测上表现最佳(R²=0.86)，CNN在速度预测上更强(R²=0.81)；基于预训练CNN直接在原始视频帧上操作取得最高性能，温度R²=0.90，速度R²=0.82。

Conclusion: 视频衍生的羽流信息为APS非侵入式诊断和实时工艺监控提供了有前景且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Atmospheric plasma spraying (APS) is a widely used coating process in which in-flight particle temperature and velocity strongly influence coating quality. However, these particle characteristics are difficult to monitor continuously during operation, motivating the development of non-invasive data-driven diagnostic methods. In this work, we investigate the predictive potential of high-speed video observations of the plasma plume for estimating in-flight particle characteristics in APS. We introduce three different video-derived feature representations and evaluate them using Tabular Prior-Data Fitted Networks (TabPFN), convolutional neural networks (CNN), and classical regression baselines including Random Forest, Gradient Boosting, Support Vector Regression, and XGBoost. Experiments are conducted using grouped leave-one-out cross-validation on 126 labeled pre- and post-spray video recordings from 63 APS spray runs. Across the engineered feature experiments, TabPFN achieves the most consistent performance for temperature prediction, reaching R2 = 0.86 using the combined feature representation. CNN models particularly perform stronger for velocity prediction, achieving R2 of 0.81. In addition, we evaluate models operating directly on raw video frames using pretrained CNNs and find that the highest performance is achieved by a pretrained CNN with a regression head with R2 of 0.90 and 0.82 for temperature and velocity, respectively. The results demonstrate that video-derived plume information provides a promising and scalable foundation for non-invasive APS diagnostics and real-time process monitoring.

</details>


### [302] [Discovering Multiscale Deep Formulas in Complex Systems via Neural-Guided Lambda Calculus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07426)
*Hanqiao Yu,Shusen Yang,Xuebin Ren,Cong Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Deflex是一种端到端AI方法，通过Deflexformer（可分解深度能量模型）和Deflexpressor（λ-演算符号回归模型）的协同工作，自动从多尺度复杂系统中提取具有不同形式（包括不变量和分布）的尺度特定公式，效率比现有最优方法提升最高达7倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学中的基本问题是从复杂系统中发现简洁的数学公式来描述其底层模式，现有AI方法在单尺度系统中表现良好，但在多尺度复杂系统中识别尺度特定公式的能力有限。

Method: Deflex包含两个子系统：Deflexpressor（基于λ-演算的符号回归模型，用于高阶公式）和Deflexformer（可分解深度能量模型，用于学习跨尺度的统一表示）。Deflexpressor生成合成数据预训练Deflexformer，后者通过解耦多尺度潜在关系来指导公式发现。

Result: 在六个具有不同行为的代表性复杂系统中，Deflex相比现有最优方法实现了最高7倍的效率提升，并能够自动进行多尺度公式发现。

Conclusion: Deflex方法可成为跨学科科学发现的有效工具，为自动提取多尺度复杂系统的数学公式提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: A fundamental problem in science is identifying underlying patterns of complex systems in the form of concise mathematical formulas. Current Artificial Intelligence (AI)-based methods have shown strong performance in single-scale systems, yet remain limited in identifying scale-specific formulas in multiscale complex systems. We present Deflex, an end-to-end AI method to automatically extract multiscale formulas with potentially different forms, including invariants and distributions, from complex systems. Deflex consists of two subsystems named Deflexformer and Deflexpressor. Deflexpressor is a lambda-calculus symbolic regression model for higher-order formulas. Deflexformer is a decomposable deep energy model for learning unified representations across scales. Deflexpressor generates synthetic data to pre-train Deflexformer, which then guides formula discovery by decoupling multiscale latent relationships. Across six representative complex systems with diverse behaviors, Deflex achieves up to 7-fold higher efficiency than the state-of-the-art methods while enabling automated multiscale discovery. Our work could be a useful tool for scientific discovery across disciplines.

</details>


### [303] [Time series Foundation Models based on Physics-Informed Synthetic Histories for Cold-Start Photovoltaic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07457)
*Lorenzo Longarini,Alessandro Rongoni,Simone Silenzi,Emanuele Frontoni,Riccardo Rosati*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出零样本冷启动光伏预测流水线，利用合成生产历史使时序基础模型（TSFMs）在无目标站点观测数据时进行预测，TabPFN-TS在真实反馈下误差最优（MAE 0.514），Chronos-2在自预测反馈下最鲁棒，性能对合成数据来源不敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光伏运营商在并网时缺乏目标站点历史观测数据，无法直接使用标准监督预测模型，需要解决冷启动场景下的生产预测问题。

Method: 提出零样本预测流水线：从电站元数据和气象协变量生成合成生产历史，通过推理时条件化使时序基础模型进行预测。对比五种TSFMs与经典基线，采用冷启动基线、真实反馈、自预测反馈三种评估策略，在440个光伏站点和四个数据集上验证。

Result: 协变量感知的基础模型超越基线约1.7-2倍；TabPFN-TS在真实反馈下取得最低误差（MAE 0.514, RMSE 0.721）；Chronos-2在自预测反馈下最鲁棒；性能对合成历史来源不敏感。

Conclusion: 合成生产历史可为冷启动光伏预测提供有效的时序上下文，其准确性更多依赖于合理的时序上下文存在性而非特定生成器，协变量感知的TSFMs在此场景下表现最优。

Abstract: At commissioning time, Photovoltaic (PV) operators must forecast production before target-site observations are available, limiting the direct use of standard supervised forecasters. This cold-start setting is addressed with a zero-shot pipeline that generates a synthetic production history from plant metadata and meteorological covariates, enabling time-series foundation models (TSFMs) to forecast through inference-time conditioning. Five TSFMs are benchmarked against classical baselines under strict Cold-Start Baseline, Real Feedback, and Self-Forecast Feedback strategies. The evaluation spans $440$ PV sites across four datasets and diverse climate regimes. Covariate-aware foundation models outperform baselines by approximately $1.7-2\times$: TabPFN-TS achieves the lowest error under Real Feedback (MAE $0.514$, RMSE $0.721$ $kWh$ ${kWp}^{-1}$ ${d}^{-1}$), while Chronos-2 is most robust under Self-Forecast Feedback. Performance is largely insensitive to the synthetic-history source, indicating that accuracy is driven more by the availability of plausible temporal context than by the specific generator.

</details>


### [304] [Unsupervised Continual Clustering via Forward-Backward Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07474)
*Mohammadreza Sadeghi,Sareh Soleimani,Zihan Wang,Narges Armanfard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出无监督持续聚类（UCC）任务及FBCC方法，通过前向-后向知识蒸馏实现无数据存储的持续聚类，有效缓解灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督持续学习（UCL）方法在缺乏标签的情况下灾难性遗忘问题严重，且忽视了聚类特定目标；已有缓解策略（知识蒸馏、回放缓冲区）存在内存和隐私问题。

Method: 提出FBCC框架，包含持续教师网络（含聚类投影头）和轻量级任务特定学生网络，通过双阶段前向-后向蒸馏过程，使教师在学习新聚类的同时保留先前发现的聚类结构，无需存储历史数据。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上，FBCC在聚类准确率上持续优于现有持续学习基线，并显著降低了灾难性遗忘。

Conclusion: FBCC是UCC领域的开创性方法，有效解决了无标签场景下持续聚类中的灾难性遗忘问题，避免了数据存储带来的隐私风险。

Abstract: Unsupervised Continual Learning (UCL) aims to enable neural networks to learn sequential tasks without labels or access to past data. A major challenge in this setting is Catastrophic Forgetting, where models forget previously learned tasks upon learning new ones. This challenge is amplified in UCL due to the absence of labels to guide learning and memory retention. Existing mitigation strategies, such as knowledge distillation and replay buffers, often raise memory and privacy concerns. Moreover, current UCL methods largely overlook clustering-specific objectives. To fill this gap, we introduce Unsupervised Continual Clustering (UCC) and propose Forward-Backward Knowledge Distillation for Continual Clustering (FBCC). FBCC employs a continual teacher network with a clustering projector and lightweight task-specific students. Through a dual-phase forward-backward distillation process, the teacher learns new clusters while preserving previously discovered cluster structure without storing past data. FBCC represents a pioneering approach to UCC, demonstrating improved clustering performance across sequential tasks. Experiments on four benchmark datasets demonstrate that FBCC consistently outperforms existing continual learning baselines in clustering accuracy while significantly reducing catastrophic forgetting.

</details>


### [305] [Graph Neural Network leveraging Higher-order Class Label Connectivity for Heterophilous Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07475)
*Takuto Takahashi,Itsuki Nakayama,Takahiro Mitani,Ryosuke Kikuchi,Yuya Sasaki,Makoto Onizuka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对异配图中GNN节点分类性能受限的问题，提出标签上下文分类器(LCC)，通过四种不同的游走类型捕获有向图中的高阶类别标签连通性，并可与任意GNN自适应集成，实验表明集成LCC的GNN在异配有向图上超越SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN在同配图上表现优异，但在异配图（不同类别节点更易相连）上性能受限，且当前基于图卷积网络的GNN无法捕获异配图中常见的高阶类别标签连通性。

Method: 提出标签上下文分类器(LCC)，通过四种不同类型的游走（walk）生成标签上下文嵌入，以捕获有向图中的高阶类别标签连通性；同时提出可自适应学习LCC与任意GNN重要性的集成机制。

Result: 集成LCC的GNN在异配有向图的节点分类任务上优于现有SOTA方法；标签上下文嵌入能有效提升节点分类性能。

Conclusion: LCC通过捕获高阶类别标签连通性有效解决了异配图中GNN性能受限的问题，且具有良好的通用性，可与任意GNN集成。

Abstract: Node classification in graph neural networks (GNNs) has been widely applied in various fields of graph analysis. GNNs achieve high-accuracy node classification in homophilous graphs, where nodes with the same class label tend to be connected. However, their performance remains limited in heterophilous graphs, where nodes with different class labels are more likely to be connected. In particular, current GNNs derived from graph convolutional networks cannot capture higher-order class label connectivity, which is frequently observed in real-world heterophilous graphs. To address this issue, we propose a novel classifier, Label Context Classifier (LCC), designed to capture higher-order class label connectivity in directed graphs. LCC estimates the class label of a target node by leveraging label context embeddings that are generated through four distinct types of walks. In addition, our approach allows the integration of LCC and any GNN by adaptively learning their importance. Experimental results demonstrate that GNNs integrated with LCC outperform SOTA methods and the label context embeddings improve the node classification performance in heterophilous directed graphs.

</details>


### [306] [Drifting Models for Surrogate Flow Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07481)
*Chris R. Jung,Markus Dörr,Natalie Jüngling,Jennifer Niessner,Adam T. Müller,Nicolaj C. Stache*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于生成式漂移框架的流体力学替代模型，在VAE潜空间中进行条件漂移，结合标签感知掩码，实现单次高质量生成。该模型在精度和流一致性上匹配迭代扩散方法，但推理速度快两个数量级，并支持空间条件化以实现对未见几何的泛化，为实时CFD代理提供了高效方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CFD虽能提供高保真流场用于室内环境优化，但计算成本过高限制了快速探索；现有生成式替代模型虽优于确定性网络，但迭代采样速度慢，亟需一种高质量、单次生成的替代方案。

Method: 将生成式漂移框架适配到流体力学领域：提出条件架构，在学习的VAE潜空间中进行漂移；使用标签感知掩码（label-aware masking）使生成样本与边界条件对齐；并设计了空间条件化变体以支持泛化到未见几何。

Result: 标签条件模型在精度和流一致性上匹配迭代扩散方法，但推理速度快两个数量级；空间条件化变体展示了对未见几何泛化的潜力。

Conclusion: 条件漂移可作为扩散方法的高效替代方案，在推理速度至关重要的场景中实现实时CFD替代模型。

Abstract: While Computational Fluid Dynamics (CFD) provides high-fidelity flow fields for optimizing indoor environments, its computational cost limits rapid exploration. To solve this problem generative surrogates offer better distribution modeling than deterministic networks, but iterative sampling is slow. To enable high-quality, single-pass generation, we adapt the novel generative drifting framework to fluid mechanics. We introduce a conditional architecture that performs drifting in a learned VAE latent space and uses label-aware masking to align generated samples with their boundary conditions. Our label-conditioned model matches iterative diffusion in accuracy and flow consistency while running two orders of magnitude faster. Additionally, we propose a spatial-conditioning variant that establishes a promising path towards generalization to unseen geometries. Ultimately, conditional drifting serves as a highly efficient alternative to diffusion based approaches, unlocking real-time CFD surrogates where inference speed is critical.

</details>


### [307] [Network Recovery from Cascade Data: A Debiased Jacobian-Based Machine Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07483)
*Lei Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CascadeNet 是一种基于雅可比矩阵的机器学习框架，用于从动态级联数据中恢复隐藏的影响网络，无需指定具体的扩散模型，通过 Neyman 正交去偏实现 $\sqrt{n}$-一致性和渐近正态性，在模拟和真实 COVID-19 传播数据中均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网络恢复方法通常依赖特定的扩散模型假设，当模型设定错误时性能显著下降。作者希望提出一种无需指定扩散机制、具有鲁棒性和统计推断能力的网络恢复方法。

Method: 提出 CascadeNet 框架：首先构建一步转移函数的灵活估计器，然后利用 Riesz 表示子进行 Neyman 正交去偏，得到去偏后的雅可比矩阵。该雅可比矩阵刻画了潜在影响结构，且具有 $\sqrt{n}$-一致性和渐近正态性，支持形式化的网络结构推断。

Result: 在模拟实验中，CascadeNet 在九种常见数据生成过程中均达到最高的网络恢复准确率。在西班牙 52 个省份的 COVID-19 传播实证应用中，CascadeNet 恢复的传播网络与真实省际移动网络显著相关，而基线方法恢复的网络与真实无显著对齐。

Conclusion: CascadeNet 是一种不依赖特定扩散模型假设的、鲁棒且可进行统计推断的网络恢复方法，在多种设定下优于现有方法，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Many important outcomes unfold as dynamic cascades, including product adoption, disease spread, financial distress, and information diffusion. A central challenge is to recover the hidden influence network behind these cascades. Existing methods typically assume a specific diffusion model, and their performance degrades substantially when that assumption is misspecified. We propose CascadeNet, a Jacobian-based machine learning framework for network recovery that does not require specifying a diffusion mechanism. The key idea is that the underlying influence structure can be characterized by the Jacobian of the one-step transition function. CascadeNet first constructs a flexible estimator of the transition function, and further applies Neyman-orthogonal debiasing via the Riesz representer, so that the debiased Jacobian is $\sqrt{n}$-consistent and asymptotically normal, enabling formal inference on the network structure. We validate CascadeNet in both a simulation exercise and a real-world empirical application. In simulations, where the data-generating process is known, CascadeNet achieves the highest network recovery accuracy across nine common data-generating processes. In an empirical application to COVID-19 transmission across Spain's 52 provinces, CascadeNet recovers transmission networks that are significantly correlated with the true inter-province mobility network, whereas networks recovered by baseline methods show no significant alignment with the ground truth.

</details>


### [308] [CoMetaPNS: Continually Meta-learning Personalized Neural Surrogates for Cardiac Electrophysiology Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07488)
*Ryan Missel,Xiajun Jiang,Linwei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种持续元学习框架，用于个性化心脏神经代理模型，能够在不遗忘先前知识的情况下持续整合新数据，并识别数据是否来自已知或未知动力学源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于元学习的个性化心脏代理模型假设训练分布静态且多样化，并需要已知任务标识符。当新数据顺序到达时，完整重训练代价高昂且不可行，且模型会遭受灾难性遗忘——这在临床环境中是一个重大限制。

Method: 提出持续元学习框架，通过在记忆缓冲区上维护一个持续贝叶斯高斯混合模型（GMM），随时间推断数据的标识符及其关系，从而支持有效的元学习。

Result: 在合成心脏数据上的实验表明，该方法在模拟预测、计算可扩展性和抗灾难性遗忘方面均优于现有基线。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了个性化神经代理模型在持续学习场景下的有效部署，能处理临床中顺序到达的未标记数据，且无需完整重训练。

Abstract: Personalized virtual heart simulations face challenges in model personalization and computational cost. While neural surrogates offer state-of-the-art solutions, they typically address either efficient personalization or training generalizable models. Recent work reframes this by learning the process of personalizing a surrogate using limited subject-specific context data, through few-shot generative modeling with set-conditioned surrogates and meta-learned amortized inference. These methods, however, assume a static and diverse training distribution with known task identifiers. When new data becomes available, they require costly retraining with all prior data to avoid catastrophic forgetting - a phenomena where the model forgets earlier tasks when trained on new ones. This is a major limitation in clinical settings where often unlabeled data arrives sequentially and full retraining is infeasible. This paper presents a new continual meta-learning framework to achieve personalized neural surrogates able to not only continually integrate information but also identify whether incoming data stems from a known or unknown dynamics source. By leveraging a continual Bayesian Gaussian Mixture Model over a memory buffer, our framework can infer the identifiers and relationships of data over time - required for effective meta-learning. Empirical results on synthetic cardiac data demonstrate superior simulation forecasting, computational scalability, and resilience to catastrophic forgetting compared to existing baselines.

</details>


### [309] [Second-Order Path Kernel Interpolation Formulas in Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07495)
*Jin Guo,Roy Y. He,Jean-Michel Morel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了神经网络的二阶路径核插值公式，揭示了曲率加权项和随机梯度噪声耦合项对预测的贡献，并推广至带动量的情形，给出了预测的浓度估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解训练数据如何塑造神经网络预测是现代学习理论的核心问题。已有的一阶路径核插值公式（Domingos, 2020）仅捕捉了梯度对齐信息，忽略了曲率（二阶信息）和随机优化中噪声的影响，需要发展更精确的高阶表征。

Method: 在确定性梯度下降的路径核积分框架基础上，推导二阶泰勒展开形式的插值公式。对于随机梯度下降，进一步引入小批量梯度噪声协方差与预测曲率的耦合项。对于带动量的SGD，分析记忆因子对权重修正的影响。最后利用浓度不等式建立预测的波动界。

Result: 1）二阶插值公式包含曲率加权的附加项；2）SGD引入预测曲率与噪声协方差的耦合项；3）动量SGD保持插值结构但权重受记忆因子修正；4）建立了终端预测围绕期望二阶表征的浓度估计，明确了波动尺度。

Conclusion: 这些结果精细化了对神经网络预测的路径核解释，为理解训练数据影响预测提供了更高阶的理论工具，统一了确定性优化与随机优化的分析框架。

Abstract: Understanding how training data shape neural network predictions is a central problem in modern learning theory. In 2020, Pedro Domingos proposed an interpolation formula valid for every model learned by deterministic gradient descent. It expresses the model's prediction as an integral, along the optimization path, of a data-dependent kernel that aligns the model's gradients at the test and training data. Such a first-order characterization remains valid for models trained with batch-based stochastic optimization. In this paper, we develop second-order forms of these interpolation formulas. We show that the leading path-kernel interpolation is supplemented by a curvature-weighted interpolation term. For stochastic gradient descent, an additional sampling-induced component appears, coupling the curvature of the prediction with the covariance of mini-batch gradient noise. We also extend the representation to stochastic gradient descent with momentum, where the interpolation structure is preserved but with the weights modified by a memory-related factor. Moreover, we establish a concentration estimate for the terminal prediction, identifying the fluctuation scale around the expected second-order representation. Together, these results provide a refinement of the path-kernel interpretation of neural network prediction.

</details>


### [310] [Accelerated Decentralized Stochastic Gradient Descent for Strongly Convex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07496)
*Ming Sun,Kun Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MG-ADSGD算法，通过结合Nesterov型原始-对偶外推与多轮快速八卦平均，在去中心化随机强凸优化中同时实现了加速的√κ和1/√(1-β)依赖，达到了当前最优的通信复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有去中心化随机优化方法无法同时加速条件数κ和网络谱间隙1-β的依赖，存在通信效率瓶颈。

Method: 将八卦轮数与小批量大小耦合，使额外通信轮次同时改善共识精度和降低梯度方差；结合Nesterov型原始-对偶外推与多轮快速八卦平均。

Result: 通信复杂度为Õ(σ²/(μnε)·log(1/ε) + √(κ/(1-β))·log(1/ε))，达到当前最优。

Conclusion: MG-ADSGD首次在去中心化随机强凸优化中同时实现加速的条件数和网络谱间隙依赖，显著降低通信复杂度。

Abstract: Decentralized stochastic optimization is a fundamental paradigm for large-scale learning over networks, where agents communicate only with their neighbors and no central coordinator is required. For strongly convex problems, communication efficiency is mainly determined by the condition number \(κ=L/μ\) and the network spectral gap \(1-β\). Although deterministic decentralized methods can simultaneously achieve accelerated \(\sqrtκ\) and \(1/\sqrt{1-β}\) dependences, no existing stochastic method attains both improvements at once. In this paper, we propose \emph{Multi-Gossip Accelerated DSGD} (MG-ADSGD), a decentralized stochastic algorithm that combines Nesterov-type primal--dual extrapolation with multi-round fast gossip averaging. The key idea is to couple the gossip depth with the mini-batch size so that additional communication rounds simultaneously improve consensus accuracy and reduce gradient variance. We show that MG-ADSGD achieves the communication complexity \[ \widetilde{\mathcal O}\!\left( \frac{σ^2}{μnε}\log\frac{1}ε +
\sqrt{\fracκ{1-β}}\log\frac{1}ε \right), \] where \(ε\) denotes the target accuracy, \(n\) is the number of nodes, and \(σ^2\) is the gradient variance. To the best of our knowledge, this bound yields the best currently available communication complexity for decentralized stochastic strongly convex optimization, up to logarithmic factors that are independent of $ε$.

</details>


### [311] [Sparse Subspace-to-Expert Sharing for Task-Agnostic Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07500)
*Fatema Siddika,Md Anwar Hossen,Tanwi Mallick,Ali Jannesari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SETA提出了一种基于稀疏专家混合的自适应子空间分解方法，通过将知识分离为任务特定专家和共享专家，解决大语言模型持续学习中的可塑性-稳定性困境，在LLaMA-2 7B和Qwen3-4B上实现了优于基线方法的早期任务知识保留和反向迁移性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型持续学习中存在可塑性-稳定性困境：获取新能力会导致对已有知识的灾难性遗忘。现有方法对参数统一处理，未能区分特定任务知识与共享能力。

Method: SETA框架通过自适应稀疏子空间分解，将知识分离为任务特定专家模块（捕获任务特有模式）和共享专家模块（捕获通用特征），采用自适应弹性锚定和路由感知正则化，在权重和路由层面共同保护共享知识，并通过统一门控网络在推理时自动检索正确的专家组合。

Result: 在多个领域特定基准上，SETA在LLaMA-2 7B和Qwen3-4B上取得了与最先进持续学习方法相当或更优的整体性能，尤其在早期任务知识保留和反向迁移方面表现突出。

Conclusion: SETA通过稀疏专家混合架构有效解决了持续学习中的可塑性-稳定性冲突，验证了任务特定与共享知识分离策略在LLM持续学习中的有效性。

Abstract: Continual learning in Large Language Models (LLMs) is hindered by the plasticity-stability dilemma, where acquiring new capabilities often leads to catastrophic forgetting of previous knowledge. Existing methods typically treat parameters uniformly, failing to distinguish between specific task knowledge and shared capabilities. We introduce Mixture of Sparse Experts for Task Agnostic Continual Learning (SETA), a framework that resolves the plasticity-stability conflict through adaptive sparse subspace decomposition into task-specific expert modules. Unlike standard updates, where tasks compete for the same parameters, SETA separates knowledge into unique experts, designed to isolate task-specific patterns, and shared experts, responsible for capturing common features. This structure is maintained through adaptive elastic anchoring and a routing-aware regularization that jointly protect shared knowledge at both the weight and routing levels and enable a unified gating network to automatically retrieve the correct expert combination during inference. Extensive experiments across diverse domain-specific benchmarks demonstrate that SETA achieves competitive or superior overall performance relative to state-of-the-art continual learning baselines, with particularly strong retention of early-task knowledge and improved backward transfer on LLaMA-2 7B and Qwen3-4B.

</details>


### [312] [Characterizing Learning Dynamics under Relative Reparameterization of Singular Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2206.08598)
*Pascal Mattia Esser,Frank Nielsen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种相对重参数化技术，从奇异模型中提取正则子模型，并在高斯混合模型和神经网络上分析梯度下降的收敛率，区分算法与信息几何对收敛行为的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统计模型学习中，奇异模型（参数空间与统计模型空间非一一映射）常因吸引子行为导致收敛速度下降。现有分析多基于参数空间操作，但缺乏通用方法处理此类奇异性。

Method: 提出相对重参数化技术，将参数空间变换为可提取正则子模型的形式；结合二阶方法与Fisher信息矩阵的显式性质，分析梯度下降在两种参数化下的收敛率。

Result: 理论分析表明重参数化可消除因奇异性导致的收敛减速；数值实验验证了高斯混合模型与神经网络中该方法的有效性；区分了算法因素与信息几何因素对收敛行为的不同贡献。

Conclusion: 相对重参数化是一种处理奇异模型的有效通用框架，能够分离算法与几何对学习动力学的贡献，为理解奇异模型的学习行为提供了新视角。

Abstract: A common way to analyze learning of statistical models is to consider operations in the models parameter space, however this becomes challenging when there is no one-to-one mapping between the parameter space and the underlying statistical model space. Such ``singular models'' occur frequently and exhibit a characteristic decrease in convergence speed of learning trajectories due to attractor behaviors. In this work, we consider a relative reparameterization technique of the parameter space, which yields a general method for extracting regular sub-models from singular models. On the example of Gaussian Mixture Models and Neural Networks we theoretically and numerically analyze the convergence rate for Gradient Descent under both parameterizations. Analyzing second-order methods and explicit properties of the Fisher Information Matrix we distinguish between differences in convergence behavior arising from algorithmic and intrinsic information-geometric aspects.

</details>


### [313] [Interpretable factorization of clinical questionnaires to identify latent factors of psychopathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2312.07762)
*Ka Chun Lam,Bridget W Mahony,Armin Raznahan,Francisco Pereira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为ICQF（可解释性约束问卷因子分解）的非负矩阵分解方法，通过正则化技术提升问卷数据中潜在因子的可解释性和稳定性，并提供了优化算法、维度检测方法，在两个独立数据集上验证了其优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统因子分析在处理问卷数据时存在因子不可解释、易受混杂变量影响、缺失数据需要显式插补等局限性。

Method: 提出ICQF方法，即带有正则化的非负矩阵分解，专为问卷数据设计；提供具有理论收敛保证的优化过程，以及自动检测潜在维度的程序。

Result: 在两个独立数据集（Healthy Brain Network和Adolescent Brain Cognitive Development）上，ICQF提升了领域专家定义的可解释性，保留了跨多种障碍的诊断信息，并在小数据集上优于竞争方法。

Conclusion: ICQF的正则化匹配了问卷数据的领域特性，能有效提升因子可解释性和稳定性，是一种优于传统方法的替代方案。

Abstract: Psychiatry research seeks to understand the manifestations of psychopathology in behavior, as measured in questionnaire data, by identifying a small number of latent factors that explain them. While factor analysis is the traditional tool for this purpose, the resulting factors may not be interpretable, and may also be subject to confounding variables. Moreover, missing data are common, and explicit imputation is often required. To overcome these limitations, we introduce interpretability constrained questionnaire factorization (ICQF), a non-negative matrix factorization method with regularization tailored for questionnaire data. Our method aims to promote factor interpretability and solution stability. We provide an optimization procedure with theoretical convergence guarantees, and an automated procedure to detect latent dimensionality accurately. We validate these procedures using realistic synthetic data. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our method in a widely used general-purpose questionnaire, in two independent datasets (the Healthy Brain Network and Adolescent Brain Cognitive Development studies). Specifically, we show that ICQF improves interpretability, as defined by domain experts, while preserving diagnostic information across a range of disorders, and outperforms competing methods for smaller dataset sizes. This suggests that the regularization in our method matches domain characteristics. The python implementation for ICQF is available at https://github.com/jefferykclam/ICQF.

</details>


### [314] [Twin: Tuning Learning Rate and Weight Decay of Deep Homogeneous Classifiers without Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.05532)
*Lorenzo Brigato,Stavroula Mougiakakou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Twin (Tune without Validation)方法，一种无需验证集即可调优学习率和权重衰减的流水线，通过利用齐次分类器的边缘最大化动态和经验标度律，在37个图像分类配置上达到1.28%的平均绝对误差，特别适用于小样本或医学影像等验证数据稀缺的场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统超参数调优需要验证集，但在小数据或医学影像等场景中，验证数据稀缺且获取成本高。作者希望消除对验证集的依赖，避免两步骤调优过程。

Method: 利用齐次网络的边缘最大化动态和经验标度律，建立训练损失与测试损失之间的数学关系。在非可分区域，训练损失单调预测测试损失；在可分区域，参数范数因边缘最大化而成为泛化性能的可靠指标，从而形成分区域的、无需验证的选择规则。

Result: 在37个数据集-架构组合的图像分类任务中，Twin与使用测试准确率选择超参数的Oracle基线相比，平均绝对误差仅为1.28%。

Conclusion: Twin在验证数据稀缺的场景（小样本学习、医学影像）中具有显著优势，提供了一种有效且无需验证集的超参数选择方法。

Abstract: We introduce Tune without Validation (Twin), a simple and effective pipeline for tuning learning rate and weight decay of homogeneous classifiers without validation sets, eliminating the need to hold out data and avoiding the two-step process. Twin leverages the margin-maximization dynamics of homogeneous networks and an empirical scaling law that links training and test losses across hyper-parameter configurations. This mathematical modeling yields a regime-dependent, validation-free selection rule: in the non-separable regime, training loss is monotonic in test loss and therefore predictive of generalization, whereas in the separable regime, the parameters' norm becomes a reliable indicator of generalization due to margin maximization. Across 37 dataset-architecture configurations for image classification, we demonstrate that Twin achieves a mean absolute error of 1.28% compared to an Oracle baseline that selects HPs using test accuracy. We demonstrate Twin's benefits in scenarios where validation data is scarce, such as small-data regimes, or difficult and costly to collect, as in medical imaging. Code available at https://github.com/lorenzobrigato/twin.

</details>


### [315] [pTNAS: Progressive Neural Architecture Search for Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.10318)
*Naili Xing,Shaofeng Cai,Lingze Zeng,Jiaqi Zhu,Peng Lu,Jian Pei,Beng Chin Ooi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出pTNAS，一种针对表格数据的渐进式神经架构搜索方法，通过过滤-精炼双阶段策略（零成本代理pTProxy + 固定预算调度）快速找到高性能架构，相比其他NAS方法速度提升达82.75倍，端到端效率比TabPFN提升4.78倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格学习领域，深度神经网络在效率和精度上具有竞争力，但缺乏数据自适应且预算感知的架构搜索方法；现有表格基础模型推理成本高，与上下文规模呈不良缩放关系。

Method: 提出pTNAS，包含过滤和精炼两阶段：过滤阶段设计pTProxy零成本代理（联合捕获可训练性和表达性）快速筛选大搜索空间；精炼阶段用固定预算调度算法从候选集中准确识别最佳架构；引入预算感知协调器全局优化预算分配。

Result: 达到全局最优架构的速度比其他NAS方法提升82.75倍；取得最佳平均预测排名；端到端效率比TabPFN提升4.78倍。

Conclusion: pTNAS证明了在表格神经网络中结合训练无关与训练评估的分阶段搜索策略在效率和效果上的有效性，为预算受限场景下的表格架构搜索提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Recent advances have shifted the paradigm of tabular learning toward tabular foundation models, yet their accuracy relies on a heavy inference cost that scales poorly with context size. Deep neural networks remain a highly competitive and more efficient modeling paradigm when equipped with well-designed architectures; however, identifying such architectures in a data-adaptive and budget-aware manner remains challenging. We propose pTNAS, the first progressive neural architecture search (NAS) approach tailored for tabular data, which enables fast identification of a viable architecture and continuously improves its search performance as more budget becomes available. pTNAS adopts a filter-and-refine optimization strategy that combines efficient training-free and effective training-based architecture evaluation. In the filtering phase, we introduce pTProxy, a novel zero-cost proxy specifically designed for tabular networks that jointly captures architectural trainability and expressivity, enabling fast filtering of large architecture search spaces. In the refinement phase, pTNAS employs a fixed-budget scheduling algorithm to accurately identify the best-performing architecture from a small set of promising candidates. We further propose a budget-aware coordinator to optimize budget allocation holistically. Experiments show that pTNAS reduces the time to reach the globally best architecture by up to 82.75 X compared with other NAS approaches, achieves the best average predictive rank, and improves end-to-end efficiency by up to 4.78 X compared with TabPFN.

</details>


### [316] [Certified Robustness to Data Poisoning in Gradient-Based Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.05670)
*Philip Sosnin,Mark N. Müller,Maximilian Baader,Calvin Tsay,Matthew Wicker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个无需修改模型或学习算法即可为潜在被篡改数据训练的模型提供可证明鲁棒性保证的框架，通过凸松弛方法过近似所有可能的参数更新集合，从而为梯度下降算法可达到的参数集提供边界，并以此证明模型在无目标/有目标投毒攻击及后门攻击下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习流程依赖大量公开数据，难以保证数据质量，因此模型容易受到投毒和后门攻击。目前尚缺乏能够在数据可能被篡改的情况下对模型行为提供可证明保证的方法。

Method: 利用凸松弛技术，对给定投毒威胁模型下所有可能的参数更新集合进行过近似，从而为任何基于梯度的学习算法可达到的参数集提供边界。基于此参数集，进一步提供模型最坏情况行为（包括模型性能和后门成功率）的边界。

Result: 在能源消耗、医学影像和自动驾驶等多个真实世界数据集上验证了方法的有效性，支持对有界/无界训练输入和标签篡改下的无目标投毒、有目标投毒以及后门攻击进行鲁棒性认证。

Conclusion: 该工作首次实现了对存在数据篡改风险下训练的模型进行可证明的鲁棒性保证，且无需修改模型架构或学习算法，为保障机器学习管道安全性提供了理论基础和实用工具。

Abstract: Modern machine learning pipelines leverage large amounts of public data, making it infeasible to guarantee data quality and leaving models open to poisoning and backdoor attacks. Provably bounding model behavior under such attacks remains an open problem. In this work, we address this challenge by developing the first framework providing provable guarantees on the behavior of models trained with potentially manipulated data without modifying the model or learning algorithm. In particular, our framework certifies robustness against untargeted and targeted poisoning, as well as backdoor attacks, for bounded and unbounded manipulations of the training inputs and labels. Our method leverages convex relaxations to over-approximate the set of all possible parameter updates for a given poisoning threat model, allowing us to bound the set of all reachable parameters for any gradient-based learning algorithm. Given this set of parameters, we provide bounds on worst-case behavior, including model performance and backdoor success rate. We demonstrate our approach on multiple real-world datasets from applications including energy consumption, medical imaging, and autonomous driving.

</details>


### [317] [Conformal Disentanglement and Latent-Space Curation: A Neural Framework for Perspective Synthesis, Differentiation and Targeted Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.15344)
*George A. Kevrekidis,Eleni D. Koronaki,Dimitris G. Giovanis,Yannis G. Kevrekidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种神经自编码器框架，从多传感器数据中分离共享和传感器特定的潜在变量，并基于解耦表示实现条件性数据生成与跨模态推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多传感器观测中，共享信息（反映底层系统）与传感器特定成分（来自测量过程或环境效应）相互混杂，而传感器独立的观测通常不可得，因此需要一种方法有效分离这些贡献。

Method: 构建神经自编码器，通过结构约束和基于正交性的正则化强制潜在变量间的几何独立性，实现共享与传感器特定潜在变量的解耦；随后在解耦的潜在子空间上调整生成模型，结合解码器条件性地合成新样本。

Result: 在多个计算示例上展示了有效的解耦效果、目标导向的数据生成以及异质传感设置中的模态填补能力。

Conclusion: 该框架能有效实现多传感器数据的解耦表示学习，支持具有指定共享或传感器特定特征的一致数据生成，并能通过一致采样实现跨传感器推断。

Abstract: Many scientific and engineering problems involve observing a common phenomenon through multiple heterogeneous sensors or measurement modalities. Such observations typically contain both information shared across sensors, reflecting the underlying system, and sensor-specific or extraneous components arising from measurement processes or environmental effects. Disentangling these contributions is essential when sensor-independent observations are unavailable. We propose a neural autoencoder framework that explicitly separates shared and sensor-specific latent variables from multi-sensor data. The architecture enforces geometric independence between latent components through structural constraints and orthogonality-based regularization, yielding interpretable and disentangled representations. Building on this representation, we then introduce a latent-space generative methodology in which generative models are tuned/"restricted" on selected disentangled latent subspaces; we then constructively combine disentangled observed latent variables to conditionally synthesize new samples via trained decoders. This enables consistent data generation with prescribed shared (or sensor-specific) characteristics. It also supports cross-sensor inference by consistently sampling distributions over plausible measurements in unobserved modalities. We demonstrate the approach on several computational examples, showing effective disentanglement, targeted data generation, and modality imputation in heterogeneous sensing settings.

</details>


### [318] [Modeling AdaGrad, RMSProp, and Adam with Integro-Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.09734)
*Carlos Heredia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaGrad、RMSProp和Adam优化算法的连续时间形式，将其建模为一阶积分-微分方程，并通过数值模拟、稳定性和收敛性分析证明这些连续模型能够准确逼近原始离散算法，为自适应优化方法提供了新的理论视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自适应优化算法（如AdaGrad、RMSProp、Adam）通常以离散形式定义，缺乏连续时间的理论框架。作者希望通过建立连续时间模型，从微分方程的角度更深入地理解这些算法的动力学行为。

Method: 将三种自适应优化算法重新表述为一阶积分-微分方程（integro-differential equations），通过数值模拟对连续模型进行求解，并与原始离散算法进行对比验证。

Result: 连续时间模型的行为与离散实现之间表现出高度一致性，验证了所提连续形式作为原始算法精确近似的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解自适应优化方法提供了新的连续时间视角，有助于后续的理论分析和算法改进。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a continuous-time formulation for the AdaGrad, RMSProp, and Adam optimization algorithms by modeling them as first-order integro-differential equations. We perform numerical simulations of these equations, along with stability and convergence analyses, to demonstrate their validity as accurate approximations of the original algorithms. Our results indicate a strong agreement between the behavior of the continuous-time models and the discrete implementations, thus providing a new perspective on the theoretical understanding of adaptive optimization methods.

</details>


### [319] [The Utility and Complexity of in- and out-of-Distribution Machine Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.09119)
*Youssef Allouah,Joshua Kazdan,Rachid Guerraoui,Sanmi Koyejo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了近似机器遗忘在效用、时间和空间复杂度上的基本权衡，提供了类似差分隐私的严格认证。对于分布内遗忘数据，简单的输出扰动经验风险最小化即可达到最优权衡；对于分布外遗忘数据，遗忘时间复杂度可能超过重新训练，为此提出了一种鲁棒带噪梯度下降变体，在不牺牲效用的前提下摊销遗忘时间复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器遗忘对于解决隐私问题和知识缺口日益重要，但现有方法常缺乏形式化保证，需要建立严格的理论基础。

Method: 1) 对近似遗忘进行严格的效用-时间-空间复杂度权衡分析；2) 对分布内数据使用输出扰动的经验风险最小化；3) 针对分布外数据提出鲁棒带噪梯度下降变体。

Result: 分布内数据上，输出扰动实现了紧的遗忘-效用-复杂度权衡；分布外数据上，单样本遗忘时间复杂度可能超过重新训练，而提出的梯度下降方法可摊销该复杂度。

Conclusion: 不同数据分布需要不同的遗忘策略：分布内数据可用简单方法实现最优，分布外数据则需要更复杂的梯度下降变体来平衡效用与遗忘效率。

Abstract: Machine unlearning, the process of selectively removing data from trained models, is increasingly crucial for addressing privacy concerns and knowledge gaps post-deployment. Despite this importance, existing approaches are often heuristic and lack formal guarantees. In this paper, we analyze the fundamental utility, time, and space complexity trade-offs of approximate unlearning, providing rigorous certification analogous to differential privacy. For in-distribution forget data -- data similar to the retain set -- we show that a surprisingly simple and general procedure, empirical risk minimization with output perturbation, achieves tight unlearning-utility-complexity trade-offs, addressing a previous theoretical gap on the separation from unlearning "for free" via differential privacy, which inherently facilitates the removal of such data. However, such techniques fail with out-of-distribution forget data -- data significantly different from the retain set -- where unlearning time complexity can exceed that of retraining, even for a single sample. To address this, we propose a new robust and noisy gradient descent variant that provably amortizes unlearning time complexity without compromising utility.

</details>


### [320] [Should You Use Your Large Language Model to Explore or Exploit?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.00225)
*Keegan Harris,Aleksandrs Slivkins*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了当前大语言模型在探索-利用权衡任务中的表现，发现推理模型在利用任务上最有潜力但成本过高，非推理模型通过工具使用和上下文摘要可在中等难度任务上提升性能，但整体仍不如简单线性回归；而在探索方面，LLM能有效处理具有语义信息的大动作空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往工作大多研究LLM解决综合性的探索-利用任务，缺乏对探索和利用能力的隔离分析。本文希望系统地、分门别类地评估LLM在探索和利用两个子任务上的独立表现。

Method: 在多种（上下文）赌博机任务中，将探索和利用任务分离，分别评估LLM的表现。针对推理模型成本过高的问题，研究了非推理模型使用工具和上下文摘要等缓解策略的效果。

Result: 推理模型在利用任务上表现最好但成本高、速度慢；非推理模型借助工具和上下文摘要可在中等难度任务上显著提升性能；但即使如此，所有LLM在利用任务上仍不如简单线性回归。在探索方面，LLM在具有内在语义的大动作空间上表现良好。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在利用任务上尚无法替代传统方法（如线性回归），但在探索具有语义信息的大动作空间时具有独特优势，可作为候选动作推荐工具。

Abstract: We evaluate the ability of the current generation of large language models (LLMs) to help a decision-making agent facing an exploration-exploitation tradeoff. While previous work has largely study the ability of LLMs to solve combined exploration-exploitation tasks, we take a more systematic approach and use LLMs to explore and exploit in silos in various (contextual) bandit tasks. We find that reasoning models show the most promise for solving exploitation tasks, although they are still too expensive or too slow to be used in many practical settings. Motivated by this, we study tool use and in-context summarization using non-reasoning models. We find that these mitigations may be used to substantially improve performance on medium-difficulty tasks, however even then, all LLMs we study perform worse than a simple linear regression, even in non-linear settings. On the other hand, we find that LLMs do help at exploring large action spaces with inherent semantics, by suggesting suitable candidates to explore.

</details>


### [321] [PolarQuant: Leveraging Polar Transformation for Efficient Key Cache Quantization and Decoding Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.00527)
*Songhao Wu,Ang Lv,Xiao Feng,Yufei Zhang,Xun Zhang,Guojun Yin,Wei Lin,Rui Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PolarQuant提出了一种针对LLM中KV缓存的高效量化方法，通过将key向量分解为二维子向量并编码为极坐标（半径和角度），解决了异常值导致的量化困难，同时将查询-键内积转化为查表操作以加速解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KV缓存是LLM内存使用的关键瓶颈，现有量化方法因key向量中存在异常值而难以有效压缩，导致过高开销。

Method: 将key向量划分为二维子向量组，利用旋转位置嵌入后这些二维向量呈现的极坐标规律结构（半径和角度平滑分布），分别量化半径和极角，而非直接量化原始key向量。

Result: 在KV缓存量化方面实现了优越的效率，通过将查询-键内积转化为查表操作加速了解码过程，同时保持了与全精度模型相当的下游性能。

Conclusion: PolarQuant有效解决了key向量量化中的异常值挑战，为LLM的KV缓存压缩和推理加速提供了一种高效且性能无损的方案。

Abstract: The KV cache in large language models is a dominant factor in memory usage, limiting their broader applicability. Quantizing the cache to lower bit widths is an effective way to reduce computational costs; however, previous methods struggle with quantizing key vectors due to outliers, resulting in excessive overhead. We propose a novel quantization approach called PolarQuant, which efficiently addresses the outlier challenge. We observe that outliers typically appear in only one of two dimensions, which are rotated together by a specific angle when rotary position embeddings are applied. When represented as two-dimensional vectors, these dimensions exhibit well-structured patterns, with radii and angles smoothly distributed in polar coordinates. This alleviates the challenge of outliers on per-channel quantization, making them well-suited for quantization. Thus, PolarQuant divides key vectors into groups of two-dimensional sub-vectors, encoding them as the corresponding quantized radius and the polar angle, rather than quantizing original key vectors directly. PolarQuant achieves the superior efficiency in KV cache quantization and accelerates the decoding process by turning the query-key inner product into a table lookup, all while maintaining the downstream performance of full-precision models.

</details>


### [322] [Chunking the Critic: A Transformer-based Soft Actor-Critic with N-Step Returns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.03660)
*Dong Tian,Onur Celik,Gerhard Neumann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种序列条件化的SAC评论家方法，使用轻量级Transformer建模轨迹上下文并聚合N步目标，无需重要性采样，在长轨迹和稀疏奖励任务上显著优于标准SAC和强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么孤立地评分状态-动作对，要么依赖actor侧的动作分块来处理长视界问题，缺乏对评论家自身的增强。本文旨在通过序列建模增强评论家，使其能够捕捉长视界和稀疏奖励问题中的关键时间结构。

Method: 使用轻量级（2层、128-256隐藏单元）Transformer对短轨迹片段进行条件化建模，并聚合N步目标来训练评论家。同时冻结评论家参数若干步以兼容CrossQ的核心思想，无需目标网络即可稳定训练，且最大更新-数据比(UTD)为1。

Result: 在局部运动基准上，该方法一致性地优于标准SAC和强离策略基线，尤其在长轨迹控制任务上取得了显著提升。

Conclusion: 序列建模和N步自举法在评论家侧对于长视界强化学习具有重要价值。

Abstract: We introduce a sequence-conditioned critic for Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) that models trajectory context with a lightweight Transformer and trains on aggregated $N$-step targets. Unlike prior approaches that (i) score state-action pairs in isolation or (ii) rely on actor-side action chunking to handle long horizons, our method strengthens the critic itself by conditioning on short trajectory segments and integrating multi-step returns -- without importance sampling (IS). The resulting sequence-aware value estimates capture the critical temporal structure for extended-horizon and sparse-reward problems. On local-motion benchmarks, we further show that freezing critic parameters for several steps makes our update compatible with CrossQ's core idea, enabling stable training \emph{without} a target network. Despite its simplicity -- a 2-layer Transformer with 128-256 hidden units and a maximum update-to-data ratio (UTD) of $1$ -- the approach consistently outperforms standard SAC and strong off-policy baselines, with particularly large gains on long-trajectory control. These results highlight the value of sequence modeling and $N$-step bootstrapping on the critic side for long-horizon reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [323] [Multi-Objective Preference Optimization: Improving Human Alignment of Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.10892)
*Akhil Agnihotri,Rahul Jain,Deepak Ramachandran,Zheng Wen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出多目标偏好优化框架MOPO，通过约束KL散度在主要目标和次要目标间平衡，无需点式奖励，在合成基准和人类偏好数据上均实现帕累托最优策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLHF和偏好优化方法假设单一目标，而人类存在多个相互冲突的目标（如有用性与无害性），缺乏天然的标量化方法，需研究多目标偏好对齐问题。

Method: 提出MOPO框架：基于约束KL正则化，最大化主要目标同时通过可调安全阈值强制执行次要目标的下界约束；直接操作成对偏好数据，无需点式奖励；推导出简洁的闭式迭代更新公式。

Result: 在合成基准上恢复帕累托最优策略；在人类偏好数据上微调得到数十亿参数模型，获得更高奖励且帕累托支配基线，优化过程稳定鲁棒。

Conclusion: MOPO有效解决了多目标偏好对齐问题，通过约束优化实现帕累托最优，且在大规模模型上表现优于现有方法。

Abstract: Post-training LLMs with RLHF and preference optimization methods (e.g., DPO, IPO) has greatly improved alignment, yet these approaches assume a single objective. In reality, humans express multiple, often conflicting objectives, such as helpfulness and harmlessness, with no natural scalarization. We study the multi-objective preference alignment problem, where a policy must balance several objectives simultaneously. We propose Multi-Objective Preference Optimization (MOPO), a constrained KL-regularized framework that maximizes a primary objective while enforcing lower bounds on secondary objectives via tunable safety thresholds. MOPO operates directly on pairwise preferences without point-wise rewards, and admits simple closed-form iterative updates. Empirically, MOPO recovers Pareto-optimal policies on synthetic benchmarks and, when fine-tuned on human-preference data, yields multi-billion parameter models that achieve higher rewards and Pareto-dominate baselines, with stable and robust optimization dynamics.

</details>


### [324] [Towards Efficient and Exact Forgetting Services in Pre-Trained-Model-based Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.12239)
*Yajiang Huang,Jianheng Tang,Kejia Fan,Huiping Zhuang,Anfeng Liu,Tian Wang,Yunhuai Liu,Mianxiong Dong,Houbing Herbert Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为ACU（Analytic Continual Unlearning）的梯度无关方法，用于解决预训练模型在持续学习场景中面临的持续遗忘问题，该方法通过递归推导最小二乘解析解，实现了高效、精确的样本级和类别级遗忘，同时保护历史数据隐私。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在持续学习中使用预训练模型已成为主流，但在移动群智感知等服务构建中，不仅需要非遗忘学习，还需要主动遗忘特定知识以保护隐私。现有的遗忘方法仅适用于单次联合遗忘，无法应对持续学习场景中顺序出现的遗忘请求，存在违反历史数据隐私和易被频繁请求淹没或降级的问题。

Method: 提出ACU（Analytic Continual Unlearning）方法，一种梯度无关的持续遗忘方法。针对每个遗忘请求，通过最小二乘法递归推导解析解（闭式解），以可解释的方式实现精确遗忘，兼容样本级和类别级遗忘请求，并能保护历史数据隐私。

Result: 理论分析和实验验证表明，ACU在遗忘有效性、模型保真度和系统效率方面均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: ACU为基于预训练模型的持续学习场景中的持续遗忘问题提供了一种高效、精确且可解释的解决方案，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: In Continual Learning (CL), using a Pre-Trained Model (PTM) as the feature extractor has become a popular practice. Accompanied by analytic classifiers, the PTM-based methods have achieved state-of-the-art performance in CL, in pursuit of the non-forgetting goal. Meanwhile, actively forgetting specific knowledge acquired during the CL phase is also essential in most service construction paradigms, for example, Mobile Crowd Sensing (MCS), where mobile edge nodes continuously collect sensory data and demand not only non-forgetting adaptation but also specific knowledge forgetting for privacy preservation. Thus, a unique problem, called Continual Unlearning (CU), arises when the forgetting requests show sequentially in CL. However, existing unlearning methods focus on single-shot joint forgetting and prove highly inadequate when applied to CU, including (1) violating the historical data privacy in CL and (2) vulnerably being overwhelmed or degraded with adversarially frequent requests. To handle the challenges of CU, we propose a gradient-free approach, called Analytic Continual Unlearning (ACU), for efficient and exact forgetting with historical data privacy preservation in PTM-based CL. In response to each unlearning request, our ACU recursively derives the analytical (i.e., closed-form) solutions via least squares in an interpretable manner. By meticulous design, our ACU is compatible with both sample-level and class-level unlearning requests. The theoretical and experimental evaluations validate our ACU's superiority in unlearning effectiveness, model fidelity, and system efficiency.

</details>


### [325] [Learnable Kernel Density Estimation for Graphs and Its Application to Graph-Level Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.21285)
*Xudong Wang,Ziheng Sun,Chris Ding,Jicong Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LGKDE框架，利用图神经网络和最大均值差异学习图核密度估计，通过扰动节点特征和图谱来优化密度估计，并在理论上证明了一致性和收敛性，实验表明在图异常检测任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图核密度估计使用手工设计的固定核函数，无法有效捕获图的结构模式和语义变化，导致性能不佳。

Method: 1) 利用GNN将每个图表示为离散分布；2) 使用最大均值差异(MMD)学习图度量用于多尺度KDE；3) 通过对节点特征和图谱进行扰动来构建负样本；4) 最大化密度相对于扰动样本的密度来学习所有参数。

Result: 在合成图分布上能有效恢复真实密度，在图异常检测的多个基准数据集上优于最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: LGKDE通过可学习的图核密度估计方法，在理论上有可靠保证，在实践中达到了优异的图密度估计和异常检测性能。

Abstract: This work proposes a framework LGKDE that learns kernel density estimation for graphs. The key challenge in graph density estimation lies in effectively capturing both structural patterns and semantic variations while maintaining theoretical guarantees. Combining graph kernels and kernel density estimation (KDE) is a standard approach to graph density estimation, but has unsatisfactory performance due to the handcrafted and fixed features of kernels. Our method LGKDE leverages graph neural networks to represent each graph as a discrete distribution and utilizes maximum mean discrepancy to learn the graph metric for multi-scale KDE, where all parameters are learned by maximizing the density of graphs relative to the density of their well-designed perturbed counterparts. The perturbations are conducted on both node features and graph spectra, which helps better characterize the boundary of normal density regions. Theoretically, we establish consistency and convergence guarantees for LGKDE, including bounds on the mean integrated squared error, robustness, and generalization. We validate LGKDE by demonstrating its effectiveness in recovering the underlying density of synthetic graph distributions and applying it to graph anomaly detection across diverse benchmark datasets. Extensive empirical evaluation shows that LGKDE demonstrates superior performance compared to state-of-the-art baselines on most benchmark datasets.

</details>


### [326] [Conflicting Biases at the Edge of Stability: Norm versus Sharpness Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.21423)
*Maria Matveev,Vit Fojtik,Hung-Hsu Chou,Gitta Kutyniok,Johannes Maly*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文论证了过参数化网络的泛化性能不能仅用单一隐式偏置解释，而是需要理解学习率如何在不同隐式正则化形式（如低范数和低锐度）之间进行动态权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究分别关注小学习率下的范数最小化和Edge-of-Stability机制下的低锐度，但缺乏对两者交互作用的综合理解，导致对梯度下降泛化性能的解释不完整。

Method: 通过实验证明学习率在训练模型的低参数范数和低锐度之间进行插值；在线性对角网络回归任务上理论证明单一隐式偏置无法最小化泛化误差。

Result: 学习率调控着范数与锐度之间的权衡；单一隐式偏置不足以解释好的泛化性能。

Conclusion: 需要更广泛的隐式正则化视角，捕捉非平凡学习率所诱导的范数与锐度之间的动态权衡。

Abstract: The remarkable generalization properties of overparameterized networks are often attributed to implicit biases, such as norm minimization at small learning rates and low sharpness in the Edge-of-Stability regime. In this work, we argue that a comprehensive understanding of the generalization performance of gradient descent requires analyzing the interaction between these various forms of implicit regularization. We empirically demonstrate that the learning rate interpolates between low parameter norm and low sharpness of the trained model. We furthermore prove that neither implicit bias alone minimizes the generalization error for diagonal linear networks trained on a simple regression task. These findings demonstrate that focusing on a single implicit bias is insufficient to explain good generalization, and they motivate a broader view of implicit regularization that captures the dynamic trade-off between norm and sharpness induced by non-negligible learning rates.

</details>


### [327] [DOPPLER: Dual-Policy Learning for Device Assignment in Asynchronous Dataflow Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.23131)
*Xinyu Yao,Daniel Bourgeois,Abhinav Jain,Yuxin Tang,Jiawen Yao,Zhimin Ding,Arlei Silva,Chris Jermaine*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Doppler是一个三阶段框架，通过训练双策略网络（操作选择策略SEL和设备放置策略PLC）来优化数据流图中的操作分配，以减少工作 conserving系统中的执行时间，在复杂机器学习工作负载上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的方法存在三个关键局限：依赖TensorFlow等批量同步系统导致设备利用不足；缺乏对底层调度机制的感知；过度依赖强化学习而忽视专家设计的有效启发式结构。因此需要一种更高效的操作分配方法。

Method: 提出Doppler框架，包含三个阶段：1) 训练SEL策略用于选择操作；2) 训练PLC策略用于将选中的操作放置到设备上；3) 三阶段训练流程形成双策略网络。

Result: Doppler在所有任务上优于所有基线方法，减少了系统执行时间，同时通过减少每个回合的训练时间展示了采样效率。

Conclusion: Doppler通过双策略网络设计有效解决了现有方法的三大局限，在性能和采样效率上均取得显著提升。

Abstract: We study the problem of assigning operations in a dataflow graph to devices to minimize execution time in a work-conserving system, with emphasis on complex machine learning workloads. Prior learning-based methods often struggle due to three key limitations: (1) reliance on bulk-synchronous systems like TensorFlow, which under-utilize devices due to barrier synchronization; (2) lack of awareness of the scheduling mechanism of underlying systems when designing learning-based methods; and (3) exclusive dependence on reinforcement learning, ignoring the structure of effective heuristics designed by experts. In this paper, we propose Doppler, a three-stage framework for training dual-policy networks consisting of 1) a $\mathsf{SEL}$ policy for selecting operations and 2) a $\mathsf{PLC}$ policy for placing chosen operations on devices. Our experiments show that Doppler outperforms all baseline methods across tasks by reducing system execution time and additionally demonstrates sampling efficiency by reducing per-episode training time.

</details>


### [328] [Bounded-Abstention Pairwise Learning to Rank](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.23437)
*Antonio Ferrara,Andrea Pugnana,Francesco Bonchi,Salvatore Ruggieri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于条件风险阈值的学习排序任务中的弃权机制，允许排序系统在不确定时交由人类专家决策，并提供了理论最优策略、模型无关的插件算法及实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 排序系统在医疗、教育、就业等高风险领域影响重大，现有安全机制中的弃权方法主要针对分类任务，尚未在成对学习排序任务中得到充分探索。

Method: 提出了一个模型无关的插件式算法，通过设定风险评估阈值来决定是否弃权；核心是对排序器的条件风险进行阈值化处理，当估计风险超过预设阈值时系统放弃决策。

Result: 在多数据集上的全面实证评估表明，所提出的弃权排序方法有效，能在不确定性较高时成功将决策转移给人类专家。

Conclusion: 本文首次将弃权机制引入成对学习排序任务，给出了理论最优策略，并验证了该方法在实际场景中的有效性，为排序系统的安全决策提供了新的解决路径。

Abstract: Ranking systems influence decision-making in high-stakes domains like health, education, and employment, where they can have substantial economic and social impacts. This makes the integration of safety mechanisms essential. One such mechanism is abstention, which enables algorithmic decision-making systems to defer uncertain or low-confidence decisions to human experts. While abstention has been predominantly explored in the context of classification tasks, its application to other machine learning paradigms remains underexplored. In this paper, we introduce a novel method for abstention in pairwise learning-to-rank tasks. Our approach is based on thresholding the ranker's conditional risk: the system abstains from making a decision when the estimated risk exceeds a predefined threshold. Our contributions are threefold: a theoretical characterization of the optimal abstention strategy, a model-agnostic, plug-in algorithm for constructing abstaining ranking models, and a comprehensive empirical evaluation across multiple datasets, demonstrating the effectiveness of our approach.

</details>


### [329] [Trace Reconstruction with Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.12927)
*Franziska Weindel,Michael Girsch,Reinhard Heckel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TReconLM，一种仅解码器Transformer模型，将轨迹重建问题建模为下一token预测任务，在合成和真实DNA数据上均显著优于现有算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DNA数据存储中的错误校正需要高效的轨迹重建算法，现有方法（包括深度学习）恢复准确率有限，亟需更优的解决方案。

Method: 采用仅解码器Transformer架构，将轨迹重建视为下一token预测任务；先在简单错误模型生成的合成数据上预训练，再在真实数据上微调以适应技术特定的错误模式。

Result: 在无错误序列恢复比例上显著超越包括先前深度学习方法在内的最先进轨迹重建算法。

Conclusion: TReconLM通过预训练+微调范式有效解决了DNA存储中的轨迹重建问题，验证了Transformer在该任务上的优越性。

Abstract: The general trace reconstruction problem seeks to recover an original sequence from its noisy copies independently corrupted by insertions, deletions, and substitutions. This problem arises in applications such as DNA data storage, a promising storage medium due to its high information density and longevity. However, errors introduced during DNA synthesis, storage, and sequencing require correction through algorithms and codes, with trace reconstruction often used as part of data retrieval. In this work, we propose TReconLM, a decoder-only transformer that solves trace reconstruction as a next-token prediction task. TReconLM outperforms state-of-the-art trace reconstruction algorithms, including prior deep-learning approaches, recovering a substantially higher fraction of sequences without error. We pretrain on synthetic data generated from a simple error model and fine-tune on real-world data to adapt to technology-specific error patterns. Code is available at https://github.com/MLI-lab/TReconLM.

</details>


### [330] [Model Recycling Framework for Multi-Source Data-Free Supervised Transfer Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.02039)
*Sijia Wang,Ricardo Henao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种模型回收框架，用于在无源数据迁移学习中高效训练模型，能够在白盒和黑盒设置下识别并重用相关源模型的子集，支持多源无监督迁移学习，为模型即服务（MaaS）提供商构建高效预训练模型库提供了可能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据隐私问题以及获取源数据进行模型训练的困难日益增加，导致需要源数据无关的迁移学习。现有迁移学习方法通常依赖源数据，限制了其在源数据不可用场景中的直接应用。此外，如何在没有源数据信息的情况下高效选择可迁移模型，以及如何在无法完全访问源模型的情况下进行迁移，也是实际挑战。

Method: 提出一个模型回收框架，该框架在白盒和黑盒两种设置下，通过识别相关源模型的子集进行重用，实现参数高效的模型训练。

Result: 该框架使得MaaS提供商能够构建高效的预训练模型库，从而实现多源无监督监督迁移学习。

Conclusion: 模型回收框架有效解决了无源数据迁移学习中的关键问题，为数据不可用场景下的迁移学习提供了可行方案，并推动了MaaS生态的发展。
