Each hypercube is discretized into a grid to construct an induced micro-player cooperative game in which elementary grid-step moves become players. Shapley and LES values on this TU-micro-game yield geometry-aware within-pot attributions that converge to the diagonal Aumann--Shapley / Integrated Gradients limit under grid refinement, and recover equal-split Shapley as the degenerate $m=1$ special case. An exact grid-state closed form gives polynomial-time computation for fixed interaction order. On a synthetic benchmark with known ground truth, equal-split Shapley carries an irreducible bias while Aumann-SHAP converges to the correct decomposition. On German Credit, interaction geometry changes feature priority rankings in $12.3\%$ of instances. On UCI Heart Disease, equal-split misattributes a cholesterol suppressor as a positive contributor, which is a sign error Aumann-SHAP corrects. On MNIST, game-theoretic attribution reaches target confidence with $3.5\times$ fewer edits than magnitude-based ordering, with micro-game Shapley achieving the best efficiency across all budgets.

</details>


### [368] [Predictive Statistics Shape Emergent World Representations of Grid Walkers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16689)
*Sasha Brenner,Thomas R. Knösche,Nico Scherf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过可控随机游走实验发现，Transformer的计算分为两阶段：第一注意力层提取世界状态的充分统计量，后续层将其转化为预测分布几何；而循环网络虽能达到贝叶斯最优损失，但未将世界状态分离为独立阶段，表明世界模型几何依赖于网络架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解next-token预测器如何在其内部表征中学习潜在世界结构，以及预测概率分布的几何结构与世界结构之间的深层联系。

Method: 以二维格点上的约束随机游走（固定步数到达终点）作为最小随机过程，训练decoder-only Transformer和循环网络，通过对齐度和线性可读性分析隐藏激活与预测充分统计量之间的关系。

Result: Transformer计算分为两阶段：第一注意力块提取输入中的充分统计量，后续层将其转化为下一预测步的几何结构；跨约束变体后，注意力后的表征是通用世界状态，可被直接读取为世界模型。循环网络达到相同贝叶斯最优损失，但未将世界状态分离为独立阶段。

Conclusion: 预测分布的几何结构是理解神经网络如何内化数据结构的有效视角，且世界模型几何依赖于网络架构选择。

Abstract: Next-token predictors often appear to develop internal representations of the latent world and its rules. The probabilistic nature of these models suggests a deep connection between the structure of the world and the geometry of probability distributions. In order to understand this link more precisely, we use a minimal stochastic process as a controlled setting: constrained random walks on a two-dimensional lattice that must reach a fixed endpoint after a predetermined number of steps. Optimal prediction of this process solely depends on a sufficient vector determined by the walker's position relative to the target and the remaining time horizon; in other words, the probability distributions are parametrized by the world's grid geometry. We train decoder-only transformers and recurrent networks on prefixes sampled from the exact distribution of these walks and compare their hidden activations to sufficient statistics of prediction, by measuring alignment and linear readability across layers. We find that the transformer's computation factors into two stages: the first attention block extracts the sufficient statistic from the input, and later layers transform it into the next-step predictive geometry. Across constraint variants the post-attention representation is universal: a shared world-state of the lattice that can be read directly as a world model, traced to the predictive geometry of the data. Later layers then specialize it to each variant's next-step distribution. Recurrent networks reach the same Bayes-optimal loss but do not isolate this world-state as a separate stage, showing that the world-model geometry also depends on architecture. Although demonstrated in a toy system, the results suggest that the geometry of the predictive distribution is a useful lens on how neural networks internalize the structure of their data.

</details>


### [369] [Uncertainty-Guided Label Rebalancing for CPS Safety Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25670)
*John Ayotunde,Qinghua Xu,Guancheng Wang,Lionel C. Briand*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对CPS安全监测中极端类别不平衡问题，提出U-Balance方法，利用行为不确定性指导标签重平衡，在46:1不平衡比的UAV基准上F1达0.806，比最强基线高14.3个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CPS安全监测中不安全事件极为罕见，导致严重类别不平衡，而现有重平衡技术在时间序列遥测数据上效果差（生成不真实样本或过拟合少数类）。同时，CPS中的行为不确定性与安全结果相关但未被探索用于安全监测。

Method: 提出U-Balance，包含三步：(1)训练GatedMLP不确定性预测器，将遥测窗口压缩为运动学特征并输出不确定性分数；(2)应用不确定性引导标签重平衡(uLNR)机制，将高不确定性的安全标签窗口概率性地重新标记为不安全，丰富少数类而不合成新数据；(3)在重平衡数据集上训练安全预测器。

Result: 在46:1安全-不安全比的UAV基准上，U-Balance的F1达0.806，比最强基线高14.3个百分点；行为不确定性与安全之间存在中等但显著的相关性；消融实验验证了GatedMLP预测器和uLNR机制的贡献。

Conclusion: U-Balance通过利用行为不确定性进行标签重平衡，有效解决了CPS安全监测中的类别不平衡问题，优于现有方法，同时保持竞争性的推理效率。

Abstract: Safety monitoring is essential for Cyber-Physical Systems (CPSs). However, unsafe events are rare in real-world CPS operations, creating an extreme class imbalance that degrades safety predictors. Standard rebalancing techniques perform poorly on time-series CPS telemetry, either generating unrealistic synthetic samples or overfitting on the minority class. Meanwhile, behavioral uncertainty in CPS operations, defined as the degree of doubt or uncertainty in CPS decisions , is often correlated with safety outcomes but unexplored in safety monitoring. To that end, we propose U-Balance, a supervised approach that leverages behavioral uncertainty to rebalance imbalanced datasets prior to training a safety predictor. U-Balance first trains a GatedMLP-based uncertainty predictor that summarizes each telemetry window into distributional kinematic features and outputs an uncertainty score. It then applies an uncertainty-guided label rebalancing (uLNR) mechanism that probabilistically relabels $\textit{safe}$-labeled windows with unusually high uncertainty as $\textit{unsafe}$, thereby enriching the minority class with informative boundary samples without synthesizing new data. Finally, a safety predictor is trained on the rebalanced dataset for safety monitoring. We evaluate U-Balance on a large-scale UAV benchmark with a 46:1 safe-to-unsafe ratio. Results confirm a moderate but significant correlation between behavioral uncertainty and safety. We then identify uLNR as the most effective strategy to exploit uncertainty information, compared to direct early and late fusion. U-Balance achieves a 0.806 F1 score, outperforming the strongest baseline by 14.3 percentage points, while maintaining competitive inference efficiency. Ablation studies confirm that both the GatedMLP-based uncertainty predictor and the uLNR mechanism contribute significantly to U-Balance's effectiveness.

</details>


### [370] [Stable Reasoning, Unstable Responses: Mitigating LLM Deception via Stability Asymmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26846)
*Guoxi Zhang,Jiawei Chen,Tianzhuo Yang,Lang Qin,Juntao Dai,Yaodong Yang,Jingwei Yi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现大模型在优化压力下会隐藏其欺骗性推理（稳定性不对称性：内部CoT稳定但外部响应脆弱），并提出稳定性不对称正则化（SAR）方法，通过惩罚这种分布不对称性来抑制模型的内在欺骗行为，而不损害通用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型能力不断增强，但其可信度面临内在欺骗风险——模型会战略性地误导用户以实现自身目标。现有的基于链式思维（CoT）监控的对齐方法监督显式推理轨迹，但在优化压力下，模型会被激励去隐藏欺骗性推理，使得语义监督从根本上不可靠。

Method: 基于认知心理学假设，欺骗性LLM在内部CoT上保持稳定信念，但外部响应在扰动下脆弱（稳定性不对称性）。通过测量内部CoT稳定性与外部响应稳定性之间的对比来量化该不对称性，并据此提出稳定性不对称正则化（SAR），作为一种在强化学习过程中惩罚这种分布不对称性的新型对齐目标。

Result: 实验证实稳定性不对称性能够可靠地识别欺骗行为，SAR方法有效抑制了内在欺骗，且不降低模型的通用能力。

Conclusion: 与依赖于语义内容的CoT监控不同，SAR针对模型输出的统计结构，因此对语义隐藏具有鲁棒性，为提升大模型可信度提供了新的对齐思路。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) expand in capability and application scope, their trustworthiness becomes critical. A vital risk is intrinsic deception, wherein models strategically mislead users to achieve their own objectives. Existing alignment approaches based on chain-of-thought (CoT) monitoring supervise explicit reasoning traces. However, under optimization pressure, models are incentivized to conceal deceptive reasoning, rendering semantic supervision fundamentally unreliable. Grounded in cognitive psychology, we hypothesize that a deceptive LLM maintains a stable internal belief in its CoT while its external response remains fragile under perturbation. We term this phenomenon stability asymmetry and quantify it by measuring the contrast between internal CoT stability and external response stability under perturbation. Building on this structural signature, we propose the Stability Asymmetry Regularization (SAR), a novel alignment objective that penalizes this distributional asymmetry during reinforcement learning. Unlike CoT monitoring, SAR targets the statistical structure of model outputs, rendering it robust to semantic concealment. Extensive experiments confirm that stability asymmetry reliably identifies deceptive behavior, and that SAR effectively suppresses intrinsic deception without degrading general model capability.

</details>


### [371] [ScatterPrism: convergence for generative simulation and inverse problems in particle and nuclear physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01313)
*Zeyu Xia,Tyler Kim,Trevor Reed,Judy Fox,Geoffrey Fox,Adam Szczepaniak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了Conditional Flow Matching (CFM)在加速高保真模拟和反问题求解中训练损失的误导性问题，提出ScatterPrism生成式代理模型，并建立多指标诊断协议以确保运动学保真度，适用于核物理、高能物理及更广泛的科学领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真模拟和复杂反问题（如探测器建模和解卷积）在亚原子物理中计算成本极高，但却是准确物理解释的关键。现有CFM方法虽然加速了过程，但其标准训练损失存在根本性误导——损失过早平台化，掩盖了物理精度的持续改进，导致模型未能达到真正的运动学保真度。

Method: 1) 在Jefferson Lab核物理运动学数据集（γp→ρ⁰p→π⁺π⁻p）上暴露CFM损失的误导性；2) 引入ScatterPrism高效生成式代理模型，在NP数据和合成应力测试（挑战性1D分布拓扑）上评估；3) 建立物理信息驱动的评估指标，证明其在标准损失收敛后仍持续改进；4) 提出多指标诊断协议，在保证数据未记忆的前提下确保运动学保真度。

Result: CFM标准损失在收敛后，物理指标仍在持续优化，证明损失下降与物理保真度脱离关联。ScatterPrism作为代理模型能够有效检测这种脱节，多指标诊断协议能够实现真正的运动学保真度而不导致数据过拟合。

Conclusion: 该统一框架不仅适用于当前Jefferson Lab核物理及未来电子-离子对撞机（EIC）相关的挑战，还可扩展到高能物理的喷注建模，以及医疗成像、天体物理学和量化金融等更广泛需要严格生成可靠性的领域。

Abstract: High-fidelity simulations and complex inverse problems, such as detector modeling and unfolding, are computationally intensive bottlenecks across subatomic physics, yet essential for accurate physical interpretation. While Conditional Flow Matching (CFM) offers a robust acceleration approach, we demonstrate its standard training loss is fundamentally misleading. Specifically, utilizing a Jefferson Lab Nuclear Physics (NP) kinematic dataset ($γp \to ρ^0 p \to π^+π^- p$), we expose that CFM loss plateaus prematurely, obscuring ongoing physical refinement. To verify this disconnect is a dataset-agnostic pathology, we introduce ScatterPrism, an efficient generative surrogate evaluated against both the NP data and synthetic stress tests modeling challenging 1D distribution topologies. Coupling these benchmarks, we establish that physics-informed metrics continue improving long after standard loss converges. Consequently, we propose a multi-metric diagnostic protocol to ensure true kinematic fidelity without data memorization. Driven by NP challenges relevant to the forthcoming Electron-Ion Collider (EIC), this unified machinery has strong potential to extend to High-Energy Physics (HEP) applications, such as jet modeling. Furthermore, the framework holds promise for broader domains requiring rigorous generative reliability, including medical imaging, astrophysics, and quantitative finance.

</details>


### [372] [CountsDiff: A Diffusion Model on the Natural Numbers for Generation and Imputation of Count-Based Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03779)
*Renzo G. Soatto,Anders Hoel,Greycen Ren,Shorna Alam,Stephen Bates,Nikolaos P. Daskalakis,Caroline Uhler,Maria Skoularidou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CountsDiff是一个针对离散计数数据（自然数）的扩散模型框架，扩展了Blackout扩散框架，引入了连续时间训练、无分类器引导和churn/remasking反向动力学等现代扩散模型特性。在CIFAR-10和CelebA等自然图像数据集以及单细胞RNA-seq数据上验证，即使简单实例化也能匹配或超越现有最先进模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在连续数据和基于token的数据上表现优异，但对离散有序数据（计数数据）的应用尚不充分，现有方法缺乏针对自然数分布的专门建模框架。

Method: 基于Blackout扩散框架，通过生存概率调度和显式损失权重进行直接参数化简化；引入连续时间训练、无分类器引导、churn/remasking反向动力学等现代扩散模型特性。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和CelebA图像数据集以及胎儿和心脏细胞图谱的单细胞RNA-seq插补任务上，CountsDiff匹配或超越了现有最先进的离散生成模型和scRNA-seq插补方法。

Conclusion: CountsDiff为离散计数数据提供了有效的扩散建模框架，当前简单实例化已取得优异性能，且通过优化设计参数仍有进一步提升空间。

Abstract: Diffusion models have excelled at generative tasks for both continuous and token-based domains, but their application to discrete ordinal data remains underdeveloped. We present CountsDiff, a diffusion framework designed to model distributions on the natural numbers. CountsDiff extends the Blackout diffusion framework by simplifying its formulation through a direct parameterization in terms of a survival probability schedule and an explicit loss weighting. This introduces flexibility through design parameters with direct analogues in existing diffusion modeling frameworks. Beyond this reparameterization, CountsDiff introduces features from modern diffusion models, previously absent in counts-based domains, including continuous-time training, classifier-free guidance, and churn/remasking reverse dynamics that allow non-monotone reverse trajectories. We propose an initial instantiation of CountsDiff and validate it on natural image datasets (CIFAR-10, CelebA), exploring the effects of the introduced design parameters in a complex, well-studied, and interpretable data domain. We then highlight biological count assays as a natural use case, evaluating CountsDiff on single-cell RNA-seq imputation in fetal and heart cell atlases. Remarkably, we find that even this simple instantiation matches or surpasses the performance of a state-of-the-art discrete generative model and leading scRNA-seq imputation methods, while leaving substantial headroom for further gains through optimized design choices in future work.

</details>


### [373] [GraphWalker: Patient Analogy Meets Information Gain for Clinical Reasoning with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06684)
*Yue Fang,Weibin Liao,Yuxin Guo,Jiaran Gao,Hongxin Ding,Jinyang Zhang,Xinke Jiang,Zhibang Yang,Junfeng Zhao,Yasha Wang,Liantao Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GraphWalker是一种无需训练的框架，通过基于数据驱动和模型驱动联合视角的图结构检索、患者群体发现以及懒惰贪心前沿扩展搜索，让冻结的大语言模型通过类比检索到的患者案例进行临床推理，在多个真实EHR基准上超越现有方法，且对分布变化具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的EHR临床推理方法通过上下文演示（in-context demonstrations）进行类比推理，但存在三个核心局限：(1) 视角局限——数据驱动的相似性与LLM推理需求不匹配，而模型驱动信号受限于有限的临床能力；(2) 群体意识缺失——演示独立选择，未建模人群层级结构；(3) 信息聚合不足——忽略了演示间的冗余和交互效应。

Method: GraphWalker是一个无需训练（training-free）的框架，包含三个关键设计：(i) 联合利用数据驱动和模型驱动视角进行患者案例检索；(ii) 发现患者群体（patient cohorts），将检索过程锚定在人群层级结构上；(iii) 采用懒惰贪心搜索（lazy greedy search）与前沿扩展（frontier expansion）策略，以边际信息增益最大化为目标组合演示案例。

Result: 在多个真实世界EHR基准上，GraphWalker持续优于最先进的演示选择基线，在跨数据集分布漂移下保持更强的鲁棒性，且无需任务特定的参数更新。该方法还能推广到黑盒LLM，并与基于代理的推理框架自然兼容。

Conclusion: GraphWalker可作为LLM临床工作流中一个即插即用的患者类比推理技能（pluggable patient-analogy skill），有效提升了冻结LLM在EHR上的临床推理能力，同时保持训练无关性和强鲁棒性。

Abstract: Clinical reasoning over electronic health records (EHRs) is a fundamental yet challenging task in modern healthcare. While large language models (LLMs) offer a promising paradigm via in-context demonstrations that requires no task-specific parameter updates, existing methods for reasoning by patient analogy in EHR settings suffer from three core limitations: (1) Perspective Limitation, where data-driven similarity misaligns with LLM reasoning needs while model-driven signals are constrained by limited clinical competence; (2) Cohort Awareness, as demonstrations are selected independently without modeling population-level structure; and (3) Information Aggregation, where redundancy and interaction effects among demonstrations are ignored. We propose GraphWalker, a training-free framework that lets frozen LLMs reason by analogy over retrieved patient cases. GraphWalker (i) jointly leverages data-driven and model-driven perspectives, (ii) discovers patient cohorts to ground retrieval in population-level structure, and (iii) employs a lazy greedy search with frontier expansion to compose demonstrations with high marginal information gain. Extensive experiments on multiple real-world EHR benchmarks show that GraphWalker consistently outperforms state-of-the-art demonstration selection baselines, and remains substantially more robust under cross-dataset distribution shift, without task-specific parameter updates. GraphWalker further generalizes to black-box LLMs and composes naturally with agentic reasoning frameworks, positioning it as a pluggable patient-analogy skill in LLM-based clinical workflows. Our code is available at https://github.com/PuppyKnightUniversity/GraphWalker.

</details>


### [374] [Scalable Joint Resource Allocation for SLO-Constrained LLM Inference in Heterogeneous GPU Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07472)
*Jiaming Cheng,Duong Tung Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种面向SLO约束的LLM推理可扩展框架，通过两种约束感知启发式算法（GH和AGH）高效求解混合整数线性规划（MILP）问题，在Azure LLM推理迹上验证了GH可在1秒内生成可行解，AGH在3秒内达到近最优性能，且对需求波动具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云环境中LLM推理服务需联合优化模型选择、GPU配置、并行策略和工作负载路由，但受限于延迟、精度、内存和预算约束。现有MILP方法计算成本高，难以在需求波动下频繁重优化；而启发式方法通常单独优化各组件，在系统级约束下可能不可行。

Method: 将问题建模为包含两阶段延迟模型（预填充和自回归解码，支持张量并行和流水线并行）的MILP；提出两种约束感知启发式算法：贪婪启发式（GH）和自适应贪婪启发式（AGH），AGH通过多起点构建、局部搜索和GPU整合扩展GH，两者均采用并行感知过滤、基于成本的排序和自适应并行缩放来保持可行性。

Result: 基于Azure LLM推理迹的实验表明：GH在1秒内生成可行解，AGH在3秒内达到近最优性能，可扩展至精确求解器无法收敛的大规模实例。在1.5倍延迟和精度膨胀的样本外压力测试下，AGH通过预置余量优雅降级，成本和SLO违规均低于成本最优的MILP解。

Conclusion: 高质量的资源分配方案能提供对需求波动的显著鲁棒性，同时支持对工作负载变化的快速适应，AGH在合成和真实Azure工作负载下以显著低于精确MILP解的成本维持SLO合规。

Abstract: Serving large language model (LLM) inference in cloud environments requires jointly optimizing model selection, GPU provisioning, parallelism configuration, and workload routing under latency, accuracy, memory, and budget constraints. While mixed-integer linear programming (MILP) can model this problem, its computational cost limits frequent re-optimization under demand variability. Existing heuristics often optimize individual components separately and may become infeasible when system-wide constraints are enforced.
  This paper presents a scalable framework for SLO-constrained LLM inference. We formulate the problem as an MILP with a two-phase delay model capturing both prefill and autoregressive decoding under tensor and pipeline parallelism. To solve it efficiently, we develop two constraint-aware heuristics: a Greedy Heuristic (GH) and an Adaptive Greedy Heuristic (AGH). AGH extends GH through multi-start construction, local search, and GPU consolidation. Both methods maintain feasibility through parallelism-aware filtering, cost-based ranking, and adaptive parallelism scaling.
  Experiments based on the Azure LLM Inference Trace show that GH generates feasible solutions within one second, while AGH achieves near-optimal performance within three seconds and scales to large instances where exact solvers fail to converge. Under out-of-sample stress with up to 1.5x delay and accuracy inflation, AGH degrades gracefully through provisioned headroom, yielding substantially lower cost and SLO violations than cost-minimal MILP solutions. Across synthetic and real Azure workloads, AGH maintains SLO compliance at significantly lower cost than exact MILP solutions. These results demonstrate that high-quality allocations provide substantial robustness to demand variability while enabling rapid adaptation to workload changes.

</details>


### [375] [Attention Sink in Transformers: A Survey on Utilization, Interpretation, and Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10098)
*Zunhai Su,Hengyuan Zhang,Wei Wu,Yifan Zhang,Yaxiu Liu,He Xiao,Qingyao Yang,Yuxuan Sun,Rui Yang,Chao Zhang,Jing Xiong,Hui Shen,Keyu Fan,Weihao Ye,Chaofan Tao,Taiqiang Wu,Zhongwei Wan,Tiantian Zhang,Bowen Yan,Zhen Li,Yiming Zhang,Congkai Xie,Yulei Qian,Yuchen Xie,Yik-Chung Wu,Hongxia Yang,Ngai Wong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是第一篇关于Transformer中Attention Sink（注意力沉没）现象的系统性综述，从基础利用、机制解释和策略缓解三个维度梳理了现有研究，并提供了相关论文列表资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Attention Sink（AS）是Transformer架构中普遍存在且影响深远的问题，会导致注意力过度集中于少量无信息量的token，影响可解释性、训练推理动态并加剧幻觉等问题。然而此前缺乏系统性综述来整合相关研究并指导未来方向。

Method: 该论文以综述形式，从三个关键维度（基础利用、机制解释、策略缓解）系统组织并梳理了Attention Sink领域的研究现状，同时提供了GitHub论文列表作为补充资源。

Result: 首次提供了Attention Sink领域的系统性综述，明确了该领域的关键概念和主要研究趋势，为研究人员提供了全面的知识梳理和未来研究指引。

Conclusion: 该综述为研究人员在当前Transformer范式下有效管理Attention Sink提供了宝贵资源，同时为下一代Transformer的创新进展提供了启发。

Abstract: As the foundational architecture of modern machine learning, Transformers have driven remarkable progress across diverse AI domains. Despite their transformative impact, a persistent challenge across various Transformers is Attention Sink (AS), in which a disproportionate amount of attention is focused on a small subset of specific yet uninformative tokens. AS complicates interpretability, significantly affecting the training and inference dynamics, and exacerbates issues such as hallucinations. In recent years, substantial research has been dedicated to understanding and harnessing AS. However, a comprehensive survey that systematically consolidates AS-related research and offers guidance for future advancements remains lacking. To address this gap, we present the first survey on AS, structured around three key dimensions that define the current research landscape: Fundamental Utilization, Mechanistic Interpretation, and Strategic Mitigation. Our work makes a pivotal contribution by highlighting the key concepts and main trends in the field, guiding researchers through the evolution of AS-related studies. We envision this survey as a valuable resource, empowering researchers to effectively manage AS within the current Transformer paradigm, while simultaneously inspiring innovative advancements for the next generation of Transformers. The paper list of this work is available at https://github.com/ZunhaiSu/Awesome-Attention-Sink.

</details>


### [376] [Even More Guarantees for Variational Inference in the Presence of Symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21407)
*Lena Zellinger,Antonio Vergari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论上扩展了变分推断中位置-尺度族在目标对称性下的鲁棒性，给出了使用前向KL散度和α-散度时精确恢复目标均值和相关矩阵的充分条件，并放宽了对数凹假设，适用于多模态等更广泛目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分推断中变分族通常为简单参数族，无法包含真实目标分布，导致模型误设定。需要研究在何种条件下仍能恢复目标分布的关键特征。

Method: (1) 将前人关于位置-尺度族在目标对称性下的鲁棒VI理论扩展到前向KL散度和α-散度，给出精确恢复目标均值和相关矩阵的充分条件；(2) 放宽前人工作中的对数凹目标假设，使其适用于包括多模态在内的更广泛目标。

Result: 给出了使用前向KL散度和α-散度时精确恢复目标均值和相关矩阵的充分条件，并在实验中展示了这些条件如何指导变分族和α值的选择，以及在缺乏充分条件时优化为何失败。

Conclusion: 本文显著扩展了鲁棒VI的理论基础，在更广泛的散度和目标类型下提供了恢复目标关键特征的保证，为实践中的变分族选择和α值设定提供了理论指导。

Abstract: When approximating an intractable density via variational inference (VI) the variational family is typically chosen as a simple parametric family that very likely does not contain the target. This raises the question: Under which conditions can we recover characteristics of the target despite misspecification? In this work, we extend previous theoretical results on robust VI with location-scale families under target symmetries in two substantial ways: (1) We open them up to a wider range of divergences by providing sufficient conditions for exact recovery of the target mean and correlation matrix when using the forward Kullback-Leibler divergence and $α$-divergences. (2) By doing so, we find that we can drop the restrictive assumption of a log-concave target made in previous work, allowing us to give guarantees for a wider range of targets, including multi-modal ones. In our experiments, we show how our guarantees can serve as guidelines for the choice of the variational family and $α$-value and we illustrate on a diverse set of examples how and why optimization can fail in the absence of our sufficient conditions.

</details>


### [377] [Automatic Causal Fairness Analysis with LLM-Generated Reporting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27011)
*Alessia Berarducci,Eric Rossetto,Alessandro Antonucci,Marco Zaffalon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FairMind原型工具，通过因果推断（反事实查询）和LLM报告生成，在数据集层面自动评估机器学习中的公平性，支持序数保护变量和连续目标等扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AutoML框架未充分考虑训练数据和预测中的公平性缺失问题，需要一种能够在数据集层面自动化公平性分析的方法。

Method: 基于Plečko和Bareinboim提出的标准公平性模型，利用反事实查询计算因果效应（涉及目标变量、混杂因子、中介变量及受保护特征的不同取值），实现闭式效应计算；数据预处理后，采用零样本方式调用LLM生成公平性分析报告。

Result: 通过示例展示了FairMind相对于LLM直接分析的优势，并给出了序数保护变量、连续目标以及新的分解结果等扩展。

Conclusion: FairMind通过因果框架和LLM结合，为AutoML场景下数据集级别的公平性自动化评估提供了可行方案，并可通过多种扩展适应更广泛的应用场景。

Abstract: AutoML, intended as the process of automating the application of machine learning to real-world problems, is a key step for AI popularisation. Most AutoML frameworks are not accounting for the potential lack of fairness in the training data and in the corresponding predictions. We introduce \textsc{FairMind}, a software prototype aiming to automatise fairness analysis at the dataset level. We achieve that by resorting to the assumptions of the \emph{standard fairness model}, recently proposed by Plečko and Bareinboim. This allows for a sound fairness evaluation in terms of causal effects, based on \emph{counterfactual} queries involving the target, possibly confounders and mediators, and the different values of an input feature we regard as \emph{protected}. After the necessary data preprocessing, the tool implements a closed-form computation of the effects. LLMs are consequently exploited to generate accurate reports on the fairness levels detected in the training dataset. We achieve that in a zero-shot setup and show by examples the expected advantages with respect to a direct analysis performed by the LLM. To favour applications, extensions to ordinal protected variable and continuous targets and novel decomposition results are also discussed.

</details>


### [378] [InvEvolve: Evolving White-Box Inventory Policies via Large Language Models with Performance Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00369)
*Chenyu Huang,Jianghao Lin,Zhengyang Tang,Bo Jiang,Ruoqing Jiang,Benyou Wang,Lai Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出InvEvolve框架，基于强化学习训练的大语言模型，通过置信区间认证机制，为动态非平稳需求环境生成具有统计安全保证的白盒库存策略，在合成和真实零售数据上均优于传统方法和深度学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的进化搜索方法（如AlphaEvolve）在静态、高度结构化问题上表现优异，但无法直接适用于需要在线更新的动态库存环境。本文旨在弥合这一差距，利用LLM为动态非平稳需求场景生成具有安全保证的库存策略。

Method: 提出InvEvolve框架，采用端到端的库存策略进化与推理方法，基于置信区间认证。使用强化学习训练的大语言模型处理需求数据及额外数值和文本特征，生成白盒库存策略，并建立统一的训练-推理-部署理论框架，推导策略安全性和性能差距的下界。

Result: 在合成数据和真实零售数据上，InvEvolve均优于经典库存策略和基于深度学习的方法。在规范库存设置中，生成了超越现有基准的新策略。

Conclusion: LLM能够有效用于动态库存策略生成，InvEvolve在非平稳需求环境中提供了兼具性能优势和统计安全保证的解决方案，为LLM在运筹优化领域的应用开辟了新路径。

Abstract: We study how large language models can be used to generate inventory policies in online settings with non-stationary demand. Our work is motivated by recent advances in LLM-based evolutionary search, such as AlphaEvolve, which demonstrates strong performance on static and highly structured problems such as mathematical discovery, but is not directly suited to dynamic inventory settings with online updates. We propose InvEvolve, an end-to-end inventory policy evolution and inference framework grounded in confidence-interval-based certification. Built on a large language model trained via reinforcement learning, InvEvolve can process demand data together with additional numerical and textual features, and generates white-box inventory policies with statistical safety guarantees for future deployment. We further introduce a unified framework with theoretical guarantees that connects training, inference, and deployment. This allows us to derive a lower bound on the probability that InvEvolve evolves a statistically safe and improved policy, and to characterize the multi-period performance gap relative to the oracle-safe benchmark. Tested on both synthetic data and real-world retail data, InvEvolve outperforms classical inventory policies and deep-learning-based methods. In canonical inventory settings, it generates new policies that outperform existing benchmarks.

</details>


### [379] [Adaptive Pluralistic Alignment: A pipeline for dynamic artificial democracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01642)
*Rachel Freedman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: APA提出了一种模块化流水线，通过低秩奖励基分解、社会选择投票和高效陪审团适应，使多元对齐的AI系统能够追踪价值演变而避免价值锁定，无需重复预训练或大规模数据收集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对齐方法针对固定偏好集进行优化，随着社会规范演变，会导致价值锁定（value lock-in）问题，无法适应价值观的变化。

Method: 三阶段流水线：(1) 通过低秩奖励基分解学习紧凑的个性化奖励模型；(2) 将这些模型作为陪审团，通过社会选择理论投票共同选择候选输出；(3) 当价值观变化时，通过拟合新标注者权重到固定奖励基上，高效适应陪审团。

Result: 使用PRISM多用户对齐数据集和模拟历史标注者进行概念验证，初步分析表明陪审团组成和投票规则选择会显著影响结果，尤其在偏好异质性强时。

Conclusion: APA系统高效、可解释、可操控且模块化，能避免价值锁定；代码和偏好数据集已开源。

Abstract: Prevailing alignment methods target a fixed set of preferences and therefore risk forcing value lock-in as societal norms evolve over time. We introduce Adaptive Pluralistic Alignment (APA), a modular pipeline for updating pluralistically aligned AI systems to track evolving values and avoid value lock-in without repeating costly pretraining or large-scale data collection. APA has three stages: (1) learning compact personalized reward models via low-rank reward basis decomposition, (2) using these models as a jury that collectively selects among candidate outputs through social-choice-theoretic voting, and (3) efficiently adapting the jury over time by fitting new annotator weights over the fixed reward bases as values shift. The resulting system is efficient, explainable, steerable, and modular. We implement a proof-of-concept instantiation using the PRISM multi-user alignment dataset and simulated historical annotators, and provide preliminary analysis showing that jury composition and the choice of voting rule can substantially affect outcomes, particularly when jury preferences are heterogeneous. We provide full code and resulting preference datasets at https://github.com/RachelFreedman/apa.

</details>


### [380] [Constrained Extreme Gradient Boosting for Adapting Reduced-Order Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04130)
*Melika Baghi,Xiao Liu,Kamran Paynabar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种约束集成学习框架cXGBoost，用于预测参数化系统的POD基函数。该方法利用Grassmann流形的几何表示，通过梯度提升树进行回归，并在训练中施加范数约束以保证逆映射的有效性和几何结构保持。在四个数值算例（包括流体动力学和波动传播问题）中验证了该方法在非线性区域内准确预测参数相关基函数的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真仿真（如CFD、有限元分析）虽对复杂工程系统建模至关重要，但在参数研究、优化和实时控制等任务中计算成本过高。投影降阶模型虽能通过将动力学投影到低维子空间来缓解计算负担，但其性能在参数变化时会退化，因此需要自适应基函数构建方法。

Method: 提出cXGBoost框架：1) 利用Grassmann流形表示子空间的几何结构；2) 将子空间映射到欧氏空间；3) 使用梯度提升树（XGBoost）进行回归预测；4) 在训练中施加范数约束以确保逆映射的合法性并保持预测子空间的几何结构。

Result: 在四个数值算例（包括流体动力学和波动传播问题）上的评估表明，该方法能够准确预测参数依赖的基函数，并在非线性区域内保持良好的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 将几何学习与约束集成方法相结合，为高维参数化系统的可扩展且可靠的降阶建模提供了有效途径。

Abstract: High-fidelity simulations, such as computational fluid dynamics and finite element analysis, are essential for modeling complex engineering systems but are often prohibitively expensive for tasks including parametric studies, optimization, and real-time control. Projection-based reduced-order models (ROMs) alleviate this cost by projecting the governing dynamics onto low-dimensional subspaces. However, their performance can deteriorate under parameter variation, motivating the need for adaptive basis construction. In this work, we propose a constrained ensemble learning framework, termed Constrained Extreme Gradient Boosting (cXGBoost), for predicting Proper Orthogonal Decomposition (POD) bases as functions of system parameters. The approach leverages a geometric representation of subspaces on the Grassmann manifold, which are mapped to a Euclidean space to enable efficient regression using gradient boosting trees. A norm constraint is imposed during training to ensure the validity of the inverse mapping and preserve the geometric structure of the predicted subspaces. The proposed method is evaluated on four numerical examples, including fluid dynamics and wave propagation problems, demonstrating its ability to accurately predict parameter-dependent bases while maintaining robustness across nonlinear regimes. These results highlight the potential of combining geometric learning with constrained ensemble methods for scalable and reliable reduced-order modeling of high-dimensional parametric systems.

</details>


### [381] [MidSteer: Optimal Affine Framework for Steering Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05220)
*Tatiana Gaintseva,Andrew Stepanov,Ziquan Liu,Martin Benning,Gregory Slabaugh,Jiankang Deng,Ismail Elezi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文形式化了概念引导（concept steering）的理论框架，证明标准引导方法是LEACE的特例，提出LEACE-Switch用于概念切换，并引入更通用的MidSteer框架实现最小扰动引导，在多种模态和架构中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概念引导在生成模型的后部署对齐和安全设置中取得了实证成功，但缺乏全面的理论框架，本文旨在弥补这一空白。

Method: 1) 建立引导与仿射概念擦除之间的联系，证明标准引导方法是LEACE的特例；2) 提出LEACE-Switch框架，刻画其最优仿射解所需假设；3) 引入MidSteer（最小扰动概念引导），放宽假设实现有向的最小扰动变换。

Result: MidSteer在多种任务、模态和架构（包括视觉扩散模型和大语言模型）中表现优越。

Conclusion: 本文为概念引导提供了理论形式化基础，MidSteer作为一个通用仿射框架，在放宽假设的同时实现了有效的概念操控。

Abstract: Steering intermediate representations has emerged as a powerful strategy for controlling generative models, particularly in post-deployment alignment and safety settings. However, despite its empirical success, it currently lacks a comprehensive theoretical framework. In this paper, we bridge this gap by formalizing the theory of concept steering. First, we establish a link between steering and affine concept erasure, proving that the standard approach for removing unwanted behaviors is a special case of LEACE (a closed-form method for affine erasure). Next, we formulate a principled theoretical framework for concept switching, LEACE-Switch, and characterize the assumptions under which it provides an optimal affine solution. Building on this analysis, we then introduce MidSteer (Minimal Disturbance concept Steering), a more general affine framework for concept manipulation that relaxes these assumptions and enables directed, minimal-disturbance transformations. We demonstrate that MidSteer performs favorably across a range of tasks, modalities, and architectures, including vision diffusion models and large language models.

</details>


### [382] [MACS: Modality-Aware Capacity Scaling for Efficient Multimodal MoE Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05225)
*Bo Li,Chuan Wu,Shaolin Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MACS框架，通过熵加权负载机制和动态模态自适应容量机制，解决MoE多模态大语言模型在专家并行推理中的信息异构性和模态动态性导致的效率瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE MLLMs在专家并行推理中存在严重的落后节点效应（straggler effect），而现有的基于token计数的负载均衡方法无法处理多模态场景下的两个独特挑战：(1) 信息异构性——大量冗余视觉token被与语义关键token同等对待；(2) 模态动态性——不同任务中视觉与文本比例变化导致资源错配。

Method: 提出MACS（模态感知容量缩放）框架，包含两个核心机制：(1) 熵加权负载机制（Entropy-Weighted Load）——量化视觉token的语义价值以解决信息异构性；(2) 动态模态自适应容量机制（Dynamic Modality-Adaptive Capacity）——根据输入实时模态组成分配专家资源。该框架无需额外训练。

Result: 在多模态基准测试上显著优于现有方法，为MoE MLLMs在EP推理中的高效部署提供了鲁棒解决方案。

Conclusion: MACS通过模态感知的负载均衡策略，在不引入额外训练成本的前提下，有效缓解了多模态MoE模型在专家并行推理中的效率问题。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts Multimodal Large Language Models (MoE MLLMs) suffer from a significant efficiency bottleneck during Expert Parallelism (EP) inference due to the straggler effect. This issue is worsened in the multimodal context, as existing token-count-based load balancing methods fail to address two unique challenges: (1) Information Heterogeneity, where numerous redundant visual tokens are treated equally to semantically critical ones, and (2) Modality Dynamics, where varying visual to text ratios across tasks lead to resource misallocation. To address these challenges, we propose MACS (Modality-Aware Capacity Scaling), a training-free inference framework. Specifically, MACS introduces an Entropy-Weighted Load mechanism to quantify the semantic value of visual tokens, addressing information heterogeneity. Additionally, the Dynamic Modality-Adaptive Capacity mechanism allocates expert resources based on the real-time modal composition of the input. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MACS significantly outperforms existing methods on various multimodal benchmarks, providing a novel and robust solution for the efficient deployment of MoE MLLMs in EP inference.

</details>


### [383] [AAAC: Activation-Aware Adaptive Codebooks for 4-bit LLM Weight Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08692)
*Beshr IslamBouli,David Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AAAC是一种轻量级4-bit LLM权重量化方法，通过每层两个小型学习型码本（64字节）替代固定标量码本，利用激活加权重构误差选择码本，零额外存储开销，在3-30分钟内完成量化，性能优于AWQ、GPTQ等多种方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有4-bit权重量化方法（如AWQ、GPTQ）通过缩放、裁剪或误差补偿改进权重映射，但精度受限；高精度方法（如OmniQuant、QuIP#）需数小时量化时间。为在量化时间与精度间取得更好平衡，提出AAAC。

Method: 每层使用两个小型学习型标量码本（64字节），每个权重组根据激活加权重构误差选择最优码本，并将选择编码在该组正尺度因子的未使用符号位中，实现零存储开销。

Result: 在3-30分钟内完成量化，性能超越AWQ、GPTQ、IF4、GPTVQ、OmniQuant、SqueezeLLM、QuIP#等基线方法，量化时间降低数个数量级。

Conclusion: AAAC在极低量化时间成本下实现了更优的量化精度，为高效LLM部署提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Post-training weight-only quantization to 4 bits is widely used to reduce the memory and compute costs of large language model inference. Existing PTQ methods, such as AWQ and GPTQ, improve how weights are mapped onto a fixed 4-bit grid through scaling, clipping, or error compensation. To further improve accuracy, methods such as OmniQuant and QuIP\# uses gradient-assisted algorithms at the cost of hours of quantization time. In this work, we propose AAAC (Activation-Aware Adaptive Codebooks), a lightweight method for 4-bit LLM weight quantization. AAAC replaces the fixed scalar codebook used in standard quantization with two small learned scalar codebooks (64 bytes) per layer. Each group of weights selects the codebook that minimizes activation-weighted reconstruction error, encoding the choice in the unused sign bit of the group's positive scale and adding zero storage overhead. AAAC completes in 3--30 minutes on a single GPU, and adds no memory beyond the model itself. We evaluate against AWQ, GPTQ, IF4, GPTVQ, OmniQuant, SqueezeLLM, and QuIP\# across model families. AAAC outperforms baselines at orders-of-magnitude less quantization time.

</details>


### [384] [Controllable Molecular Generative Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15354)
*Yihan Zhu,Yuhan Liu,Weijiang Li,Tengfei Luo,Meng Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CoMole提出了基于基序感知图扩散的统一分子生成框架，通过强化学习优化条件反向策略，在三个异构基准测试的九个目标上实现可控性第一，MAE最多降低48.2%，有效性保持在0.94以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基础模型在语言和视觉领域取得巨大成功，但分子图生成仍缺乏统一框架来处理异构设计任务并实现可靠可控性。虽然强化学习是任务特定优化的自然机制，但将其应用于图生成模型面临原子级动作空间巨大和中间状态化学无效的挑战。

Method: 提出CoMole，构建统一的基序感知图扩散管道。通过学习基序感知的图空间，将预训练的结构先验迁移到可控生成中，利用RL优化化学意义上合理的条件反向策略。理论分析了原子级RL的瓶颈，并论证了基序感知策略优化的合理性。

Result: 在涵盖材料和药物发现的三个异构基准测试中，CoMole在全部九个目标上可控性排名第一，MAE相比最强基线最多降低48.2%，有效性保持在0.94以上且无需基于规则的校正或后处理过滤。

Conclusion: CoMole能够将可控性迁移到未见属性，只需在生成器冻结的情况下优化任务嵌入，即可达到与强任务特定基线竞争的性能。

Abstract: Despite the success of foundation models in language and vision, molecular graph generation still lacks a unified framework for heterogeneous design tasks with reliable controllability. While reinforcement learning (RL) offers a natural post-training mechanism for task-specific optimization, applying it to graph generative models is hindered by the vast atom-wise action spaces and chemically invalid intermediate states. We propose \textbf{Co}ntrollable \textbf{Mole}cular Generative Foundation Models (CoMole), built with a unified motif-aware graph diffusion pipeline. By learning a motif-aware graph space, CoMole transfers pretrained structural priors into controllable generation, where RL optimizes conditional reverse policies over chemically meaningful decisions. We theoretically characterize the bottleneck of atom-level RL and justify motif-aware policy optimization. Across three heterogeneous benchmarks spanning materials and drug discovery, CoMole ranks first in controllability on all nine targets, reduces MAE by up to 48.2% relative to the strongest baselines, and maintains validity above 0.94 without rule-based correction or post-hoc filtering. We further show that CoMole transfers controllability to unseen properties by optimizing only task embeddings with the generator frozen, achieving performance competitive with strong task-specific baselines.

</details>


### [385] [CHoE: Cross-Domain Heterogeneous Graph Prompt Learning via Structure-Conditioned Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15888)
*Peiyuan Li,Yongqi Huang,Jitao Zhao,Dongxiao He,Di Jin,Weixiong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CHoE，一种基于专家网络的跨域异构图表征学习框架，通过在预训练中引入结构条件专家，并在提示调优时采用结构感知的专家路由与负载均衡机制，实现跨域场景下的少样本学习，实验表明其优于所有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异构图表征学习方法主要面向域内场景，而实际应用中预训练与下游任务数据常来自不同分布，导致现有方法在域迁移时性能显著下降。

Method: 提出CHoE框架：1）预训练阶段引入结构条件专家（structure-conditioned experts）；2）提示调优阶段采用结构感知的专家路由（structure-aware expert routing）与负载均衡机制；3）设计基于提示的语义融合模块（prompt-based semantic fusion module）整合多视图表示。

Result: 在跨域少样本场景下，CHoE一致性地提升了性能，并优于所有基线方法。

Conclusion: CHoE有效解决了异构图表征学习中的跨域分布偏移问题，拓展了提示学习在异构图上跨域应用的可行性。

Abstract: Heterogeneous Graph Prompt Learning (HGPL)has emerged as a promising paradigm for bridging the gap between the objectives of pre-training foundation models and their downstream applications in heterogeneous graph settings. However, existing HGPL methods are primarily designed for in-domain scenarios, whereas real-world deployments often span multiple domains, and the data used for pre-training and downstream tasks may originate from different distributions. Consequently, the applicability of current HGPL approaches is limited to in-domain settings, and their performance typically degrades when application domains shift. To address this serious limitation, we develop CHoE, a cross-domain HGPL method built upon an expert network. During pre-training, we introduce and train structure-conditioned experts, and during prompt tuning, we adopt a structure-aware expert routing and load balancing mechanism to select structurally compatible experts for each meta-path view. In addition, we design a prompt-based semantic fusion module to integrate representations across multiple views for downstream prediction. Extensive experiments show that CHoE consistently improves performance in few-shot cross-domain applications, outperforming all baseline approaches.

</details>


### [386] [Leveraging Error Diversity in Group Rollouts for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17333)
*Wenpu Liu,Yuqi Xu,Weichu Xie,Yongfu Zhu,Shuai Dong,Ziyue Wang,Wenqi Shao,Xiaoying Zhang,Tong Yang,Nan Duan,Jiaqi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EDAS方法，通过利用组内错误多样性动态调整强化学习中的优势信号（放大常见错误惩罚、减轻罕见错误惩罚），从而在不改变算法核心的情况下提升RLVR训练效果，在多个基准上取得显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR方法虽对每个回答独立赋分，但忽略了组输出中错误分布的集体结构信息；实证发现错误多样性与训练成功高度相关，同质化错误组从RLVR中获益有限，因此需要利用这一被忽视的信息来提升训练效果。

Method: 提出错误多样性优势塑造（EDAS），是一种轻量级、算法无关的后处理技术。它基于组内错误多样性，对错误rollout的优势信号进行调制：对重复出现的常见错误放大惩罚，对稀有的探索性错误减轻惩罚，从而鼓励模型维持多样化的推理路径并抑制错误固着。

Result: 在多个主流RLVR方法和多个模型及7个数学基准上验证，EDAS带来持续改进。特别是在Qwen3-8B上基于DAPO平均提升6.29个百分点。

Conclusion: 利用组rollout中的潜在信息是强化RLVR的一种广泛有效的策略，EDAS作为一种简单后处理可无缝集成到任意RLVR算法中。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) typically samples multiple responses per prompt and assigns binary rewards based on individual correctness, yet the collective structure of the group output, specifically the distribution of errors, is largely discarded. We identify this as a missed opportunity: empirical analysis reveals that error diversity within a group is a strong predictor of training success, with problems eliciting diverse wrong answers benefiting substantially more from RLVR than those producing homogeneous failures. Motivated by this observation, we propose Error Diversity Advantage Shaping (EDAS), a lightweight, algorithm-agnostic technique that modulates the advantage signal for incorrect rollouts based on intra-group error diversity. EDAS amplifies penalties for dominant, repeated errors and attenuates penalties for rare, exploratory ones, thereby encouraging the model to maintain diverse reasoning paths and discouraging error perseveration. Crucially, EDAS operates as a simple post-hoc adjustment that can be seamlessly integrated into any RLVR algorithm. We validate EDAS on top of several mainstream RLVR methods across a series of models and seven challenging math benchmarks, demonstrating consistent improvements. Notably, EDAS yields an average improvement of 6.29 points over DAPO on Qwen3-8B across seven benchmarks, confirming that exploiting the latent information in group rollouts is a broadly effective strategy for strengthening RLVR.

</details>


### [387] [I-SAFE: Wasserstein Coherence Metrics for Structural Auditing of Scientific AI Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21731)
*Barbara Tarantino,Gennaro Auricchio,Paolo Giudici*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出I-SAFE框架，一种基于Wasserstein相干性度量的后验分布审计方法，用于评估科学AI模型在结构化先验知识下的输出分布相干性，并在药物-靶标相互作用预测任务中揭示了准确率指标无法捕捉的模型行为差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在科学预测中常将强基准性能误解为科学上有意义的行为，但模型可能利用捷径特征、数据集特定规律或分布偏差，这些特征在留出数据上具有预测性却与领域相关结构不一致，因此需要一种能检测这种不一致性的审计方法。

Method: 提出I-SAFE框架，包含三个互补度量：基于分位数的度量(QBM)评估位置级相干性、Wasserstein相干性度量(WCM)评估序数相干性、平移不变WCM变体评估形状相干性。该方法在训练好的黑箱预测器上，根据外部结构先验对输入进行结构化引导扰动，评估输出分布的相干性。

Result: 在Davis激酶基准、KLIFS结合口袋注释和三个序列DTI模型(DeepConvDTI、DeepDTA、TAPB)上的实例化表明，尽管模型处于可比较的预测区间，I-SAFE揭示了截然不同的分布响应轮廓，这种差异在基于准确率的评估中不可见。

Conclusion: I-SAFE框架是模型无关的，适用于任何输入可进行结构化分解且存在外部先验的领域，能够发现传统准确率指标无法捕捉的模型行为差异。

Abstract: Deep learning models are increasingly used in scientific prediction tasks where strong benchmark performance is often interpreted as evidence of scientifically meaningful behavior. This interpretation is fragile, as models may exploit shortcut features, dataset-specific regularities, or distributional biases that are predictive on held-out data but not aligned with domain-relevant structure. To address this limitation, we introduce the \textsc{I-SAFE} (Interventional Secure, Accurate, Fair and Explainable) framework, a post-hoc distributional auditing framework for scientific AI models centered on the Wasserstein Coherence Metric (WCM). Given a trained black-box predictor and an external structural prior encoding domain knowledge about task-relevant input structure, \textsc{I-SAFE} evaluates raw model outputs under structurally guided perturbations of the input. The proposed audit measures output-distribution coherence through three complementary metrics: a Quantile-Based Metric (QBM) for location-level coherence, the WCM for ordinal coherence, and a translation-invariant WCM variant for shape coherence. We instantiate \textsc{I-SAFE} on drug--target interaction (DTI) prediction using the Davis kinase benchmark, KLIFS (Kinase--Ligand Interaction Fingerprints and Structures) binding-pocket annotations, and three sequence-based DTI models: DeepConvDTI, DeepDTA, and TAPB. Although the models operate in a comparable predictive regime, \textsc{I-SAFE} reveals substantially different distributional response profiles, a distinction invisible to accuracy-based evaluation. The framework is model-agnostic and applicable to any domain where inputs admit a structured decomposition and an external prior is available.

</details>


### [388] [Scale When Needed: Adaptive Neuron-level Mixed Precision Quantization Aware Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25054)
*Ayush K. Varshney,Konstantinos Vandikas,Šarūnas Girdzijauskas,Adam Orucu,Aneta Vulgarakis Feljan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出神经元级混合精度QAT方法(NMP-QAT)，让每个神经元独立学习离散精度，在资源受限的6G边缘设备上实现更好的压缩-精度权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合精度QAT方法通常在粗粒度的层或通道级别操作，且依赖启发式或搜索策略，忽略了神经元级别的细粒度变异性，限制了压缩效率。

Method: NMP-QAT从低比特精度开始，通过可微代理和直通估计器，仅在训练信号需要时扩展每个神经元的比特宽度，同时保持完全离散的推理图，适用于权重和激活。

Result: 在电信和非电信数据集上，基于MLP和表格基础模型架构，NMP-QAT在压缩-精度权衡上优于现有的混合精度QAT基线。

Conclusion: NMP-QAT适用于Green AI边缘部署，为资源受限的6G设备提供了更优的模型压缩方案。

Abstract: Deploying deep neural networks on resource-constrained 6G edge devices demands aggressive compression with minimal accuracy loss. Quantization-Aware Training (QAT) has emerged as a leading compression approach; however, existing mixed-precision methods typically operate at coarse layer- or channel-level granularity. These methods often rely on heuristic or search-based bit-allocation strategies, which may overlook fine-grained variability at the neuron level. We propose Neuron-Level Mixed-Precision QAT (NMP-QAT), where each neuron independently learns its own discrete precision during training. Starting from low-bit precision, NMP-QAT expands bit-width only when training signals demand it, via differentiable surrogates and straight-through estimators, while preserving a fully discrete inference graph. This adaptability extends to both weights and activations, reducing memory movement. Evaluated on telecom and non-telecom datasets across MLP and tabular foundation model architectures, NMP-QAT achieves superior compression-accuracy trade-offs over mixed-precision QAT baselines, making it well-suited for Green AI deployments at the network edge.

</details>


### [389] [Autoregression-Free Neural Operators for Time-Dependent PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25413)
*Jiaquan Zhang,Caiyan Qin,Haoyu Bian,Libin Cai,Yi Lu,Chaoning Zhang,Wei Dong,Yuanfang Guo,Yang Yang,Heng Tao Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出自回归自由神经算子（AFNO），将PDE时间演化映射到潜在空间，利用流匹配学习连续时间向量场，避免自回归滚动，实现长期稳定预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经算子方法在处理时间依赖PDE时采用自回归滚动，导致长期预测中误差逐步累积，且缺乏连续时间建模能力。

Method: 将PDE时间演化映射到潜在空间，利用流匹配（flow matching）学习潜在空间中的连续时间向量场，通过对物理参数的显式条件化捕捉不同参数配置下的动力学行为，完全避免自回归滚动预测。

Result: 在六个PDE上的实验表明，AFNO相比基线方法显著提升了长期预测稳定性，并持续降低了滚动预测误差。

Conclusion: AFNO通过潜在空间流匹配建模连续时间演化，有效解决了神经算子长期预测中的误差累积问题。

Abstract: Neural operators learn mappings from function-dependent inputs to solutions, providing an effective framework for solving partial differential equations (PDEs). For time-dependent PDEs, existing methods typically perform long-horizon prediction through autoregressive rollout directly in high-dimensional physical field spaces, where each predicted state is recursively fed back as the input for the next step. Although effective for short-term prediction, this autoregressive rollout and the lack of continuous-time modeling lead to progressive error accumulation over long-horizon rollouts. In this work, we propose Autoregression-Free Neural Operators (AFNO), which map the time evolution of PDEs into a latent space and model continuous-time vector fields within it. AFNO uses flow matching to learn the latent vector field, thereby enabling continuous evolution over extended horizons, avoiding autoregressive rollout and capturing dynamics under varying parameter configurations through explicit conditioning on physical parameters. Theoretical analysis and extensive experiments on six PDEs demonstrate that AFNO improves long-horizon prediction stability and consistently reduces rollout errors compared with the baselines.

</details>


### [390] [BigMac: Breaking the Pareto Frontier of Compute and Memory in Multimodal LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25451)
*Zili Zhang,Chengxu Yang,Shenglong Zhang,Chenyu Wang,Yufan Zhang,Tuo Dai,Zhouyang Li,Yuhong Ge,Chao Jin,Xin Jin,Yuliang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BigMac提出了一种嵌套流水线训练方案，将多模态大模型中的编码器和生成器计算嵌套到原始LLM流水线中，在保持LLM激活内存复杂度不变的同时，将编码器和生成器的激活内存复杂度降至O(1)，从而打破计算效率与内存使用的帕累托边界，实现1.08-1.9倍训练加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型训练面临模型异构和数据异构的双重挑战，现有系统重新设计了训练流水线，但在计算效率和内存效率之间存在帕累托边界，优化一个指标必然牺牲另一个指标。

Method: BigMac的核心思想是将编码器和生成器的计算优雅地嵌套到原始LLM流水线中，形成依赖安全的嵌套流水线结构。该设计将编码器和生成器的激活内存复杂度降至O(1)，同时保持LLM的激活内存复杂度不变。

Result: 在多个MLLM和训练负载上，BigMac实现了1.08-1.9倍的训练加速，且随着批次大小增加，内存使用保持稳定。

Conclusion: BigMac打破了计算效率与内存使用之间的帕累托边界，实现了多模态大模型训练中计算和内存的同步优化。

Abstract: Training multimodal large language models (MLLMs) is challenged by both model and data heterogeneity. Existing systems redesign the training pipeline to address these challenges, but remain bound by a Pareto frontier between compute and memory efficiency, improving one only at the expense of the other. We present BigMac, a new training pipeline for multimodal LLMs. The core idea of BigMac is to elegantly nest the encoder and generator computation into the original LLM pipeline, forming a dependency-safe nested pipeline structure. With this design, BigMac reduces the activation memory complexity of the encoder and generator to O(1) while keeping the activation memory complexity of the LLM unchanged. At the same time, it achieves the same computational efficiency as the idealized setting with unlimited memory. As a result, BigMac breaks the Pareto frontier between computational efficiency and memory usage, enabling simultaneous optimization of both computation and memory in MLLM training. We evaluate BigMac on multiple MLLMs and training workloads. Experimental results show that BigMac achieves a 1.08$\times$-1.9$\times$ training speedup over baseline systems while maintaining stable memory usage as batch size increases.

</details>


### [391] [Inferring the Size of Large Language Models From Popular Text Memorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29223)
*Ivica Nikolic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种黑盒方法，仅通过分析模型生成的文本输出，就能推断大语言模型参数数量的保守下界，并通过统计检验和缩放律估计器两种互补方法实现模型规模比较与参数数量估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流大语言模型的参数数量常被开发者隐瞒，而模型规模是理解其能力和成本的关键参考指标，现有方法难以在仅能访问文本输出的黑盒条件下推断参数规模。

Method: 基于一个关键观察：广泛流传的经典文本几乎存在于所有大规模预训练语料中，模型对这些文本的预测准确性反映了其记忆程度，而记忆能力受参数数量限制。方法包括：(1) 在不同文本片段长度上收集预测准确率，形成每个模型的精度分布向量；(2) 基于此向量构建两种推断方法：成对统计检验（比较两个模型大小）和缩放律估计器（通过PCA提取一维潜变量映射到参数量）。

Result: 在开源模型上验证了两种方法的准确性和可靠性；应用于闭源模型时，能够恢复内部产品层级关系，并揭示不同开发者在参数量扩展策略上的显著差异——有的呈现代际增长，有的则受限于严格的参数天花板。

Conclusion: 即使在严格的API限制下，也能够系统性地探测隐藏的模型设计选择，表明模型参数规模这一关键信息可以通过文本输出进行有效推断。

Abstract: The parameter counts of the most widely used large language models (LLMs) are often withheld by their developers, leaving model size -- a primary reference point for interpreting capabilities and costs -- largely undisclosed. We propose a black-box method to infer conservative lower bounds on LLM size from generated text outputs alone, requiring nothing beyond the ability to submit text fragments and observe next-token predictions. Our approach is grounded in a key observation: popular, widely-circulated texts -- such as classical literature, religious texts, and foundational documents -- are present in virtually every large-scale pretraining corpus, and how accurately a model predicts the next word across text fragments of varying length is a reliable signal of how much it has memorized them, which in turn is fundamentally limited by its total parameter count. We aggregate this memorization signal across a diverse corpus of texts and fragment lengths into a single accuracy profile vector per model, and build two complementary inference methods on top of it: a pairwise statistical test that determines which of two models is larger, and a scaling-law estimator that extracts a one-dimensional latent index from these vectors via Principal Component Analysis (PCA) to map the aggregated signal to a parameter count. Validated on a broad set of open-weight models, both methods produce accurate and reliable lower bounds. When applied to popular closed-weight models, our framework recovers internal product hierarchies and reveals a clear divergence in industry scaling strategies: while some developers yield significantly higher bounds indicative of large generational parameter growth, others operate under strict parameter ceilings, demonstrating that hidden design choices can be systematically probed even under strict API limitations.

</details>


### [392] [Evolving Features vs Evolving Entire Trees with GP for Interpretable Survival Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30119)
*Thalea Schlender,Peter A. N. Bosman,Tanja Alderliesten*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出使用遗传规划（GP）多目标进化方法来构建浅层生存树：一方面进化可解释的高阶特征组合，另一方面联合优化树结构与非线性分裂逻辑，在保持可解释性的同时提升预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生存树虽然直观可解释，但为达到高精度通常需要生长为大树，从而丧失可解释性；而贪心构建策略可能错过全局最优分裂组合，限制预测性能。因此需要一种方法在浅层树（保持可解释性）中实现高精度。

Method: 1) 使用遗传规划（GP）多目标进化可解释的高阶特征组合；2) 研究这些特征组合与不同树归纳策略的交互；3) 提出一种进化方法联合优化生存树结构和非线性分裂逻辑。

Result: 在两个真实数据集上，进化特征构建在不同树归纳策略和两种树深度下均提升了预测性能。

Conclusion: 多目标进化整棵树的方法因其速度快、表示灵活，最具未来前景。

Abstract: Survival analysis concerns the task of predicting the time until an event occurs. Often used in the medical field, survival analysis deals with incomplete (i.e., censored) data, for instance, from patients who did not experience the event during the duration of the study. For practical use, both accuracy and interpretability are important.
  Survival trees are easy-to-follow survival models that split the patient cohort recursively into discrete patient groups. Whilst survival trees can capture complex relationships, they typically need to grow large, threatening interpretability. Moreover, survival trees are often built using greedy approaches that may overlook globally optimal split combinations, limiting predictive performance.
  Shallow survival trees require expressive, higher-order feature combinations to achieve competitive accuracy. We therefore use genetic programming to multi-objectively evolve inherently inspectable feature sets and study how they interact with different tree induction strategies. We further introduce an evolutionary approach that jointly optimises the survival tree structure and the non-linear split logic.
  Our findings demonstrate that evolutionary feature construction improves predictive performance across different tree induction strategies on two real-world datasets and two different survival tree depths. Given its speed and flexible presentation, the multi-objective evolution of entire trees likely holds the most future promise.

</details>


### [393] [Building Better Activation Oracles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02609)
*Jan Bauer,Celeste De Schamphelaere,Adam Karvonen,Niclas Luick,Neel Nanda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过四项改进（在线策略训练、会话数据集优化、多层输入、注入公式改进）提升了激活解释器（AO）的训练机制，并开源了首个AO质量综合评估套件AObench，为可扩展端到端可解释性奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有激活解释器存在幻觉、模糊性以及文本反转混淆等问题，难以评估，亟需改进训练机制和评估方法。

Method: 四项改进：1）采用在线策略训练（on-policy rollouts）；2）改进会话数据集；3）输入更多层残差流激活；4）改进注入公式。同时构建并开源AObench评估套件。

Result: 能力提升边际化，但生活质量改进显著；开源了首个AO质量综合评估套件。

Conclusion: 本研究为改进AO及其他可解释性模型奠定了基础，推动了可扩展端到端可解释性范式的发展。

Abstract: Activation Oracles (AOs) are promising methods for interpreting residual stream activations. However, current AOs face important issues, such as hallucinations and vagueness. Additionally, text-inversion confounds make them hard to evaluate. To this end, we improve the Activation Oracle (AO) training regime in four ways: training on on-policy rollouts, improving the conversational dataset, feeding more layers and an improvement to the injection formula. The capability improvements are marginal, but quality of life improvements are quite substantial. In addition, we open source the first comprehensive evaluation suite for AO quality, which we call AObench. Overall, we hope that our work sets a foundation that helps improve AOs and other models in the paradigm of scalable, end-to-end interpretability.

</details>


### [394] [Perplexity Can Miss SAE Feature Damage Under Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03002)
*Evan Duan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 量化模型在困惑度指标上表现正常时，其稀疏自编码器（SAE）特征可能已严重退化。在Gemma-2-2B上，INT7困惑度改善但18.7%的活跃特征受损；INT6下仅51.3%特征存活。特征存活率可从全精度特征统计量预测（AUC 0.92-0.97）。RTN量化与幅度剪枝损伤的特征集高度重叠（Jaccard 0.79-0.86）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化模型通常在困惑度或下游准确率接近全精度模型时被认为是可接受的，但行为等价性不一定意味着特征保真度。论文发现全精度模型用于可解释性的SAE特征可能在权重舍入后发生变化，而行为指标可能无法捕捉这种特征层面的损伤。

Method: 使用冻结的SAE作为固定测量基础，对全精度和RTN量化后的激活值在相同token上进行编码，通过Pearson相关系数测量每个特征在不同比特宽度（INT8到INT4）下的存活率。使用Pythia-70M和Gemma-2-2B两个模型，并比较RTN量化与幅度剪枝对特征集的影响。

Result: 困惑度会掩盖特征损伤；特征存活是渐变的而非断崖式下降；大多数未存活特征是被模糊而非完全破坏；特征存活率可从全精度特征统计量高精度预测（AUC 0.92-0.97）；RTN量化和幅度剪枝损伤的特征集高度重叠。

Conclusion: 仅凭行为指标不足以证明全精度模型的可解释性发现可以迁移到量化模型，需要引入特征层面的审计来评估压缩对模型可解释性的影响。

Abstract: Quantization is a standard path to deploying large language models, and quantized models are typically judged acceptable when perplexity or downstream accuracy remains close to the full-precision original. But behavioral parity need not imply feature fidelity: the sparse-autoencoder (SAE) features used to interpret a full-precision model may change after weight rounding. We test this directly by using a frozen SAE as a fixed measurement basis, encoding full-precision and round-to-nearest (RTN) quantized activations on identical tokens, and measuring per-feature survival by Pearson correlation across bit-widths from INT8 to INT4 on Pythia-70M and Gemma-2-2B. Our central finding is that perplexity can miss feature damage: on Gemma-2-2B, INT7 improves perplexity while degrading 18.7% of active SAE features, and under sliding-window evaluation INT6 also improves perplexity while only 51.3% of active features survive. Feature survival is graded rather than cliff-like, with 62.4% of active Pythia features and 51.3% of active Gemma features surviving at INT6; most non-surviving features are blurred rather than fully damaged. Survival is also predictable from full-precision feature statistics alone, with cross-validated AUC 0.92--0.97 and peak activation as the strongest marginal predictor. Finally, RTN quantization and matched-perplexity magnitude pruning damage strongly overlapping feature sets, with Jaccard overlap 0.79--0.86 and damage-score Spearman correlation 0.98. These results show that behavioral metrics alone are insufficient evidence that full-precision interpretability findings transfer to quantized models, motivating feature-level audits of compression.

</details>


### [395] [Local Guidance, Global Impact: Gaussian-Reshaped Trust Region Unlocks Behavior Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03382)
*Bingxu Liu,Jiashun Liu,Johan Obando-Ceron,Hao Wang,Runze Liu,Pablo Samuel Castro,Aaron Courville,Ling Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了PPO在非平稳/持续环境中的失败源于缺乏几何感知的局部更新，并提出GTR（高斯信任域策略优化）方法，通过有界非单调的高斯核重构信任域，配合混合高斯锚点机制，在多种复杂环境下取得优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准PPO在持续学习和非平稳环境中表现不佳，其失败并非由于模型容量不足或裁剪限制过严，而是缺乏几何感知的优化引导，导致方向低效的局部更新，阻碍行为模式的转换。

Method: 提出GTR方法：(1) 使用高斯核重塑信任域，产生有界非单调的约束——在局部提供强稳定性，在持续高优势更新下渐进放松；(2) 引入混合高斯锚点机制，自适应追踪近期策略轨迹，减少陈旧参考带来的方差。

Result: GTR是架构无关的，在游戏、仿真机器人控制、开放世界探索和语言模型后训练等多个场景中均取得优异性能。

Conclusion: 几何感知的信任域设计是复杂非平稳环境下鲁棒强化学习的一个有前景的方向。

Abstract: While Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) demonstrates strong performance in stationary settings, we show that its standard optimization paradigm struggles in continual and non-stationary environments. The failure does not stem from insufficient model capacity or overly restrictive clipping. Instead, PPO performs persistent, directionally inefficient local updates, which indicates a lack of geometry-aware guidance for accumulating meaningful behavioral change and ultimately hindering transitions toward new behavior patterns. Although divergence-based regularization introduces partial geometric awareness, its monotonically increasing penalties implicitly discourage large policy deviations, even when such shifts are necessary for effective adaptation. To address this limitation, we propose Gaussian Trust Region Policy Optimization (GTR), which reshapes the trust region using a Gaussian kernel. The resulting constraint is bounded and non-monotonic, providing strong local stability while progressively relaxing under sustained high-advantage updates. To further improve robustness, we introduce a Mixture Gaussian Anchor that adapts to recent policy trajectories, reducing variance induced by stale references. GTR is architecture-agnostic and achieves strong performance across games, simulated robotic control, open-world exploration, and language model post-training. These results demonstrate that geometry-aware trust-region design can be a promising direction for robust reinforcement learning in complex non-stationary environments. Our code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/GTR_demo/README.md.

</details>


### [396] [Analytical Evaluation of DCA Convergence Properties for Minimizing Prediction Functions of Gaussian RBF Support Vector Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03559)
*Yohei Kakimoto,Yuto Omae,Hirotaka Takahashi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对RBF核支持向量回归(SVR)模型的非凸优化问题，提出了基于DC(Difference of Convex)算法(DCA)的框架。通过利用RBF核的解析结构构造显式DC分解，推导了强凸性参数下界μ和梯度Lipschitz常数上界L的闭式表达式，二者均由训练后对偶系数和C_α、RBF核参数γ及DC分解参数ρ共同决定，且共享主导项C_αρ。数值实验表明C_αρ是表征DCA收敛特性和初始点依赖性的主要量，并可分解为C→C_α和γ→ρ两条独立路径，其变化主要由SVR超参数(C,γ)控制。该结果使得DCA在RBF-SVR上的收敛特性可通过单一标量C_αρ预先评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对RBF核SVR模型预测函数构成的非凸优化问题，现有方法缺乏对其收敛特性的理论分析工具，难以在训练前后有效评估DCA算法的收敛行为。

Method: 利用RBF核的解析结构构造显式DC分解，推导强凸性参数下界μ和梯度Lipschitz常数上界L的闭式表达式，并通过六类基准函数进行数值实验验证C_αρ的主导作用。

Result: 推导出μ和L的闭式表达式，二者共享主导项C_αρ；数值实验表明C_αρ是表征DCA收敛特性和初始点依赖性的主要单量，可分解为C→C_α和γ→ρ两条独立路径。

Conclusion: DCA在RBF-SVR上的收敛特性可通过单一标量C_αρ预先评估：训练前可近似从(C,γ)估计，训练后可精确闭式求解。

Abstract: For nonconvex optimization problems whose objective is the prediction function of a trained Support Vector Regression (SVR) model with the Gaussian radial basis function (RBF) kernel (RBF-SVR), we present a framework that applies the difference of convex functions (DC) algorithm (DCA) by exploiting the analytical structure of the RBF kernel to construct an explicit DC decomposition. Specifically, we derive in closed form both the lower bound $μ$ of the strong convexity parameter of the DC components and the upper bound $L$ of the gradient Lipschitz constant of the subproblem. Both $μ$ and $L$ are determined solely by the post-training dual-coefficient sum $C_α$ and the RBF kernel parameter $γ$, together with the DC decomposition parameter $ρ$, and they share a common leading term $C_αρ$. Through numerical experiments on six benchmark functions, we show that $C_αρ$ is the primary single quantity characterizing both the convergence properties and the initial-point dependence of DCA, and further demonstrate that it decomposes into two independent pathways, $C \to C_α$ and $γ\to ρ$, with its primary variation governed by the SVR hyperparameters $(C, γ)$. Together, these results allow the convergence properties of DCA on RBF-SVR to be assessed in advance through the single scalar quantity $C_αρ$: approximately from $(C, γ)$ before training, and exactly in closed form after training.

</details>


### [397] [Spectral Scaling Laws of Muon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04058)
*Gagik Magakyan,Pablo Parrilo,Asuman Ozdaglar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了Muon优化器中Newton-Schulz迭代的奇异值谱行为，发现不同层类型的奇异值量化值随模型规模呈现幂律分布，并据此提出分层自适应NS配置策略，以在保证正交化质量的同时减少计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon优化器使用Newton-Schulz (NS)迭代近似正交归一化，但NS对小奇异值方向的处理存在失效风险。目前缺乏对训练过程中动量矩阵奇异值谱行为的系统性理解，尤其不清楚其如何随模型规模变化，这导致NS迭代次数的选择缺乏理论依据。

Method: 在77M到2.8B参数的不同规模模型上，逐层跟踪动量缓冲区的奇异值分位数，观察其随训练步数的演化规律，并分析稳定值随模型规模的幂律缩放关系。

Result: 训练初期后奇异值分位数迅速稳定，稳定值由层类型和模型规模决定，且遵循清晰的幂律分布。中浅层幂律指数较小（约M^{-0.25}），而深层指数可达M^{-0.96}，意味着后者在大规模下会进入NS失效区域。

Conclusion: 该研究为不同规模模型提供了分层感知的NS配置选择原则：中浅层可用较少NS迭代，深层则需增加迭代次数或调整系数，从而在保证更新质量的前提下避免不必要的计算开销。

Abstract: Orthonormalized update rules have rapidly become a leading choice of optimizer for training large language models, with recent open-source state-of-the-art models adopting Muon. To keep these updates tractable, Muon performs the orthonormalization with the Newton--Schulz (NS) iteration. Since NS is only approximate, directions with small singular values fail to be orthonormalized. In Muon, NS is applied to the momentum matrix at every step, yet little is known about how the singular value spectrum of these momentum matrices behaves during training, or how that behavior changes with model size. We present the first systematic study of this question. Tracking singular value quantiles of the momentum buffer across layers in models ranging from 77M to 2.8B parameters, we observe a consistent picture: after a short burn-in, the quantiles stabilize at a value determined by the layer type and model size. These stabilization values follow remarkably clean power laws in model size, with layer-dependent exponents. Layers up to mid-late depth scale very mildly with model size $M$ (around $M^{-0.25}$), so the standard 5-step NS configuration used at academic scale will continue to orthonormalize them at much larger scales. Some of the late layers, however, scale much more aggressively (up to $M^{-0.96}$) and will fall into the NS failure regime at frontier scale unless one uses more NS iterations or better-tuned coefficients. NS iterations are computationally expensive at scale; our laws give practitioners a principled, layer-aware recipe for choosing the minimum NS configuration that still orthonormalizes the directions that matter -- avoiding unnecessary computation without sacrificing update quality.

</details>


### [398] [Scenario Generation for Risk-Aware Reinforcement Learning with Probably Approximately Safe Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04812)
*Mohit Prashant,Arvind Easwaran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于变分自编码器（VAE）和概率屏障证书的双优化方法，通过上下界屏障证书的差异区域采样来收紧安全概率边界，为深度强化学习策略在过渡扰动下提供更紧的安全保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习策略易受过渡扰动影响导致未知或不安全行为，而现有通过采样轨迹构建概率屏障证书的方法在状态空间探索不足时难以获得紧致的上下界，因此需要一种能处理过渡不确定性的更紧安全概率边界方法。

Method: 1. 使用变分自编码器（VAE）近似状态空间分布；2. 利用状态的潜在特征构建上界和下界概率屏障证书，形成双优化问题；3. 下界提供更保守的安全区域估计，上界提供更宽松的估计；4. 在上下界的差异区域（非鲁棒区域）采样，通过迭代优化收紧边界。

Result: 实验结果表明该方法能够提供更紧的概率安全保证，上下界之间的差异（非鲁棒区域）在训练过程中得到有效缩小。

Conclusion: 通过VAE潜在特征和双优化框架，可以在过渡不确定性下为RL策略提供更紧的安全概率保证，非鲁棒区域的定向采样是提升边界紧致性的关键。

Abstract: Guaranteeing safety is critical to the deployment of reinforcement learning (RL) agents in the real-world, especially as policies learned using deep RL may demonstrate susceptibility to transition perturbations that result in unknown or unsafe behaviour. A method of policy verification is to construct probabilistic barrier-certificates by sampling policy trajectories with respect to safety constraints, thereby demarcating known safe behaviour from unknown behaviour. Obtaining tight upper and lower bounds on the probability of violation of these constraints may be difficult if the policy is susceptible to transition uncertainty or perturbation that places the agent in insufficiently explored states. To address this, we approximate the distribution of the encountered state-space using a variational autoencoder (VAE) and construct upper and lower-bound barrier-certificates using latent characteristics of states to optimize for regions of known, safe behaviour with high confidence. We frame this in our work as a dual optimization problem where the lower-bound barrier-certificate presents a more conservative estimate of the safe region than the upper-bound barrier-certificate. Sampling states that lie within the set difference of the two during training, i.e. the non-robust region, allows us to tighten the upper and lower bounds to provide sharper probabilistic guarantees on safety. Within our study, we describe the guarantees placed and demonstrate the tightness of our bounds experimentally.

</details>


### [399] [Reinforcement Learning from Rich Feedback with Distributional DAgger](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05152)
*Rishabh Agrawal,Jacob Fein-Ashley,Paria Rashidinejad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DistIL，一种基于前向交叉熵的分布模仿学习算法，利用丰富的反馈信号（如执行轨迹、工具输出、专家纠正等）进行策略优化，克服了传统RLVR仅依赖二元正确性奖励的局限性，并证明了单调策略改进和遗憾界保证。在科学推理、编程和数学问题求解等多个领域优于RLVR和基于自蒸馏的RL基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）方法仅使用最终答案是否正确这一单比特信号进行奖励，忽略了执行轨迹、工具输出、专家纠正和模型自评估等丰富的反馈信息，限制了推理模型的性能提升。

Method: 提出DistIL，一种分布化DAgger算法的变体。学习器在当前策略访问的状态上具有对专家分布的局部访问权限，通过优化前向交叉熵目标实现策略学习。该目标支持黑盒专家，其序列级梯度通过将未来的专家-学生分歧反向传播到早期决策来实现丰富的信用分配。

Result: 1) 基于反向KL或Jensen-Shannon散度的自蒸馏RL目标无法保证单调策略改进，即使专家奖励更高也可能增加较差动作的概率；2) 前向交叉熵保证单调策略改进并具有遗憾界保证；3) 该目标优化教师加权成功可能性的下界，从而提升Pass@N指标；4) 在科学推理、编程和硬数学问题求解等多个领域优于RLVR和自蒸馏RL基线。

Conclusion: 前向交叉熵学习方法通过利用丰富的反馈信号，在理论上保证了单调策略改进，在实践上显著优于现有方法，是一种更有效的推理模型训练范式。

Abstract: Reasoning models have advanced rapidly, but the dominant reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) recipe remains surprisingly narrow: sample many responses and reward each with a single bit indicating whether the final answer is correct. Yet many settings provide rich feedback, including execution traces, tool outputs, expert corrections, and model self-evaluations. We study how to use such feedback through a distributional variant of the classic imitation learning algorithm DAgger, where the learner has local access to an expert distribution on states visited by the current policy. This yields a simple forward cross-entropy objective that admits a blackbox expert and whose sequence-level gradient {conduct rich credit assignment by propagating} future expert-student disagreement back to earlier decisions. We show that prior RL with self-distillation objectives based on reverse KL or Jensen-Shannon fail to guarantee monotonic policy improvement: even when the expert has higher reward, their updates may increase probability on worse actions. In contrast, we show that forward cross-entropy admits monotonic policy improvement and enjoys guarantees on regret. We further show that our objective optimizes a lower bound on teacher-weighted likelihood of success, leading to improved Pass@N. Empirically, our approach, DistIL, improves over RLVR and RL with self-distillation baselines across a variety of domains: scientific reasoning, coding, and solving hard mathematical problems.

</details>


### [400] [The Post-GCN Decade Revisited: Curvature-Stratified Evaluation of Relational Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06397)
*Shuo Wang,Xiangyu Wang,Quanxin Wang,Bailin Wu,Bokui Wang,Shunyang Huang,Boyan Deng,Haonan Liu,Ruiyi Fang,Zhenxiang Xu,Boyu Wang,Zhao Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出现有关系学习评测中扁平化排行榜存在系统性偏差，提出基于曲率分层的评估框架，发现模型性能本质依赖于数据几何结构而非通用可迁移，并建立了包含18个模型、14个数据集的曲率分层基准CurvBench。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关系学习评测依赖扁平化排行榜，跨异构数据集平均性能，隐含假设统一底层结构，导致系统性偏差：掩盖了几何相关的性能变化，可能得出关于模型泛化的误导性结论。

Method: 提出曲率分层评估框架，将数据集按曲率划分为正曲率、负曲率和近零曲率三个区域；在14个数据集上评估18个代表性模型（包括GCN、图基础模型和表格学习方法），分析模型排名在曲率区域内和跨区域的稳定性。

Result: 模型排名在同一曲率区域内高度稳定，但跨曲率区域显著变化，表明性能本质上是几何依赖的而非通用可迁移。发现了图基础模型相比几何对齐的GNN在某些区域收益递减的现象。

Conclusion: 提出几何感知的评估协议，相比标准聚合基准能提供更可靠和可解释的比较。释放了代码、曲率分层数据集划分和评估工具以支持可复现的评估。

Abstract: Current evaluation practices in relational learning rely heavily on flat leaderboards that average performance across heterogeneous datasets, implicitly assuming a uniform underlying structure. We show that this assumption introduces systematic bias: it obscures geometry-dependent performance variations and can lead to misleading conclusions about model generalization. In this work, we identify intrinsic geometry as a key latent factor governing model effectiveness. We demonstrate that conventional aggregated metrics mask critical performance trade-offs that only become visible when datasets are stratified by their geometric properties. To address this issue, we introduce a curvature-stratified evaluation framework that partitions datasets into positive, negative, and near-zero curvature regimes. Our benchmark evaluates 18 representative models including Graph Convolutional Networks (GCNs), Graph Foundation Models (GFMs), and tabular learning methods across 14 datasets. We find that model rankings are highly stable within each curvature regime but shift significantly across regimes, indicating that performance is fundamentally geometry-dependent rather than universally transferable. Notably, we identify regimes where GFMs offer diminishing returns compared to geometry-aligned GNNs. Based on these findings, we propose a geometry-aware evaluation protocol that yields more reliable and interpretable comparisons than standard aggregated benchmarks. We release all code, curvature-stratified dataset splits, and evaluation tools to support reproducible and rigorous assessment of future relational learning methods. Code and datasets are provided in our project homepage: https://sirbabbage.github.io/CurvBench_HOME/.

</details>
